### [1] [Sightline: Long-Window Speculative Decoding for Fast SVG Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23807)
*Yanchao Li,Ben Gao,Jiaqing Xie,Yusu Zhang,Beilun Wang,Yanbo Wang,Xiaohua Xu,Nanqing Dong,Tianfan Fu,Zhongyu Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sightline 提出将投机解码的验证窗口扩展至草稿器的预测地平线，并配合与窗口等宽的草稿树，实现 SVG 生成的快速无损解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SVG 生成模型逐 token 前向解码速度慢；现有块式投机解码在 SVG 上常整块被接受，仅在验证窗口处截断，草稿命中稀少且成簇，且深块处准确率远高于自然语言，说明现用窗口过短，未充分利用草稿器能力。

Method: 通过游程恒等式与三个命题论证：候选足够宽时加长窗口收益递增，直至草稿器地平线。据此设计 Sightline：验证窗口长度等于草稿器地平线，草稿树宽度足以填满该窗口。

Result: 在 MMSVG、MMSVG-2M、SArena 六个基准、三个模型上的文生 SVG 与图生 SVG 中，Sightline 均为最快的无损解码器；在 SArena 统一循环控制下仍最快；在另外四个家族六个模型（SVG 代码、压缩 SVG token、Lottie JSON）的几乎所有设置中领先。分析表明优势来自被短窗口截断的轮次，涵盖无环与循环结构。

Conclusion: 矢量图形上的投机解码应“验证到草稿器所能看到之处”；更宽的窗口加更宽的草稿树可无损加速，且投机解码有助于通过渲染选样，优于按似然选样。

Abstract: Scalable Vector Graphics (SVG) generation models write a graphic as code with one forward pass per token, so drawing is slow. Speculative decoding speeds them up losslessly, and block drafters guess many tokens per pass. On SVG, many of their rounds accept the whole draft and end only at the verification window. We find that their misses are rare and clumped, and that they stay accurate far deeper into a block than on natural language. A run-length identity and three propositions show that a longer window pays once the candidates are wide, up to the drafter's horizon. We propose Sightline, which pairs a window as long as the drafter's horizon with a draft tree wide enough to fill it. Experiments cover text-to-SVG and image-to-SVG with three models on six benchmarks from MMSVG, MMSVG-2M and SArena. In every setting, Sightline is the fastest of the compared lossless decoders. On SArena, it stays the fastest when every decoder shares one loop control. It also leads in almost every setting of six models from four other families, which write SVG code, compressed SVG tokens or Lottie JSON. Analyses trace the lead to rounds that shorter windows cut off, both in loop-free content and in loops. Speculative decoding also saves time for choosing samples by rendering, which beats choosing by likelihood. These results suggest that speculative decoding on vector graphics should verify as far as the drafter sees. Code and data are available at https://github.com/LiZaiyuan0619/Sightline.

</details>


### [2] [DRIFT: Detachable Reasoning for Runtime-Flexible and Efficient End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24719)
*Emad Aghajanzadeh,Bethany Anne Wong,Leonhard Kunze,Moritz Thoma,Maximilian Forstenhäusler,Nael Fasfous,Manoj Rohit Vemparala,Pierpaolo Mori,Christos Anagnostopoulos,Daniel Mueller-Gritschneder,Ulf Schlichtmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DRIFT：一种仅255M参数的端到端自动驾驶框架，用轻量语言模型（<150M）做场景描述与动作评论，并通过蒸馏替代推理特征实现推理速率可调的灵活规划，在Bench2Drive上DS达97.03、成功率89.39%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM引入端到端自动驾驶虽能提供场景描述与推理能力，但计算开销大；且此前未能证明可在运行时开启/关闭语言推理而不损害闭环性能，阻碍了实际部署。

Method: 1) 将中间驾驶表征高效投影到语言嵌入空间，使紧凑LM（<150M）完成VQA与动作评论；2) 设计运行时灵活的规划模块，训练时使用从LM蒸馏出的替代推理特征（surrogate reasoning features），推理时可条件于LM或替代特征，从而在部署受限时完全绕过LM；3) 推理时以选定推理速率交错使用LM与替代特征。

Result: Bench2Drive上DS最高97.03、成功率89.39%（三次运行均值），与最强非语言模型相当且远小于现有VLM模型；在真实世界NAVISIM与AlpaSim基准上取得有竞争力结果，计算成本显著低于现有VLM方法。

Conclusion: 通过轻量LM与替代推理特征的灵活规划设计，DRIFT在保持强闭环驾驶性能的同时大幅降低计算开销，实现了语言推理可按需启停的可部署端到端自动驾驶方案。

Abstract: The integration of vision-language models (VLMs) into end-to-end (E2E) autonomous driving has demonstrated strong potential by providing scene description and reasoning capabilities. However, VLMs are computationally expensive, and switching language reasoning on and off at runtime without hurting closed-loop performance has not been demonstrated, which hinders practical adoption. To address these issues, we propose DRIFT, an E2E autonomous driving framework that integrates a lightweight language model (LM) for visual question answering and action commentary. By efficiently projecting intermediate driving representations into the language embedding space, we demonstrate that a compact LM (<150M parameters) can achieve strong scene description and action commentary. Furthermore, we introduce a runtime-flexible planning module that is trained on surrogate reasoning features distilled from the LM and can be conditioned on either the LM or the surrogate reasoning features at inference. This design enables bypassing the LM entirely when required by deployment constraints, without sacrificing driving performance. With only 255M parameters, DRIFT reaches up to a Driving Score (DS) of 97.03 and a Success Rate of 89.39% (mean over three runs) on Bench2Drive by interleaving its LM and surrogate reasoning at a chosen reasoning rate. This is on par with the strongest non-language models while being far more compact than existing VLM-based models. DRIFT further achieves competitive results on the real-world NAVSIM and AlpaSim benchmarks at significantly lower computational cost than existing VLM-based approaches. Project page: https://drift-e2e.github.io/

</details>


### [3] [The Elephant in the Question: Unified Models Picture the Concepts Behind Their Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24793)
*Tobias Braun,Marcus Rohrbach,Lorenzo Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 V-lens：利用统一多模态模型中图像生成器对文本隐状态的访问，实现无需训练解码器的 token 隐状态可视化，并提出 lens tuning 改善可视化准确性与文本答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可视化中间表示有助于检视模型输出背后的推理步骤；统一多模态模型在同一架构中结合理解与生成，其图像生成器可访问文本处理过程中形成的表示，这一连接尚未被用于隐状态可视化。

Method: V-lens 直接借助图像生成器将指定 token 的隐状态映射为图像，无需训练解码器；在四个扩散型与两个纯自回归统一模型上验证；通过控制实验考察内容随上下文、token 位置与深度的变化；进一步提出 lens tuning，用图像通过 V-lens 对选定隐状态进行监督。

Result: 生成图像揭示了连接问题与答案的关键中间概念，即使这些概念从未被显式命名；在 BAGEL、SenseNova-U1、HunyuanImage-3.0 上，代词指代对象的正确可视化率从 2–11% 提升至 86–94%；该视觉监督还意外提升了文本答案质量。

Conclusion: 统一多模态模型中理解与生成共享表示，可用于无损、免训练的内部状态可视化与监督；视觉层面监督可反向改善语言侧输出，提示跨模态表示对齐具有实用价值。代码已开源。

Abstract: Visualizing intermediate representations could help us inspect the reasoning steps underlying a model output. Unified multimodal models offer this opportunity by combining understanding and generation in one architecture. Their image generators can access representations formed while processing text. We introduce the V-lens, which uses this connection to visualize a chosen token's hidden state without training a decoder. Across four diffusion-based and two purely autoregressive unified models, the resulting images reveal key intermediate concepts that connect a question to its answer even when the concepts are never named explicitly. Controlled tests show how this content changes with context, token position, and depth. We then introduce lens tuning, which supervises selected hidden states with pictures through the V-lens. Across BAGEL, SenseNova-U1, and HunyuanImage-3.0, the rate of correct visualizations of pronoun referents rises from 2-11% to 86-94%. Surprisingly, such visual supervision also improves text answers without supervising the answer tokens directly. Code is available at https://github.com/multimodal-ai-lab/V-lens.

</details>


### [4] [DORMS: Image Segmentation via Depth-Ordered Region Merge Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23958)
*Furkan Mumcu,Yasin Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DORMS是一种无需神经网络或学习参数的纯算法图像分割方法，基于单目深度图构建深度有序合并树，在Cityscapes上超越所有对比的零样本神经分割模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索不依赖学习式分割模型的纯算法几何推理路径，利用现成单目深度估计器提供的深度图完成图像分割，以摆脱对神经网络训练与参数的依赖。

Method: 输入为现成单目深度估计器输出的深度图；通过确定性的并查集扫描构建深度有序合并树（depth-ordered merge trees），在合并过程中结合区域面积、深度持久性与空间接触约束引导区域合并；采用阈值搜索按指定目标区域数确定分割粒度；并引入全局深度去趋势以增强局部几何结构的可分性。

Result: 在COCO Panoptic、Cityscapes与ADE20K上的大量实验显示，相较经典分割基线有一致提升，与零样本神经分割模型相比具备竞争力；在Cityscapes上优于所有评测的零样本模型，在COCO与ADE20K上与多种方法相当。

Conclusion: 结果表明，纯算法的几何推理在无学习式分割模型的情况下仍具图像分割潜力。

Abstract: We introduce DORMS (Depth-Ordered Region Merging for Segmentation), a purely algorithmic approach to image segmentation from monocular depth maps, requiring no neural networks or learned parameters in the segmentation process. An off-the-shelf monocular depth estimator provides the input depth maps. Our method constructs depth-ordered merge trees through a deterministic union-find sweep, combining component area, depth persistence, and spatial contact constraints to guide region merging. A threshold search determines the segmentation granularity according to a specified target region count, while global depth detrending improves the separation of local geometric structures. Extensive experiments on COCO Panoptic, Cityscapes, and ADE20K demonstrate consistent improvements over classical segmentation baselines and competitive performance against zero-shot neural segmentation models. Notably, our method outperforms all evaluated zero-shot models on Cityscapes, while remaining competitive with several approaches on COCO and ADE20K. These results highlight the potential of purely algorithmic geometric reasoning for image segmentation without learned segmentation models.

</details>


### [5] [GRID-UAV: Geometry and Ray-depth Integrated Decision-making for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23491)
*Anas Anwarul Haq Khan,Amrin Kareem,Muhammad Umer Sheikh,Biplab Banerjee,Muhammad Haris Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GRID：将单目深度提升为射线校正的3D位置，并在训练阶段用冻结的多视图几何模型对齐视觉特征，从而同时获得显式与隐式3D接地，在TravelUAV上取得最优成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型用2D编码器编码图像，缺乏3D结构；无人机自主选择高度、需判断数十米外距离、且需关联前视与下视视角，单目深度估计不可靠。直接加入深度特征在长航线上几乎无增益（42.5% vs 42.6%），因此需要研究如何向策略提供3D信息。

Method: 通过失败分析发现两类3D接地方式的失效模式相反：显式3D提升（explicit lifting）航程中碰撞少但到达目标后常无法完成任务；隐式几何对齐（implicit alignment）完成率更高但碰撞更多。GRID结合两者：(1) 将单目深度提升为无人机坐标系下经射线校正的3D位置（双相机）；(2) 仅在训练时将视觉特征与冻结的多视图几何模型对齐，推理阶段无额外开销。

Result: 在显式提升基础上加入隐式对齐，使到达目标的比例由61.5%提升至69.5%（p<0.001）；在隐式对齐基础上加入显式提升，使到达后完成任务的比例由57.7%提升至61.4%（p<0.05）。在TravelUAV（3,005测试回合）所有划分上成功率最优，相对SOTA提升：已见场景9.5%、未见物体17.1%、未见地图25.4%。

Conclusion: 两种3D接地形式互补，显式提升改善到达目标能力，隐式对齐改善到达后的任务完成能力；将二者结合可在不增加推理成本的前提下显著且全面地提升空中导航策略的性能与泛化性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) encode images with 2D encoders that capture little 3D structure. A common approach is to feed the policy estimated depth, but a UAV chooses its own altitude, must judge distances tens of meters away, where monocular depth is less reliable, and must relate its forward and downward views. On long aerial routes, adding depth features directly brings no significant gain (42.5% vs. 42.6% success), so we study how 3D information should be given to the policy. Analyzing flight failures, we find that two forms of 3D grounding fail in opposite ways: explicit 3D lifting of depth collides less on the way but more often fails to finish at the target, while implicit alignment with a 3D geometry model finishes more often but collides more. We propose GRID, which combines both: (1) it lifts monocular depth into ray-corrected 3D positions in the UAV's frame for both cameras, and (2) during training only, it aligns its visual features with a frozen multi-view geometry model, at no inference cost. GRID adding explicit lifting to implicit alignment significantly increases how often the policy reaches the target ($61.5\% \rightarrow 69.5\%$, $p<0.001$), and adding implicit alignment to explicit lifting significantly increases how often it completes the task once there ($57.7\% \rightarrow 61.4\%$, $p<0.05$). On TravelUAV (3,005 test episodes), GRID achieves the best success rate on every split, with relative gains over the state of the art of 9.5% (seen), 17.1% (unseen objects) and 25.4% (unseen map).

</details>


### [6] [Autoregressive View Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23417)
*Jensen,Zhou,Samuel Rota Bulo,Denis Rozumny,Duncan Zauss,Peter Hedman,Nikhil Keetha,Philip Torr,Christian Rupprecht,Andrea Vedaldi,Peter Kontschieder*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AVM 将场景生成视为"沿相机路径的视图流"生成，通过统一 in-context 接口在图像/视频条件下自回归地生成短片段，无需显式 3D 表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 避免显式 3D 表示带来的重建误差与表示瓶颈，同时用一个统一接口覆盖重建、插值、外推等多种输入输出设定。

Method: 以视觉上下文完成重建与插值、以学习先验实现外推；从输入与生成历史中检索相关视图构成有界活跃上下文；用 flow matching 以 N-in-1-out 形式微调预训练双向视频扩散 Transformer，再在其自身 rollout 上调优因果模型。

Result: 训练规模约 8M 场景与视频片段；在相机控制、文本控制、视觉质量、动态性、稀疏与 4D 重建、长程一致性等基准上评估，生成能力与场景保持性表现良好。

Conclusion: 单一 in-context 接口即可适配多样的输入输出设定，自回归视图流是替代显式 3D 表示的一条可行路径；因果自回归调优可改善真实感、减少去噪步数并抑制长程漂移。

Abstract: We present the Autoregressive View Model (AVM), which generates a scene as a stream of views without constructing an explicit 3D representation. Through a unified in-context interface, AVM conditions on images or videos to generate short clips along a requested camera path. Visual context grounds reconstruction and interpolation, while a learned prior enables extrapolation into unseen regions. AVM retrieves relevant views from inputs and generated history to form a bounded active context. We fine-tune a pretrained bidirectional video diffusion transformer with flow matching in an N-in-1-out formulation. We then tune a causal model on its own rollouts to improve photorealism, reduce denoising steps, and limit long-horizon drift. We scale training to approximately 8M scenes and video clips. We evaluate generation capacity and scene persistence across benchmarks for camera control, text control, visual quality, dynamics, sparse and 4D reconstruction, and long-horizon consistency. The results demonstrate that AVM adapts to diverse input and output settings through one in-context interface.

</details>


### [7] [OCLDA-GS: Object-Centric Local Deformation-Aware Gaussian Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23416)
*Ashim Dahal,Nikesh Subedi,Rabab Abdelfattah,Nick Rahimi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OCLDA-GS：面向对象的流式3DGS框架，通过动态重分配对象与运动感知锚点、对象分组约束邻域、并基于对象级刚体变换做逐锚点局部形变校正，提升动态场景在线重建质量与时序一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效流式3DGS（如IGS）通过选取锚点高斯子集并做邻域插值实现快速在线重建，但会忽略对象特有的运动，并在对象边界附近引入不一致，导致大运动物体支持不足、边界精度下降。

Method: 提出OCLDA-GS：1) 动态重新分配对象与运动感知的锚点高斯，为大幅运动物体提供足够锚点支持；2) 将高斯邻域限制在对象分组内，以锐化对象边界；3) 估计对象级刚体变换（如手部运动），并据此学习逐锚点局部形变校正以刻画小幅形变（如衣物褶皱）。

Result: 在三个动态多视图视频数据集上超越先前基线：相同细化预算下，PSNR较Instant Gaussian Stream(IGS)在N3DV提升0.169 dB、Meeting Room提升0.202 dB、Google Immersive提升0.199 dB；细化时间步间平均PSNR下降分别减少9.2%（N3DV）与13.8%（Meeting Room）。

Conclusion: 以对象为中心、结合对象级刚体运动与局部形变校正的锚点分配策略，可有效提升流式3DGS的重建精度与细化步间的时序稳定性，验证了对象感知设计在FVV在线重建中的价值。

Abstract: Streaming 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) allows for online reconstruction of dynamic scenes from multi-view videos for Free Viewpoint Video (FVV). Current time and storage efficient methods rely on selecting a subset of anchor Gaussians and neighborhood interpolation for fast streamable 3DGS. However, these methods can overlook object-specific motion and introduce inconsistencies near object boundaries. We propose OCLDA-GS, an object-centric streaming framework that dynamically reallocates object and motion-aware Gaussian anchors to allocate anchor Gaussian support for objects undergoing large motion while at the same time, restricting the gaussian neighborhood within object groups for more precise object boundaries. Furthermore, it estimates object-wise rigid transformations (e.g. a hand moving) and leverages these estimates to learn per-anchor local deformation correction for small deformations (e.g. wrinkles on a shirt). Then, we conduct experiments on three dynamic multi-view video datasets and demonstrate improvements over the previous baselines alongside improved quality between refinement timesteps. Under matched refinement budgets, our method improves PSNR over Instant Gaussian Stream (IGS) by 0.169 dB on N3DV, 0.202 dB on Meeting Room and +0.199 on Google Immersive, while reducing the mean PSNR drop between refinement timesteps by 9.2% and 13.8% in N3DV and Meeting Room, respectively.

</details>


### [8] [Mapping the Design Space of Feature Sampling for Optical Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23822)
*Liping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAGE 用逐像素各向异性高斯期望替代单点采样，把采样支撑（范围与方向）变成可学习的显式设计变量，仅增1.5% MACs即显著提升光流零样本跨数据集泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光流方法（如RAFT的固定半径代价体积查找、WAFT的单点特征索引）对所有位置采用同一采样方案，未依据区域特性设计采样，往往冗余或不足。作者据此提出核心问题：特征采样的支撑（聚合证据的范围与方向）是否应针对每个查询位置单独学习。

Method: 提出 SAGE（Sampling with Anisotropic Gaussian Expectation）：在当前光流估计为中心的固定候选网格上，以逐像素各向异性高斯期望替代单点采样；由正定精度矩阵控制采样支撑的尺度、各向异性与朝向，单点采样作为其极限情形。

Result: 仅增加1.5% MACs，明显改善零样本跨数据集泛化。仅用 FlyingChairs 与 FlyingThings 训练时，Sintel clean/final 的 EPE 分别降低24.2%与16.4%，KITTI F1-epe 与 F1-all 分别降低7.2%与11.6%。

Conclusion: 将采样支撑作为显式、可分析的设计变量是有效的，为理解方向性特征聚合如何影响对应学习提供了原则性框架，并验证了逐位置自适应采样的必要性。

Abstract: The computation of correlation features is crucial for optical flow estimation. Existing methods (e.g., RAFT) perform fixed-radius lookups on a cost volume, yielding a set of correlation features for each position; alternatively, WAFT performs single-point feature indexing, yielding one correlation feature vector per position. None of them designs a suitable correlation feature sampling scheme for each position according to regional characteristics. Our insight is that not every position requires the same sampling scheme. Applying an identical sampling scheme to every position is either redundant or insufficient and ignores the specific characteristics of the corresponding region. This paper rethinks the design of feature sampling and investigates the following question: should the support of feature sampling---i.e., the extent and directions over which evidence is aggregated---be learned separately for each query position? To study this question, we propose SAGE (Sampling with Anisotropic Gaussian Expectation). This method replaces single-point sampling with a per-pixel anisotropic Gaussian expectation on a fixed candidate grid centered on the current optical flow estimate. A positive-definite precision matrix controls the scale, anisotropy, and orientation of the sampling support, with single-point sampling included as its limiting case. Consequently, the sampling support becomes an explicit and analyzable design variable and provides a principled way to analyze how directional feature aggregation affects correspondence learning. Extensive experiments show that SAGE, while adding only 1.5% multiply-accumulate operations (MACs), effectively improves zero-shot cross-dataset generalization. When trained only on FlyingChairs and FlyingThings, SAGE reduces the end-point error (EPE) by 24.2% and 16.4% on Sintel clean and final, and reduces KITTI F1-epe and F1-all by 7.2% and 11.6%.

</details>


### [9] [Keep the Reasoning, Read the Image: On-Demand Visual KV for Multi-Turn Reasoning VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23769)
*Ziyi Wang,Xiyue Zhang,Bing Hu,Guangmin Song,Chenyi Zhang,Li Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的ShelfKV：将全部视觉条目常驻CPU内存，每步解码把当前查询打分最高的视觉条目换入打分最低文本条目所占的注意力槽位，使图像永不被逐出、仅在需要时占用GPU注意力槽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理型VLM的KV cache淘汰策略让图像与推理共享同一预算，增长的推理把视觉条目挤出；多轮对话中图像损失随轮次显著放大（首轮-8分→一轮推理后-24分），说明图像缺的是保护而非容量。

Method: 在现有保留策略之上叠加：所有视觉KV条目保存在CPU内存；每个解码步按当前查询对视觉条目打分，将高分视觉条目换入低分文本条目的注意力槽位；并验证"固定视觉保留区"与"等量预算"的对照实验。

Result: 四个基准（三个对话式）、两种保留策略、两个推理VLM：对话场景下较最强基线DBTrimKV在同等GPU显存下最高提升20.3分，DBTrimKV用两倍显存时仍领先8.7分；稳定优于同等显存的固定视觉保留区，且不损害单轮问题准确率。

Conclusion: 图像应被保护免受推理挤占，但只在被读取时按需占用注意力槽位；共享预算下的视觉条目需要的是保护机制而非更大容量。

Abstract: Reasoning vision-language models (VLMs) think for thousands of tokens before they answer, and retention policies keep the KV cache affordable by pruning it as the reasoning grows. We find that this pruning sacrifices the image in multi-turn conversations: the image and the reasoning share one budget, so the growing reasoning crowds the visual entries out. A chart question that loses 8 points to the full cache when asked first loses 24 points when asked after a round of reasoning. Protecting a small visual reserve from eviction recovers most of this loss, while adding the same slots to the shared budget recovers considerably less. The image therefore lacks protection, not capacity. Based on this finding, we propose ShelfKV, a training-free method that works on top of existing retention policies. It keeps every visual entry in CPU memory and, at each decoding step, swaps the visual entries that the current query scores highest into the attention slots of the text entries it scores lowest. The image is never evicted, yet it occupies attention slots only while it is read. We evaluate ShelfKV on four benchmarks, three of them conversational, with two retention policies and two reasoning VLMs. In conversations, it improves the strongest existing policy, DBTrimKV, by up to 20.3 points at matched GPU memory, and still leads by 8.7 points when DBTrimKV uses twice the memory. It also consistently outperforms a fixed visual reserve of matched GPU memory in these settings, without hurting accuracy on standalone questions. These results show that the image should be protected from the reasoning but read only on demand.

</details>


### [10] [GTA: Generative Tower of Agents for Scalable Holonic Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23727)
*Zeng Tao,Yunuo Chen,Xiang Feng,Yumeng He,Peihao Li,Nanxiang Jiang,Yaowei Guo,Fanlian Zeng,Yin Yang,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GTA（Generative Tower of Agents），将自适应场景层级中的每个单元建模为 holon（既是自包含构建任务又是可组合场景单元），通过并行智能体自顶向下递归规划、自底向上生成与组装资产，并统一接口传递场景级需求，实现可扩展世界建模并生成含动态人物的4D世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展世界建模需在有限时间与上下文预算下，协调场景级目标与细粒度物体及动态角色；现有方法难以兼顾结构化布局、细节保真与高吞吐效率。

Method: GTA 将场景表示为自适应层级，每个单元为 holon（自包含构建任务+可组合场景单元）。并行智能体自顶向下递归规划层级，生成资产，再自底向上组装与精修；统一接口将场景级需求逐层下达，约束局部构建使各 holon 适配上层整体。随后在组装环境中填充受场景引导外观与运动的动态人物。

Result: 通过高吞吐并行执行加速可扩展世界建模，同时保持良好结构化布局与细粒度资产细节；最终生成包含动态人物的已实现4D世界。

Conclusion: 以 holon 抽象与并行递归规划—组装范式，GTA 在效率、层级结构与细节之间取得平衡，为可扩展4D世界建模提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Scalable world modeling requires coordinating scene-wide goals with fine-grained objects and dynamic characters under limited time and context budgets. We introduce GTA (Generative Tower of Agents), which represents every unit at every level of an adaptive scene hierarchy as a holon, both a self-contained construction task and a composable scene unit. Parallel agents recursively plan the hierarchy top-down, generate assets, and assemble and refine them bottom-up. Uniform interfaces carry scene-wide requirements down the hierarchy, guiding local construction so that each holon fits into the larger one. GTA accelerates scalable world modeling through high-throughput parallel execution while preserving well-structured layouts and fine-grained asset details. GTA then populates the assembled environments with dynamic human characters whose appearance and motion are guided by the scene, yielding realized 4D worlds. Project page: https://zt-zeng-tao.github.io/GTA/

</details>


### [11] [MoMoVL: Modality-Aware Adaptive Compression for On-Device Sparsely-Activated Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23695)
*Dongkeun Lee,Wonjun Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoMoVL：面向 MoE 架构视觉语言模型（VLM）的模态感知自适应混合精度量化框架，联合利用逐专家激活画像与逐模态量化敏感度来分配比特宽度，在端侧内存预算内实现跨模态推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧 VLM 需求增长（隐私、低延迟），MoE 虽计算高效但多专家结构带来显存/内存压力。混合精度量化适合 MoE 因专家重要性不均衡，但现有工作忽略了 VLM 中视觉与文本 token 的模态特异性对专家量化的影响。

Method: 基于经验发现的"模态诱导的专家间差异"，构建模态感知自适应压缩框架：联合建模每个专家的激活分布画像（per-expert activation profile）与每种模态的量化敏感度（per-modality quantization sensitivity），据此指导比特宽度分配。

Result: 相比 SOTA 混合精度基线，精度-压缩权衡更优；在其均匀精度基线基础上内存占用最多降低 15.44%，精度仅小幅下降。代码与数据已开源。

Conclusion: 在 MoE-VLM 量化中显式建模模态差异是提升端侧部署效率的关键，MoMoVL 在内存受限条件下取得更优的精度-压缩帕累托前沿。

Abstract: The rise of the agentic web has been accompanied by growing demand for on-device Vision-Language Models (VLMs) that perform diverse cross-modal web tasks with enhanced privacy and reduced latency. Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models are widely adopted in modern VLMs for compute-efficient scalability, yet their multi-expert architecture poses deployment challenges due to substantial memory demands. Mixed-precision quantization is an effective compression technique that reduces bit-widths while adapting to the heterogeneous hardware budgets of edge devices, particularly well-suited for MoE due to its inherent structural sparsity, which causes imbalanced importance among experts. While prior work has applied quantization for efficient MoE inference at the edge, we point out that in MoE-based VLMs, the modality-specific characteristics of visual and text tokens further impact expert quantization. To address this issue, we present MoMoVL, a modality-aware adaptive compression framework for MoE-based VLMs that brings vision-language inference within reach of on-device resource budgets. Motivated by our empirical findings on modality-induced disparities across experts, MoMoVL jointly exploits the per-expert activation profile and per-modality quantization sensitivity to guide bit-width assignment. Experimental results show that MoMoVL improves the accuracy-compression trade-off relative to state-of-the-art mixed-precision baselines and reduces memory footprint by up to 15.44% with a modest drop in accuracy over its uniform-precision baseline. Our code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/sco-edge/MoMoVL.

</details>


### [12] [Weight Space Programming for Vision Language Model Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23615)
*Avinash Goutham Aluguvelly*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在权重空间编程中，智能体对SmolVLM-256M与LFM2-VL-450M注入权重故障（投影器行缩放、视觉编码器噪声）后能定位并精确修复，但修复依赖故障计算残留的低位比特；将低位舍入抹去后，智能体在SmolVLM上完全失败，仅靠提示中一句指定统计量（如行范数分布）才能重新定位受损行，而交换行列无法被自发发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往权重空间编程研究关注"能否编辑成功"，却未追问成功的修复究竟依赖何种信息。作者希望厘清：无梯度直接改写权重的修复能力，是来自对模型结构的理解，还是来自故障生成过程本身留下的可检测痕迹。

Method: 在两个视觉语言模型（SmolVLM-256M、LFM2-VL-450M）权重中植入人工故障（投影器行重缩放、视觉编码器噪声），由编码智能体逐条对照原始权重核验其编辑；比较16位精度权重与经舍入抹除低位痕迹的权重两种条件；并测试提示中给出统计量线索、以及交换行列等保值变换下智能体的表现。

Result: 未加提示时智能体即可发现重缩放行与编码器噪声，推断未知缩放因子，将大部分乃至全部权重精确还原，准确率恢复至接近原始水平，且其写出的程序可无反馈地修复同模型未见检查点。舍入低位后，各模型对测试问题的回答不变，但智能体不再精确还原任何权重：SmolVLM上完全找不到故障，LFM2-VL上唯一收益来自重缩放答案词元嵌入并改变回答"是"的频率。重新在低位标记受损权重可恢复上述编辑定位；舍入权重仍保留重缩放的统计证据（存储值分布、行范数），提示中加入一句指明该统计量即可将编辑引导至受损行；交换行列在会话预算内均未被自发发现。

Conclusion: 权重空间修复的成败高度依赖故障生成过程残留的低位痕迹，而非仅为语义或统计理解；建议后续权重修复研究报告权重的数值格式，并设置"舍入对照"以区分真实修复与痕迹利用。

Abstract: In weight-space programming, a language model edits a network's weights directly, without gradients. We ask what a successful repair of this kind relies on. We plant faults in the weights of two vision-language models, SmolVLM-256M and LFM2-VL-450M, and check each edit made by a coding agent against the original weights. Without being told where to look, the agent finds rescaled rows in the projector and noise in the vision encoder of both models, infers the unknown scale factors, restores most or all of the rescaled weights to their exact original values, and returns accuracy to near its original level; programs that it writes from such weights repair unseen checkpoints of the same model without feedback. These repairs depend on a trace of how the faults were computed. The original weights have 16-bit precision, and faults computed in 32-bit arithmetic leave low bits that only the damaged weights carry. Rounding those bits away leaves each model's answers to our test questions unchanged, and the agent, unaided, then restores no weight exactly: on SmolVLM it no longer finds the damage, and in a single session on LFM2-VL its only gain comes from rescaling answer-token embeddings, which changes how often the model answers yes. Marking the damaged weights again in their low bits brings the edits back to them. Rounded weights still hold statistical evidence of the rescaling, in the distribution of stored values and in row norms, and one sentence in the prompt that names such a statistic directs the agent's edits to the damaged rows on both models. Swapped rows or columns, which leave the set of stored values unchanged, are not found unaided on either model within our session budgets. We recommend that studies of weight repair report the number format of the weights and include a rounded control.

</details>


### [13] [Learning Where and How Much to Sample: Volume-Adaptive k-Space Acquisition for Accelerated MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24081)
*Reyhaneh Neshatavar,Mohsen Yavartanoo,Kyoung Mu Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出体自适应的k空间采集框架，联合学习整个MRI体数据"采样哪些位置"与"各层采样多少"，并配套设计敏感的跨层重建网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速MRI通常采用预定义采样模式和统一的分层采样预算，忽略了切片间的解剖差异与层间关联，导致采样效率和信息利用率不高。

Method: 仅利用ACS自校准信号，从体数据全局解剖信息出发自适应分配各切片的采样预算并挑选有信息的k空间行；设计"切片—频率联合细化模块"协调采样决策，同时严格保持体级总采集预算不变；重建端采用敏感度感知的同步跨层重建网络，从ACS估计线圈敏感度并渐进细化相邻切片重建，施加硬k空间数据一致性约束；两网络以重建损失联合优化，推理时无需全采样k空间。

Result: 在膝关节与脑部MRI、多种对比度和多个加速倍数上的实验表明，采集框架呈现出预期的自适应行为，并对其重建性能进行了评估。

Conclusion: 体级自适应的联合采样与重建方法可在固定总体采集预算下实现更合理的采样分配，验证了跨切片信息与自适应采样策略在加速MRI中的有效性。

Abstract: Magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is an essential medical imaging technique, but its high costs and long acquisition times can limit accessibility. Accelerated MRI reduces acquisition time through k-space undersampling, yet conventional methods often rely on predefined sampling patterns and uniform slice budgets, overlooking anatomical variations and inter-slice relationships. In this paper, we propose a volume-adaptive k-space acquisition framework that jointly learns where and how much to sample across an entire MRI volume. Using only acquired auto-calibration signal (ACS) measurements, our method exploits volume-wide anatomical information to adaptively allocate sampling budgets across slices while identifying informative k-space lines. A learned joint slice--frequency refinement module coordinates sampling decisions while preserving an exact total volume-level acquisition budget. To reconstruct the acquired measurements, we design a sensitivity-aware synchronous cross-slice reconstruction network that estimates coil sensitivities from ACS measurements and progressively refines neighboring-slice reconstructions while enforcing hard k-space data consistency. Both networks are jointly optimized using reconstruction-based supervision without requiring fully sampled k-space at inference. Experiments on knee and brain MRI across multiple contrasts and acceleration factors demonstrate the adaptive behavior of our acquisition framework and evaluate its reconstruction performance.

</details>


### [14] [PatchAdaNet: Unsupervised class incremental learning using local patches and global adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24137)
*Kamel Aouaidjia,Yanchao Duan,Zihui Zhen,Xin Li,Yongfei Lv,Chongsheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无监督类增量学习框架 PatchAdaNet：用分块自编码器的全局冻结层+可学习适配器+全局特征质心（GFC）+EWC，实现不存数据、固定内存的增量图像分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物学习依赖早期建立、长期稳定且可复用的表征，以最小干扰整合新经验。现有回放方法需存储样本/特征，内存随任务数增长，且可能保留任务特定噪声而损害泛化，因此需要一种无数据存储、固定内存、抗表征漂移的类增量方法。

Method: 1) 利用图像共享结构特征（边缘、角点、形状），将图像切分为 patch，在外部数据上训练独立自编码器重建各 patch；2) 将各 patch 编码器层聚合为跨任务通用、全程冻结的全局层；3) 在其上训练可学习 adapter 重建整图，形成全局图像表征；4) 引入固定大小的全局特征质心 GFC（adapter 输出的滑动平均，每任务后更新）以惩罚表征漂移，无需存数据；5) 对可学习层施加 EWC 约束重要权重；6) 在冻结隐表征与 GFC 上训练线性分类器用于类增量评估。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、miniImageNet 与 5-Datasets 上，于 CNN 与 Vision Transformer 两种设置下进行类增量评估，并给出两种架构在相同类增量设定下的对比研究；代码已公开。

Conclusion: PatchAdaNet 以分块自编码器冻结通用特征、adapter 重建整图表征，配合 GFC 与 EWC 抑制漂移，在固定内存、无样本存储条件下完成无监督类增量学习，并验证其在 CNN 与 ViT 架构上的通用性。

Abstract: Biological learning systems acquire knowledge incrementally through broad, reusable representations established early and kept largely stable, while integrating new experience with minimal interference with previously learned knowledge. Replay-based methods approximate this by storing samples or features from previous tasks and mixing them into the current task. However, memory buffers grow with the number of tasks and may retain task-specific noise that reduces generalization. To address these limitations, we propose PatchAdaNet (Patch Adapter Network), an unsupervised class-incremental learning method for image classification that continually updates feature representations using autoencoders. Since images share structural features such as edges, corners, and shapes, we first divide each image into patches and train independent autoencoders on external data to reconstruct each patch. The layers of all patch encoders are aggregated into global frozen layers that generalize across tasks and remain fixed throughout learning. Learnable adapters then build global image representations from these features to reconstruct the entire image. To prevent feature drift without storing data, we introduce the Global Feature Centroid (GFC), a fixed-size running average of adapter outputs updated after each task to penalizes representation drift. Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC) is applied to the learnable layers to preserve previously learned features by constraining important weights. Finally, a linear classifier is trained on the frozen latent representation and GFC for class-incremental evaluation. We evaluate PatchAdaNet in both CNN and Vision Transformer settings on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, miniImageNet and 5-Datasets, providing a comparative study of the two architectures under identical class-incremental settings. Code is available.

</details>


### [15] [EVF-Bench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for E-commerce Visual Effects in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24116)
*Run Ling,Ke Cao,Runze He,Jie Gao,Ao Ma,Wei Feng,Zhanjie Zhang,Hao Wang,Jinghe Hu,Guibing Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出电商视觉特效基准EVF-Bench（六维评测）与配套数据集EVF-32K（3.2万+图文视频三元组），系统评测代表性视频生成模型及领域适配变体，发现无任何配置在全部六维度领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商视觉特效（EVF）是以产品为中心的动态视觉呈现，常配促销文字，对吸引注意力和传递产品信息至关重要。但通用的视觉质量与文本相关性指标无法判断生成视频是否保持产品外观、文字可读性以及效果连贯性与情境合理性，缺乏面向任务的评测手段。

Method: 构建EVF-Bench基准，含六个评测维度：通用维度为视觉质量与文本相关性，EVF专属维度为运动、产品一致性、文本稳定性与创意合理性；同时构建EVF-32K数据集，包含32000余条面向电商场景的精选图像-文本-视频三元组，用于任务对齐研究与模型适配；并据此系统评测代表性视频生成模型及其领域适配变体。

Result: 各模型/配置表现出互补优势，没有任何被评测配置在全部六个维度上均取得领先。

Conclusion: EVF-Bench与EVF-32K共同提供了面向任务的评测框架与配套数据资源，支撑电商视频生成研究。

Abstract: E-commerce visual effects (EVF) are dynamic, product-centered visual presentations in e-commerce videos, often accompanied by promotional text. They play an important role in attracting viewer attention and conveying product information. However, general measures of visual quality and text relevance alone do not establish whether generated videos preserve product appearance, maintain readable text, and produce coherent, contextually appropriate effects. To address this issue, we introduce EVF-Bench, a dedicated benchmark spanning six evaluation dimensions: visual quality and text relevance as general dimensions, and motion, product consistency, text stability, and creative rationality as EVF-specific dimensions. To complement the benchmark and support task-aligned research and model adaptation, we further construct EVF-32K, a dataset of over 32000 curated image-text-video triples tailored to e-commerce scenarios. Using this suite, we systematically evaluate representative video generation models and their domain-adapted variants. The results reveal complementary strengths, with no evaluated configuration leading all six dimensions. Together, EVF-Bench and EVF-32K provide a task-oriented evaluation framework and a supporting data resource for e-commerce video generation.

</details>


### [16] [CausalDrive: Causal Decomposition for Data-Efficient End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24080)
*Xiaobao Wei,Jingxian Wu,Lirong Yang,Qing Han,Yuan Gao,Ziwei Yang,Shuai Yang,Weiwei Liu,Chang Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CausalDrive，将专家轨迹因果分解为"名义意图"（确定性预测）与"驾驶风格"（概率建模残差）两部分，实现数据高效端到端自动驾驶策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接模仿专家轨迹会把"去哪里"的名义意图与"怎么开"的多模态驾驶风格耦合在一起，不同因素组合可产生相似轨迹，导致因果贡献不清、监督目标纠缠，限制了专家数据的利用效率。

Method: (1) 由导航指令、地图拓扑与自车状态构建去风格化的反事实名义轨迹，为Nominal Intention Planner提供不依赖专家轨迹的监督；(2) 以其预测的名义意图和周围智能体为条件，用扩散模型Style Execution Policy将驾驶风格建模为名义与专家轨迹残差的多模态分布。

Result: NAVSIM v1上PDMS 91.2，NAVSIM v2上EPDMS 89.5，达到或超过SOTA；在有限专家数据下表现稳健，并在挑战性交互场景中具明显优势。

Conclusion: 因果结构化的监督减少了常规场景中共享名义行为的冗余学习，将专家监督聚焦于有信息量的执行变化，提升数据利用率与交互场景性能。

Abstract: Efficient use of expert demonstrations is essential for end-to-end autonomous driving. However, directly imitating expert trajectories couples nominal intention, which specifies where the vehicle should go, with multimodal driving styles, which determine how that intention is executed. Different combinations of these factors can yield similar expert trajectories, obscuring their respective causal contributions and forcing the policy to learn both from a single entangled target. This ambiguity limits how effectively the policy can exploit expert demonstrations. We propose CausalDrive, a causal decomposition framework for data-efficient driving policy learning that separates deterministic nominal intention prediction from probabilistic driving style modeling. Specifically, we construct counterfactual nominal trajectories from navigation command, map topology, and ego state by removing style dependent effects, providing a Nominal Intention Planner with supervision independent of expert trajectories. Conditioned on the predicted nominal intention and surrounding agents, a diffusion-based Style Execution Policy models diverse driving styles as a multimodal distribution over residuals between nominal and expert trajectories. This causally structured supervision reduces redundant learning of shared nominal behavior in routine scenarios, directing expert supervision toward informative execution variations and improving data utilization. CausalDrive achieves 91.2 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 and 89.5 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2, matching or surpassing state-of-the-art performance. Further experiments demonstrate strong performance with limited expert data and clear advantages in challenging interactive scenarios.

</details>


### [17] [Semantic-aware Contrasting with Explicitly Disentangled Representations for AI-Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24038)
*Jiaquan Yu,Jingyi Xie,Zeyu Zhang,Yunhang Qian,Xiaobin Hu,Jiawei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出语义对齐的大规模AIGV检测数据集SAVGE（2,369,822条视频）及模型无关训练框架EDSC，通过显式语义-伪影解耦与组内对比学习提升检测器鲁棒性与泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AIGV检测基准中真实与生成视频常来自不同语义分布，导致检测器倾向于依赖物体、场景、动作等虚假相关（spurious correlations），而非生成模型固有的伪影特征。

Method: 构建SAVGE数据集：以真实视频配对的caption作为文生视频提示，将样本组织为真实-伪造语义组，提供组级对应与文本语义锚点。提出EDSC框架：先将视觉语义特征与文本表示对齐，再在伪影分支中显式抑制语义信息，并在每个语义组内对真伪样本进行对比；采用两阶段优化，结合caption引导的语义监督与组内对比学习。

Result: 在SAVGE与GenVideo上的大量实验表明，EDSC在多数骨干网络-指标组合上均有提升，在困难评测设置下增强鲁棒性，并提升跨数据集泛化能力。

Conclusion: 语义对齐对AIGV检测至关重要，显式语义-伪影解耦是一种有效的鲁棒检测器学习策略。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of AI-generated videos (AIGVs) has raised critical concerns about content authenticity. Existing benchmarks suffer from a fundamental limitation: real and fake videos are often collected or generated from different semantic distributions. As a result, detectors may rely on spurious correlations involving objects, scenes, or actions rather than intrinsic artifacts introduced by generative models. To this end, we introduce SAVGE, a large-scale semantically aligned AIGV detection dataset comprising 2,369,822 videos, including 424,115 real videos and 1,945,707 generated videos. SAVGE organizes samples into real-fake semantic groups by using captions paired with real videos as prompts for text-to-video generation, thereby providing both group-level correspondence and textual semantic anchors. Based on SAVGE, we further propose Explicit Disentangling and Semantic-aware Contrasting (EDSC), a model-agnostic training framework for learning artifact-focused representations with reduced semantic interference. EDSC first extracts and aligns visual semantic features with textual representations, and then explicitly suppresses semantic information in the artifact branch while contrasting real and fake samples within each semantic group. By integrating caption-guided semantic supervision with intra-group contrastive learning in a two-phase optimization strategy, EDSC encourages detectors to capture generation-related cues under matched semantic conditions. Extensive experiments on SAVGE and GenVideo demonstrate that EDSC improves most evaluated backbone-metric combinations, enhances robustness under challenging evaluation settings, and strengthens cross-dataset generalization. These results highlight the importance of semantic alignment in AIGV detection and validate explicit semantic-artifact disentanglement as an effective strategy for robust detector learning.

</details>


### [18] [SIRAZ: Sparse Inter-Layer Residual Aggregation for Zero-Shot Visual Neural Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23991)
*Neha,Mudasir Ganaie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SIRAZ框架，通过模拟人类视觉层级加工来对齐神经视觉表征，实现零样本图像检索，在THINGS-EEG与THINGS-MEG数据集上取得高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经视觉解码方法学到的表征与生物视觉系统的层级加工机制存在本质差异，需要缩小这一差距以更好地对齐脑信号与视觉刺激。

Method: SIRAZ框架包含三部分：利用受中央凹与巨细胞通路启发的Difference-of-Gaussians变换联合建模早期视觉加工；在多深度提取图像中间表征并通过Residual Gated Adapter Mechanism进行语义对齐；引入Direction-aware Multi-Scale Temporal Attention Encoder建模神经信号时间动态，将神经表征对齐至最终视觉表征。

Result: THINGS-EEG数据集被试内设置下top-1达92.5%、top-5达99.27%；THINGS-MEG数据集top-1为50.25%、top-5为75%。

Conclusion: 将生物启发的视觉表征与结构化神经动态模态相结合，可提升跨模态对齐效果与零样本图像检索性能。

Abstract: Brain-computer interfaces aim to establish direct communication between neural activity and external systems, making interpretation of brain signals an important problem. In this context, neural visual decoding seeks to infer visual information from neural activity by learning a mapping between brain responses and the corresponding visual stimuli. It investigates how visual information emerges from neural signals and relates to perceptual processing while enabling the reconstruction or retrieval of visual stimuli from neural signals. Our brain primarily follows a hierarchical pathway to process visual information captured by the eye. However, neural representations learned by existing methods differ fundamentally from those produced by the biological visual system. To bridge this gap and mimic the human vision hierarchy, we introduce Sparse Inter-layer Residual Aggregation for Zero-shot (SIRAZ). SIRAZ is a framework that aligns neural visual representations by jointly modeling early visual processes via fovea- and magnocellular-pathway-inspired Difference-of-Gaussians transformations. To align the semantic information with neural information, it extracts multiple intermediate representations of the image at multiple depths and passes them through the Residual Gated Adapter Mechanism. To study the temporal dynamics of neural signals, the Direction-aware Multi-Scale Temporal Attention Encoder is introduced to align the learned neural representation with the final visual representation. The proposed framework achieves 92.5% top-1 and 99.27% top-5 accuracy in the intra-subject setting on the THINGS-EEG dataset, and 50.25% and 75% top-1 and top-5 accuracy in the THINGS-MEG dataset. The results indicate that combining biologically motivated visual representations with the structured modalities of neural dynamics improves cross-modal alignment and zero-shot image retrieval.

</details>


### [19] [Dual-Manifold Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23964)
*Hongxu Chen,Chun Wang,Yanghao Wang,Hongxiang Li,Zhen Wang,Zhixiang Zhang,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DM-VAE用自身低维分支充当拓扑骨架来约束高维潜流形，免去外部基础模型依赖，配合TRA与IG_TS，在ImageNet-1K 256²上实现rFID 0.12、gFID 1.20（有CFG）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜空间扩散模型（LDM）中，扩大VAE潜维度是提升重建质量上界的关键，但缺乏显式结构约束的无正则化扩张会导致潜流形崎岖、病态，生成时潜码漂移出流形（off-manifold问题）。现有做法借助外部基础模型（如DINOv2）作为拓扑正则器来平滑流形，但作者认为将潜空间绑定到冻结的外部先验会限制泛化：受其预训练分布与语义偏置约束，在分布外域上难以捕捉忠实的视觉细节。作者观察到低维潜空间天然形成更平滑、更利于生成的流形，故提出利用内部表示作为拓扑正则器。

Method: 提出双流形VAE（DM-VAE）：将高维重建分支与一个提取干净拓扑骨架的低维分支配对，把高维流形锚定到该平滑低维子空间。在此基础上进一步引入拓扑表示对齐（TRA），在训练中监督生成器中间特征；以及带拓扑骨架的内部引导（IG_TS），在推理时引导结构成分同时保留高频细节。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K（256×256）上，无基础模型依赖的DM-VAE取得SOTA的rFID 0.12，生成FID50K为1.28（无CFG）与1.20（有CFG）；与DINOv2正交结合后进一步提升至1.11。

Conclusion: 以VAE内部低维表示作为拓扑正则器可在无需外部基础模型的前提下有效平滑潜流形、缓解off-manifold问题，同时保持泛化性与高频细节；且与外部先验方法相互正交、可叠加增益。

Abstract: In latent diffusion models, expanding the latent dimension of variational autoencoders (VAEs) is essential to push the upper bound of reconstruction quality. However, without explicit structural constraints, such unregularized expansion easily yields a rugged and ill-conditioned latent manifold, causing generated latents to drift off the manifold during generation (i.e., off-manifold problem). Recent works smooth this manifold by using external foundation models (e.g., DINOv2) as a topological regularizer. However, we argue that tying the latent space to frozen external priors inherently restricts generalization: bound by their pre-training distributions and semantic biases, they may fail to capture faithful visual details on out-of-distribution domains. Observing that low-dimensional latents naturally form a smoother, generation-friendly manifold, we propose dual-manifold VAE (DM-VAE) to leverage internal representations as a topological regularizer. Specifically, DM-VAE pairs a high-dimensional reconstructive branch with a low-dimensional branch that extracts a clean topological skeleton, anchoring the high-dimensional manifold to this smooth subspace. Building upon this framework, we further introduce topological representation alignment (TRA) to supervise intermediate generator features during training, and internal guidance with topological skeleton (IG_TS) to guide structural components while preserving high-frequency details at inference. On ImageNet-1K (256x256), our foundation-model-free DM-VAE achieves a state-of-the-art rFID of 0.12 and a generation FID50K of 1.28 (w/o CFG) and 1.20 (w/CFG), and further reaches 1.11 when orthogonally combined with DINOv2.

</details>


### [20] [SIGNTOK: A Text-Aligned Continuous Pose Tokenizer for Sign Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23944)
*Nabeela Khan,Gaurav Rai,Bowen Wu,Sihan Tan,Benjamin Yen,Takeshi Ashizawa,Kazuhiro Nakadai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有SLG的生成空间（pose/量化码/重建潜空间）主要由签名者身份而非语言内容组织；SIGNTOK通过运动学、句子级与词级三个仅训练用对齐头塑造连续姿态tokenizer的潜空间，将同句跨签名者最近邻率由2%提升至82%，并在三个基准上提升回译BLEU-4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本条件手语生成（SLG）的效果不仅取决于生成器，还取决于生成所在的空间。作者发现现有空间（原始姿态空间、量化码空间、为重建训练的潜空间）主要由签名者身份组织，而非语言内容：在CSL-Daily上，测试片段在原始姿态空间中的最近邻仅有2%是同一句子的他人签名版本。这一表征偏差限制了生成器对文本内容的利用。

Method: 提出SIGNTOK——一个连续姿态tokenizer，其潜空间由三个仅在训练阶段使用的预测头共同塑造：(1) 运动学头，预测运动动力学；(2) 句子级头，将整段手语与整体文本对齐；(3) 词级头，将短时运动窗口与内容词对齐。推理/生成时仅使用tokenizer的编码-解码结构，不加这些头。

Result: 同句跨签名者最近邻率从2%提升至82%；在三个手语基准上，同一生成器在SIGNTOK潜空间而非姿态空间中训练，在六个基准-评分器组合中的五个上提升了回译BLEU-4，且关节准确率相当；词级对齐在每个基准上的回译分数均高于句子级对齐。

Conclusion: 生成空间的语义组织方式对手语生成至关重要；以语言内容（尤其是词级细粒度对齐）而非签名者身份塑造tokenizer潜空间，可在不牺牲姿态保真度的情况下显著改善文本-手语映射与生成质量。

Abstract: Text-conditioned sign language generation (SLG) depends not only on the generator but also on the space in which signing is generated. Existing approaches generate directly in pose space, in quantized code spaces, or in learned latent spaces trained for reconstruction. We find that these spaces are organized largely by signer identity rather than linguistic content: on CSL-Daily, a test clip's nearest neighbor in raw pose space is the same sentence signed by another person in only 2% of cases. To address this, we propose SIGNTOK, a continuous pose tokenizer whose latent space is shaped by three training-only heads: a kinematics head that predicts motion dynamics, a sentence-level head that aligns signing with the overall text, and a word-level head that aligns short motion windows with content words. Together, these objectives raise the same-sentence cross-signer nearest-neighbor rate to 82%. On three sign language benchmarks, the same generator trained in the SIGNTOK latent instead of pose space raises back-translation BLEU-4 in five of six benchmark-scorer settings at comparable joint accuracy, and word-level alignment gives higher back-translation scores than sentence-level alignment on every benchmark.

</details>


### [21] [SixthView: A Benchmark for Hidden-View Reconstruction and 3D Assembly Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23891)
*Pooya Khosravi,Jen-Feng Chang,Fnu Pramono*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SixthView 基准：给定五个正交视图及各部件形状信息，预测第六个正交视图并对缺失视图给出密集部件标签网格；任务刻意保证在几何与观测一致的所有合法装配下目标视图唯一，用于测试符号装配与坐标推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推断三维装配的未见面需要推理部件摆放与遮挡；不同装配可匹配同一组观测视图却产生不同隐藏视图，导致目标天然歧义。因此需要一个受控基准来检验模型是否真正进行符号化装配与坐标推理，并区分"从图像恢复形状"与"利用精确符号几何"两类能力。

Method: 构建 602 个合成 polycube 装配（2–10 个部件），每个条目要求对缺失视图输出密集部件标签网格，支持精确视图级与单元级评测；通过穷举验证所有与精确部件几何及观测标签网格一致的合法装配都产生同一目标视图，从而消除歧义。设置纯图像变体以考察渲染视图中的遮挡导致形状不可辨的情形；评测三个视觉语言模型、四种输入条件（图像＋精确文本、分离图像、合并图像表等）。

Result: 在 552 个完成评测的条目上，图像＋精确文本的精确网格准确率为 33.2%–85.4%；在分离图像上加入精确文本带来 19.7–63.0 个百分点提升，而在精确文本上加入图像仅变化 −0.5 至 +2.1 个百分点；分离图像面板比合并图像表高 6.7–11.1 个百分点。

Conclusion: 性能强烈依赖于能否获得精确符号信息；一旦几何被显式给出，图像带来的额外收益很小，说明当前模型在遮挡下的形状恢复与符号装配推理仍受限，基准可用于诊断该能力缺口。

Abstract: Inferring the unseen side of a 3D assembly requires reasoning about part placement and occlusion. Different assemblies can match the observed views while producing different hidden views, leaving the target ambiguous. We introduce SixthView, a benchmark in which models predict a sixth orthographic view from five observed views and information about the component shapes. The benchmark contains 602 synthetic polycube assemblies with 2--10 parts and requires a dense grid of part labels for the missing view, allowing both exact-view and cell-level evaluation. For each item, we exhaustively verify that all legal assemblies consistent with the exact component geometry and observed label grids yield the same target view. With these inputs, the task tests symbolic assembly and coordinate reasoning. The image-only variants additionally require shape recovery from rendered views, where occlusion can make different 3D shapes look identical. We evaluate three vision-language models under four input conditions. Across 552 items with complete evaluations, exact-grid accuracy with images plus exact text ranges from 33.2% to 85.4%. Adding exact text to separate images improves accuracy by 19.7--63.0 percentage points, whereas adding images to exact text changes it by only $-0.5$ to $+2.1$ points. Separate image panels outperform a merged sheet by 6.7--11.1 points. The results show that performance depends strongly on access to exact symbolic information, with little further improvement from images once the geometry is specified. GitHub: https://github.com/facebookresearch/sixthview_benchmark

</details>


### [22] [NullTok: empirical-null token selection for training-free long video understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23881)
*Zhang Ziyue,Juwei Jian,Baiyang Song,Yunshan Zhong,Jun Peng,Yiyi Zhou,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练方法 NULLTOK，通过构建经验零分布将目标亲和度校准为蒙特卡洛 p 值，实现时空联合的视觉 token 筛选与预算分配，在有限视觉 token 预算下提升长视频理解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解中视觉 token 数量庞大，现有帧选择与 token 剪枝两范式直接串联时，帧级相关性估计的噪声会导致不可逆的证据丢失。因此需从查询相关证据的时空分布角度重新审视筛选问题，降低不可靠的帧级估计对后续剪枝的负面影响。

Method: 对全部帧的每个 patch，用 64 个分组匹配的无关控制查询构建经验零分布，刻画无目标证据时的期望得分；将目标亲和度相对同位置参考校准为右尾蒙特卡洛 p 值（在条件可交换假设下为离散超均匀）；再进行校准筛选与高召回语义守卫生成候选 token，随后做确定性视觉去重与时空联合预算分配；全程免训练。

Result: 在两个代表性 MLLM 上、64 个全帧当量（FFE）的 token 上限、无字幕与音频条件下，Video-MME 上分别达到 65.81%（冻结 LLaVA-Video-7B）与 68.85%（Qwen3-VL-8B），在同预算下优于所有对比基线。

Conclusion: 统计分析式的零分布校准可替代带噪的帧级相关性估计，为有界视觉 token 预算下的长视频理解提供有效、免训练且可迁移的 token 筛选方案。

Abstract: Long video understanding remains a daunting challenge for existing multimodal large language models (MLLMs), mainly due to the large number of visual tokens accumulated in long videos. Currently, frame selection and token selection are two main paradigms for addressing this issue, both aiming to select relevant visual information to form a compact input for MLLMs. A natural approach combines these two paradigms by selecting frames before pruning tokens, but noisy frame-level relevance estimates can cause irreversible evidence loss. In this paper, we study this problem from the perspective of assessing the spatiotemporal distribution of query-specific evidence and propose a novel and training-free approach termed NULLTOK. For every patch across all frames, we construct an empirical-null distribution from 64 group-matched irrelevant control queries, capturing the scores expected in the absence of target-specific evidence. Target affinities are calibrated against these same-position references into right-tail Monte Carlo p-values, which are discrete super-uniform under a conditional exchangeability assumption. Calibrated screening and a high-recall semantic guard produce candidate tokens for deterministic visual deduplication and joint spatiotemporal budget allocation. To validate NullTok, we apply it to two representative MLLMs and conduct extensive experiments on a set of video benchmarks. The experimental results demonstrate its effectiveness under a bounded visual token budget. For example, it achieves 65.81% and 68.85% on Video-MME using frozen LLaVA-Video-7B and Qwen3-VL-8B backbones, respectively, at a cap of 64 full-frame equivalents (FFE), without subtitles or audio. These results outperform all evaluated baselines on Video-MME under the same budget for both backbones. Code is available at https://github.com/potato-kitty/NullTok.

</details>


### [23] [Grounding Radiology Reports in 3D Chest CT: Results of the ReXGrounding Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23862)
*Mohammed Baharoon,Mahmoud Hussain Alabbad,Sung Eun Kim,Xiaoman Zhang,Lucas Bijnens,Zirong Chen,Jiayu Wang,Xi Ouyang,Mingyang Yu,Weiqiu Jin,Xiaodan Ye,Dijia Wu,Kwang-Hyun Uhm,Inhwa Son,Rachel van 't Hull,Tim Veenboer,Cees Snoek,Kevin Groot Lipman,Daesung Kim,Junha Im,Youngjun Jun,Sangwon Lee,Katelyn Morrison,Adam Perer,Mijin Yun,Ali Piracha,Reem Alwasiah,Talal Yahyan,Abdulrahman Alamoudi,Mashael Alshehri,Rayan Alsager,Hannah Hancock,Anna M. Hunkemöller,Peter Youssef,Mona A. Alhammad,Afnan Aljumayan,Brecht Van Berkel,Eric Bijnens,Saud Alhussain,Maarten Steyvers,Rolf Symons,Bryan Eelen,Ayed Alaklubi,Maha Buraykan Alshammari,Amirah Fahad Alshammeri,Tomas Gilvydis,Ibrahim AlTurki,Monerah Almohideb,Ali Alburkani,Shahad Alhomaiani,Fatima Jabali,Maximilian Rokuss,Moritz Langenberg,Yannick Kirchhoff,Benjamin Hamm,Klaus Maier-Hein,Ibrahim Ethem Hamamci,Sezgin Er,Michael Moritz,Hassan R. Alomaish,Luyang Luo,Pranav Rajpurkar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了首个面向报告发现级3D分割的ReXGrounding挑战赛（MICCAI 2026），最优方法Dice达0.375、命中率0.77，接近两位独立放射科医生间的一致性水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对影像报告中每条发现的逐一分割可实现病灶的量化测量与纵向追踪，并使AI生成的报告可核验；然而现有AI模型在该任务上表现不佳，亟需标准化评测平台推动进展。

Method: 组织ReXGrounding挑战赛：输入平扫胸部CT及报告中的发现集合，输出对应的3D分割集合。使用ReXGroundingCT数据集的2,992例训练与200例验证扫描，在由认证放射科医生穷尽标注的300例测试集（582条发现）上评测；共48支队伍、15个国家、760次提交。

Result: 最佳方法Dice 0.375、命中率0.77，与两组独立放射科医生间一致性（Dice 0.32、命中率0.76）相当；在100例赛前基准上Dice 0.390，优于赛前最强模型VoxTell的0.285（前六名中五队基于其微调）。非局灶性发现分割仍显著更难，实例F1（0.34）低于读者间一致性（0.47），且模型存在"分割正确异常但位置错误"的问题。

Conclusion: 顶尖方法在Dice上已达到放射科医生间一致性水平，但非局灶性发现、多实例发现以及对文本所描述解剖位置的遵循仍是开放难题。

Abstract: Segmenting each reported finding in a radiology scan would allow findings to be measured and tracked over time and let AI systems generate reports that are verifiable, but so far AI models have performed poorly at this task. To advance this task, we organized the ReXGrounding Challenge at MICCAI 2026, the first challenge dedicated to it. Given a non-contrast chest CT scan and the set of findings from its report, participants trained models to produce the corresponding set of 3D segmentations. The challenge used 2,992 training and 200 validation scans from the ReXGroundingCT dataset, and evaluated methods on a held-out test set of 300 scans with 582 findings, exhaustively annotated by board-certified radiologists. In total, 48 teams from 15 countries made 760 submissions, and the best method reached a Dice of 0.375 and a hit rate of 0.77, matching agreement between two independent radiologist groups (Dice 0.32, hit rate 0.76, on a 50-scan subset). On the 100 scans of the pre-challenge benchmark, the winner reached a Dice of 0.390, against 0.285 for VoxTell, the best model before the challenge, which five of the six best teams fine-tuned. Non-focal findings remained far harder to segment than focal ones, instance F1 stayed below inter-reader agreement (0.34 vs. 0.47), and models sometimes segmented the right abnormality in the wrong location. Top methods thus match radiologist agreement in Dice, but non-focal findings, findings with multiple instances, and adherence to the anatomical location described in the text remain open problems.

</details>


### [24] [Can AI Understand Indonesian Children's Book Illustrations? Compliance, Safety, and the Cultural Alignment Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23887)
*Rian Adam Rajagede,Rochana Prih Hastuti,Fitri Hasanah Amhar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文以印尼政府《图书分级指南》为评估框架，检验视觉语言模型（VLM）能否在合规性、安全性与文化对齐三项标准上可靠地评判和生成儿童绘本插图。结果显示VLM在显性标准上可靠，但在文化对齐上系统性失明，AI生成插图也呈现同样的盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童绘本插图承担发展、教学与文化责任，且常被正式规范化。随着AI系统（作为评估者和生成者）进入儿童出版流程，需要回答其能否可靠满足结构、安全与文化标准。既有评估框架多针对显性标准，隐性文化维度是否被AI捕捉尚不清楚。

Method: 采用印尼政府发布的《图书分级指南》（含四个阅读层级的明确视觉标准）作为评估框架。首先在真实出版的儿童绘本页面上评测VLM对指南标准的识别与应用能力，并与人类基线对比；随后用VLM作为评审对两个生成模型的插图进行评分，并与人类插画师评分者的子集结果交叉验证。

Result: VLM在合规性、安全性等显性标准上与人类基线一致、表现可靠；但在文化对齐上表现不足，系统性遗漏人类评分者标记为文化不当的内容。AI生成插图呈现相同模式：满足结构与安全标准，却无法捕捉人类评分者一致识别的隐性文化细节。

Conclusion: VLM可作为显性标准（结构合规、安全）的可靠评估与生成工具，但不足以胜任文化对齐判断；AI生成插图在隐性文化细节上存在系统性盲点。儿童出版中的AI应用需补充文化敏感度机制与人工审核。

Abstract: Children's book illustrations carry developmental, pedagogical, and cultural responsibilities that are often formally codified in guidelines. As AI systems begin entering children's publishing pipelines, both as evaluators and as generators, a natural question arises: can they reliably meet the structural, safety, and cultural standards required? We investigate this across three criteria: guideline compliance, safety, and cultural alignment, using Indonesia's Book Leveling Guideline as a government-issued evaluation framework with explicit visual criteria for four reading levels. To understand how well VLMs can identify and apply these guideline criteria, we first evaluate them on real published children's book pages and compare their assessments against a human baseline. To then examine whether AI-generated illustrations meet the same standards, we apply the VLM judge to images produced by two generation models and cross-check them against a subset of human illustrator raters. We find that VLMs are reliable on explicit criteria such as compliance and safety, but fall short on cultural alignment, systematically missing content that human raters flag as culturally inappropriate. AI-generated illustrations show the same blind spot: they satisfy structural and safety criteria but fail to capture implicit cultural details that human raters consistently identify.

</details>


### [25] [Temporal Image Representations for Mouse Dynamics Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23859)
*Junyi Li,Charles Devlen,Daqing Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出在鼠标动态认证中引入包含速度和速率信息的图像表示（XYPlot与Pairwise Distance），结合预训练2D-CNN与ViT在位置感知/位置无关设置下评估，在多个数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统鼠标动态认证依赖手工特征或时序表示，近期以轨迹图像结合预训练视觉模型的方法虽有效，但多为仅空间的表示（将光标坐标绘制为轨迹图），丢弃了有助于区分用户的时序特征。

Method: 提出两种融合速度/速率信息的图像表示：XYPlot（直观展示运动路径）和Pairwise Distance (PWD)（利用整幅画布编码鼠标事件间关系）；在位置感知与位置无关两种设置下，分别用预训练2D-CNN和ViT架构进行评估。

Result: 引入时序信息后性能优于纯空间表示；位置感知变体总体优于位置无关变体；在Balabit和TWOS上达到SOTA并扩展至ChaoShen、DFL。位置感知PWD+时序编码+2D-CNN组合最佳，平均EER分别为Balabit Test-1 5.21%、Test-2 12.70%、TWOS 4.94%、ChaoShen 6.16%、DFL 0.59%。

Conclusion: 将时序（速度/速率）信息编码进图像表示可显著提升鼠标动态认证效果，其中位置感知PWD表示配合2D-CNN表现最优，并在多个数据集上验证了方法的有效性与泛化性。

Abstract: Mouse dynamics authentication has traditionally relied on handcrafted features or time-series representations that capture both spatial and temporal characteristics of mouse movements. More recent approaches have modeled mouse data as images, enabling the use of powerful pretrained computer vision models. However, prior work primarily focuses on spatial-only representations where the cursor coordinates are plotted as a trajectory image. These representations discard the temporal characteristics which can help discriminate users. In this work, we propose image representations that incorporate speed and velocity information for mouse dynamics authentication. We investigate two mouse data representations, a XYPlot representation that intuitively shows the path of movement, and a Pairwise Distance (PWD) representation that utilizes the entire image canvas to encode relationships between mouse events. Both representations are evaluated under both location-aware and location-free settings using pretrained 2D-CNN and ViT architectures. We found that the incorporation of temporal information improved performance over spatial representations alone, and that the location-aware variants generally outperformed the location-free. We achieve state-of-the-art performance on two datasets, Balabit and TWOS, and extend our evaluation to two other datasets. We found that in general, location-aware PWD with temporal encoding combined with the 2D-CNN architecture provided the best results, with mean EERs of 5.21% and 12.70% on the Balabit Test-1 and Test-2 sets, respectively, 4.94% on TWOS, 6.16% on ChaoShen, and 0.59% on DFL.

</details>


### [26] [SCOPE: Single-Cell Omics Prediction from Histology via RNA-Protein Prior Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24104)
*Leyang Tao,Xuan Gong,Luyuan Xie,Arjun Beniwal,Yuqi Tan,Kun-Hsing Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCOPE：利用RNA-蛋白生物先验知识实现跨模态共享表示，从H&E图像统一预测单细胞RNA与蛋白表达，并可泛化至未见标志物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从常规H&E图像预测细胞的RNA与蛋白含量可将空间分子谱分析扩展到已有切片档案，但H&E-空间蛋白组与H&E-空间转录组配对数据受限于标志物数量少、高质量单细胞级数据稀缺，单组学监督不足。

Method: 提出统一框架SCOPE（Single-Cell Omics Prediction via RNA-protein prior Enhancement）。核心思路是RNA与蛋白虽为不同模态，但其生物学关系提供可共享知识：引入生物学启发的标志物先验增强共享的基因级表示，通过基因-蛋白对应关系与分子关联实现跨模态知识迁移；两任务共享同一骨干网络；并设计标志物相关性模块以预测未见标志物。

Result: 覆盖16个器官的测试集上，平均RNA Pearson相关系数较最强基线提升8.8%；两个外部蛋白队列上，已见标志物平均Pearson提升9.7%，未见标志物提升11.9%。

Conclusion: 跨组学生物学互补信息可有效缓解单组学监督稀缺问题，SCOPE以共享骨干与标志物相关性设计同时提升RNA/蛋白预测精度并支持未见标志物外推，拓展了空间分子谱分析的适用边界。

Abstract: Predicting the RNA and protein content of cells from routine H&E images could extend spatial molecular profiling to existing slide archives. However, both H&E-spatial proteomics and H&E-spatial transcriptomics paired datasets are limited by the number of available markers and the insufficient availability of high-quality, cell-resolved data. We present SCOPE (Single-Cell Omics Prediction via RNA-protein prior Enhancement), a unified framework that leverages biologically complementary information between RNA and proteins to address the limited availability of single-omics supervision. Our key insight is that, although RNA and proteins represent distinct molecular modalities, their biological relationships provide valuable shared knowledge that can benefit single-omics prediction tasks. To this end, SCOPE introduces biologically informed biomarker priors to enhance shared gene-level representations, enabling knowledge transfer across RNA and protein modalities through gene-protein correspondence and molecular associations. By exploiting these complementary relationships, SCOPE enables both prediction tasks to benefit from a shared backbone enriched with cross-omics biological knowledge. Furthermore, its biomarker correlation design facilitates the prediction of unseen markers, extending spatially resolved molecular profiling beyond directly supervised targets. Across test datasets spanning 16 organs, SCOPE improves the average RNA Pearson correlation over the strongest baseline by 8.8%. On two external protein cohorts, it improves the average Pearson correlation over the strongest baseline by 9.7% for seen markers and 11.9% for unseen markers.

</details>


### [27] [APS: Adaptive Partitioning-Based Frame Selection for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24079)
*Junxiang Wen,Yinxi Zhao,Jiawei Chen,Jingying Hu,Lijun Wang,Zhen Qin,Shuiguang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的APS框架，将视频帧选择中的时间分区建模为复杂度正则化的拟合问题，用动态规划联合优化分区边界与局部模型，使自适应分区指导预算分配与帧选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解中，密集视觉输入的计算代价过高；帧选择是高效替代方案，但现有基于区域的方法依赖固定或预定义的时间划分，可能导致次优时间区域，进而损害后续预算分配与帧选择效果。

Method: APS为training-free框架：把时间分区表述为复杂度正则化的拟合问题，通过动态规划同时优化分区边界与各分区内的局部模型，使分区自适应于query-frame相关性的局部变化；再依据query相关性与时间跨度分配帧预算，在有限视觉预算下选取信息量大的帧。

Result: 在Video-MME、MLVU、LongVideoBench上，APS在多种LVLM骨干上均稳定提升性能；相对均匀采样的平均增益在对比方法中最高。消融验证了自适应分区的有效性，并显示其对模型规模、相似度编码器及先进LVLM具有泛化性。

Conclusion: 自适应、数据驱动的时间分区优于固定分区，可作为免训练插件提升长视频帧选择效率与下游LVLM表现，且具备跨模型与跨编码器的通用性。

Abstract: Recent advances in large vision-language models (LVLMs) have significantly improved video understanding capabilities, yet processing long videos remains challenging due to the substantial computational cost of extensive visual inputs. Frame selection offers an efficient alternative, but many existing region-based methods rely on fixed or predefined temporal partitions, which may result in suboptimal temporal regions, compromising subsequent budget allocation and frame selection. In this work, we propose Adaptive Partitioning-Based Frame Selection (APS), a training-free framework that adapts temporal partitions to local variations in query-frame relevance. Specifically, APS formulates temporal partitioning as a complexity-regularized fitting problem, jointly optimizing partition boundaries and local models through dynamic programming. The resulting adaptive temporal partitions guide frame budget allocation based on query relevance and temporal extent, enabling informative frame selection under limited visual budgets. Extensive experiments on Video-MME, MLVU, and LongVideoBench demonstrate that APS consistently improves performance across diverse LVLM backbones, achieving the highest average gains over uniform sampling among competing methods. Further analyses validate the effectiveness of adaptive partitioning and demonstrate its generalizability across model scales, similarity encoders, and advanced LVLMs.

</details>


### [28] [AeroRSI: Recursive Self-Improvement for Aerial Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24077)
*Shiwei Wu,Yuwen Pan,Zhi Li,Yaowen Li,Yan Zhou,Xuemiao Xu,Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AeroRSI：让无人机VLA策略从自身失败中递归自改进，利用"从修复动作继续飞行"来验证修复质量，三轮迭代将2B策略成功率从46.4%提升至67.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型虽能让无人机遵循自然语言指令，但在闭环飞行中仍频繁失败。修复失败需要"在策略自身到达的状态上避免失败的动作"，这类监督信号人工飞行员无法大规模标注，规则规划器也难以可靠提供。

Method: 提出递归自改进框架AeroRSI。核心洞察：失败策略应通过"从修复动作继续飞行"来评判修复。据此，采样动作乃至直接模仿会损害策略的规则性提案，都成为可靠监督。由于episode在首次失败即终止，后续失败被掩盖，因此每轮重新生成监督：锚定当前策略的失败状态、以继续飞行的结果验证修复、在保持其他区域行为的前提下蒸馏修复。

Result: 在TravelUAV上，三轮迭代将监督式2B参数策略成功率从46.4%提升至67.5%，碰撞减少一半；在相同初始化下较此前SOTA高10.6个点，且无需额外人工标注或推理开销。

Conclusion: 通过"继续飞行验证修复"的机制，可把策略自身的失败转化为可扩展的监督信号，实现无人干预的递归自我改进，显著提升无人机VLA闭环导航性能。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models enable unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to follow natural-language instructions, yet still fail frequently in closed-loop flight. Correcting these failures requires repairs, i.e., actions that avoid them at the states the policy itself reaches, which human pilots cannot label at scale and rule-based planners cannot supply reliably. In this paper, we present AeroRSI, a recursive self-improvement framework in which an aerial navigation policy iteratively learns from its own failures. Our key insight is that a failing policy should judge its own repairs by flying on from them. Judged in this way, its sampled actions and even rule-based proposals that hurt it when imitated directly become reliable supervision. Moreover, an episode terminates at its first failure, concealing subsequent failures until the earlier ones are repaired. AeroRSI thus renews its supervision in every round, anchoring the failures of the current policy, verifying repairs by its continuation, and distilling them while keeping its behavior elsewhere. On TravelUAV, three rounds raise the success rate of a supervised 2B-parameter policy from 46.4% to 67.5% and reduce collisions by half, surpassing the previous state of the art by 10.6 points from the same initialization without extra human labels or inference cost. Code and models are available at https://github.com/starsholic/AeroRSI.

</details>


### [29] [CURVTON-205K: A Mask-Free Difficulty-Stratified Synthetic Dataset for Robust and Scalable Virtual Try-On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24042)
*Dipan Bartaula,Anuj Shrestha,Ankit Belbase,Anish Bishwakarma,Saroj Poudel,Samip Ghimire,Shashank Bhatta,Eric Arazo,Binod Bhattarai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可扩展数据生成流水线，构建205K三元组合成数据集CURVTON-205K，覆盖长尾衣物与外观、姿态、遮挡及视觉条件，按三个难度层级组织，提升四种VTON架构的试穿保真度与跨数据集泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有2D虚拟试穿研究多聚焦架构创新，而遮挡处理不准、姿态不受约束、对欠代表衣物与外观因素覆盖不足等失效模式，与数据集设计及长尾数据分布紧密耦合，真实场景鲁棒性仍不足。

Method: 设计可扩展数据生成流水线，协调专用编辑模型与生成模型，并由视觉语言模型（VLM）引导，在无需解析掩码或稠密姿态标注的情况下合成"源人物—目标衣物—试穿图"三元组；据此构建CURVTON-205K（205K三元组），显式扩大衣物、人物外观、姿态、遮挡与视觉条件覆盖，并划分为三个难度层级以支持难度感知实验。

Result: 分析了数据集多样性；在四种VTON架构上验证其提升试穿保真度的潜力，并展示跨数据集泛化能力。

Conclusion: 从数据侧而非仅架构侧应对VTON失效模式是有效路径；合成数据流水线可低成本扩展覆盖长尾分布，难度分层设计支持可控评测与难度感知研究。

Abstract: Recent advances in 2D virtual try-on (VTON) have largely focused on architectural innovation, yet robust performance in diverse real-world conditions remains challenging. We argue that failure modes in VTON, such as inaccurate occlusion handling, unconstrained poses, and poor coverage of under-represented garments and appearance factors, are tightly coupled to dataset design choices and long-tail data regimes. We present a scalable data generation pipeline that coordinates specialized editing and generation models with vision-language model (VLM) guidance to synthesize triplets without requiring parsing masks or dense-pose annotations. Using this pipeline, we create CURVTON-205K, a curated synthetic dataset of 205K triplets: source person, target garment, and corresponding try-on image. CURVTON-205K is explicitly designed to broaden coverage across garments, person appearance, poses, occlusions, and visual conditions. Additionally, CURVTON-205K is organized into three difficulty tiers, enabling controlled evaluation under varying levels of difficulty and supporting difficulty-aware experimentation. We analyze the dataset diversity and demonstrate its potential to improve try-on fidelity across four VTON architectures and its cross-dataset generalization.

</details>


### [30] [Semi-Supervised GAN-Based Segmentation of CTPA Scans for Pulmonary Emboli Boundary Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24018)
*Maria Thoma,Michalis Savelonas,Foteini Malli,Dimitris Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于GAN的半监督分割方法，利用大量无标注CTPA扫描检测肺栓塞，在少量标注数据下Dice达88.75%、HD95为1.74，优于全监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺栓塞（PE）致死率高，延迟诊断可致命；CTPA是临床金标准，但人工判读耗时且易出错，且高质量标注数据稀缺，因此需要能在少量标注样本下保持准确分割的方法。

Method: 基于生成对抗网络的半监督分割框架，利用大规模无标注CTPA扫描。损失设计包括：Dice损失与交叉熵损失应对CTPA图像的高度类别不平衡；特征匹配损失促使生成器输出与真实标签难以区分的解剖合理分割图；自训练损失强化对高置信度伪标注样本的学习。

Result: 在公开数据集上评估，仅用有限数量标注CTPA扫描仍保持准确分割，Dice系数88.75%，HD95为1.74，优于全监督基线。

Conclusion: 多损失协同的GAN半监督框架可有效缓解标注稀缺与类别不平衡问题，为CTPA中肺栓塞分割提供了一种数据高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Pulmonary embolism (PE) is a pulmonary vascular disease associated with high morbidity and mortality, where delayed diagnosis can be fatal. While computed tomography pulmonary angiography (CTPA) is the clinical gold standard for PE detection, interpreting these scans manually is time-consuming and error-prone. In this work, we propose a semi-supervised segmentation method, employing generative adversarial networks (GANs) to leverage large pools of unlabeled CTPA scans. The proposed method addresses more effectively the challenges of CTPA image segmentation than previous methods by employing the dice loss and the cross-entropy loss to tackle the highly imbalanced nature of CTPA scans, the feature matching loss to encourage the generator to produce anatomically plausible segmentation maps that are indistinguishable from ground truth, as well as the self-training loss to reinforce learning from highly-confident pseudo-labeled samples. The experimental evaluation on a publicly available dataset shows that the proposed method maintains accurate segmentation, despite a limited number of labeled CTPA scans. with a Dice coefficient of 88.75% and a 95th percentile Hausdorff distance (HD95) of 1.74, outperforming fully supervised baselines.

</details>


### [31] [How Complete Must an Environment Be to Train a Capable GUI Agent?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23946)
*Jingxing Wang,Teng Wang,Xingyu Lou,Zihan Guo,Jun Wang,Weiwen Liu,Weinan Zhang,Jianghao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 文章提出 MobileGym，将 GUI 智能体训练环境沿"奖励覆盖、数据覆盖、特征覆盖"三个维度解耦调控，实证测量各维度完整度对训练效果的影响，并据此构建更优的 MobileGym-Lite。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GUI 智能体工作为降低构造成本、批量搭建环境而各自牺牲环境的部分完整性，但均凭工程经验设定三个维度，未量化其对训练效果的影响。核心问题是：训练一个有能力的智能体，环境需要多完整、在哪些维度上完整。

Method: 构建 MobileGym，对奖励覆盖（验证器奖励的目标达成轨迹）、数据覆盖（任务读写之外的应用数据）、特征覆盖（任务之外的应用功能）三维度分别独立调整，训练 Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct，并统一在原始完整环境中评估各策略。进一步分析不同 episode 加权方式的作用机制，并据结论构建 MobileGym-Lite（保留奖励与数据覆盖完整、剪除冗余特征）。

Result: 仅奖励单一参考路径使成功率下降 10.53 个百分点；移除任务不需的记录同样降低成功率；剪除无用特征反而使成功率从 51.61% 升至 56.13%，且增益完全来自环境未被改动的那部分任务。该增益在每 episode 等权时反转，在仅对剪枝任务 episode 做平衡时消失；不做剪枝时，等权/长度加权会强化长绕路成功样本，侵蚀其他任务上的技能。MobileGym-Lite 在完整环境中以 55.48% 成功率优于 Full（51.61%），且构建成本更低。

Conclusion: 环境完整性的价值随维度而异：奖励覆盖与数据覆盖应保持完整，冗余特征可安全剪除。合理剪枝配合恰当的 episode 加权能提升泛化与构建效率，为 GUI 环境设计提供可度量的准则。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents are widely trained in executable GUI environments. To lower cost, build environments in batches, and reduce manual effort, each work trades away part of the environment through its construction choices. We describe these choices along three dimensions: reward coverage, the goal-reaching trajectories the verifier rewards; data coverage, the application data the agent is exposed to beyond what the task reads or writes; and feature coverage, the application features beyond what the task uses. Existing works set each dimension on engineering grounds, but they do not measure its effect on training. How complete must an environment be to train a capable agent, and along which dimensions? In MobileGym, we adjust each dimension separately, train Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct, and evaluate every policy in the original full environment. A verifier that rewards only one reference path lowers success by 10.53 points, and removing unneeded records also lowers success. Pruning unneeded features instead raises success from 51.61% to 56.13%, entirely on tasks whose environments it left unchanged. The gain reverses when each episode is weighted equally and vanishes when only episodes on the pruned tasks are balanced. Without pruning, length weighting there reinforces long detour successes that erode skills on other tasks. Following these findings, we build MobileGym-Lite, which keeps reward and data coverage complete and prunes unneeded features. It outperforms Full in the full environment (55.48% versus 51.61% success) with lower construction cost.

</details>


### [32] [What Right-Angle Corners Reveal About an Uncalibrated Camera: Convex Cones, Certified Bounds and Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23934)
*Michael Werman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 从图像中三条相互垂直棱边交汇形成的角点出发，证明了每个角点对应一个显式的相机参数凸集，仅焦距未知时该集合可用闭式区间表示，从而把多角点标定转化为凸可行性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立方体或房间的直角角落（三条互相垂直的边在一点交汇）在图像中呈现为三条线段交于一点。作者希望弄清楚这类角点能揭示未标定相机的哪些内参信息，即如何在不依赖标定物几何先验的条件下从单幅图像中约束焦距等内参。

Method: 将每个直角角点视为对内参的约束，构造与其相容的相机参数集合，并证明该集合是显式凸集；在只有焦距未知时，将其化简为闭式表示的区间。基于此，把多角点标定表述为凸可行性问题，并分析角点对焦距给出上界/下界（单侧或双侧约束）的条件，以及在顶点已知情形下，对测得的棱边方向误差给出有界误差认证。

Result: 几乎每个角点都能给出焦距的下界；文中刻画了一个角点还能同时给出上界的情形，以及多个角点能精确确定焦距的条件。在顶点已知时，所得到的界可针对测量棱边方向的有界误差进行认证。

Conclusion: 直角三面角点是单幅图像中内参的强几何约束来源：单角点即可给出凸约束，多角点组合可形成凸可行性标定框架，并能对测量误差提供可认证的界，为无标定物、基于场景几何的相机自标定提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: The corner of a box or a room, where three perpendicular edges meet, appears in an image as three segments meeting at a point. We ask what such junctions reveal about an uncalibrated camera's intrinsic parameters. Each junction is consistent with an explicit convex set of cameras; when only the focal length is unknown, this set is an interval in closed form. These sets are computed from the image alone, so calibrating from many junctions is a convex feasibility problem. Almost every junction bounds the focal length from below; we characterize when one also bounds it from above, and when many junctions determine it exactly. For a known vertex, the bounds can be certified against any bounded error in the measured edge directions.

</details>


### [33] [ReflectScene:MLLM-Guided Closed-Loop Diffusion for 3D Indoor Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23845)
*Jiawei Shen,Jie Feng,Junpeng Zhang,Guanbin Li,Wanyi Chen,Weisheng Dong,Guangming Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReflectScene，一个由 MLLM 引导的闭环扩散框架，用于文本驱动的三维室内场景生成，通过层次化先验场景理解、语义奖励评估与决策条件化轨迹一致扩散，将语义诊断转化为对象级修正并融入去噪过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散方法主要依赖静态文本条件，难以评估演化中的场景并把语义差异转化为可执行的对象级修正；基于 MLLM 的后处理精修虽能提供语义反馈，但与去噪过程解耦，可能破坏场景连贯性。

Method: 框架包含三部分：(1) Hierarchical Prior-Grounded Scene Understanding，利用冻结的多模态大语言模型将结构化布局与多视角视觉证据对齐，解析对象级上下文与全局语义；(2) MLLM-Guided Semantic Reward Evaluation，将 MLLM 对候选修正的诊断与评估蒸馏为轻量级 Reward Predictor，降低修正决策计算开销；(3) Decision-Conditioned Trajectory-Consistent Diffusion，通过瞬时条件化与持续干净估计投影，将选定修正注入去噪轨迹，仅调整目标对象布局而保持其余布置不变。

Result: 在 3D-FRONT 数据集上的实验表明，该方法在保持有竞争力的生成质量与多样性的同时，提升了功能一致性与语义对齐程度。

Conclusion: 将 MLLM 语义诊断以闭环方式嵌入扩散去噪轨迹，可在不破坏场景连贯性的前提下实现精准的对象级布局修正。

Abstract: Text-conditioned 3D indoor scene generation requires layouts satisfying geometric, functional, and semantic constraints. However, existing diffusion methods rely primarily on static textual conditions, limiting their ability to assess evolving scenes and translate semantic discrepancies into executable object-level corrections. MLLM-based post-hoc refinement provides semantic feedback but remains decoupled from denoising, potentially disrupting scene coherence.We propose ReflectScene, an MLLM-guided closed-loop diffusion framework. First, we introduce Hierarchical Prior-Grounded Scene Understanding that aligns structured layouts with multi-view visual evidence, to interpret object-specific context and scene global semantics via a frozen multi-modal large language model (MLLM). Second, we propose MLLM-Guided Semantic Reward Evaluation to distill MLLM's diagnoses and assessments on possible candidate corrections into a lightweight Reward Predictor, enabling correction decision making with reduced computation burden. Finally, Decision-Conditioned Trajectory-Consistent Diffusion is proposed to integrate selected corrections through transient conditioning and persistent clean-estimate projection, correcting object layout in the generated scene while preserving non-target arrangements throughout denoising.Experiments on 3D-FRONT demonstrate improved functional consistency and semantic alignment while maintaining competitive generation quality and diversity.

</details>


### [34] [SWIFT: Slow World, Instant Freshness, and Timely Action for Asynchronous Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23813)
*Jiazhe Wang,Tianyi Yang,Ruihang Li,Fan Lu,Xianming Zeng,Jianyun Xu,Xingliang Liu,Lei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SWIFT：面向部署的异步世界-动作系统，将世界模型更新移出"观测→动作"关键路径，在保持实时控制的同时保留生成式世界先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶场景在模型计算过程中持续变化，使"观测新鲜度"与"动作交付时间"成为世界-动作规划的核心矛盾。在自动驾驶中保留丰富的生成式世界先验，往往因世界模型的沉重计算而拖慢实时控制，二者难以兼顾。

Method: 采用双GPU异步架构：后台GPU上运行慢速Video DiT，将环视历史流式转化为只读的预测性世界上下文，并用"预测跨度"训练以覆盖更新间隔与后台世界计算耗时；前台GPU上，轻量"新鲜度载体"在每步将当前环视图像编码为门控KV bank，使快速Action DiT无需等待即可融合实时视觉反馈与最新已完成的世界上下文。

Result: 开环NAVSIM：配轨迹评分器达94.2 PDMS，单次决策198.4 ms；闭环Bench2Drive：80.30 Driving Score，前台上109.9 ms延迟；迁移至Waymo E2E隐藏测试得8.0050 RFS，nuScenes上平均终点误差0.54 m、平均碰撞率0.03%。四个基准上均优于对比的世界-动作方法。

Conclusion: 通过异步解耦世界更新与动作生成，SWIFT在保留生成式世界模型先验的同时满足实时性要求，实现了精度-延迟的有利权衡，并具备跨数据集泛化能力。
