Abstract: Driving scenes keep changing during model computation, making both observation freshness and action-delivery time central to world-action planning. To retain rich generative world priors in autonomous driving without stalling real-time control, we present SWIFT (Slow World, Instant Freshness, and Timely action), a deployment-oriented asynchronous world-action system that moves world updates off the observation-to-action critical path. On a background GPU, a slow Video DiT streams surround-view history into read-only predictive world context, trained with a predictive span that covers both the update interval and background world computation. On a foreground GPU, a lightweight freshness carrier encodes current surround-view images into a gated key/value bank at every step, letting a fast Action DiT combine live visual feedback with the latest completed world context without waiting. On open-loop NAVSIM, SWIFT reaches 94.2 PDMS at 198.4 ms per decision with a trajectory scorer; on closed-loop Bench2Drive, it achieves an 80.30 Driving Score at 109.9 ms foreground latency. SWIFT further transfers to the Waymo E2E hidden test (8.0050 Rater Feedback Score) and nuScenes (0.54 m mean endpoint error, 0.03% mean collision rate). Across all four benchmarks, SWIFT achieves the best results among compared world-action methods, establishing a favorable accuracy-latency trade-off.

</details>


### [35] [ROBIN++: Unified Copyright Protection and Tamper Localization for Diffusion Models via Dual-Domain Synergistic Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23793)
*Huayang Huang,Siqi Zeng,Qian Wang,Bo Du,Yu Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ROBIN++ 提出双域协同（Dual-Domain Synergistic）水印范式，将鲁棒性版权水印注入频域、脆弱性定位水印注入空间域，验证时再耦合两者相互增强，缓解版权鲁棒性与篡改定位灵敏度之间的固有权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成内容水印方法因在单一域内耦合了相互冲突的信号，天然存在"版权鲁棒性 vs 定位灵敏度"的权衡；作者希望打破这种单域纠缠，显式解耦两类需求。

Method: 1) 注入阶段显式解耦：用对抗优化算法（Adversarial Optimization Algorithm）在生成过程中向频域嵌入版权水印，最大化提取鲁棒性且无视觉伪影；同时将内容感知扰动生成器（Content-Aware Perturbation generator）集成到潜空间，注入空间域定位水印，保证对空间篡改敏感且对版权水印干扰最小。2) 验证阶段再耦合：设计双分支协同检测器，实现 Frequency-to-Spatial Synergy（以鲁棒频谱先验作全局锚点指导局部异常检测）与 Spatial-to-Frequency Refinement（用预测的篡改掩码校正受损潜特征）。

Result: 大量实验表明，ROBIN++ 在版权恢复与篡改定位精度两方面均显著优于现有方法，有效缓解了两任务间长期存在的权衡问题。

Conclusion: 通过"注入时解耦、验证时耦合"的双域协同设计，可在不牺牲视觉质量的前提下同时获得高鲁棒版权验证与高灵敏篡改定位，为生成内容溯源与完整性认证提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Digital watermarking provides a promising solution for verifying the provenance and integrity of generated content. However, existing methods often suffer from an inherent trade-off between copyright robustness and localization sensitivity due to the entanglement of conflicting signals within a single domain. In this paper, we propose ROBIN++, a paradigm that shifts from single-domain entanglement to a Dual-Domain Synergistic design. Our key insight is to explicitly decouple robustness and fragility during injection by assigning them to distinct domains, while re-coupling them for mutual reinforcement during verification. Specifically, we introduce an Adversarial Optimization Algorithm to inject a frequency-domain copyright watermark during generation, maximizing extraction robustness without visual artifacts. Complementarily, a Content-Aware Perturbation generator is integrated into the latent space to inject the spatial-domain localization watermark, ensuring sensitivity to spatial tampering with minimal interference to the copyright watermark. During verification, we propose a dual-branch synergistic detector that facilitates Frequency-to-Spatial Synergy, where robust spectral priors act as global anchors for local anomaly detection. Conversely, a Spatial-to-Frequency Refinement strategy is employed to rectify corrupted latent features using predicted tamper masks. Extensive experiments show that ROBIN++ significantly outperforms existing approaches in both copyright recovery and tamper localization accuracy, effectively mitigating the long-standing trade-off between the two tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/Hannah1102/ROBIN.

</details>


### [36] [Detect What You Need: Chain-of-Causal Reasoning for 3D Intent Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23754)
*Zihao Zhang,Aming Wu,Yang Li,Yahong Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Chain-of-Causal Reasoning (CoCR)框架，通过显式的"意图—功能—物体"推理链与因果启发的功能依赖图，将3D意图定位中的抽象意图与目标物体显式对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D Intention Grounding（3D-IG）中的意图抽象且非描述性，需从非描述性意图中推断潜在功能需求并与物体级3D表征对齐；现有方法依赖隐式意图—物体匹配，存在逻辑断层，可解释性、鲁棒性和泛化能力有限。

Method: CoCR逐步将复杂意图分解为有序功能需求，形成显式的意图—功能—物体推理链；在此基础上构建因果启发的功能依赖图以建模需求—属性关系，并引入因果—视觉对齐模块，将功能感知表征与3D点云的几何—语义特征对齐，实现结构化功能推理与视觉证据的双向验证。

Result: 在3D Intention Grounding与3D Visual Grounding任务上的大量实验表明，该方法提升了意图感知的物体定位性能。

Conclusion: 显式因果功能推理链可有效弥合抽象意图与3D物体表征之间的鸿沟，提升3D-IG的可解释性、鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Accurately matching human intentions in 3D space is an important goal of artificial intelligence. Recently, 3D Intention Grounding (3D-IG) has emerged, aiming to localize target 3D objects given a natural-language intent. Unlike conventional visual grounding with descriptive referring expressions, 3D-IG intents are abstract and non-descriptive, making object localization substantially more challenging. This requires inferring latent functional requirements from non-descriptive intents and aligning them with object-level 3D representations. However, existing methods largely rely on implicit intent-object matching, leading to logical gaps and limited interpretability, robustness, and generalization. To address these challenges, we propose Chain-of-Causal Reasoning (CoCR), a causality-inspired functional dependency reasoning framework that explicitly bridges abstract intentions and candidate objects through intermediate functional requirements. Specifically, CoCR progressively decomposes complex intents into ordered functional requirements, thereby forming an explicit intent-function-object reasoning chain that links abstract intentions to object suitability. Building on this chain, we construct a causality-inspired functional dependency graph to model requirement-attribute relationships and introduce a causal-visual alignment module that aligns function-aware representations with the geometric-semantic features of 3D point clouds, enabling bidirectional verification between structured functional reasoning and visual evidence. Extensive experiments on 3D Intention Grounding and 3D Visual Grounding demonstrate that our method enhances intent-aware object localization. Our code is available at https://2490o.github.io/CoCR.

</details>


### [37] [Image-Condition-Controlled Diffusion Network with Large-Scale CT Pretraining for CT-Free Attenuation Correction in Dynamic Cardiac PET](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23672)
*Zhejia Zhang,Scott M. Leonard,Jinkui Hao,Ryan J. Avery,Richard L. Weinberg,Bo Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 我们提出 LUCID：利用 10,000 例公开胸部 CT 预训练扩散模型，从无 CT 配对数据中为动态心脏 ⁸²Rb PET 合成 μ-map，并引入体素级时间标准差（TSD）评估帧间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态 PET 定量心肌灌注依赖准确的衰减校正，临床 μ-map 多来自 CT，但 CT 带来额外辐射暴露以及呼吸/心脏运动导致的 PET-CT 配准误差；有监督的无 CT 方法又依赖稀缺的配对 NAC PET-CT 数据，而无配对 CT 数据更易获得。

Method: LUCID 框架分三步：(1) CNN 由每帧 NAC PET 预测初始 μ-map；(2) 以 CT-RATE 10,000 例胸部 CT 预训练的扩散主干，对 CNN 预测加入低噪声扰动后经单步去噪进行精炼，并通过 ControlNet 以对应 NAC PET 帧为条件；(3) 逐帧预测做体素级平均得到最终 μ-map。另提出体素级时间标准差（TSD），无需参考 μ-map 即可量化帧间变异性。

Result: 在 102 例内部数据上评估，LUCID 取得最佳定量精度，同时保持有竞争力的帧间一致性。消融显示：仅用训练患者内部 CT 预训练的扩散主干精炼，精度反而不如单独 CNN 预测，说明大规模 CT 预训练是关键。

Conclusion: 大规模无配对 CT 预训练可有效迁移解剖先验，实现无 CT 的动态心脏 PET 衰减校正；预训练数据规模与多样性比单纯微调更重要。

Abstract: Positron emission tomography (PET) provides quantitative information on myocardial perfusion. In dynamic PET, tracer kinetics are tracked through a series of reconstructed time frames. Reliable attenuation correction is essential for accurate measurements. Clinical attenuation maps ($μ$-maps) are typically derived from computed tomography (CT), which adds radiation exposure and may introduce PET-CT misregistration due to respiratory and cardiac motion. Moreover, supervised CT-free methods rely on paired non-attenuation-corrected (NAC) PET and CT data, which are scarce, whereas large-scale unpaired CT data are more readily available. To address these challenges, we propose Large-scale Unpaired CT-Informed Diffusion (LUCID), a framework that transfers anatomical priors learned from 10,000 chest CT volumes in the public CT-RATE dataset to $μ$-map synthesis for dynamic cardiac $^{82}$Rb PET. First, a convolutional neural network (CNN) predicts an initial $μ$-map from each NAC PET frame. Second, the CT-pretrained diffusion backbone refines a low-noise perturbation of the CNN prediction through a single denoising step, conditioned on the corresponding NAC PET frame via ControlNet. Finally, frame-wise predictions are averaged voxel-wise to obtain the final $μ$-map. We further introduce the voxel-wise temporal standard deviation (TSD) to quantify inter-frame variability without requiring a reference $μ$-map. We evaluated LUCID on an in-house dataset of 102 patients and found that it achieved the best quantitative accuracy while maintaining competitive inter-frame consistency. Ablation studies showed that refinement with a diffusion backbone pretrained on in-house CT data from the training patients was less accurate than the CNN prediction alone, highlighting the importance of large-scale CT pretraining.

</details>


### [38] [EyeDiT: Anatomy-Grounded Multi-Modal Eye Generation and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23670)
*Yonghan Lee,Zachary Willms,Kun Liu,Pushkar Anand,Ali Behrooz,Dmitri Model,Dinesh Manocha,Yatong An*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EyeDiT：以解剖结构为基础的多模态 3D 眼睛生成与仿真模型，借助 OCT、解剖点云与近眼图像训练，实现对视轴、瞳孔与视角的可控合成，并显著缩小眼动追踪的仿真-真实域差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成眼睛生成器以图像为中心，缺乏高质量三维解剖结构，控制维度基本局限于视线方向，且不提供解剖标签，难以支撑 AR/VR 眼动追踪数据的高效扩充。

Method: 构建多模态流程：先训练 Multi-Modal Anatomy VAE，将 OCT、解剖标注点云与近眼图像编码为解剖感知的潜表示；再用 Eye Latent DiT 以视轴、瞳孔位置与半径、受试者身份为条件生成潜样本；最后配合高斯解码器实现新视角仿真。

Result: 新视角合成 PSNR 达 24.9 dB（单视角输入基线 21.8 dB）；在合成数据上训练公开眼动追踪网络，仿真-真实差距为 3.5° 对比其他生成器的 8.4°，接近真实数据训练网络；与真实数据联合训练可进一步提升至 2.3°（基线 2.6°）。

Conclusion: 解剖驱动的多模态生成范式可同时提升合成数据的几何真实感与下游眼动追踪的迁移性能，为 AR/VR 眼动追踪数据合成提供可行方案。

Abstract: Synthetic eye data are critical for rapid development of eye tracking (ET) in AR/VR applications. However, existing synthetic eye generators are largely image-centric and lack a high-quality underlying 3D anatomy of the eye, which limits their control mainly to the gaze and provides no anatomical labels. To address this problem, we propose EyeDiT, an anatomy-grounded, multi-modal 3D eye generation and simulation model trained with optical coherence tomography (OCT), labeled anatomy point clouds and near-eye images. Our pipeline first trains a Multi-Modal Anatomy Variational Autoencoder (VAE), which encodes an anatomy-aware latent representation from all input modalities. Then, our Eye Latent DiT is trained to generate latent samples conditioned on the optical axis, the pupil position and radius, and the subject identity. Together with Gaussian decoders for novel-view simulation, our model enables control over the pupil, subject identity and viewpoint for data synthesis. We show that our model outperforms previous eye image synthesis models in novel-view synthesis (24.9 against 21.8 dB PSNR from a single view), and that a public ET neural network trained on our synthetic data shows a smaller sim-to-real gap than when trained on data from other generators (3.5$^\circ$ vs. 8.4$^\circ$ gaze error), approaches a network trained on real data, and further improves ET performance when combined with real data (2.3$^\circ$ vs. 2.6$^\circ$). More information is available on the project page: https://lyhan12.github.io/eyedit

</details>


### [39] [Trust the Ray, Not the Depth: Ray-Lateral Agreement for Multi-View 3D Human Pose Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23513)
*Farnoosh Koleini,Ahmed Helmy,Pu Wang,Xiwu Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Ray-Lateral Agreement (RLA)：把每个单目网格估计沿其自身视线滑动以消除网络焦距假设带来的深度偏置，再按高斯误差逐点做闭式最大似然融合，并用沿视线方向的残差进行鲁棒加权，在多相机公开基准上取得比所有三角化基线更低的 worst-case 误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在校准多相机 rig 的每个相机后放一个单目网格估计器时，重建人体会相差一米以上。原因是几何性的：每个估计落在网络所假设焦距隐含的深度上，而非真实相机焦距对应的深度，误差几乎完全沿各自视线方向，而该方向可由标定精确给出。

Method: RLA 遵循"信射线、不信深度"：第一步将每个估计沿自身射线滑动，去除深度偏置且不改变形状；第二步逐点融合多视角，采用精度随视线方向变化的高斯误差模型，最大似然解为闭式，介于平均与中点三角化之间，并在三角化奇异处保持良态。鲁棒性方面，因正确视角也会沿射线不一致，基于全残差的权重会误罚好视角，故用每个视角在每个点上仅沿该视角射线的残差的 ray-lateral agreement 进行打分加权。

Result: 在多个多相机公开基准数据集上，跨两、三、四相机设置以及三种单视角姿态估计器，RLA 的 worst-case 误差一致低于所有三角化基线。

Conclusion: 误差本质上是沿视线的深度偏置，而非形状误差；沿射线滑动加射线方向残差加权是简单、闭式且鲁棒的融合方案，在 worst-case 意义下优于传统三角化。

Abstract: Put a monocular mesh estimator behind every camera of a calibrated rig, and the reconstructed bodies land a meter or more apart. The cause is geometric: each estimate sits at the depth implied by a focal length the network assumed, not the one the camera has. Its error therefore points almost entirely along its own viewing ray, and the calibration tells us that direction exactly. To address this, we introduce the Ray-Lateral Agreement (RLA), which follows one rule: trust the ray, not the depth. RLA first slides every estimate along its own rays, which removes the depth bias and leaves the shape untouched. It then fuses the views one point at a time, under a Gaussian error whose precision follows each viewing ray. The maximum-likelihood solution is closed-form, interpolates between averaging and midpoint triangulation, and stays well conditioned where triangulation is singular. Robustness is the harder half, because correct views also disagree along their rays. Any weight built on the full residual therefore punishes good views together with bad ones. Ray-lateral agreement instead scores every view, at every point, by its residual across that view's own ray alone. We evaluated RLA on multi-camera public benchmark datasets. Across two, three, and four cameras and three per-view pose estimators, RLA consistently achieves lower worst-case error than all triangulation baselines.

</details>


### [40] [MedHarness: A Unified Budgeted Runtime for Reliable Medical Imaging Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23501)
*Haofan Wu,Le Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedHarness 是一个"注册式多表面运行时可靠性层"，将医学影像智能体回答后的控制问题重构为共享决策对象挂载问题，用单一共享策略配合各表面配方适配器，统一实现"停止/开启更多证据/修复/拒绝"控制，从而在有限验证预算下同时降低错误接受率与远程调用成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具调用式医学影像流程要么按数据集锁定某一表面特定的证据顺序，要么固定走全深度验证路径，导致成本高、错误接受多，且同一控制器无法跨模态共享；当浅层检查与深度证据检验对同一病例给出冲突结论时，缺少共享控制器来决定何时停止、开更多证据、修复或拒绝，问题呈"病态"（ill-posed）。

Method: 提出 MedHarness，在提议者（proposer）初始预测之后进行后答案控制，将控制重构为共享决策对象挂载。三项设计原则：Shared Decision Object（共享决策对象）、Minimum-Sufficient Verification（最小充分验证）、Intermediate Repair（中间修复）。一个共享策略加各表面配方适配器，新影像表面只需"配方注册"即可接入同一 stop/open-evidence/repair/reject 控制器。

Result: 512 例在线池中，相对全验证基线错误接受降低约47%，在86.1%覆盖率下接受准确率83.2% vs 72.9%（基线为全量放行）；在失败富集的多表面压力排序中，匹配上下文负载设置下每例远程服务调用估计降低约71%；三表面留出检验与另一语言模型迁移均保持相同控制排序。

Conclusion: 单一共享控制器可通过配方注册跨影像表面复用，在有限验证预算下以更低服务成本显著减少错误接受并提升接受准确率，且控制排序在留出表面与模型迁移下保持稳定，代码已开源。

Abstract: Medical imaging agents already return answers on heterogeneous imaging surfaces, yet deployment still faces a limited verification budget where each wrong accept can harm clinical trust. When a thin check and a deeper evidence pass disagree on the same case, deciding when to stop, open more evidence, repair, or reject without a shared controller remains ill-posed. Tool-using workflows often lock a surface-specific evidence order per dataset, or a private always-open full-depth verification path; those pipelines stay costly, leave many wrong accepts, and cannot share one controller across modalities. We present MedHarness, a registered multi-surface runtime reliability layer that reformulates post-answer control as shared decision-object attachment after a proposer's initial prediction. Under Shared Decision Object, Minimum-Sufficient Verification, and Intermediate Repair, one shared policy with surface recipe adapters lets a new imaging surface join the same stop / open-evidence / repair / reject controller by recipe registration. On a 512-case live pool, MedHarness cuts wrong accepts versus a full-verification baseline by about 47% and raises accepted accuracy to 83.2% vs. 72.9% at 86.1% coverage versus full release under that baseline. On a failure-enriched multi-surface stress ranking, the same controller cuts estimated remote service calls per case by about 71% versus that baseline under a matched context-load setting. A held-out three-surface check and an alternate language-model transfer keep the same control ordering. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/MedHarness-paper-release-A7F3/.

</details>


### [41] [Reference-Guided Data Curation for 3D Medical Vision-Language Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23484)
*Faidra Patsatzi,Ashwin Kumar,Chong Wang,Curtis P. Langlotz,Reinhard Heckel,Akshay S. Chaudhari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种离线、免训练、参考集引导的数据策展方法，用少量高质量参考样本为大规模未筛选医学图像-报告对独立提取各模态质量信号，经秩聚合形成单一排序以指导样本选择，从而提升3D视觉-语言模型预训练的数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨机构采集的大规模医学影像数据质量参差，未筛选的图像-报告对池存在冗余、类别不平衡与跨机构异质性，直接用于3D VLM预训练效果受限；需要一个低成本、可复用的数据策展方案。

Method: 离线、免训练、参考集引导的策展流程：以小型高质量参考集为锚，对大规模未筛选池按模态分别提取质量信号（如相似度/质量评分），再通过秩聚合（rank aggregation）将多信号融合为单一排序，据此进行样本选择；在35k 3D腹部CT体数据-报告对（两家机构）与58k 2D胸片图像-报告对上验证。

Result: 3D CT上，用5k参考集与约3.3倍更少训练数据，策展模型的零样本疾病分类性能与SOTA基线Merlin持平；2D胸片上，仅用74个参考样本、最多10倍更少数据即达到或超过全池训练模型的零样本性能。消融显示：排序在参考集重采样下稳定，更小参考集仍优于随机采样，标签重平衡无增益。

Conclusion: 参考集引导的离线免训练策展可在显著降低数据量与标注需求的同时保持甚至提升3D/2D医学VLM的零样本诊断分类性能，且方法稳定、可复现（已开源）。

Abstract: Training 3D vision-language models (VLMs) on large medical imaging datasets collected across institutions is constrained by data quality. Vast, uncurated pools of image-report pairs are characterized by redundancy, class imbalance, and cross-institutional heterogeneity. We address this challenge of data curation for improving 3D VLM pretraining by proposing an offline, training-free, reference-guided data curation recipe. Our approach leverages a small, high-quality reference set to independently extract quality signals for each modality of a large, uncurated pool and merges them through a rank-aggregation step into a single ranking that guides sample selection. We validate this method on datasets comprising 35k 3D abdominal CT volume-report pairs from two institutions and 58k 2D chest X-ray image-report pairs. On 3D CT, using our reference-guided data curation strategy, our curated model matches the zero-shot disease classification performance of a state-of-the-art baseline (Merlin) while using $3.3\times$ less training data and a reference set of 5k samples. On 2D chest X-rays, our method yields a curated model that matches or exceeds the zero-shot disease classification performance of a model trained on the full pool while using up to $10\times$ less training data and a reference set of only 74 samples. Ablations show that the selection is stable under resampling of the reference, that smaller reference sets still outperform random sampling, and that label rebalancing does not improve performance. To support reproducibility, we release our code.

</details>


### [42] [Isthmus: Bridging Supervision Islands Restores Uncertainty and Stability in One-Step Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23616)
*Luming Yang,Siqing Li,Haoxian Liu,Zhenyu Bu,Jiazheng Zhu,Alper Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文发现一步流映射采样器丢失了采样所依赖的不确定性，根源在于训练时间对（start/end times）的"孤岛"问题，并提出"地峡（isthmus）"方案将训练对沿三角形边缘连接到对角线，从而在不改网络、损失与推理代码、无额外开销的情况下显著提升一步采样的稳定性与校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一步流映射被广泛用作后验采样器，其关键在于样本所携带的不确定性。然而作者发现一步采样器会丢失这种不确定性并变得不稳定：其样本相比同一网络的多步采样方差减小、去相关；直接监督一步跳跃反而更糟——样本进一步塌缩到条件均值，且训练时间延长时崩溃。作者将这两类失败归因于训练放置起止时间对的位置。

Method: 通过分析时间三角形上训练对的分布，指出只有对角线上的对从数据中获得目标，而用于一步采样的对要么未被监督，要么处于"孤岛"（自一致性恒等式无法将其连接到对角线的一对对，目标既不决定一步映射也不锚定其训练）。为此提出"地峡"（isthmus）：将5%的训练对沿三角形中连接一步对与对角线的整条边放置，使一步映射的误差可由沿该边的自一致性残差上界约束。

Result: 在网络、损失和推理代码不变且无额外开销的条件下，地峡在多种流映射目标、MRI重建、人脸修复以及CIFAR-10与ImageNet 32x32无条件生成上均提升一步采样。在脑部MRI上，将一步校准恢复到0.96、与多步样本的相关性恢复到0.93；两者在不同随机种子间波动大幅减小，且训练时长延长至三倍时该一致性仍成立。

Conclusion: 一步流映射的不确定性丢失与不稳定性源于训练时间对的分布缺陷（孤岛），通过"地峡"沿三角形边缘对齐训练即可在不增加成本的前提下修复，并带来跨任务、跨目标、可扩展到更长训练的稳健一步采样改进。

Abstract: One-step flow maps are increasingly used as posterior samplers, where the point of sampling is the uncertainty that the samples carry. We find that the one-step sampler loses this uncertainty and becomes less stable. Compared with multi-step samples of the same network, its samples lose variance and decorrelate. Supervising the one-step jump directly makes this worse; the samples collapse further toward the conditional mean and break down when training is extended. We trace both failures to the locations where training places its pairs of start and end times. Only pairs on the diagonal of the time triangle receive targets from data, and the pair used for one-step sampling is either left unsupervised or supervised on an island, a set of pairs that the self-consistency identity does not connect to the diagonal, where the objective neither determines the one-step map nor anchors its training. We bridge the island with an isthmus, which places 5% of the training pairs along the whole edge of the triangle that joins the one-step pair to the diagonal. The error of the one-step map is then bounded by the self-consistency residual along this edge. With the network, the loss and the inference code unchanged and at no added cost, the isthmus improves one-step sampling across several flow-map objectives, in MRI reconstruction, face inpainting, and unconditional generation on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet 32x32. On brain MRI, it restores one-step calibration to 0.96 and the correlation with multi-step samples to 0.93; both vary far less across seeds, and the agreement holds when training is extended to three times its length.

</details>


### [43] [GaussLearner: 3D Gaussian Primitives as Self-Regulating Continual Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23736)
*Gaoxiang Linghu,Haonan Zhou,Xuanhe Liu,Weili Xu,Kaiyue Zhou,Jun Xiao,Gaoang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将3D高斯基元本身视为自调节的持续学习器，提出GaussLearner，为每个高斯建立"观测—自调节—适应"的闭环持续学习回路，在CL-Splats、WAT和自建CL-3D数据集上平均PSNR比最强基线提升3.41 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对新观测直接微调已有3D高斯场会导致对既往重建内容的灾难性遗忘；现有方法（选择性局部优化、回放、参数正则化）仍难以兼顾"适应场景变化"与"保留仍然有效的内容"。

Method: 核心思想是把3D高斯基元视为自调节的持续学习器。每一步新状态下：1）将当前观测中的像素级不一致归因到贡献该像素的高斯；2）每个高斯通过一个学习权重进行自调节，该权重在当前观测与跨状态可靠性之间做平衡；3）该权重同时支配高斯参数适应与优化器记忆刷新；4）随着不一致被消解，适应结果更新每个高斯对未来状态的可靠性。最终场为全局耦合的局部自调节学习器群体。

Result: 在CL-Splats、WAT及自建CL-3D数据集上，平均PSNR较最强基线提升3.41 dB；在CL-3D上于变化视角与未变化视角均取得最佳重建质量。

Conclusion: 以基元级闭环自调节替代全局层面的启发式策略，可有效缓解灾难性遗忘并同时实现场景变化的适应；基元级可靠性-权重机制为3D高斯场的持续学习提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Continual learning of 3D Gaussian fields aims to maintain faithful reconstructions of the evolving world. However, directly fine-tuning an existing Gaussian field on new observations can cause catastrophic forgetting of previously reconstructed content. To address this challenge, existing methods employ selective local optimization, replay, or parameter regularization, yet still struggle to balance adaptation to scene changes with preservation of still-valid content. Our key insight is to view 3D Gaussian primitives themselves as self-regulating continual learners. Based on this insight, we introduce GaussLearner, which equips each Gaussian with a closed continual-learning loop connecting observation, self-regulation, and adaptation at the primitive level. At each novel state, GaussLearner first attributes pixel-wise inconsistencies in current observations to their contributing Gaussians. Each Gaussian then self-regulates through a learning weight that balances current observations against cross-state reliability. This weight governs both Gaussian parameter adaptation and optimizer memory refresh. As current inconsistencies are resolved, the adaptation outcome renews each Gaussian's reliability for future states. The resulting field is a globally coupled population of locally self-regulating learners. Across scenes from CL-Splats, WAT, and our CL-3D dataset, GaussLearner improves average PSNR by 3.41 dB over the strongest baseline. On CL-3D, it achieves the best reconstruction quality on both changed and unchanged views.

</details>


### [44] [ORGANIC3D: Grounding 3D CT Vision Language Models in Organ Level Image Text Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23529)
*Zelong Liu,Hamza Ahmed,Wynn Wingyi Lee,Mickael Torjman,Alexander Dimitrakakis,Jiahua Lu,Timothy Deyer,Bachir Taouli,Zahi A. Fayad,Xueyan Mei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ORGANIC3D框架，通过受控解剖干预评估并提升3D CT视觉语言模型的器官级图文对齐，在保持SOTA分类性能的同时显著改善证据依赖性与解剖忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在3D CT分类上准确率高，但高准确率并不说明模型预测是否依赖正确的解剖证据，存在"对齐失败"隐患，缺乏系统性评估与改进手段。

Method: 构建基准，对12个视觉编码器和7个生成式VLM采用匹配患者比较、器官擦除、病灶替换、侧别扰动等干预；将每条发现句与解剖感知图像特征对齐，并在编辑后扫描上训练；扩展至生成式VLM以促进证据依赖的问答与报告生成，仅用公开数据训练。

Result: 在CTRATE、Merlin及留出集RADChestCT上取得SOTA分类AUROC；侧别准确率77%（此前最高66%）；生成模型在VQA上持平或超越所有公开VLM，报告准确率最高；器官移除后撤回96–99%目标发现（其他模型最高69%）。

Conclusion: ORGANIC3D既是检验3D CT VLM是否以解剖证据为基础的测试框架，也能在提升预测准确率的同时改善器官级图文对齐。

Abstract: Vision language models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance in 3D computed tomography (CT) classification, but high accuracy does not reflect whether their predictions rely on the correct anatomical evidence. We introduce ORGANIC3D, a framework for evaluating and improving organ level image text alignment through controlled anatomical interventions. Our benchmark assesses 12 vision encoder checkpoints and 7 generative VLMs using matched patient comparisons, organ erasure, lesion replacement, and laterality perturbations, revealing substantial alignment failures even among top performing models. To learn organ level image text correspondence, ORGANIC3D aligns each finding sentence with the anatomy aware image features and trains on edited scans to anchor this alignment in the organ's image evidence. We further extend this framework to generative VLMs, promoting evidence dependent question answering and report generation. Trained exclusively on public data, ORGANIC3D achieves SOTA classification AUROC on CTRATE, Merlin, and the heldout RADChestCT dataset and reaches 77% laterality accuracy, compared with at most 66% for prior models. Its generative VLM matches or exceeds all evaluated publicly released VLMs on visual question answering, achieves the highest report accuracy, and retracts 96 to 99% of target organ findings after organ removal, versus a maximum of 69% for other models. Together, these results establish ORGANIC3D as a framework for testing whether 3D CT vision language models ground their predictions in anatomical evidence and for improving organ level alignment alongside predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [45] [TOGBench: Benchmarking and Improving Tiny-Object Generation Fidelity in Aerial Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23840)
*Chanhwi Jeong,Dongmin You,Seungwook Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对航空影像中微小目标生成保真度被忽视的问题，提出对象级定量基准 TOGBench，并提出两种训练/推理期技巧提升布局到图像生成器渲染微小目标的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度生成模型已能合成逼真图像，但航空影像受俯视、广域成像特性影响，车辆、船舶、飞机等目标仅占几个像素。现有生成模型对微小目标的表现明显劣于大目标，而以往的生成评估侧重提示词遵循或人类偏好，缺乏对微小目标是否被忠实渲染的考察，存在评估空白。

Method: 构建 TOGBench——一个面向航空影像、以对象级、定量方式评估微小目标生成保真度的基准；并设计两种简单的训练期/推理期技术，用于增强布局到图像（layout-to-image）生成器渲染小目标的能力。

Result: 所提出的两种技术在 layout-to-image 生成器上提升了 TOGBench 上的性能，改善了微小目标的渲染效果。

Conclusion: 微小目标生成是航空影像生成模型中被长期忽视的短板；TOGBench 填补了对象级定量评估的空白，配套的轻量训练/推理技巧可有效改进现有生成器，为该方向提供了基准与方法基线。

Abstract: Advances in deep generative models now enable the synthesis of realistic imagery. However, aerial imagery is dominated by tiny objects, such as vehicles, ships, and aircraft, which occupy only a few pixels owing to the overhead, wide-area nature of aerial imaging. Existing generative models struggle substantially more with tiny objects than with larger ones. This limitation has been overlooked, as prior work on generative evaluation emphasizes prompt following or human preference rather than whether small objects are rendered faithfully. To address this gap, we introduce TOGBench, a quantitative, object-level benchmark for evaluating tiny-object generation fidelity in aerial imagery. We further propose two simple train-/inference-time techniques that improve the ability of layout-to-image generators to render small objects, raising their performance on TOGBench.

</details>


### [46] [ACUS: An Angular Calibrated Uncertainty Score for Incomplete-View CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23752)
*Zihan Deng,Chuanzhi Xu,Quankeng Huang,Yujie Liu,Zhenzhen Gong,Junjie Huang,Tianye Niu,Lequan Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ACUS指标，按与最近采集视图的角距离分箱评估预测区间可靠性，揭示稀疏/有限弧CT预测的间隙依赖过自信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视图与有限弧CT的预测常被当作投影补全，仅用与单一真值图像的相似度（如PSNR）排名；当查询角远离所有采集测量时，学习先验可能虚构扫描未约束的解剖结构，相似度仍可很高。现有协议无法回答"预测可靠性如何随与采集视图的距离变化"这一基本问题。

Method: 提出角校准不确定性分数ACUS：对留出投影上的名义90%预测区间计算Winkler区间分数，并按与最近采集视图的角距离分箱；该分数同时计入区间宽度并对落在区间外的像素施加较大漏检惩罚，使窄区间仅在覆盖率成立时才有益。另以采集角度上的塌缩（collapse）作为预测器的必要一致性检查。

Result: 在胸部与牙科锥束轨迹上，区间宽度随角间隙增大而增大；经验覆盖率在77%至92%之间；点估计的PSNR随宽度上升而下降。最近邻、插值及点补全方法在给定单一残差尺度时，远端报告覆盖率为零，并产生区间外距离惩罚。

Conclusion: ACUS能够暴露仅靠点估计PSNR无法测量的、依赖于角间隙的过自信问题，为CT预测可靠性提供了随角距离校准的评估框架。

Abstract: Sparse-view and limited-arc computed tomography (CT) are commonly treated as projection completion: a network fills an unmeasured view, and the result is ranked by similarity to one ground-truth image. That score can be high even when the query angle is far from every acquired measurement, because a learned prior may invent anatomy the scan never constrained. Existing protocols therefore leave a basic question unanswered: how does predictive reliability change with distance from the acquired views? We propose the Angular Calibrated Uncertainty Score (ACUS) to answer that question. ACUS is the Winkler interval score of a nominal 90% predictive interval on held-out projections, binned by distance to the nearest acquired view. It charges interval width and adds a large miss penalty when a held-out pixel falls outside the interval, so a narrow band helps only when coverage holds. We use collapse on acquired angles as a necessary consistency check on the predictor. On chest and dental cone-beam orbits, interval width grows with the angular gap. Empirical coverage ranges from 77% to 92%, and the peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) of the point estimate falls as the width rises. Nearest-neighbor, interpolation, and point completion, each given one residual scale, have zero reported far coverage and incur outside-distance penalties. ACUS exposes gap-dependent overconfidence that a point-estimate PSNR alone does not measure.

</details>


### [47] [Do 3D Point Representations Encode Architectural Functions? Probing Supervised and Pretrained Models on Urban Facades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23720)
*Abdiaziz Muse,Jiju Poovvancheri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文探究3D点云表示是否编码建筑元素的"功能"(function)而非仅是语义标签。在22栋移动LiDAR建筑、每元素15个功能属性的留楼(leave-building-out)设定下，对比手工描述子、从头训练网络与冻结的自监督点云Transformer。结果表明：从头训练网络（含108M参数PTv3）均无法明显超越手工描述子(0.610 mAP)，而冻结的Utonia达0.732 mAP，增益可归因于预训练(随机权重对照0.649/0.652)。预训练特征在各标注楼栋数下均优于描述子，且微调会侵蚀该优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义标签只说明建筑元素"是什么"，而非"做什么"：外观相同的窗户可有可开/固定之别，柱可能承重或仅装饰。作者因此提出核心问题——3D点表示是否编码这类建筑功能。

Method: 在22栋移动LiDAR扫描建筑上标注每元素15个功能属性；采用留出整栋建筑的泛化评估；比较三类特征：手工描述子、从头训练的网络(PointNet至108M参数PTv3)、冻结的自监督点云Transformer；统一用同一线性层作为读出器；并设置同架构随机权重对照以隔离预训练贡献，统计上采用bootstrap建筑置信区间。

Result: 功能模糊元素上，手工描述子达0.610 mAP，从头训练网络无一明显超越；冻结Utonia达0.732 mAP。随机权重对照分别为0.649与0.652，据此判定预训练增益为+0.080(95%建筑区间[+0.035,+0.124])。预训练特征在所有测试的标注楼栋数下均提升，而描述子与随机特征趋于饱和。

Conclusion: 在测试配置中，微调反而侵蚀预训练带来的收益；在当前数据规模下，建筑功能更适合从预训练表示中"读出"，而非从头"学得"。这提示功能理解应依赖自监督预训练表示，而非增加模型容量或微调。

Abstract: Semantic labels say what a building element is, not what it does: two identical-looking windows on one facade may be operable or fixed, a column may carry load or only decorate. We ask whether 3D point representations encode such architectural function. On 22 mobile-LiDAR buildings with 15 functional attributes per element, we hold out entire buildings and compare hand-crafted descriptors, networks trained from scratch and frozen self-supervised point transformers. Descriptors and frozen features are read by the same linear layer. No network trained from scratch, from PointNet to a 108M-parameter PTv3, clearly exceeds the descriptors (0.610 mAP on functionally ambiguous elements), whereas frozen Utonia reaches 0.732. Random-weight controls of the same architectures (0.649 and 0.652) attribute the gain to pretraining (Utonia: +0.080, 95% building interval [+0.035, +0.124]). Pretrained features improve at every tested number of labelled buildings while descriptors and random features plateau, and, in the configurations we tested, fine-tuning erodes the benefit: at today's data scale, function is better read from pretrained representations than learned from scratch.

</details>


### [48] [CoScan: Multi-Scale Content-Adaptive Space-Filling Scans for Causal State-Space Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23717)
*Wei Dong,Terry Ji,Yan Min,Shahram Shirani,Jun Chen,Han Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoScan 提出一种内容自适应的扫描顺序设计：用可微代理监督学习图像相关的空间填充遍历，并通过最小生成树粗化实现多尺度扫描构建，配合频率引导的起点选择，为因果视觉 SSM 提供连贯的 1D 序列化，插入现有 Mamba 骨干后可显著且低开销地提升图像复原性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择性状态空间模型（SSM）用于图像复原时，必须把 2D 特征图线性化为 1D token 序列以进行因果状态更新，因此性能对扫描顺序高度敏感。现有确定性扫描虽遵循空间填充原则，但固定且与内容无关，在退化图像上易产生碎片化、语义不一致的上下文。

Method: 1) 通过可微代理监督学习图像相关的空间填充遍历（learned space-filling traversal），由扫描质量目标驱动，鼓励短程连贯性与因果可预测性，同时保持空间邻接性；2) 为层级复原骨干设计高效多尺度扫描构建——对细粒度最小生成树（MST）进行粗化，得到跨特征分辨率的连贯扫描；3) 频率引导的起点选择策略，将信息量大的结构置于序列前缀，强化因果上下文形成。

Result: 作为即插即用模块接入现有 Mamba 类骨干后，CoScan 在多个图像复原基准上取得一致提升，开销极小；在高层视觉任务上也显示出有前景的结果。代码将开源。

Conclusion: 扫描顺序是因果视觉 SSM 中被忽视但关键的设计维度；以内容自适应的空间填充遍历替代固定确定性扫描，并与多尺度构建和起点选择协同，是一种通用、轻量的性能增强方案。

Abstract: Selective state-space models (SSMs) have recently shown strong potential for efficient image restoration. A central difficulty, however, is that a 2D feature map typically needs to be linearized into a 1D token sequence for causal state-space updates, making restoration performance highly sensitive to the imposed scan order. Existing deterministic scans follow space-filling principles but remain fixed and content-agnostic, and can yield fragmented or semantically inconsistent contexts under degradations. We propose \textbf{CoScan}, a \textbf{co}ntent-adaptive \textbf{scan}-order design for causal vision SSMs. CoScan learns image-dependent space-filling traversals via differentiable proxy supervision driven by \textbf{scan-quality objectives} that \textit{encourage short-range coherence and causal predictability, while preserving spatial adjacency}. To support hierarchical restoration backbones, we introduce an \textbf{efficient multi-scale scan construction by coarsening a fine-level minimum spanning tree}, producing coherent scans across feature resolutions. In addition, a \textbf{frequency-guided start selection strategy} is designed to \textit{place informative structures early in the sequence prefix to strengthen causal context formation}. Plugged into existing Mamba-based backbones, CoScan yields consistent improvements across several restoration benchmarks with minimal overhead and shows promising results on high-level vision tasks. Code will be released at https://github.com/dongw22/CoScan

</details>


### [49] [LightSEAD: Lightweight SE(3)-Aware Keypoint Detector through Selective Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23661)
*Mayowa Adebambo,Alexander D. Rast,Andrew Bradley*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LightSEAD，一个仅6层卷积、84,289参数、SE(3)感知的轻量关键点检测器，通过校准Harris伪标签初始化并结合视角/外观感知监督与选择性知识蒸馏，在保持竞争力的重复性同时实现极高推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习性能提升常伴随架构复杂化与算力需求激增。作者探索"以少胜多"的替代路径：不靠堆叠网络规模，而通过结构化监督（伪标签、视角/外观感知监督、知识蒸馏）让小模型获得强性能。

Method: LightSEAD为仅含6个卷积层、84,289个可学习参数的轻量关键点检测器：(1) 用校准的Harris伪标签初始化；(2) 引入视角感知与外观感知的监督信号；(3) 采用选择性知识蒸馏；(4) 进一步针对混合精度推理优化（含Coarse变体）。

Result: 在HPatches、KITTI 06、4Seasons上取得有竞争力的重复性；在MegaDepth-1500上验证了对相对位姿估计的下游适用性；LightSEAD Coarse在RTX 4090上约909 FPS、Jetson Orin Nano Super上约139 FPS。

Conclusion: 结构化监督可在极小模型上实现强性能，证明轻量SE(3)感知关键点检测器具备实时性与边缘部署潜力，为"以少胜多"的设计范式提供实证支持。

Abstract: The pursuit of improved performance in deep learning has often been accompanied by increasing architectural complexity and computational demands. We explore an alternative direction: achieving more with less through structured supervision. We introduce LightSEAD, a lightweight SE(3)-aware keypoint detector initialised with calibrated Harris pseudo-labels and enriched through viewpoint-aware and appearance-aware supervision and selective knowledge distillation. LightSEAD comprises only six convolutional layers and 84,289 learnable parameters, achieving competitive repeatability across HPatches, KITTI 06 and 4Seasons while demonstrating its downstream applicability to relative pose estimation on MegaDepth-1500. Further optimised for mixed-precision inference, LightSEAD Coarse achieves approximately 909 FPS on an NVIDIA RTX 4090 and 139 FPS on a Jetson Orin Nano Super.

</details>


### [50] [VIBRA: Backward-Compatible Cross-Modal Retrieval with Consensus Barycenters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23606)
*Shubing Yang,Xutong Liu,Dongfang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VIBRA，用多视图质心作为旧空间查询并让新编码器继承这种共识，实现向后兼容的跨模态检索升级，在 50 个设置中全部最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨模态检索系统升级时重编码数十亿条目代价高昂，需要向后兼容检索：新编码器直接查询旧模型已编码的 gallery。现有兼容方法要么贴合旧查询、精度受限于旧模型，要么每次查询都同时运行新旧编码器。

Method: 在旧 gallery 上匹配相关性；用多个视图（多个模型/编码器视角）的质心纠正单模型误差，该质心本身即旧空间中的一个查询；再训练新编码器继承这一共识，使部署时缺席的模型仍能塑造每次查询。理论分析给出：当各视图最难的不相关条目不同时，质心相对视图平均值获得间隔收益，并刻画单编码器可达的收益份额。

Result: 在 6 个 CLIP、MetaCLIP、SigLIP2 骨干间的 5 次升级、5 个基准共 50 个设置中，VIBRA 均为最佳兼容方法；Recall@1 较最强基线提升最多 5.6 点，较旧模型提升最多 18.0 点，而新编码器延迟增加不到 1 ms。

Conclusion: VIBRA 在不重编码 gallery、不额外部署开销的前提下超越所有兼容基线，并显著优于旧模型，为检索系统的高效升级提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Backward-compatible retrieval enables a new encoder to query the gallery that an old model has already encoded, so that a cross-modal retrieval system can be upgraded without re-encoding billions of items. Existing compatible methods either fit the old query and stay near the accuracy of the old model, or run the old and the new encoder for every query. We propose VIBRA, which matches relevance on the old gallery, corrects the errors of a single model with the barycenter of several views, which is itself a query in the old space, and trains the new encoder to inherit this consensus, so that models absent at deployment still shape every query. We show that the barycenter gains margin over the average of its views exactly when their hardest irrelevant items differ, and we characterize the share of this gain that one encoder can reach. On five benchmarks with five upgrades among six CLIP, MetaCLIP and SigLIP2 backbones, VIBRA is the best compatible method in all 50 settings, improving Recall@1 by up to 5.6 points over the strongest baseline and by up to 18.0 points over the old model, while adding less than 1 ms to the latency of the new encoder.

</details>


### [51] [ExiST: Existence-Aware Self-Training for Semi-Supervised Multi-Organ Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23581)
*Maxx Richard Rahman,Prakhar Kumar,Wolfgang Maass*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文识别出半监督多器官分割中自训练的一个失败模式：小器官被漏检时被赋予高置信度背景伪标签，反复迭代后尾器官被"灭绝"。为此提出Existence-aware Self-Training (ExiST)，将缺失器官视为"未决"而非"不存在"，并证明其损失函数不会将候选器官推向缺失。Synapse 20%标注达77.1 Dice，AMOS 5%标注达75.9 Dice，超越现有SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半监督多器官分割需从少量标注扫描中学习十余种器官，而最小器官占前景不足1%。作者发现该场景特有的自训练失效模式：教师网络在某扫描中漏检小器官时，该器官所有体素都被打上高置信度的背景伪标签，学生网络因此被训练成"器官不存在"，经多轮扫描累积后尾器官被彻底抑制。试点实验中移除胆囊伪标签使其在AMOS上的Dice从36.5降至1.0，证实问题严重性。

Method: 提出ExiST框架，包含三部分：(1) 伪标签构建：对独立训练的多网络做留一法一致性共识生成伪标签；(2) 质量门控：在验证扫描上对伪标签进行质量筛选；(3) 存在性推断：逐扫描、逐器官判断该器官是否被找到。对每个"未决"器官，将该扫描的背景体素赋予集合值目标（set-valued target），采用边缘交叉熵损失（接受任一答案）与跳过不确定体素的Dice项进行训练；理论上证明该损失既不会将候选器官推向缺失，也不会偏向集合中任一成员，决策交由有标注证据决定。

Result: ExiST在多个基准上超越当前最先进方法：Synapse数据集使用20%标注扫描时平均Dice达77.1；AMOS数据集使用5%标注扫描时达75.9。理论分析表明该损失函数对未决器官保持中性、不诱导缺失。试点研究量化了器官漏检导致的性能崩塌（胆囊Dice 36.5→1.0）。

Conclusion: 将自训练中"漏检器官"视为未决（unresolved）而非缺席（absent）是解决半监督多器官分割中小器官消亡问题的关键。ExiST通过一致性伪标签、质量门控与存在性感知的集合值损失，在不偏向任何假设的前提下利用未标注数据，显著提升尾器官的保留率与整体分割精度，验证了"存在性不确定性"建模的有效性。

Abstract: Semi-supervised multi-organ segmentation should learn more than a dozen organs from a handful of labeled scans, and the smallest organs occupy less than one percent of the foreground. We identify a failure mode of self-training that is specific to this regime. When a teacher misses a small organ in a scan, every voxel of that organ receives the confident pseudo-label background, and the student is trained toward the organ absence. Repeated over many scans, this loop extinguishes tail organs. In a pilot study, removing the gallbladder from the pseudo-labels reduced its Dice on AMOS from 36.5 to 1.0. We argue that a missing organ should be treated as unresolved rather than absent and propose Existence-aware Self-Training (ExiST). It builds pseudo-labels by leave-one-out consensus of independently trained networks, screens them with a quality gate on validation scans, and infers, per scan and per organ, whether the organ was found. For every unresolved organ, the background voxels of that scan receive the set-valued target, trained with a marginal cross-entropy that accepts either answer and a Dice term that skips uncertain voxels. We show that this loss never pushes a candidate organ toward absence and never favors one member of the set, which leaves the decision to labeled evidence. ExiST outperforms the current state-of-the-art methods and reaches 77.1 mean Dice on Synapse with 20% labeled scans and 75.9 on AMOS with 5% labeled scans.

</details>


### [52] [Where Global Context and Weight Sharing Work in Compact Vision Backbones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23446)
*Michael Schroeder*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在EfficientNet-B0上用受控实验回答两个混合架构问题：全局自注意力应插在哪里最有效，以及重复块能否共享权重而不损失表征能力。零门控残差自注意力适配器插在P3/P4/P5可稳定超越匹配对照+0.95个百分点；在Stage 6交替使用两个共享块模板可减参10.9%且仅损失1.84个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑卷积骨干（如EfficientNet）通过局部深度可分离卷积实现良好参数效率，但混合设计中两个结构性问题尚未澄清：(1) 全局自注意力相对纯卷积在何处能真正提升识别性能；(2) 重复块共享权重是否会损失表征能力。

Method: 在ImageNet-1k（50,000张验证集）上以EfficientNet-B0做受控实验。实验一：对比BiFPN多尺度特征金字塔包裹骨干（单独及配合stage attention）与保持MBConv主干不变、在P3（28×28窗口化）、P4（14×14全局）、P5（7×7全局）插入轻量零门控残差自注意力适配器，并设bit-for-bit匹配的plain-B0对照，采用88轮stateless replay训练，统计学到的注意力幅度随语义深度变化。实验二：在重复MBConv块上评估循环权重共享（每pass独立BatchNorm，+1,536参数/pass），比较事后卷积核平均、单模板stage tying（减参27.6%）与Stage 6交替双模板[B1,A,B,A]方案。

Result: BiFPN包裹未超过plain B0（69.99%–71.81% vs 72.86%），配合stage attention达73.15%。保持MBConv主干并插入零门控注意力适配器单独达74.02%，在88轮匹配对照下为73.88% vs 72.92%，提升+0.954个百分点；注意力幅度随深度单调上升（P3 27.2% → P4 45.4% → P5 66.3%）。权重共享方面：事后卷积核平均使精度崩至17.03%；单模板stage tying（减参27.6%）仅66.10%；Stage 6交替双模板减参10.9%（582K权重），单轮内回升+10.82点至70.92%，仅比不共享对照低1.84点。

Conclusion: 全局自注意力的收益来自在深层语义阶段以轻量、零门控残差方式插入，而非外包多尺度金字塔；注意力幅度随语义深度递增印证深层更依赖全局建模。重复块权重大幅共享需保留块间多样性（交替模板+独立BN统计）才可行，激进共享（核平均、全阶段单模板）不可取。

Abstract: Compact convolutional backbones such as EfficientNet achieve strong parameter efficiency through local depthwise separable convolutions, yet two structural questions remain central to hybrid design: where does global self-attention improve recognition over pure convolution, and can repeated blocks share weights without losing representational capacity? We investigate both questions on ImageNet-1k (50,000-image validation) using controlled EfficientNet-B0 experiments. First, we show that wrapping a classification backbone in multi-scale feature pyramids (BiFPN) fails to outperform a plain MBConv trunk (69.99% - 71.81% vs. 72.86% plain B0) until paired with stage attention (73.15%). In contrast, keeping the MBConv trunk intact and inserting lightweight zero-gated residual self-attention adapters at stages P3 (28x28 windowed), P4 (14x14 global), and P5 (7x7 global) reaches 74.02% standalone and outperforms a bit-for-bit matched plain-B0 control by +0.954 percentage points (73.88% vs. 72.92%) across an 88-epoch stateless replay schedule, with learned attention amplitude rising monotonically with semantic depth (27.2% at P3 -> 45.4% at P4 -> 66.3% at P5). Second, we evaluate recurrent weight sharing across repeated MBConv blocks with pass-specific BatchNorm statistics (+1,536 parameters per pass). While post-hoc kernel averaging degrades accuracy to 17.03% and single-template stage tying (-27.6% parameters) plateaus at 66.10%, alternating two shared block templates ([B1, A, B, A]) in Stage 6 cuts total model parameters by 10.9% (582K weights) and recovers by +10.82 points in a single epoch to 70.92% (-1.84 points vs. unshared control).

</details>


### [53] [Bits, Not Boxes: Label-Free Layer Selection for Visual Localization in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23432)
*Haisheng Fu,Gexin Huang,Haikai Zhao,Jie Liang,Zhenman Fang,Jingning Han,Xiaoxiao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 WhereBits，利用注意力本身（在无标注问题上测得的“看哪里”比特数）识别 VLM 中能定位查询目标的层，无需框标注或固定规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力图可低成本反映 VLM 的注视位置，但注意力汇（attention sinks）使其定位不可靠；对比式提示虽能抑制 sink，却只有部分层能定位目标对象。现有方法依赖框标注或固定规则寻找这些层。

Method: 作者发现问题会改变注意力头对图像的关注量以及关注位置，两种效应可精确分离；其中后者（以比特度量、无需标注问题）指示哪些层具备定位能力。WhereBits 将这些“看哪里”比特转化为层先验，并结合逐头对比得分；三个设计常数一次性在标注 COCO 数据上固定，新模型或新分辨率仅需无标注问题。

Result: 在八个 VLM 上，WhereBits 在 COCO 与 RefCOCO 上的指向准确率与经框调优的层相当，且目标 token 排序更优；优势在留出模型家族与更高分辨率下依然成立。COCO 指向准确率 0.72，平均注意力为 0.22；在免训练 zoom-in 中，其裁剪在强制选择评测下将 V* 准确率提升 6.0 个百分点。

Conclusion: 注意力信号本身即可无监督地选出定位层，WhereBits 提供了一种低成本、可迁移、跨模型与分辨率的定位层选择方案，并可直接增益下游视觉定位任务。

Abstract: Attention maps cheaply show where a vision-language model (VLM) looks, but attention sinks make them unreliable for localization. Contrasting the question with a generic prompt suppresses sinks, yet only some layers localize the queried object. Existing methods find these layers with box annotations or fixed rules. We show that attention itself can identify them. A question changes how much a head attends to the image and where this attention goes. The two effects separate exactly, and the second, measured in bits on unlabeled questions, indicates which layers localize. WhereBits turns these where-to-look bits into a layer prior and combines it with per-head contrastive scores. Its three design constants were fixed once on labeled COCO data, and a new model or resolution needs only unlabeled questions. Across eight VLMs, WhereBits matches the pointing accuracy of box-tuned layers on COCO and RefCOCO and ranks target tokens better. This advantage holds on a held-out model family and at higher resolutions. Its COCO pointing accuracy is 0.72, against 0.22 for mean attention. In training-free zoom-in, its crops raise V* accuracy by 6.0 points under forced-choice evaluation.

</details>


### [54] [Inherited Adapter Experts for Replay-Free Domain-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24145)
*Gyutae Oh,Hyunmin Kong,Jitae Shin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lamarck 复用免训练的 Mahalanobis 路由器，转而强化各领域专家：在 ViT 深层 block 后加入小型可训练适配器以校正全部图像 token，并让每个专家从上一专家初始化，从而在无回放的领域增量学习中以极低开销取得领先准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无回放的领域增量学习需在不保留旧域图像、且测试图像无领域标签的条件下、对同一类别集合在连续领域上训练。已有逐域学习器冻结预训练 ViT 并为每个新域训练额外专家，测试时由路由器选择专家。近期工作着力训练路由器，但分析表明免训练 Mahalanobis 路由器相对"每张图都用其真实域专家"仅损失不到 2.7 个点，即瓶颈在专家而非路由器，因此应把精力投入到强化专家上。

Method: 1) 沿用免训练 Mahalanobis 路由器，不做额外路由训练；2) 强化专家：在深层 ViT 每个 block 之后插入小型可训练适配器，对所有图像 token 进行校正；3) 专家初始化：每个专家从上一领域专家初始化，不增加额外成本，利用共享类别集使误路由到后续专家时的性能损失更小。

Result: 适配器在深度伪造基准 CDDB-Hard 上带来 4.4 个点的最终准确率提升；在同一协议下与十二个冻结骨干基线对比，Lamarck 在 CDDB-Hard 上领先 5.2 个点位居第一，在 DomainNet 与 OfficeHome 上排名第二，且平均准确率最高。

Conclusion: 在无回放领域增量学习中，路由并非准确率瓶颈，强化逐域专家更为有效；通过深层 token 级适配器与链式专家初始化即可在免训练路由下取得最优平均性能，说明该范式下"更强专家 + 简单路由"是高效可行的方向。

Abstract: Replay-free domain-incremental learning trains a model on the same classes in successive domains without keeping earlier images. Per-domain learners freeze a pre-trained ViT and train an additional expert for each new domain: the small set of parameters and the classification head learned on that domain. Test images carry no domain label, so a router selects one expert for each image. Recent work trains this router. However, a training-free Mahalanobis router costs the analyzed learners under 2.7 points of final accuracy relative to always selecting each image's own-domain expert. The experts, not this router, limit accuracy. Our learner, Lamarck, therefore uses this router and strengthens the experts. Its adapters, small trainable layers that correct every image token after each deep ViT block, add 4.4 points of final accuracy on the deepfake benchmark CDDB-Hard. In addition, each expert is initialized from its predecessor at no extra cost: the domains share their classes, so an image misrouted to a later expert loses less. Against twelve frozen-backbone baselines under one protocol, Lamarck is first on CDDB-Hard by 5.2 points, second on DomainNet and OfficeHome, and highest in mean accuracy.

</details>


### [55] [IDEA: Intent-Dominance Evidence Audit for Inference-Time Backdoor Detection in GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24102)
*Zhizhuo Kou,Sizai Hou,Jian Yang,Ge Sun,Junyu Luo,Wenkai Zhang,Sirui Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IDEA框架，在推理时逐步检测GUI智能体后门激活，无需触发器知识或攻击数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源GUI智能体被下载部署后可能被植入视觉触发器后门，在干净数据上通过验收测试，构成供应链风险；现有样本级检测器面向图像分类器和CLIP类编码器，缺乏针对GUI智能体的步骤级后门检测。

Method: 发现干净步骤由指令主导动作，而未被引用的屏幕区域劫持时出现"意图—证据主导性反转"签名。IDEA包含：意图反事实探测（ICP）度量重定向指令对决策状态的位移；区域因果审计（RCA）度量消融候选区域对决策状态的位移；门控保形评分在同等离流形距离的干净步骤上比较两种位移，提供有限样本假阳性保证。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-3B上针对五类攻击族实验，IDEA在受控假阳性率下稳定检测后门激活，优于现有SOTA检测方法，每次告警均可定位触发区域，推理开销增加适中。

Conclusion: IDEA实现无需触发器知识和攻击数据的步骤级后门检测，兼具检测、定位与理论保证，适用于GUI智能体供应链安全场景。

Abstract: Organizations increasingly deploy downloaded open-weight GUI agents. A backdoored agent passes acceptance tests on clean data and misbehaves only when a visual trigger appears on screen, turning an unvetted checkpoint into a supply-chain risk. However, existing sample-level detectors target image classifiers and CLIP-style encoders, leaving step-level backdoor detection for GUI agents unaddressed. On clean steps, the instruction drives the action; when an unreferenced screen region hijacks the step, the instruction loses control over the decision state or that region dictates it instead. We term this signature intent--evidence dominance inversion and propose IDEA (Intent-Dominance Evidence Audit) to detect backdoor activation at inference time for each step without trigger knowledge or attack data. Specifically, Intent Counterfactual Probing (ICP) measures how far redirected instructions move the decision state, and Region Causal Audit (RCA) quantifies how far ablating a candidate region moves it. A gated conformal score compares both displacements against clean steps at the same distance from the clean manifold, providing a finite-sample false-positive guarantee. Extensive experiments on Qwen2.5-VL-3B across five attack families show that IDEA consistently detects backdoor activation under a controlled false-positive rate and outperforms state-of-the-art detection methods, while localizing the trigger region for every alarm and adding only modest inference overhead.

</details>


### [56] [STAR++: A Physical-Aware Benchmark and Framework for Astronomical Super-Resolution across Multiple Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24023)
*Guohang Zhuang,Jiaze Li,Kuo-cheng Wu,Hao Du,Jinyang Huang,Xiang Zhang,Wanli Ouyang,Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出大规模天文超分辨基准STAR++（约170万通量一致图像对、36个滤光片）及通量不变超分模型FISR++，通过滤光片感知适配器与空间歧义解耦损失，实现跨未见滤光片的通量保真高分辨率重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天文超分需从多滤光片低分辨率观测重建高分辨率星场，但存在两方面瓶颈：数据层面缺乏支持场级（field-level）ASR、尤其覆盖未见滤光片的大规模基准；方法层面现有ASR方法难以在跨空间分辨率时保持恒星通量，且需重建星点位置与轮廓等高频成分，问题比自然图像超分更病态。

Method: 构建STAR++基准：基于HST观测，采用通量保持的数据生成流程保证跨分辨率光度一致性，含约170万通量一致图像对、36个滤光片，并提出通量误差（FE）指标从物理角度评估。提出FISR++模型：在通量保持模块之外引入滤光片感知适配器（FAA），利用滤光片元数据使表示适配不同光谱与光学条件；并提出空间歧义解耦损失（SADL），将空间歧义建模为像素位移不确定性，缓解条件均值重建的过度平滑。

Result: 大量实验表明，FISR++在重建质量与通量精度上稳定优于现有ASR方法，并能泛化到未见滤光片，验证了其在天文光度保真超分上的有效性。

Conclusion: STAR++与FISR++共同构成面向物理一致性的天文超分框架：前者提供场级、跨滤光片的大规模评测基础与FE物理指标，后者以FAA和SADL解决滤光片泛化与空间歧义平滑问题，推动了光度忠实的天文超分研究。

Abstract: Astronomical super-resolution (ASR) aims to reconstruct high-resolution star fields from low-resolution telescope observations across multiple filters for astrophysical analysis. However, developing a physics-aware ASR framework faces two challenges: 1) Data view: no large-scale benchmark supports field-level ASR, especially on unseen filters. 2) Method view: Existing ASR methods often fail to preserve stellar flux across spatial resolutions. Moreover, ASR requires reconstructing high-frequency components, including accurate star locations and profiles, making it more ill-posed than natural-image SR. To tackle these problems, we introduce STAR++, a large-scale benchmark comprising approximately 1.7 million flux-consistent image pairs across 36 filters, derived from Hubble Space Telescope (HST) observations. STAR++ uses a flux-preserving data generation pipeline to ensure photometric consistency across resolutions and provides the Flux Error (FE) metric to evaluate ASR models from a physical perspective. To address method-level challenges, we propose a Flux-Invariant Super-Resolution Plus (FISR++) model that generalizes to unseen filters while accurately reconstructing flux-consistent high-resolution images. Beyond the flux-preserving modules, FISR++ introduces two novel components. Specifically, a Filter-Aware Adapter (FAA) incorporates filter metadata to adapt image representations to varying spectral and optical conditions. In addition, a Spatial Ambiguity Disentangle Loss (SADL) models spatial ambiguity as pixel-shift uncertainty, mitigating the smoothing of learned conditional mean reconstructions caused by this ambiguity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FISR++ consistently outperforms existing ASR methods in reconstruction quality and flux accuracy, while generalizing to unseen filters, highlighting its effectiveness for photometrically faithful ASR.

</details>


### [57] [Rethinking Contrastive Targets in Radiology Language-Image Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24016)
*Yunhe Gao,Ashwin Kumar,Jiaming Liu,Chong Wang,Maya Varma,Fuying Wang,Stefania L. Moroianu,Louisa Fay,Sergios Gatidis,Akshay S. Chaudhari,Curtis Langlotz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLIP式图文对比预训练在放射学中因"配对身份目标"系统性偏差（未配对报告仍共享临床发现）而出现两类失败：零样本评估先升后降、大batch训练失效；作者用合成基准CLEVR-Radiology验证该结构性原因，提出目标错配理论并给出极简的兼容性修正CorLIP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP式对比预训练把batch内其他报告一律视为负样本，但放射报告中大量未配对报告与给定图像共享临床发现，使配对身份目标存在系统性偏差而非仅有噪声。现有对比学习理论未系统解释两种失败现象：零样本性能在训练损失持续下降时先达峰后退化；训练无法从大batch中获益。作者假设这是结构性问题而非放射学特异问题。

Method: 1) 构建CLEVR-Radiology合成测试平台，仅保留"重叠事实结构"以检验其是否足以复现两类失败；2) 在证据支持下提出目标错配（target-misspecification）理论，用单一确定性梯度偏差解释两类失败，并说明该偏差随batch size放大；3) 据此提出最小化修正CorLIP：文本只要其临床断言与图像一致即视为正样本，无需改动架构。

Result: CLEVR-Radiology仅凭重叠事实结构即复现两类失败，支持结构性假设。CorLIP在基准上取得提升之外，还大幅消除峰值后退化，使大batch训练由崩溃转为成功，显著放大数据规模带来的收益，且是唯一在B=2048下仍保持稳定的配置；该修正可从胸部X光迁移到3D CT模态。

Conclusion: 放射学CLIP预训练的两类失败源于配对身份目标的系统性目标错配，可由单一确定性梯度偏差统一解释，并随batch规模放大。基于兼容性的正样本重定义（CorLIP）能在不改架构的前提下同时修复退化、解锁大batch与数据规模收益，并具备跨模态可迁移性。

Abstract: CLIP-style contrastive image-text pretraining is widely used in radiology data, where each image is paired with one report and every other report in the batch is treated as a negative. Under this recipe, two failures appear, neither of which is systematically addressed by existing contrastive learning theory: zero-shot evaluation peaks then degrades while training loss keeps decreasing, and training fails to learn from large batches. We hypothesize the cause is structural rather than radiology-specific: many unpaired reports share clinical findings with a given image, so CLIP's paired-identity target is systematically biased, not merely noisy. To test this, we construct CLEVR-Radiology, a synthetic testbed that preserves only this overlapping-fact structure and reproduces both failures. With evidence that the structure is sufficient to reproduce the failures, we develop a target-misspecification theory that explains both failures from a single deterministic gradient bias amplified by batch size, and use it to motivate a minimal compatibility-based correction, CorLIP: text is treated as positive whenever its clinical assertions agree with the image, with no architecture changes. Beyond benchmark improvements, CorLIP largely removes the post-peak degradation, makes large-batch training succeed where it previously collapsed, substantially amplifies the gains from more data, and is the only configuration that remains stable up to B=2048. The correction transfers from chest X-ray to 3D CT modalities.

</details>


### [58] [FlyVQA: Seeing Video Frame Interpolation Quality through Fly Eyes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24012)
*Baoliang Chen,Lingyu Zhu,Zhiwei Zhong,Shiqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 利用冻结的果蝇视觉连接组构建全参考视频质量评估模型，通过复眼采样与连接组感知读出量化帧插值视频的时序失真，效果优于同规模的CNN/RNN/Transformer基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉质量评估的终极目标是复现人类视觉的神经计算，但人脑视觉回路过于复杂。新发布的果蝇视觉连接组提供了可计算的生物学生物起点，其运动通路刻画充分，可用于评估时序失真的感知影响。

Method: 采用共享且冻结的果蝇视觉回路做全参考质量评估：复眼采样将参考与失真序列映射入回路；连接组感知读出量化细胞类型特异性活动的差异以及兴奋—抑制突触动力学差异；再用参考引导的时序池化与轻量回归头融合为质量分数。

Result: 在多种插值方法上，与人类主观评分的吻合度优于参数量相当的CNN、RNN和Transformer基线。

Conclusion: 结果支持以生物机制为根基的感知质量建模路径，即冻结的生物视觉回路可作为评估时序失真的有效先验。

Abstract: The ultimate ambition of visual quality assessment is to reproduce the neural computations of human vision and thereby achieve full alignment with human perception. However, the immense complexity of human visual circuitry makes this goal difficult to realize. Recently released fly visual connectomes provide a tractable biological starting point. Despite differences between fly and human vision, the fly's well-characterized motion pathways motivate us to investigate their potential for assessing the perceptual impact of temporal distortions. In this paper, we use a shared, frozen fly visual circuit to perform full-reference quality assessment of frame-interpolated videos, whose artifacts can disrupt temporal consistency. Specifically, compound-eye sampling maps reference and distorted sequences into the circuit, while a connectome-aware readout quantifies discrepancies in cell-type-specific activity and excitatory--inhibitory synaptic dynamics. Reference-guided temporal pooling and a lightweight regression head integrate these differences into a quality score. Experiments across multiple interpolation methods demonstrate better agreement with human ratings than CNN, RNN, and Transformer baselines with comparable parameters, supporting a biologically grounded approach to perceptual quality modeling.

</details>


### [59] [HHW-Ego: DSLR-Quality Enhancement for Multi-Source Wearable Ego Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24010)
*Yunfei Tong,Qianqian Sun,Jixiang Luo,Dell Zhang,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出定制退化管线生成配对样本，并构建"配对微调增强+高光谱色温精修"两阶段框架，同时发布HHW-Ego多源配对数据集（5,000组，含高光谱、HDR与穿戴式第一视角数据），显著提升可穿戴第一视角图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴第一视角图像在智能眼镜、具身智能等场景需求旺盛，但因缺乏配对高质量参考图像及专用增强系统，其画质远逊于手机拍摄。

Method: (1) 设计定制化退化管线，生成与可穿戴相机图像相匹配的配对样本，以抬高增强性能上限；(2) 两阶段增强框架：Stage-1 在配对数据集上微调高效模型以增强真实可穿戴图像，Stage-2 利用高光谱数据精修色温以提升质量；(3) 构建HHW-Ego数据集，融合高光谱、HDR与可穿戴第一视角数据，覆盖室内外与昼夜场景，共5,000个图像组。

Result: 在留出配对数据上，Stage-1 微调使 Real-ESRGAN 与 PFT-SR 的 PSNR、SSIM、Lab 距离与 LPIPS 均获提升；在真实可穿戴图像上，通过无参考指标、人类偏好、色域分析与视觉对比刻画了 Stage-2 的效果。

Conclusion: 配对数据合成与两阶段增强框架可有效弥合可穿戴相机与手机之间的画质差距，高光谱色温精修进一步改善真实场景图像的色彩与整体质量。

Abstract: Wearable ego-centric images are now in high demand for scenarios ranging from daily smart glass usage to embodied intelligence. But the image quality is far behind smart phone due to the lacking paired high-quality reference images and the dedicated enhancement systems. To solve this, a customized degradation pipeline is designed to generate paired samples matching wearable camera images, boosting the upper limit of enhancement performance. Besides a two-stage enhancement framework is further built: first, tuning an efficient model on the paired dataset to enhance real wearable images; second, using hyperspectral data to refine color temperature for better quality. Moreover we present HHW-Ego, a multi-source paired dataset integrating with Hyperspectral, High-dynamic range and Wearable Ego-centric data, which includes 5,000 image groups spanning indoor/outdoor and day/night scenes. On held-out paired data, Stage-1 fine-tuning improves PSNR, SSIM, Lab distance, and LPIPS for Real-ESRGAN and PFT-SR. On real wearable images, no-reference metrics, human preference, gamut analysis, and visual comparisons characterize the effects of Stage 2.

</details>


### [60] [Freeforward4d: Annotation-free feed-forward 4d reconstruction of driving scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24007)
*Luosong Guo,Xiang Yang,Chaoyang Li,Ao Gao,Jiangming Shi,Yanyun Qu,Zhizhong Zhang,Jingyu Gong,Yuan Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeForward4D 通过将预训练视觉模型的多视图/单目几何先验统一校准到同一度量坐标系，实现仅用 RGB 视频的前馈 4D 驾驶场景重建，无需数据集相机、LiDAR 深度或 3D 框标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈 4D 重建方法依赖数据集提供的相机参数、LiDAR 深度或 3D 框标注，难以应用于未标注的驾驶视频；而预训练视觉模型虽可仅从 RGB 预测相机、深度和目标轨迹，但其多视图预测存在短窗口、独立尺度与坐标系不一致，单目深度仅逐图度量，几何互相冲突。

Method: 核心思路是将上述先验统一到单一度量坐标系。具体地：以单目度量深度为基准校准相互重叠的预测窗口，并在时间上配准到统一坐标系；在此几何上跨相机进行 3D 关联 2D 轨迹；目标运动采用分层建模，将共享目标速度与局部高斯修正相结合；训练与推理均仅使用 RGB 视频。

Result: 在 Waymo 上取得 27.66 dB PSNR 与 0.836 SSIM，且无需尺度对齐即可恢复度量尺度的相机与深度；全图 PSNR 超过依赖标注的 STORM、SLARM-F、UFO、DGGT 与 StreetForward，并在 8 秒窗口上取得最佳 RGB 保真度；仅用 RGB 微调即可适配 nuScenes 与 Argoverse 2。

Conclusion: 统一校准后的视觉先验可替代数据集标注，支撑纯 RGB 的前馈 4D 重建，并在多个驾驶数据集上取得有竞争力的渲染质量与良好的跨数据集可迁移性。

Abstract: Feed-forward 4D reconstruction enables efficient creation of renderable driving scenes for autonomous driving simulation. However, most existing methods rely heavily on dataset-provided cameras, LiDAR depth, or 3D box annotations, limiting their applicability to unannotated driving videos. Pretrained visual models can predict cameras, depth, and object tracks from RGB alone, but their geometry is mutually inconsistent: multi-view predictions come in short windows with independent scales and frames, while monocular depth is metric only per image. Our key insight is that, once reconciled into a single metric frame, these priors can replace dataset annotations for feed-forward 4D reconstruction. We present \textbf{FreeForward4D}, which calibrates overlapping prediction windows against monocular metric depth and registers them into one frame across time. On this geometry, 2D tracks are associated across cameras in 3D, and objects move through hierarchical motion that combines a shared object velocity with local Gaussian corrections, so that both training and inference use only RGB videos. On Waymo, FreeForward4D achieves 27.66 dB PSNR and 0.836 SSIM while recovering metric-scale cameras and depth without scale alignment. It surpasses STORM, SLARM-F, UFO, DGGT, and StreetForward, which rely on dataset annotations, in full-image PSNR and attains the best RGB fidelity on 8-second windows. RGB-only fine-tuning further adapts the framework to nuScenes and Argoverse 2. Code will be released.

</details>


### [61] [ReSynth: Constructing Synthetic Supervision for Multi-Image Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23996)
*Yilin Wang,Xin Yu,Xinyi Guo,Xiangxi Zheng,Dongxing Mao,Linjie Li,Zhengyuan Yang,Alex Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReSynth，用可控图像生成把单图感知转化为跨图像关系监督数据，纯合成数据训练即可显著提升VLM的多视图比较能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM通常将多视角/前后对比视图作为独立图像输入，难以建立跨图像关联；而训练此类能力需要"已知关系的图像组"（同一物体不同视角、同场景变化前后），这类数据难以从现有照片库大规模采集。

Method: 将单图感知与可控图像生成结合生成关系监督：(1) 局部编辑生成前后对比对；(2) 视角生成产出同一实例的多视图；(3) 经校验的场景内容支撑计数与属性比较。具体以Qwen3-VL-8B的captioning、grounding、counting能力配合Qwen-Image-2.1的生成与编辑能力，先校验单图事实（计数、属性），再组合事实构造多图QA，最终约440K问题，用GRPO训练，训练中不使用真实图像。

Result: 在约440K ReSynth问题上用GRPO训练，Qwen2.5-VL-7B与Qwen3-VL-8B在BLINK、MUIRBench、VLM2-Bench上平均提升17.73与6.10分；Qwen2.5-VL-7B单图性能基本保持，Qwen3-VL-8B在四个评测基准上全部提升。8B感知模型在BLINK与MUIRBench上超过Qwen3-VL-235B。

Conclusion: 合成式的跨图像关系监督是一种有效且可扩展的数据构建范式，可在无真实图像训练的条件下提升多图推理能力，且不显著损害单图感知；强模型可被较小模型通过该监督方式超越。

Abstract: People often understand the world by comparing different views of it. Vision-language models (VLMs), however, receive these views as separate images and still struggle to relate them. One problem is data. Training on these tasks requires groups of images with a known relationship: the same object from different viewpoints, or the same scene before and after a change. Such examples are hard to collect at scale from existing photo collections. We introduce ReSynth, which turns single-image perception and controllable image generation into cross-image relational supervision. Localized editing produces before-and-after pairs, viewpoint generation produces multiple views of the same instance, and verified scene contents support comparisons of counts and attributes. ReSynth combines Qwen3-VL-8B's captioning, grounding, and counting capabilities with Qwen-Image-2.1's generation and editing capabilities. It verifies single-image facts such as object counts and attributes, then combines these facts to construct multi-image QA. Using GRPO on approximately 440K ReSynth questions, with no real images during training, Qwen2.5-VL-7B and Qwen3-VL-8B improve by 17.73 and 6.10 average points on BLINK, MUIRBench, and VLM2-Bench, while single-image performance is broadly preserved on Qwen2.5-VL-7B and improves across all four evaluated benchmarks on Qwen3-VL-8B. Notably, this supervision enables the 8B perception model itself to outperform Qwen3-VL-235B on BLINK and MUIRBench.

</details>


### [62] [SimplePro: A Simple Baseline for Proactive Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23954)
*Yilin Wang,Xiangxi Zheng,Dongxing Mao,Linjie Li,Zhengyuan Yang,Alex Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练基线 SIMPLEPRO，通过显式报告规则与结构化流式上下文，使冻结的 VLM 在主动式视频理解中超越专用系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动式视频理解要求模型不仅决定答什么，还要判断新证据何时值得响应、何时应保持沉默。现有 VLM 擅长离线长视频理解，但在主动式流式场景中表现不佳，已有工作依赖任务特定强化学习或辅助触发模块，成本高且复杂。

Method: 作者发现显式报告规则与结构化流式上下文能显著提升冻结 VLM 的主动响应表现。据此构建 SIMPLEPRO：每个决策步将近期高分辨率帧、有界历史观测与既往答案记录组合，判断新信息是否值得回应，需要时生成简洁答案；随后过滤连续重复答案，同时保留原始输出作为后续决策的上下文。全程无参数更新、无辅助触发器。

Result: 在 4B 骨干下，SIMPLEPRO 在 OVO-Bench、StreamingBench-PO 与 ProactiveVideoQA 上一致超过专用主动式系统；且随骨干从 2B 增至 32B，整体性能持续提升。

Conclusion: 简单的免训练方法即可作为主动式视频理解的强基线，作者将开源代码与推理流水线以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Proactive video understanding requires vision-language models (VLMs) to decide not only what to answer as a video unfolds, but also when newly arrived evidence warrants a response and when to remain silent. Although modern VLMs excel at offline long-video understanding, they struggle in proactive streaming settings, leading prior work to introduce task-specific reinforcement learning or auxiliary triggering modules. We find that explicit reporting rules and structured streaming context substantially improve a frozen VLM's proactive response performance. We exploit this observation to build SIMPLEPRO, a training-free baseline for proactive video understanding. At each decision step, the model combines recent high-resolution frames, bounded historical observations, and prior answer records to determine whether new information warrants a response and, when needed, generate a concise answer. The system then filters consecutive duplicate answers while retaining the raw outputs as context for subsequent decisions. Without any parameter updates or auxiliary triggers, SIMPLEPRO with a 4B backbone consistently outperforms specialized proactive systems across OVO-Bench, StreamingBench-PO, and ProactiveVideoQA. SIMPLEPRO shows overall performance gains as backbone size increases from 2B to 32B. We hope this simple yet effective method will serve as a strong baseline for proactive video understanding, and we will release our code and inference pipeline to support future research.

</details>


### [63] [VOL-Agent: Programming Your Film via Video Orchestration Language Agentic System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23950)
*Hongji Yang,Yucheng Zhou,Lingting Zhu,Shengju Qian,Xinye Li,Huan Zheng,Dongyue Lu,Wei Chow,Xin Wang,Cheng-zhong Xu,Jianbing Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可编程视频智能体 VOL-Agent，基于视频编排语言 VOL，将静态编译验证与运行时动态加载解耦，实现分钟级、故事连贯视频的生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频基础模型只能生成高质量但时长受限的片段，难以编排为分钟级、故事连贯的影片；而现有视频智能体高度依赖自然语言规划或预定义工作流，导致生产过程难以编程、定制和扩展。

Method: 构建 Video Orchestration Language（VOL）及其智能体 VOL-Agent：1）静态编译与验证 + 运行时动态加载，实现结构可靠性与执行灵活性的解耦；2）技能驱动执行器从开放世界仓库动态解析工具与技能，并支持自然语言定义的用户技能；3）可从学习到的知识中创作新的导演技能，并通过反馈自我改进、积累影视制作知识；4）引入结构化记忆机制记录与召回世界及实体状态，维持跨镜头实体与场景一致性；5）借助评论家（critic）反馈进行局部修订，并保留可复用的制作知识与经验。

Result: 在三个基准上，VOL-Agent 能够以较低成本生成整体质量较高的分钟级视频，并保持跨镜头的实体与场景一致性。

Conclusion: 以可编程编排语言为核心、结合技能驱动执行、结构化记忆与反馈自改进的视频智能体，可有效解决长视频生成中的编排可编程性与跨片段连贯性问题，并为后续任务复用积累制作知识与经验。

Abstract: Recent video foundation models generate high-quality and limited-length clips, but orchestrating them into minute-level, story-coherent films remains an open problem. Existing video agents highly rely on natural-language planning or predefined workflows, which make their production hard to program, customize, and extend. Therefore, we introduce VOL-Agent, a programmable video agent built on a Video Orchestration Language (VOL). With static compilation and verification, followed by runtime dynamic loading, VOL-Agent decouples structural reliability from execution flexibility. Through the skill-driven executor, VOL-Agent dynamically resolves tools and skills from an open-world repository and supports user-defined skills in natural language. Moreover, VOL-Agent is capable of crafting new director skills from learned knowledge and improves itself through feedback to accumulate filmmaking knowledge. To overcome the disconnect between different segments, we apply a structural memory mechanism to record and recall world and entity states, sustaining entity and scene consistency across different shots. Critic feedback supports local revision, while reusable filmmaking knowledge and experiences can be retained for subsequent tasks. Experiments on three benchmarks show that VOL-Agent generates minute-scale videos with strong overall quality and limited cost.

</details>


### [64] [Angular Conditional Distillation: Overcoming Classifier Geometry Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23941)
*Xuefei Zhang,Ruoqi Wang,Lei Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出角度条件蒸馏（ACD），通过角度坐标接口（ACI）重构学生分类器以消除幅值与偏置影响，并用尺度不变条件蒸馏（SICD）只迁移条件非目标关系，从而解决分类器几何失配问题，实现SOTA的KD性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准logit目标把可迁移的语义关系与依赖架构的参数化伪影纠缠在一起，导致师生分类器几何不一致，直接迁移不可靠。因此需要明确“在何处迁移”和“迁移什么”两个问题。

Method: 方法分两部分：1）Angular Coordinate Interface (ACI)：用角度坐标重建学生分类器，抑制logit的幅值与偏置效应，构建几何兼容空间，回答“在哪里迁移”；2）Scale-Invariant Conditional Distillation (SICD)：标准化输出，仅迁移非目标类别的条件关系，过滤不可迁移的绝对置信度，回答“迁移什么”。

Result: 大量实验显示ACD在现有KD方法中达到SOTA；作为通用即插即用模块，可一致地提升现有蒸馏流程，且无需特征适配器、不增加推理开销。

Conclusion: ACD通过解耦几何对齐（ACI）与选择性知识迁移（SICD），有效缓解分类器几何失配，是一种高效、通用且无推理代价的KD增强方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Classifier outputs provide a fundamental and univer- sally available knowledge interface for knowledge distil- lation (KD). However, standard logit objectives entangle transferable semantic relations with architecture-dependent parameterization artifacts, causing a classifier geometry mismatch that makes direct knowledge transfer unreliable. To address this, we propose Angular Conditional Distilla- tion (ACD). First, the Angular Coordinate Interface (ACI) reconstructs the student classifier using angular coordi- nates, suppressing magnitude and bias effects to provide a geometry-compatible space. Then, Scale-Invariant Condi- tional Distillation (SICD) standardizes outputs and exclu- sively transfers conditional non-target relations, filtering out untransferable absolute confidence. Together, ACI de- fines where to transfer, while SICD defines what to transfer. Extensive experiments show that ACD achieves state-of-the- art performance over existing KD methods. Moreover, as a universal drop-in replacement, it consistently improves existing distillation pipelines while requiring no feature adapters or additional inference-time cost. Code is avail- able at https://github.com/laniusnan/ACD.

</details>


### [65] [TRoPE: Topology-aware Relative Positional Encoding for 4D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23933)
*Chengzhuo Gui,Zhenglin Zhou,Fan Ma,Zongxin Yang,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对视频到4D网格生成中的"粘连伪影"（geodesically远但空间近的顶点错误同步运动）问题，提出拓扑感知相对位置编码TRoPE与顶点级尾部风险目标，并构建MeshSticking-4D基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈式视频到4D网格方法在运动解码时无法区分空间邻近性与网格连通性，导致跨部件运动泄漏，使测地距离远但空间接近的顶点获得相似运动信息，产生粘连伪影；同时缺少专门刻画与量化该失效模式的基准。

Method: TRoPE：从图拉普拉斯中提取谱特征，基于多尺度扩散特征间的距离构造成对拓扑关系，并通过旋转相位（rotary phases）调制顶点—锚点注意力；另引入顶点级尾部风险目标，强化对持续高误差顶点的约束。

Result: 在MeshSticking-4D上，最差顶点误差降低39.4%，分离失败率由49.35%降至34.90%；在Motion-80与Motion324-Subset上尾部误差更低，迁移至Mesh4D表明跨骨干网络适用。

Conclusion: 显式引入网格拓扑连通性可有效抑制跨部件运动泄漏与粘连伪影，兼顾整体运动精度，且具备跨数据集与跨骨干的通用性。

Abstract: Video-to-4D mesh generation has made substantial progress in recovering dynamic 3D geometry and motion from monocular videos. However, commonly used feed-forward approaches remain prone to sticking artifacts, where geodesically distant vertices move together incorrectly when they become spatially close. We attribute this failure to cross-part motion leakage: spatially nearby yet structurally distant vertices may retrieve similar motion information when motion decoding fails to distinguish spatial proximity from mesh connectivity. To address this issue, we introduce TRoPE, a topology-aware relative positional encoding that makes mesh connectivity explicit in the interaction between vertex queries and mesh-supported motion sources. TRoPE derives spectral features from the graph Laplacian and constructs pairwise topological relations from distances between multi-scale diffusion features, using these relations to modulate vertex--anchor attention through rotary phases. We further introduce a vertex-wise tail-risk objective that emphasizes persistently high-error vertices to explicitly suppress sticking artifacts. To better characterize and quantify these failures, we introduce MeshSticking-4D, a dedicated benchmark for this failure mode. Experiments on MeshSticking-4D show that TRoPE suppresses sticking artifacts while improving overall motion accuracy. Compared with the baseline, it reduces the worst-vertex error by 39.4% and lowers the separation failure rate from 49.35% to 34.90%. Additional evaluations on Motion-80 and Motion324-Subset show lower tail errors on diverse objects, while transfer to Mesh4D demonstrates applicability across backbones.

</details>


### [66] [Beyond Additive Composition: Interventional Modeling of State-Dependent Cross-Degradation Responses for Composite Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23889)
*Zongda Tang,Jie Feng,Wenda Zhao,Mingtao Feng,Yuping Liang,Ronghua Shang,Weisheng Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PROBE干预式框架，显式建模复合退化间的状态依赖交互响应，并构建SCD-15基准，在多个复合复原数据集上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有复合图像复原方法将复合退化分解为独立因子并依赖孤立先验引导复原，但共存退化本质上是相互依赖的：一种退化的表现形式会随其他退化状态动态变化，产生非可加交互，因子分解范式无法刻画。

Method: 形式化定义状态依赖的跨退化响应；构建统一退化轴，通过对齐视觉与文本的退化-清晰偏移实现；在轴上用标定坐标头估计当前退化状态，并注入双向特征扰动以探测当前状态下的跨坐标响应；将响应与强度匹配的单退化参考对比，量化内在非可加偏差；最终以响应描述子与状态坐标联合引导复原。同时构建SCD-15基准，覆盖4种退化因子在3个严重度下的全部15种非空组合。

Result: 在CDD-11、AWMM-100K与SCD-15复合复原数据集上均达到SOTA；模型紧凑且GPU显存需求适中。

Conclusion: 以干预式思想显式建模退化间交互，替代独立因子分解范式，是处理复合退化的有效途径，并需配套覆盖完整退化组合的评测基准。

Abstract: Composite image restoration aims to recover images corrupted by multiple coexisting degradations. Existing methods predominantly decompose composite corruptions into individual factors, relying on these isolated priors to guide restoration. However, coexisting degradations are inherently interdependent: the manifestation of one degradation dynamically shifts with the states of others, inducing non-additive interactions that factor-wise paradigms fail to capture. To bridge this gap, we formulate state-dependent cross-degradation responses and propose PROBE, an interventional framework that explicitly models how coexisting degradations influence one another. Specifically, we first construct unified degradation axes by aligning visual and textual degraded-clean offsets. Along these axes, a calibrated coordinate head estimates current degradation states, while bidirectional feature perturbations are injected to probe cross-coordinate responses under the current states. Contrasting these responses with corresponding intensity-matched single-degradation references quantifies intrinsic non-additive deviations, allowing the resulting response descriptors and state coordinates to jointly guide restoration. For comprehensive evaluation, we introduce SCD-15, a new benchmark covering all 15 nonempty combinations of four degradation factors at three severity levels. Experiments demonstrate that PROBE achieves state-of-the-art performance on the CDD-11, AWMM-100K, and SCD-15 composite restoration datasets using a compact model with modest GPU memory requirements.

</details>


### [67] [Game2Player: Scaling Gameplay Experience for Visual Agents through Environment-Player Co-Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24154)
*Xirui Jiang,Yuxi Chen,Rui Yang,Feng Luo,Pengcheng Wang,Bingxuan Li,Zihao Zhao,Yushan Li,Han Wang,Manling Li,Yang Zhang,Shiyu Chang,Tong Zhang,Huan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Game2Player 框架，协同开发游戏环境与玩家智能体，可规模化收集可验证的游戏经验；构建了 568 个已验证网页游戏、3,436 个任务、15.7K 条轨迹（307 小时）数据集与 PlayerBench 基准，并用进度加权 SFT 训练 VLM 智能体取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏是视觉智能体的理想测试平台，需感知、长程规划与精确控制。但人工采集游戏数据成本高，视频学习需推断缺失动作；现有数据集游戏覆盖面窄、缺少显式任务与可验证结果。

Method: Game2Player 闭环：Builder 智能体改造现有网页游戏，暴露内部状态并基于状态定义进度度量与成功校验的任务；Coding Player 与 VLM Player 尝试任务并向 Builder 反馈问题以修复，使游玩同时充当环境验证与训练数据采集。基于此构建数据集，并采用进度加权 SFT（对推进任务进展的步骤赋予更大权重）训练 VLM 智能体。

Result: 构建 568 个已验证网页游戏、3,436 个任务，采集 15.7K 条轨迹（307 小时），并留出 50 个游戏作为 PlayerBench。Game2Player-9B 在 PlayerBench 上进度由 14.3% 提升至 31.2%，在外部 GameWorld 基准上由 30.0% 提升至 53.1%。

Conclusion: 通过构建、验证并从既有游戏世界中学习，为视觉智能体规模化扩展游戏经验提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Games are a rich testbed for visual agents, demanding perception, long-horizon planning, and precise control. However, collecting human gameplay is costly, and learning from gameplay videos requires inferring missing actions. Existing datasets also cover a limited range of games, often without explicit tasks or verifiable outcomes. To address these gaps, we introduce Game2Player, a framework that co-develops game environments and players to collect diverse and verifiable gameplay experience at scale. In the Game2Player loop, a Builder agent adapts existing web games, exposes their internal state, and defines tasks with state-based progress measures and success checks. A Coding Player and a VLM Player then attempt the tasks and report problems to the Builder for repair, so that play serves as both environment validation and training data collection. With Game2Player, we build 568 verified web games with 3,436 tasks, collect 15.7K trajectories (307 hours of gameplay), and hold out 50 games as the benchmark PlayerBench. To evaluate the collected experience as training data, we train VLM agents with progress-weighted SFT, assigning greater weight to steps that advance task progress. Game2Player-9B improves progress from 14.3% to 31.2% on PlayerBench and from 30.0% to 53.1% on the external GameWorld benchmark. More broadly, our work suggests a path for visual agents to scale their gameplay experience by building, verifying, and learning from existing game worlds.

</details>


### [68] [SEER: Semantic Evidence for Executable Region Propagation in Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24152)
