*Junyang Chen,Xiao Li,Yiguo Qiao,Wenbin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SEER：一种免训练测试时修复层，将OVSS预测后修复建模为证据条件下的标签传播，在八个基准上平均mIoU达58.0且不更新权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 免训练开放词汇语义分割（OVSS）虽已借助CLIP类表征取得进展，但残余错误常呈局部化、结构化，并与当前激活的标签空间相关；有效修正须同时满足视觉合理、目标词表合法、且可在分割分数场中执行。

Method: 提出SEER（Semantic Evidence and Executable Region propagation）：将预测后修复视为证据条件下的标签传播。由OVSS分数空间提出紧凑候选标签，冻结VLM进行闭集区域证据验证，执行门控判定证据是否合法且语义可操作；被接受的标签经OVSS侧策略传播——高置信一致区域采用宽传播，物体或边界风险情形采用保守的分数锚定传播。

Result: 在八个标准OVSS基准上平均mIoU达58.0，且无需更新OVSS权重；匹配输入对照实验隔离出修复机制本身的作用，资源测量报告了完整冻结系统的开销。

Conclusion: SEER以证据验证加执行门控的免训练修复框架，在不改动OVSS权重的前提下稳定提升分割精度，并提供机制归因与成本评估。

Abstract: Training-free open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) has made strong progress by adapting CLIP-like representations to dense prediction, but its remaining errors are often local, structured, and tied to the active label space. A successful correction must be visually plausible, legal under the target vocabulary, and executable in the segmentation score field. We propose SEER, a training-free test-time repair layer for Semantic Evidence and Executable Region propagation. SEER casts post-prediction OVSS repair as evidence-conditioned label propagation. The OVSS score space proposes compact candidate labels, a frozen VLM verifies closed-set regional evidence, and an execution gate certifies whether the evidence is legally and semantically actionable. The accepted label is then propagated by an OVSS-side policy: coherent high-confidence regions allow broad propagation, while object- or boundary-risk cases use conservative score-grounded propagation. Across eight standard OVSS benchmarks, SEER reaches 58.0 average mIoU without updating OVSS weights. Matched-input controls isolate the repair mechanism, while resource measurements report the cost of the complete frozen system.

</details>


### [69] [Keep the Image in Mind: A Visual Board for Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23872)
*Haiming Zhu,Zhiwei Chen,Ding Cao,Tianxiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出以Visual Board为核心的推理框架，将视觉观察、不确定解释与派生推理分离到结构化、容量受限、可修订的工作区中，使前提显式可审计，从而检测视觉误读并触发针对性重检，无需任务特定训练即显著优于独立模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步视觉推理中，早期视觉误读会作为错误前提持续影响后续推理；作者认为视觉证据在推理中的“表示方式”与“收集多少”同样重要。将观察与推断不断追加到无限增长的对话中，会使错误前提难以被发现和纠正。

Method: 提出Visual Board-centered harness：在结构化、容量受限、可修订的工作区中分离视觉观察、不确定解释和派生推理。使每个前提显式化、可审计，便于纯文本critic检测“所见”与“所推”之间的冲突并触发对原始图像的定向复检。还基于harness轨迹进行监督微调。

Result: 无需任务特定训练，在4B、8B、Qwen3-VL-Flash、32B配置下于Euclid30K、SolidGeo、ChartQA柱状图上一致超越独立模型，提升最高达11.68、18.42和24.82个百分点。相同工具、critic与交互限制下，结构化Board比共享自由笔记高6.67–13.00分，比无Board重试高7.35–18.80分，且token用量减少7.9%–38.2%。SFT进一步提升直接作答与无critic工具使用，并向图表推理正向迁移。

Conclusion: 视觉证据的表示结构（而非仅其数量）是提升多步视觉推理可靠性的关键；结构化的可审计工作区能有效抑制错误前提的传播，并在效率与性能上均带来收益。

Abstract: Multi-step visual reasoning suffers when early visual misinterpretations persist as incorrect premises in subsequent reasoning. We argue that how visual evidence is represented during reasoning matters as much as how much of it is gathered. Rather than appending observations and inferences to an ever-growing dialogue, we introduce a Visual Board-centered harness that separates visual observations, uncertain interpretations, and derived reasoning within a structured, capacity-bounded, and revisable workspace. This separation makes each premise explicit and auditable, so that conflicts between what is seen and what is derived become detectable even by a text-only critic and can trigger targeted reinspection of the original image. Without task-specific training, our harness consistently outperforms standalone models across 4B, 8B, Qwen3-VL-Flash, and 32B configurations on Euclid30K, SolidGeo, and ChartQA bar charts, with gains of up to 11.68, 18.42, and 24.82 percentage points, respectively. Under identical tools, critic, and interaction limits, the structured Board outperforms shared free-form notes by 6.67 to 13.00 points and no-Board retries by 7.35 to 18.80 points while using 7.9\% to 38.2\% fewer tokens. Supervised fine-tuning on harness trajectories further improves direct answering and critic-free tool use, with positive transfer to chart reasoning.

</details>


### [70] [MobileWeave: Program-First Synthesis of Dependency-Linked Tasks for Training Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23860)
*Ziyi He,Xian Wu,Qiyuan Chen,Haitao Li,Cehao Yang,Yinghao Zhu,Xichen Zhang,Xiao Chen,Yushi Feng,Lequan Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MobileWeave 将移动端 GUI 任务合成为基于可控模拟器的可执行程序，通过类型化能力图与显式依赖关系建模组合性，配合教师轨迹与停滞恢复监督训练，在多个基准上显著提升组合型工作流性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生移动 GUI 智能体需大规模训练轨迹，覆盖单 App 功能与多目标工作流，自动任务合成是扩展数据的有前景途径。但现有合成管线把任务写成自由文本，子目标间的依赖关系隐含不明；而组合本身是一种独立能力——策略可能单独完成每个子任务，却无法将其串联为完整工作流。因此需要显式建模子目标依赖的任务合成方式。

Method: 提出 MobileWeave：以可控模拟器的 App 实现为来源，将每个 App 抽象为带跨 App 共享语义类型的类型化能力图；通过显式的值依赖与对象依赖，将原子程序组合为单 App 与跨 App 的复合程序；再以原生执行验证每个程序，并从同一程序派生指令与完成校验。训练数据包括为这些任务采集的教师轨迹，以及在策略自身停滞点处的恢复监督。由此训练 MobileWeave-4B/8B。

Result: MobileWeave-8B 相对基座模型在 MobileGym 上提升 26.7 分，在 AndroidWorld、AndroidLab、MobileWorld 上分别提升 8.6、21.3、17.6 分；4B 规模下获得可比增益。

Conclusion: 除性能提升外，研究表明组合能力可从任务结构中学习：子目标间的显式依赖作为训练信号，能教会策略组合其已掌握的技能。作者开源数据、模型与代码。

Abstract: Native mobile GUI agents require large-scale training trajectories covering both individual app functions and multi-goal workflows within and across apps, which makes automatic task synthesis a promising way to scale training data. Existing synthesis pipelines, however, produce each task as free-form text, leaving implicit how its subgoals depend on one another. This matters because composition is a skill in its own right: a policy may complete each subtask of a workflow on its own, yet fail to chain these subtasks into the complete workflow. We present MobileWeave, which instead synthesizes every task as an executable program derived from the app implementations of a controllable simulator. MobileWeave abstracts each app into a typed capability graph with semantic types shared across apps, composes atomic programs into single- and cross-app composite programs through explicit value and object dependencies, and verifies each program by native execution, deriving both its instruction and its completion check from the same program. Trained on teacher trajectories collected for these tasks and on recovery supervision at the policy's own stalls, MobileWeave-8B improves its base model by 26.7 points on MobileGym and by 8.6, 21.3, and 17.6 points on AndroidWorld, AndroidLab, and MobileWorld, with comparable gains at 4B. Beyond these gains, we find that composition is learnable from task structure: explicit dependencies between subgoals act as a training signal that teaches a policy to compose skills it already masters. We release data, model, and code at https://maxxnotmarx.github.io/MobileWeave/ .

</details>


### [71] [How Well Do Generalist CT Foundation Models Differentiate Liver Tumors?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24122)
*Yosuke Yamagishi,Tomohiro Kikuchi,Shouhei Hanaoka,Takeharu Yoshikawa,Osamu Abe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该回顾性研究评估通用CT基础模型（RADAR、Merlin）在肝脏肿瘤鉴别诊断中的能力，发现其零样本性能有限，但仅通过线性探针即可大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用CT基础模型虽能高精度检出多种病灶，但其区分已检出病灶（如不同肝脏肿瘤类型）的能力尚未被充分评估，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用单机构公开多期CT数据集（517例，含肝细胞癌、肝内胆管癌、乳腺癌及结直肠癌肝转移、血管瘤四类），对RADAR与Merlin在零样本设置下（利用其既有输出）及线性探针设置下（冻结图像特征，仅训练线性分类器；分层五折交叉验证重复五次）进行评估。

Result: 零样本下，RADAR在门静脉期四分类准确率为54.4%（95% CI: 49.9–58.8），κ=0.37，并将31%（421例中129例）恶性肿瘤预测为血管瘤；采用四期特征线性探针后准确率升至79.9%（95% CI: 76.6–83.4），κ=0.72，恶性肿瘤误判为血管瘤比例降至1%（421例中5例）；Merlin准确率为60.9%（95% CI: 56.9–65.0）。

Conclusion: 通用CT基础模型零样本鉴别肝脏肿瘤能力有限，但仅经线性探针即可显著改善，提示其特征具备较强可分性，需针对性适配以用于病灶细分任务。

Abstract: Generalist CT foundation models detect many findings with high performance, but their ability to differentiate detected lesions remains largely unevaluated. This retrospective study evaluated two generalist models, RADAR and Merlin, for differentiating liver tumors using a public single-institution multiphase CT dataset (517 cases: hepatocellular carcinoma, intrahepatic cholangiocarcinoma, breast and colorectal cancer liver metastases, and hemangioma). The models were evaluated in a zero-shot setting using their existing outputs and with linear probing, in which only a linear classifier was trained on frozen image features (stratified fivefold cross-validation repeated five times). In the zero-shot setting, RADAR achieved an accuracy of 54.4% (95% CI: 49.9, 58.8) and a $κ$ of 0.37 for four-class differentiation at the portal venous phase and predicted 31% (129 of 421) of malignant tumors as hemangioma. With linear probing of four-phase features, its accuracy improved to 79.9% (95% CI: 76.6, 83.4) with a $κ$ of 0.72, and 1% (five of 421) of malignant tumors were predicted as hemangioma; Merlin achieved an accuracy of 60.9% (95% CI: 56.9, 65.0). Generalist CT foundation models showed limited zero-shot ability to differentiate liver tumors, which improved substantially with linear probing alone.

</details>


### [72] [RoughCut-Bench: A Benchmark for Automatic Video Rough-Cut with Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24098)
*Ruisi Zhao,You Li,Sihan Chen,Aosong Cheng,Chixiang Ma,Sen Pei,Zhen Yang,Hehe Fan,Dongliang He,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RoughCut-Bench基准与智能体工作流，用于自动视频粗剪，覆盖视觉驱动与语音驱动两类场景，并评估LVLM生成可执行剪接计划的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频粗剪是媒体制作核心环节，但LVLM在视频任务上的进展尚未延伸到自动粗剪，缺乏统一评测基准与成熟的、可产出可执行剪接计划的工作流。

Method: 构建RoughCut-Bench：视觉驱动场景覆盖高光检测与选取、源视频排序、用户需求遵循；语音驱动场景基于ASR转写，覆盖语气词去除、表达去重、低价值内容裁剪。提出智能体工作流引导LVLM输出结构化剪接计划。

Result: 基准含101个视觉驱动案例（831段原始视频、1476条高光标注）与84个语音驱动案例（带清理片段标注）。视觉场景下采用与标注对比及人机对齐的智能体评估器评定渲染成片质量；实验表明现有LVLM生成专家级剪接计划的能力仍有限。

Conclusion: 自动粗剪任务仍具挑战，RoughCut-Bench与相应工作流为后续研究提供评测基础与可复用基线。

Abstract: Video rough-cut is a core stage in media production workflows, organizing raw media materials into an initial video cut. Despite recent advances in large vision-language models (LVLMs) for video-related tasks, automatic rough-cut remains underexplored, without a comprehensive benchmark for evaluation or mature workflows for producing executable cut plans. To address the evaluation gap, we introduce RoughCut-Bench, which covers two practical settings: (1) a visually driven setting over video cases, covering highlight detection and selection, source-video ordering, and adherence to user requirements when provided; and (2) a speech-driven setting over automatic speech recognition (ASR) transcripts, covering filler removal, expression deduplication, and low-value content trimming. Across these settings, the benchmark contains 101 visually driven cases, including 831 raw videos with 1,476 highlight annotations. It also includes 84 speech-driven cases, each consisting of an ASR transcript with annotated cleanup spans. The evaluation compares cut plans with these annotations in both settings and additionally uses a human-aligned agentic evaluator to assess rendered-cut quality in the visual setting. To apply LVLMs to automatic rough-cut, we further propose agentic workflows that guide them to produce structured cut plans from input materials. Experiments show that current LVLMs remain limited in producing expert-aligned cut plans. Our benchmark, code, and prompts are released at https://whalesong-zrs.github.io/RoughCut-Bench-Project-Page.

</details>


### [73] [From Restoration to Redrawing: Revitalizing Ancient Chinese Characters by SVG Code Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24084)
*Weiguang Zhang,Linhua Dong,Bin Dong,Shanshan Jiang,Maizhen Ning,Xiaoqiang Kang,Kun Zhang,Hao Xu,Xiaowei Huang,Wei Wang,Kaizhu Huang,Qiufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ReDraw——两阶段视觉语言模型，将汉简字符修复重构为 SVG 矢量代码生成与编辑：先忠实矢量化/保守描摹可见笔画，再以骨架思维链与结构感知强化学习预测骨架、追加路径重绘严重残缺笔画，并构建多层数据合成管线生成 1.2M 样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汉简墨迹破损、笔画残缺；现有图像到图像修复方法可能改变已观测笔画，且对笔画几何控制有限。受专家手工描摹实践启发，需要一种可控、不改动已有笔画的修复范式。

Method: 两阶段 VLM：第一阶段将可见笔画几何忠实矢量化为 SVG，或对轻度退化笔画保守描摹；第二阶段在骨架思维链推理与结构感知奖励的强化学习引导下，先预测骨架后预测轮廓，通过追加路径重绘严重残缺笔画，同时保持已有 SVG 路径不变。数据端构建解耦墨迹、材质与退化的多层合成管线，生成 1.2M 样本，并从 320 个标注真实字形衍生最多 6,400 个适配对。

Result: 实验展示了合成数据上的修复性能，以及对真实汉简字形的适应能力。

Conclusion: 以矢量代码生成/编辑替代像素级修复，可在不破坏已观测笔画的前提下可控地补全残笔，具备向真实汉简迁移的潜力；代码与演示将在接收后公开。

Abstract: Hanjian manuscripts written on Han Dynasty bamboo and wooden slips are invaluable heritage of ancient Chinese writing but suffer from damaged ink and incomplete strokes. Existing image-to-image restoration methods may alter observed strokes and offer limited control over stroke geometry. Inspired by experts' manual redrawing practices, we address these issues by introducing \textbf{ReDraw}, a two-stage vision-language model(VLM) that reformulates character restoration as Scalable Vector Graphics(SVG) code generation and editing. Stage one targets faithful vectorization of observed strokes geometry or conservative tracing of mildly degraded strokes. Guided by skeleton chain-of-thought reasoning and reinforcement learning with structure-aware rewards, stage two predicts skeletons before contours to redraw severely incomplete strokes by appending paths while keeping existing SVG paths unchanged. To support training, we develop a multi-layer data synthesis pipeline that disentangles ink, material, and degradation to generate 1.2M samples, and derive up to 6,400 adaptation pairs from 320 annotated real glyphs. Experiments demonstrate synthetic restoration performance and adaptability to real Hanjian glyphs. Code and demo will be available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [74] [ProtoBridge-CLIP: Observation-Masked Finding Prototypes for Medical Image--Report Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24002)
*Jinrui Wu,Yun Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出\model{}，将医学报告中的"确认发现、显式否定、未提及概念"区分处理：在对称全局图文对齐之外，加入观测掩码的发现级监督与确认概念的软关系目标，文本派生原型对齐局部视觉token，避免将不同证据状态压缩为单一研究级目标。在三项CT与五项胸片基准上取得一致的宏AUROC提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射报告区分确认发现、显式否定与未提及概念，但常规全局图文对比学习将其压缩为单一研究级目标，导致否定与未提及信息被混淆，监督信号语义不精确。

Method: \model{}由三部分组成：(1) 对称全局图像—报告对齐；(2) 观测掩码的发现级监督，将文本派生发现原型与局部视觉token对齐，并把不确定与未提及概念从直接发现标签中排除；(3) 对确认报告概念的软关系目标。辅以组件消融、配对与临床检索、定位、扰动鲁棒性、校准及逐发现分析。

Result: CT基准宏AUROC：CT-RATE 73.6%、RAD-ChestCT 66.5%、INSPECT 68.8%；胸片基准：ChestXray14 80.2%、CheXpert 91.4%、PadChest 78.5%、RSNA 86.5%、SIIM-ACR 90.8%。相对匹配的仅全局对照，发现与关系目标在各数据集上均带来一致增益。

Conclusion: 在医学图文表示学习中区别对待已观测与未观测报告概念具有实际价值，可稳定提升多模态表征与下游发现识别性能。

Abstract: Radiology reports distinguish affirmed findings, explicit negations, and unmentioned concepts, but conventional global image--report contrastive learning often compresses these different evidence states into a single study-level target. We introduce \model{}, which combines symmetric global image--report alignment with observation-masked finding supervision and a soft relation objective over affirmed report concepts. Text-derived finding prototypes are aligned with local visual tokens, while uncertain and unmentioned concepts are excluded from direct finding labels. We evaluate the framework on three CT benchmarks and five chest-radiograph benchmarks, together with component ablations, paired and clinical retrieval, localization, perturbation robustness, calibration, and per-finding analyses. \model{} achieves macro-AUROC values of 73.6%, 66.5\%, and 68.8% on CT-RATE, RAD-ChestCT, and INSPECT, and 80.2%, 91.4%, 78.5%, 86.5%, and 90.8% on ChestXray14, CheXpert, PadChest, RSNA, and SIIM-ACR, respectively. Relative to the matched global-only controls, the findings and relation objectives yield consistent gains across the evaluated datasets. These results demonstrate the practical value of treating observed and unobserved report concepts differently during medical image--text representation learning.

</details>


### [75] [ProxySearch: Decoupled Inference-Time Scaling for Diffusion Models via Asymmetric Noise-Rank Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23929)
*Seungwook Kim,Jongmin Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究蒸馏少步扩散模型能否作为噪声搜索中的教师代理，发现"闭式图像统计可迁移、学习式语义打分不可迁移"的显著不对称性，并据此提出 PROXYSEARCH，用代理单步过滤+教师语义择优的联合约束框架，在同等教师轨迹预算下将统计目标收紧 21–23% 且不损失语义奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理期噪声搜索能提升文生图质量，但每个查询需多次多步教师 rollout，成本极高。少步蒸馏模型已普遍发布，但其作为噪声搜索内部代理的可用性尚不清楚，作者希望量化并利用其代理潜力以降低搜索开销。

Method: 在三种蒸馏范式与三种骨干（DiT、MMDiT、UNet）上测量代理-教师排序一致性；设置配对零假设对照，定位一致性来源（粗统计源于第一步去噪，细粒度统计源于蒸馏）。基于该不对称性设计 PROXYSEARCH：语义奖励约束内容、数值目标约束图像统计；代理在第 1 步按统计匹配筛选候选，教师仅对存活者做语义奖励择优，以避免目标间的权重权衡。

Result: 主池上闭式图像统计从代理到教师可迁移（Spearman ρ 最高 0.80），而学习式语义打分不可迁移（ρ ≤ 0.12）；配对零假设对照将排序来源分别归因于首步去噪（粗统计）与蒸馏（细粒度统计）。在 FLUX-1024 上，同等教师轨迹数下统计目标收紧 21–23%，未检测到所优化奖励的损失。

Conclusion: 少步蒸馏模型是可用的"统计维度"代理而非语义代理；利用这一不对称，可在不牺牲语义质量的前提下以极低代理成本显著提升统计可控性，为高效噪声搜索提供了无需调权的联合约束范式。

Abstract: Noise search at inference time improves text-to-image diffusion but requires many multi-step teacher rollouts per query. Distilled few-step models are now routinely released, yet their potential as proxies inside noise search is uncharacterized. We measure proxy-teacher rank agreement across three distillation regimes and three backbones (DiT, MMDiT, UNet) and find a sharp asymmetry. On our main pool, closed-form image statistics transfer from the proxy to the teacher (Spearman $ρ$ up to 0.80), while learned semantic scorers do not ($ρ\le$ 0.12). Paired null controls trace this rank to the first denoising step for coarse statistics and to distillation for finer ones. We use the asymmetry for noise search under a joint constraint: a semantic reward specifies content, and a numeric target specifies image statistics. In PROXYSEARCH, the proxy filters candidates by statistical match in 1 step, and the teacher returns the survivor with the highest semantic reward. No weight trades one objective against the other. On FLUX-1024, PROXYSEARCH tightens the statistical target by 21-23% at the same number of teacher trajectories, with no detected loss on the optimized reward.

</details>


### [76] [GRAFT: Graph-Regularized Prototype Fields for Realistic Test-Time Adaptation of VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23591)
*Maxx Richard Rahman,Wolfgang Maass*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出图正则锚点位移场传导方法GRAFT，将适配后的类原型建模为文本锚点加语义图上的位移场，用高斯马尔可夫随机场做部分池化并显式建模类别存在性，推理为闭式无梯度，在11个基准和4个ImageNet偏移上均超过零样本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有传导式与测试时自适应方法在批量中仅含少量词表类别或数据流相关时，常低于零样本基线。作者将其归因于共同的缺陷：每个类别拥有从自身样本独立估计的参数，导致缺失或稀有类别停留在有偏的文本锚点上，或被迫拟合外来样本。

Method: 将适配后的原型表示为文本锚点加一个位移场，位移场通过高斯马尔可夫随机场先验在语义类图上进行部分池化；同时引入显式的类别存在性模型。推理在缓存特征上为闭式、无梯度。仅锚点收缩与单一全局平移是该先验的两个极端；缺失类可证明为存在类的调和延拓；池化强度可用经验贝叶斯选取。

Result: 在11个基准与4个ImageNet偏移的贴近实际的批量与在线协议下，GRAFT在所有设置中均优于零样本模型，并在类别稀疏情形下优于基于锚点的传导方法。

Conclusion: 通过语义图上的位移场池化替代各类独立参数，可有效缓解类别缺失与数据流相关带来的偏差，是一种无梯度、计算高效且稳健的视觉语言模型零样本分类器适配方案。

Abstract: Unlabeled test data can improve the zero-shot classifier of a vision-language model, yet transductive and test-time adaptation methods often fall below the zero-shot baseline when only a few vocabulary classes are present in a batch or when streams are correlated. We trace this failure to a shared design: every class owns independent parameters estimated from its own samples, so absent or rare classes are left at biased text anchors or fitted to foreign samples. We propose Graph-Regularized Anchor-displacement Field Transduction (GRAFT), which models the adapted prototypes as text anchors plus a displacement field that is partially pooled over a semantic class graph through a Gaussian Markov random field prior, together with an explicit class-presence model. Inference is closed-form and gradient-free on cached features. Anchor-only shrinkage and a single global shift are the two extreme settings of the prior; absent classes are provably harmonic extensions of the present ones, and the pooling strength can be chosen by empirical Bayes. Under the realistic batch and online protocols on eleven benchmarks and four ImageNet shifts, GRAFT improves over the zero-shot model in every setting and over anchor-based transduction where few classes are present.

</details>


### [77] [DOCK: Dual-Stream-Inspired Self-Supervised Object-Centric Representations Grounded in Robot Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23578)
*Pengju Liu,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文揭示冻结视觉编码器的潜空间距离无法可靠度量物体离目标的远近（排名相关≤0.11），并提出DOCK学习与物理运动一致的物体表征以改善潜空间规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜空间规划器以预测未来图像与目标图像的嵌入距离作为代价，隐含假设嵌入距离反映场景离目标的远近。作者用仿真执行的真实未来（消除预测误差）检验该假设，发现冻结DINO/V-JEPA特征距离几乎不追踪物体到目标的距离（排名相关最高仅0.11，而物体掩码质心达0.87），且被背景与机器人patch主导，读出的更多是机械臂而非物体信息。这促使作者从腹侧（what）与背侧（where/how）视觉通路获得灵感，构建一个变化与实测物理运动一致的物体表征。

Method: 提出DOCK，包含三部分：(1) 物体token（what）：从patch特征池化得到；(2) 空间标尺（where）：使读出坐标之差等于实测位移；(3) 动力学预测器（how）：在物体token上预测。利用“被抓物体随夹爪运动”这一先验，用关节角前向运动学得到物体位移作为无需标注的代理；在21个仿真厨房场景的9,951条持物搬运轨迹中挖掘404K位移对进行训练。

Result: 作为规划代价时，DOCK在21个任务中有15个把物体放置到位，与读取真实物体位置的oracle相当；掩码几何为10，冻结特征距离为9。在可见未见机械臂的情况下仍解决最多任务，且其排序无需任何适配即可迁移到含未见物体实例与类别的留出布局。

Conclusion: 冻结特征嵌入距离不适合作潜空间规划的物体定位代价；通过自监督学习与物理运动对齐的物体表征（what/where/how三要素），可显著提升潜空间规划器在仿真操作任务中的成功率与泛化性。

Abstract: Latent-space planners select actions by comparing the embedding of a predicted future image with that of a goal image. This assumes that distance in the embedding reflects how far the scene is from the goal. We profile this assumption with simulator-executed futures, which removes prediction error. For six frozen DINO and V-JEPA encoders, feature distance barely tracks the object's distance to its goal: the rank correlation is at most 0.11, against 0.87 for object-mask centroids. It is dominated by background and robot patches, and frozen-feature readouts capture the arm far better than the object. Inspired by the ventral (what) and dorsal (where/how) visual streams, we propose DOCK, which learns an object representation whose changes agree with measured physical motion. A grasped object moves with the gripper, so forward kinematics on the joint angles gives a proxy for the object's displacement without annotation. From 9,951 held-object transport trajectories in 21 simulated kitchen scenes, we mine 404K displacement pairs and train three components: an object token pooled from patch features (what), a spatial ruler that makes differences of readout coordinates equal to measured displacements (where), and a dynamics predictor over object tokens (how). Used as a planning cost, the learned representation places the object in 15 of 21 tasks, as many as an oracle that reads the true object position, while mask geometry reaches 10 and frozen feature distance 9. It still solves the most tasks when the unseen robot arm is visible, and its ranking transfers to held-out layouts with unseen object instances and categories without any adaptation.

</details>


### [78] [Beyond the Sink Token: Sink-Guided Key Editing in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23516)
*Hui Zhu,Saleh Zare Zade,Rafi Ibn Sultan,Xiangyu Zhou,Dongxiao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文揭示VLM中sink-key方向对注意力分配的调控作用，并提出免训练的SHRINK方法，通过削减可见key中与sink对齐的分量来提升推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究多关注sink token"为何"吸引注意力，但未探究sink-key方向本身如何影响对图像与问题token的注意力分配，缺乏可操作的注意力调控机制。

Method: 在四个被探查的VLM上进行干预实验：放大与正sink对齐的分量、放大负分量，观察后续层中注意力变化；据此提出SHRINK——在生成过程中对可见key的sink对齐分量做部分削减，属免训练方法；并进一步用sink对齐key分量的重缩放替代GIFT与ACG的注意力操作。

Result: 放大正sink对齐分量整体提升注意力、放大负分量则降低，且效应跨层持续；SHRINK在九个基准的采样推理下，使LLaVA-OneVision-7B和Qwen2.5-VL-3B平均准确率分别提升5.1和2.7个百分点，Qwen2.5-VL-7B与Gemma-3-4B增益较小；重缩放sink对齐key分量可在幻觉基准上以相当准确率替代GIFT和ACG的注意力操作。

Conclusion: 原生sink key可作为超越其自身位置的注意力调控参照系；基于sink对齐分量缩放是一类简洁、免训练且具普适性的注意力引导手段，可统一解释并简化现有注意力引导方法。

Abstract: In vision-language models (VLMs), a few sink tokens attract a disproportionate share of attention. While previous studies have examined why these tokens attract attention, we investigate how the sink-key direction affects attention to image and question tokens. Across four probed VLMs, boosting the magnitude of positive sink-aligned components increases attention on average, whereas boosting negative components decreases it; these responses persist through later layers. This identifies the native sink key as a reference for controlling attention beyond its own position. Motivated by this finding, we propose SHRINK, a training-free method that partially reduces sink-aligned components across visible keys during generation. On nine benchmarks under sampled reasoning, it improves mean accuracy by 5.1 and 2.7 percentage points on LLaVA-OneVision-7B and Qwen2.5-VL-3B, with smaller gains on the other two main models, Qwen2.5-VL-7B and Gemma-3-4B. Beyond SHRINK, rescaling sink-aligned key components can replace the attention operations of two existing attention-steering methods, GIFT and ACG, with comparable accuracy on hallucination benchmarks.

</details>


### [79] [mmTherm: Absolute 3D Human Pose Estimation from Low-Resolution Thermal Images and mmWave Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23477)
*Zhaoxin Chang,Badii Jouaber,Daqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: mmTherm 轻量框架融合热成像与毫米波雷达的几何互补信息，在未见受试者上实现绝对3D人体姿态估计，跨用户绝对关节误差降低33%、根节点定位误差降低38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝对3D人体姿态估计需同时恢复人体姿态与全局位置。毫米波雷达隐私友好且可测距测向，但点云稀疏导致问题欠约束；低分辨率热成像可提供身体方向与结构的互补观测。作者评估发现，强特征级融合基线在泛化到未见受试者时定位与HPE优势大幅衰减，径向定位甚至劣于纯雷达基线，因此需要显式利用两种模态的几何互补性。

Method: 提出 mmTherm：显式结合热成像与毫米波雷达的互补几何属性。从热图像中估计身体结构与关节方向，结合雷达的距离信息进行人体定位与3D姿态重建；框架设计轻量，参数量与推理计算量显著更少。

Result: 在 OctoNet 数据集上，相较于最强对应基线，跨用户绝对关节误差降低33%，根节点定位误差降低38%，同时推理阶段参数量与计算量大幅减少。

Conclusion: 显式建模模态几何互补性优于朴素特征级融合，可在跨受试者泛化场景下兼顾精度与效率，为隐私保护的绝对3D人体姿态估计提供有效方案。

Abstract: Absolute 3D human pose estimation (HPE) aims to recover both a person's body pose and their global position in 3D space. Privacy-preserving millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar provides distance and direction measurement capabilities, but its sparse point clouds make HPE under-constrained. Low-resolution thermal imaging can provide complementary observations of body direction and structure. However, our evaluation shows that a strong feature-level fusion baseline loses much of its localization and HPE advantage when generalizing to unseen subjects, with radial localization becoming less accurate than a radar-only baseline. Motivated by this finding, we introduce mmTherm, a lightweight framework that explicitly incorporates the complementary geometric properties of thermal imaging and mmWave radar. It combines body structure and joint directions estimated from thermal images with range information from radar to localize the body and reconstruct its 3D pose. Experiments on OctoNet show that mmTherm reduces cross-user absolute joint error by 33% and root localization error by 38% relative to the strongest corresponding baselines, while requiring substantially fewer parameters and less computation at inference.

</details>


### [80] [TransFIRA+: Transfer Learning for Biometric Recognizability Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23442)
*Allen Tu,Kartik Narayan,Joshua Gleason,Jennifer Xu,Matthew Meyn,Tom Goldstein,Vishal M. Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TransFIRA 是一种轻量框架，将可识别性直接锚定在预训练编码器的嵌入空间中，通过预测类中心相似度(CCS)与类中心角分离度(CCAS)实现无质量标注的人脸图像质量评估，并在 BRIAR 数据集上取得 SOTA 验证性能；其扩展版 TransFIRA+ 通过事后 sigmoid 校准将框架推广到跨相机多模态行人识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监控、视频与网络图像中的生物识别面临姿态、模糊、光照、遮挡和距离的极端变化，而视觉质量指标无法预测输入对已部署编码器是否可识别。现有人脸图像质量评估方法依赖视觉启发式、人工标注或昂贵的生成式流程，其预测与编码器的决策几何相脱节。

Method: TransFIRA 从预训练编码器的嵌入中推导类中心相似度(CCS)和类中心角分离度(CCAS)，学习从图像直接预测二者而无需质量标注；用预测 CCS 进行模板加权，以 CCAS>0 作为天然过滤阈值。TransFIRA+ 针对非裁剪监控视频中的跨相机多模态行人识别，因身体可识别性分数在窄区间饱和导致直接加权近似均匀，引入事后 sigmoid 校准，无需重训练即可将饱和分数转换为有效权重。

Result: 在 BRIAR 上，模板加权使 TAR@10⁻⁶ FMR 从均匀平均的 0.085 提升至 0.598，达到 SOTA 人脸验证性能；预测分数还能揭示图像退化如何影响编码器特定的可识别性。经校准后，BRIAR 训练的 TransFIRA 预测器在两个未见多相机数据集上提升了人脸与身体融合识别性能，优于检测器置信度加权和未校准的 CCS 秩加权。

Conclusion: TransFIRA 与 TransFIRA+ 共同表明，以编码器为锚的可识别性可跨生物特征模态与识别场景泛化。

Abstract: Biometric recognition in surveillance, video, and web imagery must contend with extreme variation in pose, blur, illumination, occlusion, and distance, where visual quality metrics fail to predict whether inputs are recognizable to the deployed encoder. Existing face image quality assessment methods rely on visual heuristics, curated annotations, or costly generative pipelines, leaving their predictions detached from the encoder's decision geometry. We introduce TransFIRA, a lightweight framework that grounds recognizability directly in embedding space. TransFIRA derives class-center similarity (CCS) and class-center angular separation (CCAS) from a pretrained encoder's embeddings and learns to predict them from images without quality annotations. Using predicted CCS for template weighting and CCAS>0 as a natural filtering threshold yields state-of-the-art face verification performance on BRIAR, raising TAR at $10^{-6}$ FMR from 0.085 with uniform averaging to 0.598. The predicted scores also reveal how image degradations affect encoder-specific recognizability. TransFIRA+ extends this framework to cross-camera multimodal person identification in untrimmed surveillance video. In this setting, body recognizability scores saturate within a narrow range, making direct weighting effectively uniform. We introduce a post-hoc sigmoid calibration that transforms these saturated scores into informative weights without retraining. After calibration, the BRIAR-trained TransFIRA predictor improves fused face and body identification on two unseen multi-camera datasets, outperforming detector-confidence weighting and uncalibrated CCS-rank weighting. Together, TransFIRA and TransFIRA+ demonstrate that encoder-grounded recognizability generalizes across biometric modalities and recognition settings.

</details>


### [81] [GramStatTexNet: Efficient, Interpretable, and Neuro-Inspired Texture Analysis-by-Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23755)
*Vasha DuTell,Zuyu Yun,Anne Harrington,Michael Y-S Fang,Mark Hamilton,Christian Kövesdi,Edward H. Adelson,William T. Freeman,Ruth Rosenholtz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GramStatTexNet，一个混合式分析与合成框架，将经典Gabor金字塔结构与Gramian相关性统计结合，实现可解释、紧凑的纹理表示与合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络纹理合成真实感强但表示不透明、过参数化，与生物视觉关联有限；神经科学启发的金字塔模型虽可解释、紧凑，却依赖少量手工挑选的统计量，难以泛化与扩展。

Method: 构建混合分析-合成框架：采用经典模型的多尺度Gabor滤波器结构，并引入Gramian相关性统计，组织为结构化的滤波器族；每个统计量携带具名滤波器族身份，可据此将合成保真度分解为各族贡献，并学习低维可解释嵌入；进一步通过空间池化扩展至外周纹理，用时空滤波器组扩展至动态纹理。

Result: 在多样纹理类别与多个感知指标上，与经典和深度纹理合成基线比较，该混合表示在保持有竞争力合成质量的同时，具备紧凑性与可解释性优势，并支持保真度的逐族分解与可解释低维嵌入。

Conclusion: GramStatTexNet为分析和建模视觉信息提供了统一、可解释的框架，可自然扩展到空间、时间与偏心度维度。

Abstract: Deep networks achieve high realism in texture synthesis but often rely on opaque, over-parameterized representations that have limited direct connection to biological vision. Neuroscience-informed pyramid models are interpretable, compact, and biologically inspired, but rely on small, hand-curated statistics sets that are difficult to generalize or extend. We introduce GramStatTexNet, a hybrid analysis-by-synthesis framework that combines the multi-scale Gabor filter structure of classical models with the flexibility of Gramian-based correlations, organized into structured filter families. This structured representation enables analyses that are challenging for deep-feature methods: each statistic carries a named filter-family identity, allowing us to decompose synthesis fidelity into per-family contributions and to learn an interpretable, low-dimensional embedding that preserves perceptual texture content. We compare our framework to classical and deep texture-synthesis baselines across diverse texture categories, and on multiple perceptual metrics, highlighting the compactness and interpretability advantages of this hybrid representation, alongside competitive synthesis quality. This same framework extends naturally to peripheral textures via spatial pooling, and to dynamic textures using a spatiotemporal filter bank. GramStatTexNet provides a unified, interpretable framework for analyzing and modeling visual information with natural extensions across space, time, and eccentricity. Project page: https://vdutell.github.io/gstn

</details>


### [82] [What Do Learned 3D Models Add to Localization from Posed References?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23697)
*Jaehyun Kim,Wonseok Kang,Jeongmin Lee,Tae-Wan Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 比较学习式3D模型的相机预测与几何估计在少样本位姿定位中的作用，发现细粒度阈值下几何估计+额外参考更优，学习式方法仅在特定参考选择与场景下增益明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 避免构建3D点图，仅用少量带位姿参考图像完成查询图像定位；需厘清学习式3D模型相较传统几何估计究竟带来何种增量、以及失败发生的环节。

Method: 在8个学习式模型及变体、5个测试集上系统对比：改变参考图像预算、交换位姿分量、对模型可见的参考进行干预（如遮蔽/移除远距位姿、鲁棒对齐）。

Result: 细阈值下，几何估计加一张参考在Cambridge与NAVER B1上显著优于所有双参考模型且从不显著更差（单独或作为共享接受门后的回退）；三参考时更优方法依场景而定。学习式平移幅度的增益依赖参考选择：自选分散图像对使12-Scenes上8个模型中7个提升，而两个排名最高检索参考下5个模型均无提升。NAVER江南站检索回其他地点参考时，各模型失败阶段不同；鲁棒对齐使Pi3X与MapAnything接近按需增加参考的效果（差3.3个百分点）。受控实验中，用近邻参考对齐可基本恢复VGGT、Pi3X与MapAnything，但Depth Anything 3不行；位姿条件化的Pi3X仅当远距位姿被遮蔽且排除于其输入位姿归一化时才恢复。

Conclusion: 学习式3D模型的相机预测在少参考定位中并未稳定超越几何估计；其增益高度依赖参考选择、场景及对齐策略，失败主要源于位姿归一化与含异地参考的输入。

Abstract: Localizing a query from a few posed reference images avoids building a 3D point map. We ask what the camera predictions of learned 3D models add to geometric estimation from the same references, and where failures arise. Across eight learned models and variants and five test sets, we compare reference budgets, swap pose components, and intervene on the references a model sees. At fine pose-error thresholds, geometric estimation with one additional reference is significantly more accurate than every model on two references on Cambridge and NAVER B1 and never significantly worse, alone or as a fallback behind a shared acceptance gate; with the same three references, the more accurate approach depends on the scene. The gain from the learned translation magnitude depends on reference selection: it improves seven of the eight models on 12-Scenes with the spread-out pairs we select, but none of the five we test with the two top-ranked retrieved references. Where retrieval also returns references from other locations, at NAVER Gangnam Station, models fail at different stages. A robust alignment brings Pi3X and MapAnything within 3.3 pp of adding references as needed. In controlled tests, aligning on the nearby references largely restores VGGT, Pi3X and MapAnything but not Depth Anything 3, and pose-conditioned Pi3X recovers only if the far poses are masked and left out of its input-pose normalization.

</details>


### [83] [Learning What to Degrade: A Recoverability-Aware Curriculum for Reliable Smart-Glasses Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23674)
*Yaopeng Wang,Qingliang Wang,Yilun Jin,Xiujin Liu,Han Yue,Chen Xia,Renjie Zhu,Chuang Sheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HR-referenced语义评估协议揭示生成式SR会捏造细节、恢复式SR会模糊关键证据两种失效模式；并提出ReDiC（可恢复性-难度课程）仅修改训练退化生成，在NAFNet/SwinIR/MambaIR上平均提升PSNR约2.5dB、语义分数约3分，并经LLM与人工评测在200张真实智能眼镜图像上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能眼镜图像退化会模糊视觉语言模型（VLM）所需的细小文字与细粒度结构，现有SR方法在视觉辅助任务中的语义可靠性尚不清楚，且恢复式SR训练中难以在“输入足够困难”与“保留可恢复信息”之间取得平衡。

Method: 1）构建以高分辨率参考为基准的语义评估协议，对比恢复图像与对应HR参考上的VLM输出；2）提出ReDiC课程学习策略，结合与模型无关的可恢复性代理指标与模型相关的恢复难度来自适应调节训练退化强度——可恢复且易恢复时提高强度，维持困难但可恢复的条件，可恢复性低时降低强度；仅改动训练退化生成，不改变SR架构与重建目标。

Result: 在NAFNet、SwinIR、MambaIR上，合成基准平均PSNR提升约2.5dB，HR-referenced语义分数提升约3分；在200张真实智能眼镜拍摄图像上，基于LLM与人工的评测进一步确认增益。

Conclusion: 语义评估揭示生成式SR的细节幻觉与恢复式SR的证据模糊两类失效模式，说明恢复式SR需提升忠实细节恢复；ReDiC以即插即用的课程式退化调节有效提升重建质量与下游语义表现，具有良好通用性。

Abstract: Degradations in smart-glasses images obscure small text and fine-grained structures needed by vision-language models (VLMs) for visual assistance. We introduce an high-resolution (HR)-referenced semantic evaluation protocol that compares VLM outputs on restored images with those on corresponding HR references. Our evaluation reveals two failure modes: generative SR can synthesize misleading details, whereas restoration-oriented SR can leave critical evidence blurred or ambiguous. These findings motivate improving faithful detail recovery in restoration-oriented SR, where a key training challenge is to provide sufficiently difficult inputs while retaining recoverable visual information. We propose ReDiC (Recoverability-Difficulty Curriculum), which combines a model-independent recoverability proxy with model-relative restoration difficulty to adapt training degradation severity. ReDiC increases severity when inputs are recoverable and restoration is easy, maintains challenging but recoverable conditions, and reduces severity when estimated recoverability is low. It modifies only training degradation generation, leaving the SR architecture and reconstruction objective unchanged. Across NAFNet, SwinIR, and MambaIR, ReDiC improves average PSNR by approximately 2.5 dB and HR-referenced semantic scores by about 3 points over the corresponding baselines on synthetic benchmarks, with further gains confirmed by LLM-based and human evaluations on 200 real smart-glasses captures.

</details>


### [84] [PRISM: Profiling and Representation of Individual Stereotypical Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23646)
*Austin A. MBaye,Savik Kinger,Matthew S. Goodwin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PRISM表示：用跟踪掩码与稠密光流刻画重复性运动（强度、空间范围、频率、节律性），与解剖姿态表示互补；在合成条件、手部动作、破坏实验及自闭症刻板运动数据上验证其可恢复受控参数并揭示姿态标签内差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解剖姿态（如COCO-17关键点）提供可解释的关节结构，但不存在固定的物理关键点集合能完整刻画一个人的运动；仅靠姿态轨迹会遗漏运动表达方式（强度、幅度、频率、节律）等信息。

Method: PRISM基于跟踪掩码与稠密光流构建四项测量：躯干相对光流测强度，逐帧掩码变化测空间范围，带符号光流递归估计频率，持久滑动窗口几何描述节律性；辅以理想化手部实验、可控破坏敏感性实验、两种姿态方法审计与36,930段外部骨架片段审计，另加VLM定量频率比较与profile-conditioned解码器生成示意骨架动作。

Result: 1,890个合成条件中可恢复受控的速度、幅度与频率并暴露观测极限；手部实验显示相同精确COCO-17轨迹下存在被遗漏的变异；在1,404段六名参与者标注的复杂刻板运动片段中，总光流与姿态运动收敛，但同坐标示例与重复会话画像显示同一行为标签内强度、范围、频率和节律性的差异；姿态方法审计显示观测不完整且依赖处理选择。

Conclusion: PRISM将数值画像与可视示例相连，提供可解释的方式来比较同一行为在标签或事件计数之外的具体表达差异，与姿态表示互补。

Abstract: An anatomical pose provides interpretable joint structure, but no fixed set of physical landmarks can fully specify a person's movement. We introduce PRISM, a complementary representation that characterizes repetitive movement using tracked masks and dense optical flow. Torso-relative flow measures intensity, frame-wise mask variation measures spatial extent, signed-flow recurrence estimates frequency, and persistent sliding-window geometry describes the rhythmicity of the movement. Across 1,890 synthetic conditions, the measurements recover controlled speed, amplitude, and frequency while exposing observation limits. An idealized hand-motion experiment demonstrates variation omitted by identical exact COCO-17 trajectories; controlled corruption experiments separately examine measurement sensitivity. Audits of two pose methods on the same data cohort and 36,930 external skeleton clips document incomplete observation and dependence on processing choices. In 1,404 annotated complex stereotypical motor movement bouts from six autistic participants, total flow converges with pose motion, while matched-coordinate examples and repeated-session profiles show differences in intensity, extent, frequency, and rhythmicity within behavior labels. A separate VLM comparison evaluates quantitative frequency estimation, and a profile-conditioned decoder links the measurements to illustrative skeletal movements. PRISM connects numerical profiles to visible examples, providing an interpretable way to compare how the same behavior is expressed beyond its label or event count.

</details>


### [85] [Mean Curvature Minimizing Foliations Of A Vector Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23765)
*Tabish A. Syed,Marc Theveneau,Benedikt Wirth,Rustum Choksi,Kaleem Siddiqi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种从单一取向矢量场推断局部平滑曲面叶状结构的数学模型与算法：通过求解与其正交且李括号为零的第二矢量场，并最小化平均曲率，实现高效并行的曲面估计，在脑白质纤维束与透明化心肌组织等合成与生物数据上完成验证，达到微米尺度重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然界中流线型结构（如哺乳动物脑白质纤维束、心肌细胞排列）普遍存在，且被认为通过形成局部光滑曲面来优化功能；但从与局部取向一致的单矢量场中恢复这种叶状结构仍是一个未解决的开放问题，且医学影像应用（脑图谱纤维束、心脏组织显微成像）迫切需要该能力。

Method: 建立"薄片/叶状结构"的数学模型并提出推断算法：给定输入矢量场，估计一个与之处处正交的第二矢量场，并要求二者李括号为零（保证可积性/叶状化）；同时以平均曲率最小化来平衡曲面似然与低表面积，计算相应能量的第一变分，并给出可高效并行实现的数值求解方法。

Result: 在合成数据与生物数据上验证了方法的有效性：包括脑图谱中白质纤维束与透明化心脏壁组织的共聚焦显微图像；对后者可在前所未有的微米尺度上重建与局部心肌细胞方向一致的曲面。

Conclusion: 该工作将叶状结构的恢复形式化为带可积性约束（零李括号）与曲率正则化的正交矢量场估计问题，提供了具有一阶变分与并行数值实现的可行框架，可在医学影像中的纤维束与细胞尺度组织上实现高分辨率曲面重建。

Abstract: Streamline patterns are ubiquitous in nature. Prominent examples include white matter fibre tracts in the mammalian brain and arrangements of elongated cells, such as cardiomyocytes in the heart wall. It has been posited that such organisations foliate into locally smooth surfaces to optimise function. However, the recovery of these foliations from a single vector field aligned with the local orientation of the underlying sheets remains an open problem. We address this challenge by introducing a mathematical model of a sheet and developing an algorithm for sheet inference, from measurements of the oriented structures. Given an input vector field, our model estimates a second vector field that is orthonormal to it, while also requiring the Lie bracket of the two to vanish. To balance sheet likelihood against low surface area, we also reduce the mean curvature of the recovered local surface. We compute the first variation of an associated energy and provide a numerical method which admits an efficient, parallel implementation for sheet estimation. We demonstrate our approach on both synthetic and biological examples, motivated by applications to medical imaging. These include white matter fibre tracts from a brain atlas and confocal microscopy images of cleared heart wall tissue. For the latter we are able to reconstruct sheets aligned with local cardiomyocyte directions at unprecedented (micrometre) scale.

</details>


### [86] [PReMind: When Personal Memory Meets Visual Hazards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23808)
*Yeongtak Oh,Donghyeok Lee,Joonhee Oh,Jaewon Jeong,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PReMind-Bench诊断基准与两阶段强化学习框架PReMind，联合训练个性化记忆调用与视觉危险响应，联合成功率从12.9%提升至28.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化视觉-语言助手需同时识别场景中的物理危险并应用用户个人背景（过敏、疾病、行动限制等），但现有模型往往满足其一而失败于另一，仅针对安全或个性化训练会在联合成功上留下缺口。

Method: 构建PReMind-Bench基准，分项测量记忆访问、视觉定位、拒答（abstention）与危险响应，并在相互依赖案例上评估联合成功；提出两阶段强化学习框架PReMind，结合按需调用与强制调用（总是检索记忆）两类交互共同训练基于证据的记忆使用与危险响应。

Result: 4B规模下，相互依赖集联合成功率由12.9%升至28.6%；主测试集上危险响应适当性与正确拒答均有提升；增益在强制调用评估、第二LLM评判及三个Phase II随机种子下保持稳定，多种子平均下对未见危险类别亦有提升。

Conclusion: 个性化助手须在评估与训练中显式联结视觉证据与相关用户背景，才能实现安全且个性化的联合响应。

Abstract: Personalized vision-language assistants use visual observations and personal memories to provide advice tailored to the user. When the observed scene contains a physical hazard, personal context, such as allergies, medical conditions, or mobility constraints, can change what help is appropriate. Responding appropriately therefore requires recognizing the visible hazard and applying the relevant person's information. We introduce PReMind-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark that evaluates this combination across diverse hazards and personal-memory conditions. It separately measures memory access, grounding, abstention, and hazard response, and assesses joint success on interdependency cases. Existing models often satisfy one requirement while failing the other, and training only for safety or personalization leaves a gap in joint success. We therefore propose PReMind, a two-phase reinforcement learning framework that combines on-demand and forced-call interactions (memory always retrieved) to jointly train grounded memory use and hazard response. At 4B, PReMind raises joint success from 12.9% to 28.6% over its base on the interdependency set and improves hazard adequacy and correct abstention on the main test. Joint-success gains persist under forced-call evaluation and under a second LLM judge across three Phase II seeds; averaged over these seeds, joint success also improves on unseen hazard groups. These findings highlight the need to evaluate and train personal assistants to connect visual evidence with the relevant person's context.

</details>


### [87] [When accuracy hides risk: risk-controlled confirmation of the critical view of safety under expert disagreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23781)
*Bowen Xiang,Xiao Yu,Mingze Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示腹腔镜胆囊切除术中CVS自动化判定标准的阈值校准规则存在结构性缺陷：错误集中于专家存在分歧的视频，且识别器越准确该问题越严重；提出"认证式确认"（certified confirmation）风险控制规则，通过多重检验选阈值，在整体、争议视频、厂商/国家分组及CVS可见前均以概率1-δ保证假确认率≤α，在SAGES 300例测试集上将争议视频假确认从8/39降至2/39，代价是及时确认充分视频比例由35%降至10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已部署的CVS自动检查需在放置第一个夹钳前决定是否确认CVS，而标准规则仅按平均假确认率校准阈值。作者指出该规则会把误差集中在专家意见不一致的"争议视频"上，且这一失败是结构性的：当参考标签来自专家池抽样的投票时，在固定平均假确认率下最大化敏感度的规则同时也最大化争议视频的假确认率，且识别器越准确该比例越高。平均准确率掩盖了这一风险，故需显式保护争议病例。

Method: 形式化分析参考标签为专家委员会投票情形下的阈值规则失效机制；提出"认证式确认"——一种风险受控的监控规则，阈值经多重检验（multiple testing）选取，使得以至少1-δ的概率，整体假确认率≤α，且在争议视频子集、厂商与国家分组内以及CVS尚未可见的情形下同样满足；争议标签仅用于校准。在SAGES CVS Challenge的300例测试视频（18个国家）上进行预注册、哈希锁定的评估，并与标准校准对比。

Result: 标准校准在39例争议视频中假确认8例（0.21；对α=0.10的单侧p=0.037），而认证式确认仅确认2例（单侧95%上界0.15）。开发数据上，对未参与校准的厂商与国家分组保护依然有效。代价是及时确认充分视频的比例由35%降至10%。

Conclusion: 平均准确率无法反映CVS自动确认在专家分歧病例上的集中风险；争议样本必须被显式保护。认证式确认以可量化的覆盖损失换取对争议视频、分组与早期帧的可证明风险控制，适用于部署前的安全校验。

Abstract: Automated assessment of the critical view of safety (CVS) in laparoscopic cholecystectomy has advanced rapidly, but a deployed check must decide, before the first clip, whether to confirm CVS. The standard rule calibrates a threshold so that false confirmations are rare on average. We show that this rule concentrates its errors on disputed videos, which one of three expert raters judged sufficient, and that the failure is structural. When reference labels are votes of a panel sampled from a larger expert pool, the rule that maximizes sensitivity at a fixed average false-confirmation rate also maximizes false confirmation of disputed videos, and for recognizers that recover expert judgement with less noise this rate rises with accuracy. We propose certified confirmation, a risk-controlled monitoring rule whose threshold is selected by multiple testing so that, with probability at least 1 - δ, false confirmation is at most α overall, among disputed videos, within vendor and country groups, and before CVS is visible, using disputed labels only to calibrate. In a pre-specified, hash-locked evaluation on the 300 test videos (18 countries) of the SAGES CVS Challenge, standard calibration falsely confirmed 8 of 39 disputed videos (0.21; one-sided p = 0.037 against α = 0.10), whereas certified confirmation confirmed 2 (one-sided 95% upper bound 0.15). On development data, protection held for vendor and country groups left out of calibration. The cost was confirming 10% rather than 35% of sufficient videos on time. Average accuracy hides this risk; disputed cases must be protected explicitly.

</details>


### [88] [DetectViT: Test-time Backdoor Detection for Vision Transformers via Inter-Head Attention Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23770)
*Siquan Huang,Yijiang Li,Xingfu Yan,Ningzhi Gao,Leyu Shi,Ying Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DetectViT，一种无需训练数据、模型输出与触发器先验的 ViT 测试时后门检测方法，基于多头注意力劫持现象构建一致性分数与熵方差分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT 作为多模态模型的视觉编码器多依赖第三方预训练权重，易被植入后门；现有测试时检测依赖预测标签或中间嵌入，跨任务通用性差且计算开销大。

Method: 分析触发器对多头注意力的影响，识别"完全注意力劫持"（头间空间重叠异常高）与"部分注意力劫持"（注意力分布集中度方差异常大）两类模式；据此构造 inter-head consistency score 与 inter-head entropy variance score，并用来自任意来源的小规模 OOD 校准集估计阈值。

Result: 在 4 类攻击、DeiT/CLIP/LLaVA 多种架构及图像分类与描述任务上，仅用 64–128 个 OOD 校准样本即显著优于所有基线；BadCLIP 上 TPR 达 100%、FPR 为 0.17%；仅利用推理中已算出的注意力权重，额外开销可忽略。

Conclusion: 注意力层面的头间异常是 ViT 后门的稳健判别信号；DetectViT 提供了一种高效、免标签、免触发先验的通用检测范式。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) now underpin the visual encoders of most multimodal models, but this success comes at a cost: relying on third-party pretrained checkpoints leaves these systems open to backdoor attacks. Existing test-time detection methods rely on either the predicted label or intermediate embeddings, limiting their applicability across diverse tasks and incurring substantial computational overhead. In this work, we study how backdoor triggers affect the multi-head attention mechanism of ViTs and identify two distinct anomalous patterns: $\textit{complete attention hijacking}$, causing the attention to exhibit abnormally high inter-head spatial overlap, and $\textit{partial attention hijacking}$, causing an abnormally large variance in the concentration of the attention distribution. Motivated by these findings, we propose $\textbf{DetectViT}$, a test-time backdoor detection method that requires neither training data, model outputs, nor any prior knowledge of the trigger. DetectViT quantifies the two hijacking phenomena via an inter-head consistency score and an inter-head entropy variance score, with thresholds estimated from a small out-of-distribution (OOD) calibration set drawn from an arbitrary source. Across four representative attacks, diverse architectures (DeiT, CLIP, and LLaVA), and downstream tasks (image classification and captioning), DetectViT substantially outperforms all baselines with as few as 64--128 OOD calibration samples, achieving a true positive rate of $100\%$ and a false positive rate of $0.17\%$ on BadCLIP. Notably, it introduces negligible additional overhead by relying solely on attention weights already computed during inference. Code is available at: https://github.com/siquanhuang/DetectViT.

</details>


### [89] [A Hardware-Aware Comprehensive Study of Edge Vision Deployment with A Unified Hardware-Model-Runtime Mapping Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23705)
*Le Duc An,Le Duc Thanh,Vo Hoai Viet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了边缘硬件约束、视觉模型架构与部署软件工具链之间的关系，建立三层边缘硬件分类体系，汇总跨功耗谱（数十瓦至毫瓦级）的图像分类与目标检测基准结果，并提出两层硬件-模型映射框架，给出按性能优先级的选型矩阵与架构设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习快速发展推动计算机视觉向资源受限的边缘设备部署，但边缘硬件架构的极度异构性使视觉模型的选型与设计面临重大挑战；现有研究缺乏对硬件约束、模型架构与部署工具链之间关系的系统衔接。

Method: 构建三层边缘硬件分类体系（中端GPU与专用NPU/TPU、嵌入式MPU或SoC、微控制器MCU）；聚合与分析跨越数十瓦至毫瓦级裸机MCU的多源图像分类与目标检测基准结果；基于实证发现提出两层硬件-模型映射框架。

Result: 提供覆盖Speed、Balanced、Accuracy三类性能优先级的模型选型矩阵，并提炼可操作的架构设计原则：峰值SRAM预算、分辨率优先的缩放策略、算法-编译器协同设计。

Conclusion: 指出工具链碎片化、算子不兼容及Vision Transformer可行性等关键部署挑战，并展望端侧自适应与可持续Edge AI等未来研究方向。

Abstract: The rapid growth of deep learning has driven the deployment of computer vision directly on resource-constrained edge devices. However, the extreme heterogeneity of edge hardware architectures poses major challenges in selecting and designing suitable vision models. This survey bridges the gap between edge hardware constraints, model architectures, and deployment software toolchains. We establish a comprehensive taxonomy classifying edge hardware into three distinct tiers: mid-range devices (GPUs and dedicated NPUs or TPUs), dedicated embedded MPU or SoC processors, and microcontrollers (MCUs). We aggregate and analyze multi-source benchmark results for image classification and object detection across the computing spectrum, spanning from tens of watts down to milliwatt-scale bare-metal MCUs. Based on these empirical findings, we propose a two-tiered hardware-to-model mapping framework that provides concrete model selection matrices across performance priorities (Speed, Balanced, Accuracy) and actionable architectural design principles (peak SRAM budgeting, resolution-first scaling, and algorithm-compiler co-design). Finally, we highlight critical deployment challenges including toolchain fragmentation, operator incompatibility, and Vision Transformer feasibility, and outline promising future research directions in on-device adaptation and sustainable Edge AI.

</details>


### [90] [Structured Extraction of Diffraction Data from Covalent Organic Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23680)
*Shenghan Gao,Zhiling Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"视觉大语言模型（Vision LLM）+ 经典计算机视觉（CV）"协同的流水线，将文献中仅以图像形式存在的PXRD图谱自动还原为像素级精度的可复用曲线，并构建开放数据库COF-PEAK。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PXRD是COF等材料的核心结构证据，但数十年来测得的大量图谱仅以论文插图形式存在，未被结构化保存。自动提取困难在于图谱版式差异极大——从单条曲线到含标签、插图、相邻子图的密集堆叠图，传统CV方法难以应对。因此需要既能理解复杂版面、又能保证曲线精度的自动化方案。

Method: 采用两级流水线：(1) 视觉大语言模型（Vision LLM）负责版面语义理解，定位衍射图区域及其中的各条曲线，并给出每条曲线的粗略轨迹草图；(2) 经典计算机视觉方法对粗略轨迹进行精修，将其提升至像素级精度，最终得到经过标定的衍射曲线。整个流程应用于3,954篇COF文献。

Result: 共提取11,445条经标定的衍射曲线，并发布在开放数据库COF-PEAK中。与人工数字化的60个图版对比，提取峰位的中位偏差仅为0.07°。此外，数字化曲线可支持对峰显著度（crystallinity的潜在指标）随合成条件变化的追踪分析，而这是原文通常仅报告少量峰角所无法实现的。

Conclusion: Vision LLM的语义/版面理解能力与经典CV的精确几何提取能力互补，可高效地将文献插图转化为可复用的实验数据，为材料数据的规模化挖掘与二次分析提供可行路径。

Abstract: Powder X-ray diffraction (PXRD) is the primary structural evidence for covalent organic frameworks (COFs) and many other materials. Yet decades of measured patterns exist only as images in published figures. Automatic recovery is difficult, because PXRD figures vary widely in format, from a single curve to crowded stacks with labels, insets, and neighboring plots that challenge traditional computer vision. This work presents a pipeline in which a vision large language model (LLM) interprets the layout of each figure, locating the diffraction plot and its curves and sketching a coarse trace of each, while classical computer vision (CV) refines every trace to pixel-level accuracy. Applied to 3,954 COF articles, the pipeline produces 11,445 calibrated diffraction curves, released in the open database COF-PEAK. Compared with a hand digitization of 60 panels, extracted peak positions differ by a median of only 0.07 degrees. Whereas papers usually report only a few peak angles, which limits further analysis, the digitized curves enable tracking how peak prominence, a possible marker of crystallinity, changes with synthesis conditions. Vision LLMs and classical CV can thus turn such figures into reusable experimental data.

</details>


### [91] [Object Pose: The Link between Weak Perspective, Paraperspective, and Full Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24095)
*Radu Horaud,Fadi Dornaika,Bart Lamiroy,Stephane Christy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对DeMenthon与Davis的迭代位姿估计方法给出代数推导，指出其属于以零阶（弱透视）或一阶（仿透视）近似透视相机模型的更广方法类；提出迭代仿透视位姿计算方法，分析其收敛性与标定误差敏感性，并与非线性优化方法比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透视相机模型下的位姿求解通常需非线性优化，而DeMenthon与Davis的迭代弱透视方法缺乏代数解释与理论刻画。作者希望从代数层面厘清该方法的本质，将其纳入统一的近框架，并寻求收敛性更优的替代方案。

Method: 对DeMenthon-Davis方法进行代数推导，统一刻画以零阶（弱透视）和一阶（仿透视）近似透视模型的两类方法；详细给出适用于非共面与共面物点的迭代仿透视位姿计算流程；引入处理旋转矩阵正交约束的简便方式；分析各方法的收敛性及对相机标定误差的敏感性，并推导最优实验布置。

Result: 理论分析表明迭代仿透视方法比迭代弱透视方法具有更好的收敛性质；给出关于标定误差敏感的定量分析及相应的最优实验配置；将该方法与非线性优化方法的精度进行对比实验。

Conclusion: 迭代仿透视方法在收敛性上优于迭代弱透视方法，且一阶近似框架为透视位姿估计提供了统一的代数视角；所提正交约束处理与实验布置准则有助于提升实用性，其结果与非线性优化方法具有可比性。

Abstract: Recently, DeMenthon and Davis [4,5] proposed a method for determining the pose of a 3-D object with respect to a camera from 3-D to 2-D point correspondences. The method consists of iteratively improving the pose computed with a weak perspective camera model to converge, at the limit, to a pose estimation computed with a perspective camera model. In this paper we give an algebraic derivation of DeMenthon and Davis' method and we show that it belongs to a larger class of methods where the perspective camera model is approximated either at zero order (weak perspective) or first order (paraperspective). We describe in detail an iterative paraperspective pose computation method for both non coplanar and coplanar object points. We analyse the convergence of these methods and we conclude that the iterative paraperspective method (proposed in this paper) has better convergence properties than the iterative weak perspective method. We introduce a simple way of taking into account the orthogonality constraint associated with the rotation matrix. We analyse the sensitivity to camera calibration errors and we define the optimal experimental setup with respect to imprecise camera calibration. We compare the results obtained with this method and with a non-linear optimization method.

</details>


### [92] [Benchmarking Reasoning Reliability,Prior_Evidence Contrast,and Spectral Discrimination in Remote-Sensing Geological Image Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24068)
*Jun Wang,Fengpeng Li,Wei Han,Yuxuan Li,Tianjin Huang,Jining Yan,Sheng Wang,Kan Sun,Lizhe Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出高光谱地质解释诊断基准HyGeoBench（1万题、三维度、十类任务）及工具增强框架HyGeoAgent，系统评估并改进多模态大模型在地质岩性解释中的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠岩性解释需要基于视觉证据的地质识别与推理；多模态大模型在遥感中展现潜力，但其地质解释的可靠性尚不明确，缺乏针对推理可靠性、先验与证据冲突、光谱判别力的诊断手段。

Method: 构建HyGeoBench：基于高光谱影像的1万题诊断基准，涵盖推理可靠性、先验-证据对比、光谱判别三个维度，十类任务，含单选与开放两种作答格式，融合高光谱卫星影像、地质标注与经核实证据。提出HyGeoAgent：工具增强框架，通过自适应工具调用整合区域观测、文本知识检索、同波段图像检索与原生PCA分析。

Result: 评估显示当前模型在细粒度识别与证据支撑解释上存在明显局限；HyGeoAgent在各诊断维度上相对对应基模型取得一致性提升，改善地质影像解释表现。

Conclusion: HyGeoBench为地质多模态解释提供细粒度诊断工具，HyGeoAgent验证了自适应工具调用对提升证据驱动地质解释可靠性的有效性。

Abstract: Reliable lithology interpretation requires accurate geological recognition and reasoning grounded in visual evidence. Although Multimodal Large Language Models show promise in remote sensing, their reliability in geological interpretation remains unclear. We introduce HyGeoBench, a diagnostic benchmark of 10,000 questions based on hyperspectral imagery across three dimensions: reasoning reliability, prior_evidence contrast, and spectral discrimination. It covers ten task types, with both multiple-choice and open-ended response formats. Integrating hyperspectral satellite imagery with geological annotations and verified evidence, HyGeoBench examines reasoning errors, conflicts between geological priors and visual evidence, and cross-band inconsistencies. Evaluations reveal limitations in fine-grained recognition and evidence-grounded interpretation. To address these challenges, we propose HyGeoAgent, a tool-augmented framework integrating region observation, text knowledge retrieval, same-band image retrieval, and native PCA analysis through adaptive tool use. Experiments demonstrate consistent improvements over the corresponding base models, improving geological image interpretation across diagnostic dimensions.

</details>


### [93] [R$^{3}$Sieve: Suppressing Unconditional Information from Attention to Answers for High-Resolution VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24060)
*Yifan Cao,Yawen Zhang,Xiaocui Yang,Faxian Wan,Shi Feng,Daling Wang,Yifei Zhang,Shangshu Yu,Xianbing Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: R³Sieve 是一个无需训练的框架，将高分辨率VQA推理分为定位、裁剪与回答三阶段，通过反事实参考抑制各阶段的无条件信息干扰，从而更有效利用小目标证据，在三个基准上取得最高总体准确率，较基座模型提升最多21.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率VQA需要模型定位与问题相关的小目标并据此作答，要求细粒度感知。已有方法侧重获取更好的视觉证据（图像裁剪），但仅有证据不够，如何有效利用证据同样关键。作者认为小目标证据会被"无条件信息"遮蔽——这些信息即使在其所依赖的条件被移除后仍可被观察到，从而妨碍小目标证据的有效利用。

Method: 提出 R³Sieve（Residual-Region-Response Sieve），一种无需训练的框架。将推理划分为三阶段并引入不同的反事实参照以在各阶段抑制干扰：1）定位阶段——共享子空间消除器（SSC）利用通用图像描述构建反事实参照，获得问题相关注意力图；2）裁剪阶段——独特贡献定位器（UCS）从视觉与非视觉特征构建反事实参照，识别具有区分性与互补性的裁剪区域；3）回答阶段——内容对齐投票器（CAV）构建无图像分支以校准答案响应。

Result: 在三个高分辨率VQA基准、多种设置下，R³Sieve 的总体准确率在对比方法中最高，相较基座模型最高提升21.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 高分辨率VQA的关键不仅在于获取视觉证据，更在于全流程中有效利用证据；以反事实参照分阶段抑制无条件信息干扰是一种有效且无需训练的解决路径。

Abstract: High-resolution visual question answering (VQA) requires models to identify question-relevant small objects in high-resolution images and select the correct answer based on what they recognize, demanding fine-grained perception. Previous methods focus on obtaining better visual evidence (image crops), but obtaining it alone is insufficient, using it effectively is equally crucial. Visual evidence should therefore be utilized as effectively as possible throughout inference. We argue that small-target evidence is obscured by unconditional information that remains observable in subsequent processing even after the condition on which it should depend is removed. Such information hinders the effective use of small-target evidence. We propose R$^{3}$Sieve (Residual-Region-Response Sieve), a training-free framework. We divide inference into three stages---localization, cropping, and answering---and introduce distinct counterfactual references to suppress interference at each stage. During localization, the Shared-Subspace Canceller (SSC) uses a generic image description to construct a counterfactual reference and obtain question-relevant attention maps. During cropping, the Unique-Contribution Spotter (UCS) constructs counterfactual references from visual and non-visual features to identify distinctive, complementary crop regions. During answering, the Content-Aligned Voter (CAV) constructs an image-free branch to calibrate answer responses. Experiments on three high-resolution VQA benchmarks show that R$^{3}$Sieve achieves the highest overall accuracy among compared methods across diverse settings, improving base models by up to 21.5 percentage points.

</details>


### [94] [Choosing Supervised Post-Processing for Frozen Person Detectors: Information, Object Changes, and Complete Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24003)
*Tianyi Zhou,Zijun Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在冻结检测器上游统一比较多种后处理读出，发现加入原生集合上下文的Full100读出精度/召回更优，且经等效优化后与元分类开销相近（约95 ms/请求），但特征维度与净增益不足以作为采纳依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结检测器的候选假设需经监督式后处理（readout）才能转化为最终输出；选择读出方式必须同时权衡"哪些信息能提升检测"与"处理成本"，而现有做法缺乏在统一候选、监督与工作点条件下对多种可选信息的系统比较。

Method: 在共同候选、共同监督与共同操作点条件下，比较候选分数、类分布、几何特征、原生集合(native-set)摘要以及自适应邻域元分类(MetaDetect式)等读出方案；用匹配数/未匹配预测数衡量增益与损失，并做执行等效(execution-equivalent)优化后在固定64图负载上测量端到端延迟。评估集：CrowdHuman 320张留出图像、另一319图队列，以及MOT17Det 7个序列223帧的源冻结迁移。

Result: 320图集上，向97维读出加入原生集合上下文多出275个匹配、96个未匹配预测；319图队列中Full100匹配6004个目标，源选择raw WBF为5848，MetaDetect式自适应为6002，Full100相对raw净增156（新增300、丢失144）。等效优化后Full100与元统计读出在64图工作负载上均约95 ms/请求。迁移到MOT17Det得正点结果，而主MIAP结果无提升。

Conclusion: 应采用信息量充分的输出读出，同时保留经优化的元分类作为有竞争力的备选；特征维度与检测净增益单独均不可作为采纳判据。

Abstract: A frozen detector computes hypotheses that can support better final outputs through supervised post-processing. Choosing a readout requires knowing which available information improves detection and what its processing costs. We compare candidate scores, class distributions, geometry, native-set summaries and adapted neighborhood meta classification under common candidate, supervision and operating-point conditions. On 320 held-out CrowdHuman images, adding native-set context to a 97-dimensional readout yields 275 more matches and 96 more unmatched predictions. On a separate 319-image cohort, Full100 matches 6004 objects,versus 5848 for source-selected raw WBF and 6002 for a MetaDetect-style adaptation. Its gain over raw contains 300 added and 144 lost discoveries. After execution-equivalent optimization, Full100 and the meta-statistics readout both take approximately 95 ms per complete warm request on a fixed 64-image workload. Source-frozen transfer gives positive point results on 223 MOT17Det frames from seven sequences, while the main MIAP result shows no improvement. The study supports using sufficiently informative output readouts while retaining optimized meta classification as a competitive option. It also shows why feature dimension and net detection gain alone are unreliable guides to adoption.

</details>


### [95] [WaveView: The Novel View Is in the Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23994)
*Kaiwen Wang,Jan-Hendrik Pauls,Yinzhe Shen,Frank Bieder,Christoph Stiller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WaveView 将源视图的 warp 结果注入预训练生成器初始噪声的相位（基于 DT-CWPT），在保持架构不变、开销可忽略的前提下提升生成式新视角合成的几何一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式 NVS 分两类：显式 warp 结合视频扩散模型，或隐式编码相机参数/3D 线索。两者均引入额外推理开销，且生成结果布局常偏离真值。需要一种既约束几何又不增加复杂度的方案。

Method: 利用双树复小波包变换（DT-CWPT）将 warp 后的源视图注入初始噪声的相位，实现局部、各向异性的相位注入。由于只在相位上施加约束，生成器在遮挡解除区域与极线方向上受到的约束更少；网络架构不变，额外开销可忽略。

Result: 在 DL3DV 基准上，各重叠区间均优于所有基线；在最难区间，共视区域 PSNR 比同生成器的 MetaView 高 0.84 dB，且速度快 2.1 倍。动态 NVS 中，用相同噪声微调 AnyView，使其在 AnyViewBench 上的零样本 PSNR 从 11.58 提升至 12.35 dB。

Conclusion: 相位域注入是一种轻量且有效的几何条件化方式，可同时适用于静态与动态 NVS，并在精度与效率上取得双赢，具备良好可迁移性。

Abstract: Generative novel view synthesis (NVS) employs a diffusion model to render a static or dynamic scene from a target camera view. Explicit warp methods complete a reprojection of the source with video diffusion models, and implicit methods encode the camera parameters or 3D cues. Both add inference overhead, and the generated layout often drifts from the ground truth. We present WaveView, which injects the warped source into the phase of the initial noise of a pretrained generator. The Dual-Tree Complex Wavelet Packet Transform (DT-CWPT) allows local and anisotropic phase injection. The generator is thus constrained less in disoccluded regions and along the epipolar line. WaveView keeps the architecture and adds negligible overhead. On the DL3DV benchmark, it outperforms all baselines in every overlap bin. In the hardest bin, it gains 0.84 dB PSNR on the covisible region over MetaView, which shares its generator, while running 2.1 times faster. For dynamic NVS, fine-tuning AnyView with the same noise raises its zero-shot PSNR on AnyViewBench from 11.58 to 12.35 dB.

</details>


### [96] [Agreement Is Not Accuracy: LiDAR Elevation Errors Hidden in LiDAR-Camera Extrinsics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23972)
*Yeon Kun Lee,Dongseok Yang,Deok-Kyeong Jang,Choongho Chung,Junyoung Lee,Hyeongseok So*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出LiDAR与相机在标定后的一致性并不能证明任一传感器内部几何正确，因为系统性的LiDAR高程偏差可被外参吸收；作者提出用同步LiDAR强度与相机估计的棋盘位姿联合估计传感器特定的高程校正与每段记录的外参，并证明该校正能显著提升几何精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外参标定通常以LiDAR与相机观测一致为目标，但一致性可能被外参"吸收"掉LiDAR内部的系统性高程偏差（elevation bias），导致标定看似优良却掩盖了内部几何误差。作者希望揭示这一失效模式，并提供无需大型标定场的检验与估计手段。

Method: 1) 利用同步的LiDAR强度与相机估计的棋盘位姿；2) 通过射线-平面求交定位棋盘格，而不使用LiDAR报告的测距值，避免测距误差直接影响查表位置；3) 联合估计一个跨多段记录共享的传感器特定高程校正，以及每段记录各自的外参；4) 在匹配的强度与校正模型下进行查表，并与实测点查表对比残差。

Result: 在六套同型号传感器、同设计的装置上，排除记录外的校正将平均平移参考距离从7.57 mm降至3.04 mm；在独立结构上，相对独立密集相机扫描参考，减小了面间夹角误差并改善图像边缘对齐；旋转装置后，角度改善最大的平面对发生转移，符合仅含高程校正的预期；而在射线-平面模型内，标定靶投影与闭环一致性残差变化很小。

Conclusion: 研究说明外参标定后的一致性不足以确立LiDAR内部几何正确；同步观测常见棋盘格即可实际估计并检验高程校正，无需专用大型标定场，作者计划公开代码与数据以复现主要结果。

Abstract: LiDAR and cameras provide complementary measurements for 3D vision, yet agreement between their observations does not establish the accuracy of either sensor's internal geometry. We show that systematic LiDAR elevation biases can be absorbed into the estimated extrinsics. Using synchronized LiDAR intensities and camera-estimated checkerboard poses, we jointly estimate a sensor-specific elevation correction shared across recordings and extrinsics for each recording. Ray-plane intersections locate the checker pattern without using reported ranges, preventing range errors from directly moving the lookup. Under matched intensity and correction models, this lookup gives smaller camera-consistency residuals than measured-point lookup. Across six identically designed rigs using the same sensor models, recording-excluded corrections reduce the mean translation-reference distance from 7.57 to 3.04 mm. On separate structures, they reduce inter-plane angle errors against references from independent dense camera scans and improve image-edge alignment. Rolling the rigs shifts the plane pairs exhibiting the largest angular improvements, as predicted for an elevation-only correction. Within the ray-plane model, calibration-target projections and loop-consistency residuals change little despite these geometric improvements. These findings show why agreement after extrinsic calibration need not establish correct internal LiDAR geometry. Synchronized observations of a familiar checkerboard provide a practical route to estimating and testing elevation corrections without a dedicated large calibration field. We plan to release code and data for reproducing the main results.

</details>


### [97] [TOMAS: Topology-Aware Operation Modeling and Adaptive Staging for Egocentric Hand Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23970)
*Guanzhong Sun,Junyi Ma,Yuxuan Wu,Erhang Zhang,Chenyang Xu,Yanzi Miao,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TOMAS：面向自我中心视频手部预测的拓扑感知操作建模与自适应分阶段框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一物体在不同操作意图下会产生显著不同的手部轨迹，而现有方法未充分挖掘关节式手部运动与物体语义之间的内在关系，难以刻画这种依赖操作类型的运动模式。

Method: 提出拓扑感知的手-物推理机制，将手部骨骼结构与关节运动学动态同物体语义及空间关系关联，联合推断潜在交互目标与隐含操作类型，形成物体-操作表征作为预测条件，并按各数据集标注做监督适配；同时引入自适应分阶段机制，将未来手部运动组织为具有可学习时间边界的有序隐阶段，并结合阶段特定几何子目标进行轨迹细化。

Result: 在多个公开基准上，TOMAS 在多维度、多目标手部运动预测任务中取得最优性能。

Conclusion: 显式建模手-物拓扑关系与操作语义、并以可学习的阶段化几何子目标约束预测，可有效提升自我中心手部轨迹预测的准确性与泛化性。

Abstract: Forecasting future hand movements from egocentric videos plays an important role in understanding human intentions and hand-object interactions. However, different manipulation intentions can lead to substantially different hand trajectories even when interacting with the same object. Existing methods have not sufficiently explored the intrinsic relationships between articulated hand movements and object semantics to capture such operation-dependent motion patterns. Therefore, we propose TOMAS, a topology-aware operation modeling and adaptive staging framework for egocentric hand forecasting. Specifically, we develop a topology-aware hand-object reasoning mechanism that associates hand skeletal structures and articulation dynamics with object semantics and spatial relationships to jointly infer potential interaction targets and latent manipulation operations. The resulting object-operation representations provide interaction conditions for future hand trajectory prediction, with supervision adapted to the annotations available in each dataset. Furthermore, we introduce an adaptive staging mechanism that organizes future hand movements into ordered latent stages with learnable temporal boundaries. Stage-specific geometric subgoals are then incorporated to guide trajectory refinement according to the underlying manipulation patterns. Experimental results on multiple publicly available benchmarks demonstrate that TOMAS achieves state-of-the-art performance in multi-dimensional and multi-target hand motion forecasting.

</details>


### [98] [MitoWorld: A Stochastic World Model of the Single-Cell Cycle under Genetic Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23953)
*Ting Long,Xiaojing Sun,Cheng Han,Zhicheng Ji,Yingnian Wu,Jiebo Luo,Heng Fan,Dongfang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MitoWorld：一个随机世界模型，将基因敲低后单细胞未来的连续外观演化建模为高斯参考隐空间中的随机过程，以分裂风险（hazard）终止，并由条件扩散渲染器解码为图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因敲低后单细胞的未来外观演化与分裂时机均不确定；现有方法只预测终点或群体水平变化，未联合建模个体轨迹与分裂时间。

Method: 构建随机世界模型：外观在隐空间连续演化并保持高斯参考（普通转移可证保持高斯性、噪声与动力学耦合），配对观测可辨识离散转移；分裂由风险函数刻画，转化为生存似然以检验真实周期长度；条件扩散渲染器将隐状态转为图像。

Result: 在覆盖17种敲低及对照的325条留出MitoCheck轨迹上，FID降低34.6%，视频指标JEDi降低45.6%；隐空间预测区间达到近名义的90%覆盖率，且比自由拟合噪声的模型窄35%；风险函数给出的细胞周期长度分布KS距离接近基于模型的有限样本参考。

Conclusion: 可检验的单细胞随机动力学为延时扰动筛选提供了生成式工具。

Abstract: A cell observed after a gene knockdown has an uncertain future, both in how its appearance will evolve and when it will divide. Existing approaches predict endpoints or population-level changes without jointly modeling individual trajectories and division timing. In this work, we present MitoWorld, a stochastic world model that generates these future appearances as continuous evolution in a Gaussian reference latent, terminated by a division hazard and converted into images by a conditional diffusion renderer. The ordinary transition provably preserves its Gaussian reference, tying its noise to its dynamics; paired observations identify its discrete transition where stationary marginals cannot. The hazard turns division timing into a survival likelihood that can be tested against real cycle lengths. On 325 held-out MitoCheck tracks spanning 17 knockdowns and a control, MitoWorld lowers FID by 34.6\% and the video metric JEDi by 45.6\% relative to the strongest baselines. Its latent-space prediction intervals achieve near-nominal 90\% coverage, are 35\% narrower than those of a model with freely fitted noise. The hazard also yields cell-cycle length distributions with KS distance close to the model-based finite-sample reference. Testable single-cell stochastic dynamics thus offer a generative tool for time-lapse perturbation screens.

</details>


### [99] [HOForce: Motion and Force Reconstruction of Hand-Object Interaction Using a Single-View RGB-D Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23871)
*Jiahui Wang,Wanqing Du,Yuanbin Zou,Zhefeng Xu,Feng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 HOForce，从单视角 RGB-D 视频与已知物体属性中跟踪手-物交互并恢复交互力，同时以力作为遮挡区域的位姿约束以提升跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手-物交互重建在单相机条件下具有大范围采集的实用性，但严重遮挡使位姿跟踪与交互力估计困难；现有方法多只做运动跟踪，未显式恢复接触力，也未利用力约束改善遮挡区域位姿。

Method: 联合建模双手操作时两手指尖力，以及双物体场景中物体间接触力；将重建的力作为遮挡区域位姿约束反哺跟踪；在 MuJoCo 中复现物体运动，并通过适配迁移至其他物理引擎。

Result: 在 DexYCB 与 TACO 上手/物运动精度更高，DexYCB 上手-物穿透更少；在带触觉传感器标注指尖力的自建数据集上，法向力 MAE 较最强基线降低 24.5%。

Conclusion: 单视角视频中的力恢复可行且与运动跟踪相互促进，所恢复力可用于物理仿真中的物体运动复现，并具备跨引擎可迁移性。

Abstract: Reconstructing hand-object interactions is an important task, and achieving it with a single camera makes large-scale capture practical but challenging due to heavy occlusions. We introduce HOForce to track hands and objects, and further recover interaction forces from single-view RGB-D video and known object properties. For two-hand manipulation, we jointly model fingertip forces of both hands and the contact forces between objects when two objects are presented. The reconstructed forces also improve motion tracking by giving pose constraints in the occluded regions. HOForce recovers hand and object motion more accurately on DexYCB and TACO and produces less hand-object penetration than interaction baselines on DexYCB. On our dataset of fingertip forces recorded with tactile sensors, HOForce reduces normal-force MAE by 24.5% relative to the strongest baseline. The recovered forces reproduce object motion in MuJoCo and transfer to other engines after adaptation.

</details>


### [100] [On Demand, Not Uniformity: Adaptive Expert Orchestration for Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23865)
*Mengxin Qin,Kun Wei,Weitao An,Jiahua Li,Xu Yang,Cheng Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CIRA-MoE框架，用“可学习阈值路由”产生的专家接受边界作为统一覆盖信号，据此选择性地向覆盖不足的层注入新专家，并按token调节新老专家的残差贡献，在UCIT与CoIN上取得MCIT最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MoE的多模态持续指令微调方法通过冻结历史专家、新增专家来缓解遗忘，但忽视了任务间差异化的适配需求：探测显示任务在层级梯度压力与token级预测难度上存在显著异质性，因而留下两个未决问题——何处需要额外容量，以及新专家对单个token应以多强程度参与。

Method: CIRA-MoE以可学习阈值路由导出专家接受边界（acceptance margin），作为统一覆盖信号。其一，利用历史接受证据指导新专家向覆盖不足的层选择性注入；其二，利用历史专家与当前专家的相对证据调制token级残差贡献，并配合“仲裁一致性”正则促进专家特化。

Result: 在UCIT与CoIN基准上的综合实验表明，CIRA-MoE在MCIT任务上优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 以接受边界统一刻画层覆盖与token级贡献强度，是可同时回答“何处扩容”与“扩多少”的有效路径，可在缓解灾难性遗忘的同时提升多模态持续指令微调的适配能力。

Abstract: Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning (MCIT) enables Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to adapt to sequential instruction data while retaining previously acquired knowledge. While recent Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)-based methods mitigate forgetting by freezing historical experts and introducing new ones, they largely overlook the varying adaptation demands across tasks. Probing incoming tasks reveals pronounced heterogeneity in layer-wise gradient pressure and token-wise prediction difficulty. This observation highlights two unresolved decisions: where additional capacity is needed and how strongly new experts should contribute to individual tokens. To address both questions, we propose the Coverage-Calibrated Expert Injection and Residual Arbitration framework (CIRA-MoE), which derives expert acceptance margins via learnable threshold routing to serve as a unified coverage signal. Specifically, historical acceptance evidence guides the selective injection of new experts into insufficiently covered layers. Meanwhile, relative evidence between historical and current experts modulates token-wise residual contributions, coupled with arbitration-consistent regularization to promote expert specialization. Comprehensive experiments on UCIT and CoIN demonstrate that CIRA-MoE outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods in MCIT.

</details>


### [101] [NIRJALA: Draining the Sea out of Gaussian Splatting with Three-Path Optics and Reversible Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23450)
*Latchan Chhetri,Aman Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NIRJALA，一个联合恢复水下无水质感与几何的3D高斯泼溅框架；其核心是三路径成像模型TRIVENI（在直透射与后向散射之外加入距离饱和的前向散射项），并证明该模型使反演增益在任何距离上有界，同时给出后向散射与衰减估计误差向颜色传播的界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下图像每个像素是衰减的场景辐射、后向散射光与到达相机途中前向散射光的混合，存在严重病态性（多种场景—水体组合可解释同一像素）。现有水下高斯泼溅方法仅建模前两项，并用以距离指数衰减的透射率做除法去除水体，导致反演增益随距离无界增长，去水结果不稳定。

Method: （1）TRIVENI三路径成像模型：直透射+后向散射+距离饱和的前向散射项，并理论证明反演增益有界、误差传播有界；（2）PRATIBIMBA可逆物理锚：将柔性神经水体模型与十参数物理模型双向绑定，使场景内容无法"藏"进水体参数；（3）SAMARASA物理约束目标集合，含按距离分箱的颜色先验；（4）构建SAJALA数据集：Blender Cycles路径追踪的30个场景，提供无水图像、深度、法线与受控水色/浊度真值。

Result: 在SAJALA上，相较最强基线PSNR由24.34 dB提升至24.76 dB，深度AbsRel由0.109降至0.091；在真实SeaThru-NeRF场景上LPIPS由0.194降至0.175，渲染速度44 FPS。论文还讨论了水下外观指标与几何指标不一致的现象及评估局限。

Conclusion: 将前向散射纳入成像模型可使水下逆问题良态可控，配合物理锚定的神经水体建模与配套合成基准，可在无监督/物理先验条件下同时提升去水外观与几何重建质量；但外观与几何评价存在分歧，评估范围仍有限。

Abstract: Water does not just tint an underwater photograph; it rewrites it. Each pixel mixes attenuated scene radiance, backscattered light, and scene light scattered forward on its way to the camera, and many combinations of scene and water explain the same pixels. Most Gaussian-splatting methods for underwater scenes model only the first two effects and remove the water by dividing by a transmission that decays exponentially with range, so their inversion gain grows without bound. We present NIRJALA (Sanskrit for "without water"), a 3D Gaussian Splatting framework that recovers water-free appearance and geometry together. TRIVENI, a three-path image formation model, adds a range-saturating forward-scattering term to direct transmission and backscatter; we prove that this keeps the inversion gain bounded at every range and bound how errors in the estimated backscatter and attenuation propagate into the recovered colours. PRATIBIMBA, a reversible physics anchor, ties a flexible neural water model to a ten-parameter physical model in both directions, so scene content cannot hide inside the water. SAMARASA collects the physics-informed objectives, including a range-binned colour prior. To score water removal against ground truth, we built SAJALA ("with water"), a 30-scene suite path-traced in Blender Cycles with water-free images, depth, and normals under controlled water colour and turbidity. On SAJALA, NIRJALA raises PSNR from 24.34 to 24.76 dB and lowers depth AbsRel from 0.109 to 0.091 against the strongest baseline; on real SeaThru-NeRF scenes it lowers LPIPS from 0.194 to 0.175 while rendering at 44 FPS. We also discuss where appearance metrics and geometry disagree underwater, and the limits of our evaluation.

</details>


### [102] [SEE: Unlocking the Hidden Perception of Frozen VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23962)
*Alin-Ionut Popa,Tuo Zhang,Dimitrios Dimitriadis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SEE是一种无需训练的推理方法，让冻结的VLM在低置信度时"再看一眼"自己指向的区域，通过放大裁剪重新作答并以置信度加权投票融合，显著提升小目标与密集文本图的细粒度识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM能把握图像整体内容，却常忽略问题所依赖的细节（如大图中的小目标、密集信息图中的数字）。但即便答案错误，模型所指区域往往仍包含证据，这一现象可被利用。

Method: 提出Self-Elicited Evidence（SEE），完全免训练：对冻结VLM，当初始答案置信度低时，提示模型自行提出细节可能出现的区域，在这些放大裁剪图上重新回答问题，并将结果与原始预测通过置信度加权投票聚合；模型还可输出自身转写文本以应对文本密集图像。无需检测器或OCR引擎。

Result: 在五个骨干模型上，SEE使所有小目标基准提升2.3–13.1个点，并在小目标任务上优于五个免训练基线。搭载SEE的Qwen3.5-4B超过Qwen3.5-9B的直接作答，并与6倍大的Qwen3.5-27B持平。

Conclusion: 利用模型自身指向区域作为证据来源的"再看一次"范式，可在零训练成本下有效弥补VLM的细粒度感知缺陷，且具备良好的骨干通用性与对模型规模的有效替代性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) grasp the overall content of an image but often miss the fine details a question hinges on, such as a small object in a large photo or a number in a dense infographic. Yet even when their answer is wrong, the region they point to often contains the evidence. We introduce Self-Elicited Evidence (SEE), a training-free method that lets a frozen VLM look again where it points. When the initial answer has low confidence, SEE prompts the model to propose regions where the details may appear, answers the question on these enlarged crops, and aggregates the answers with the original prediction through confidence-weighted voting. The model also answers with its own transcript, which helps on text-rich images. SEE needs no detector or OCR engine. Across five backbones, SEE improves every small-object benchmark by 2.3-13.1 points and outperforms five training-free baselines on small objects.
