With SEE, Qwen3.5-4B surpasses the direct answer of Qwen3.5-9B and matches the 6 times larger Qwen3.5-27B.

</details>


### [103] [RAID: Representation Autoencoder for Intrinsic Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24521)
*Claudia Cuttano,Tamy Boubekeur,Elena Garces,Giuseppe Vecchio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RAID框架，通过"先重建后分解"的解耦策略，在DINOv3预训练表征上实现单图本征分解，仅165M参数、比扩散方法快100倍，且精度competitive或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图本征分解本质上是病态问题，需从单张RGB中解耦强耦合的外观因素。现有方法借助扩散先验缓解歧义，但代价是模型庞大、需迭代采样且具随机性。作者旨在证明精确分解并不依赖大型生成模型。

Method: 在强预训练表征DINOv3之上解耦"重建"与"分解"两阶段：(1) X→X教师：用共享解码器从DINOv3特征重建各本征图，无需RGB→本征的配对监督；(2) 基于DINOv3的学生模型通过轻量、模态特定的投影，从RGB预测对应的教师表征，从而可在部分标注数据集上训练，并在单次前向中预测所有本征量。模型在结构化隐空间中预测本征属性。

Result: RAID为紧凑的165M参数模型，比扩散方法快约100倍；尽管规模小，分解质量达到competitive或更优水平，且对输入分辨率有更好的鲁棒性；学到的投影产生可解释的模态特定特征空间。

Conclusion: 精确的本征分解无需大型生成模型，借助强预训练表征与重建/分解解耦的架构即可用小模型高效实现，并兼具鲁棒性与可解释性。

Abstract: Single-image intrinsic decomposition is an inherently ill-posed problem, requiring the disentanglement of strongly coupled appearance factors from a single RGB observation. Recent methods address this ambiguity with diffusion priors, at the cost of large, iterative, and often stochastic models. We show that accurate decomposition does not require a large generative model. Our key idea is to decouple reconstruction from decomposition on top of a strong pretrained representation. First, an $X \rightarrow X$ teacher learns to reconstruct each intrinsic map from DINOv3 features using a shared decoder, without paired RGB-to-intrinsic supervision. Then, a DINOv3-based student predicts the corresponding teacher representations from RGB through lightweight, modality-specific projections, enabling training on partially annotated datasets and predicting all intrinsics in a single forward pass. \textbf{RAID}, predicts intrinsic properties in the structured latent space, enabling a compact 165M-parameter model, 100x faster than diffusion-based methods. Despite its compact size, it achieves competitive or superior decomposition quality, with improved robustness to input resolution. Additionally, our learned projections yield interpretable, modality-specific feature spaces.

</details>


### [104] [PhysResponse: Probing the Physics of Video Generation with Controlled Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24419)
*Yuhan Li,Shuran Zhang,Haixin Tian,Baonuo Rao,Weipeng Li,Yujia Hou,Zhenyu Wang,Zijun Wang,Luoyang Lin,Lijing Luo,Jiehui Huang,Kaiyuan Yang,Jingbin Tan,Panwen Hu,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PhysResponse 基准，从"场景条件模拟器反演"和"临界转变事件"两个层面衡量视频生成模型对物理参数（重力、摩擦、初速度）的响应能力；发现现有模型误差高出物理正确视频一个数量级以上，并构建 72,000 条可控参数仿真视频作为训练信号，微调后误差显著下降且可迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型被寄望为"世界模拟器"，不仅要求运动合理，还要求动力学正确响应指定的物理参数。然而现有模型、数据集与基准主要关注物理合理性（plausibility），对"物理参数响应性"（按给定重力、摩擦、初速度生成相应运动）缺乏系统评测与训练信号。

Method: 1) 构建 PhysResponse 基准，含两个互补层级：(a) 场景条件模拟器反演——从生成视频中反推有效重力、摩擦系数与初速度；(b) 事件评估——检验临界转变附近及跨越临界转变时的结果正确性。2) 评测 10 个图像到视频模型。3) 构建 72,000 条仿真视频，覆盖 20 类多物体交互类型且参数受控，用于微调。4) 进一步结合数值编码器继续训练。

Result: 即使误差最低的模型，其参数误差也比物理正确视频高出一个数量级以上；模型很少能同时复现一对临界转变的两种结果；两个层级暴露出互补的失效模式，且 PSNR 等视觉指标无法捕捉。在仿真数据上微调可降低参数误差，并泛化到未见交互类型；同时可迁移至真实视频与外部物理基准。加入数值编码器继续训练后，未见类型上的三项误差均进一步下降。

Conclusion: 当前视频生成模型在物理参数响应性上存在系统性缺陷，且难以被常规视觉指标发现。PhysResponse 提供了评测框架与可扩展的仿真训练数据，为构建"遵循物理条件而非仅外观逼真"的世界模拟器奠定基础。

Abstract: Video generation models are increasingly viewed as world simulators, which requires not only plausible motion but dynamics that respond correctly to specified physical parameters. Existing models, datasets and benchmarks, however, largely target physical plausibility, leaving this physical parameter responsiveness insufficiently explored. We introduce PhysResponse, a benchmark that measures it at two levels: scene-conditioned simulator inversion recovers the effective gravity, friction and initial speed of generated motion, and event evaluation tests outcomes near and across critical transitions. Evaluating ten image-to-video models, we find that even the lowest parameter errors exceed those of physically correct videos by over an order of magnitude, that models rarely reproduce both outcomes of a transition pair, and that the two levels expose complementary failures that visual metrics such as PSNR miss. To supply the missing training signal, we construct 72,000 simulated videos of 20 multi-object interaction types with controlled parameters; fine-tuning on them lowers parameter errors, even on held-out interaction types, and transfers to real videos and an external physics benchmark. Continued training with a numerical encoder further lowers all three errors on held-out types. PhysResponse provides a foundation for world simulators that follow physical conditions, not just appearances.

</details>


### [105] [SCOUT: Risk-Guided Foundation-Model Escalation for Robust Tissue Detection in Whole-Slide Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24366)
*Cristian Tommasino,Antonio M. Rinaldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SCOUT，一种风险引导的级联框架：用轻量审计网络估计缩略图组织分割的误差分布，仅对高风险区域调用冻结的病理基础模型进行选择性细化，再用非对称共识规则融合预测，并以校准的切片级复核分数控制未标记失败的边缘率；在多来源多染色基准上将需修正的病例组比例从38.1%降至18.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全切片图像组织检测中，高平均分割精度会掩盖淡染组织、小碎片与染色异质等失败情形；病理基础模型虽具可迁移表征，但全量推理计算代价高，且易引入假阳性组织预测，因此需要一种既高效又可控的风险感知推理策略。

Method: SCOUT由三部分组成：(1) 轻量审计网络估计初始缩略图组织掩膜的误差空间分布，优先筛选候选区域；(2) 仅对这些区域调用冻结的病理基础模型，控制计算开销；(3) 采用非对称共识规则将基础模型预测与初始掩膜融合，以召回漏检组织同时抑制假阳性；并引入经校准的切片级复核分数，在标定假设下约束未标记病例组失败的边缘率。

Result: 在公开多来源、多染色基准上，需掩膜修正的病例组比例由最佳公开检测器的38.1%降至18.6%，免疫组化场景提升尤为显著；源域留出实验显示其泛化性优于在同一标注上训练的分割网络；选择性推理的观测失败率不高于处理全部候选区域；下游淋巴结分类在使用SCOUT掩膜时，与所评估的三个公开检测器中的两个达到非劣效。基于开放许可基础模型的变体已以含训练权重的可安装Python包形式公开。

Conclusion: 风险引导的基础模型推理是应对异质组织病理数据组织检测的有效范式，兼顾了分割可靠性、计算效率与假阳性可控性，并具备可复现的工程化交付形态。

Abstract: Reliable tissue detection is essential for whole-slide image analysis, yet high average segmentation accuracy can conceal failures involving pale tissue, small fragments, and heterogeneous staining. Pathology foundation models offer transferable representations, but exhaustive inference is computationally demanding and may introduce false-positive tissue predictions. We propose SCOUT (Selective Correction of Uncertain Tissue), a risk-guided cascade that selectively refines an initial thumbnail-based tissue mask. A lightweight audit network estimates the spatial distribution of segmentation errors and prioritizes candidate regions for processing by a frozen pathology foundation model. An asymmetric consensus rule integrates the resulting predictions with the initial mask to recover missed tissue while limiting false positives. A calibrated slide-level review score additionally controls the marginal rate of unflagged case-group failures under the calibration assumptions. Evaluation on a public multi-source, multi-stain benchmark shows that SCOUT reduces the proportion of case groups requiring mask correction from 38.1% for the best released detector to 18.6%, with particularly pronounced improvements in immunohistochemistry. Source-held-out experiments demonstrate better generalization than a segmentation network trained on the same annotations, while selective inference achieves an observed failure rate no higher than processing all candidate regions. Downstream, lymph-node classification with SCOUT masks is non-inferior to that obtained with two of the three released detectors evaluated. A variant built on an openly licensed foundation model is publicly released as an installable Python package with trained weights. These results support risk-guided foundation-model inference as an effective approach to tissue detection across heterogeneous histopathology data.

</details>


### [106] [Can Weight-Space Programming Alter Learned Ethical Priorities?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24330)
*Kai Yi Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究"权重编辑"能否改变模型在"守信承诺"与"惠及更多人"之间的权衡，并在概念监督文本模型与YOLO11n试点上验证了选择性优先级编辑的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望检验能否对模型已学到的价值优先级做"选择性"修改——只改变特定权衡（正式承诺 vs 普通承诺的覆盖门槛），而不破坏其他行为（小差距冲突成功率、事实准确性）。同时指出复用事实答案的评估方式会掩盖权衡差异。

Method: 在概念监督文本模型上直接编辑拟合的选择权重，使推翻正式承诺所需的收益阈值高于普通承诺；在六个独立训练的文本读取器上做目标冲突测试，并设置独立分类器对照；在COCO128上用YOLO11n做偏置编辑试点，并与等价的分数校准对比。

Result: 六个读取器在100%目标普通冲突与83.3%目标正式冲突上均选择较大多数群体（两种顺序皆然）；保留小差距冲突成功率与事实准确性；剩余正式冲突失败与受益人数字读取错误同时出现。YOLO11n试点中小目标匹配由3/43提升至9/43，分数校准可复现保留的检测结果。

Conclusion: 结果属探索性，表明在具有显式权衡机制的模型中可测试对学习优先级的选择性修改；该方法能否在结构较弱的语言模型中发现此类修改仍待研究。

Abstract: We investigate whether a weight edit can alter how a model trades off honoring a commitment against benefiting more people. In a concept-supervised text model, we alter fitted choice weights so that the benefit required to override a formal pledge is larger than for an ordinary promise. Across six independently trained text readers, the edit selects the larger group in both orders on 100% of targeted ordinary conflicts and 83.3% of targeted formal conflicts. It preserves the source model's success rates on smaller conflicts and its factual accuracy. The remaining formal-conflict failures coincide with errors reading a beneficiary number. A separate classifier demonstrates why this distinction matters: reusing a factual answer passes an unconditional choice test but fails to preserve small-gap promises. In a YOLO11n pilot on COCO128, a bias edit increased small-target matches from 3/43 to 9/43, and an equivalent score calibration reproduced the retained detections. These exploratory results show a way to test selective changes to learned priorities in a model with an exposed trade-off mechanism. Whether the method can discover such changes in less structured language models remains open.

</details>


### [107] [End-to-End Landsat Land Surface Temperature Reconstruction with AlphaEarth Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24243)
*Shengjie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出端到端U-Net框架A²E²，融合年温度周期（ATC）、地球系统模式粗分辨率LST与AlphaEarth嵌入，实现Landsat 30 m地表温度重建，MAE 0.78 K、RMSE 1.01 K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Landsat 30 m分辨率的LST数据应用广泛，但云污染导致无法获得空间连续观测，需要有效的重建方法。

Method: A²E²为端到端U-Net：ATC提供物理引导的热先验，粗分辨率LST提供时间背景，U-Net利用观测像元将嵌入场转换为逐日精细尺度校正；并与无空间校正基线及以年均光谱反射率引导的同构U-Net对比。

Result: 在纽约市2022年四景季节性分布Landsat 8/9影像上，MAE 0.78 K、RMSE 1.01 K，较无空间校正基线误差降低60.1%，较年均反射率引导的U-Net降低3.7%；对清晰像元距离与被遮挡像元比例不敏感；ECOSTRESS独立验证表明地球基础模型嵌入优于年均光谱波段。

Conclusion: 融合物理先验、粗分辨率背景与基础模型嵌入可稳定重建云污染下的Landsat LST，地球基础模型嵌入在该任务中优于传统光谱特征。

Abstract: Land surface temperature (LST) data at Landsat's 30 m resolution is important for many applications, but cloud contamination prevents its spatially continuous observations. Here, we propose A$^2$E$^2$, an end-to-end U-Net that integrates annual temperature cycle (ATC), coarse LST from Earth system modeling, and AlphaEarth embeddings for Landsat LST reconstruction. The ATC supplies a physics-guided thermal prior, the coarse LST provides a temporal background, and the U-Net converts the embedding field into day-specific fine-scale correction using observed pixels. Experimental results on four seasonally distributed Landsat 8/9 scenes over New York City in 2022 show that A$^2$E$^2$ achieves 0.78 K mean absolute error and 1.01 K root mean squared error, reducing error by 60.1% relative to baseline method without spatial correction and by 3.7% relative to the same U-Net guided by annual mean spectral reflectance. Sensitivity analysis shows stable performance regardless of distance to clear pixels and percentage of blocked pixels. Independent validation with ECOSTRESS LST shows that Earth foundation model embeddings have better performance than annual mean spectral bands for LST reconstruction.

</details>


### [108] [ViLFlow: Parallel Vision-Language Generation with Continuous Latent Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24240)
*Kevin Zhai,Bian Sun,Zhenyi Wang,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViLFlow是一个连续流式视觉-语言生成模型，将输出嵌入的联合建模与并行token解码相结合，在VQA和图像描述任务上显著优于自回归与掩码扩散基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言生成主要依赖自回归（逐token顺序生成）和离散扩散（每个去噪步独立预测token）两类范式，前者生成效率低、后者缺乏步间连贯的token级依赖。作者希望构建一种连续流模型，兼得嵌入空间联合建模与并行解码的优势。

Method: 提出ViLFlow：将预训练连续语言生成模型适配到VQA与图像描述，采用（1）学习式图像引导——视觉适配的教师模型提供图像引导预测作为额外去噪监督，去噪器直接复现该预测，保证每步生成保留一个图像条件预测；（2）预测感知token解码——用去噪器预测与配对参考文本训练token解码器，将连续输出转为词，解码器可独立适配；（3）在图像描述任务中，解码器于后期去噪阶段提供文本反馈，使已解码词参与后续细化。

Result: 在VQAv2上比自回归和掩码扩散基线最高提升8.46个百分点，在无GQA训练的情况下GQA提升4.50个百分点；COCO Karpathy测试集上CIDEr较掩码扩散提升16.25、较采样自回归提升17.81。

Conclusion: 连续流式建模结合并行token解码可有效用于视觉-语言生成，且解码器可独立适配并反哺去噪过程，为多模态生成提供了自回归与离散扩散之外的高效替代方案。

Abstract: Vision-language generation has predominantly relied on autoregressive and discrete diffusion models. Autoregressive models generate tokens sequentially, while discrete diffusion models typically predict tokens independently within each denoising step. We propose ViLFlow (Vision-Language Flow), a continuous-flow vision-language model that combines joint modeling of output embeddings with parallel token decoding. ViLFlow adapts pretrained continuous language generation to visual question answering and image captioning through learned image guidance and prediction-aware token decoding. A visually adapted teacher provides image-guided predictions as additional denoising supervision. The denoiser learns to reproduce these predictions directly, retaining one image-conditioned prediction per generation step. We train the token decoder on denoiser predictions and paired reference text to improve the conversion of continuous outputs into words. The decoder can be adapted independently, improving answering and captioning without further denoiser training. For captioning, it also supplies text feedback during late denoising, allowing decoded words to inform subsequent refinement. ViLFlow outperforms autoregressive and masked-diffusion baselines by up to 8.46 percentage points on VQAv2 and 4.50 points on GQA without GQA training. On COCO Karpathy test, it improves CIDEr by 16.25 over masked diffusion and 17.81 over sampled autoregression.

</details>


### [109] [WM^MLLM: Turning MLLMs into World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24193)
*Baiang Li,Enze Xie,Jincheng Yu,Jin Wang,Haozhe Liu,Yitong Li,Juanxi Tian,Felix Heide*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 WM^MLLM，首个基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的交互式世界模型：将世界状态外化为带修订历史的全景图，由 MLLM 把用户请求转写为对该状态的显式操作并进行校验，图像/视频生成器仅负责提出视图与呈现，从而实现单图下的持久、可编辑世界生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型把"世界"保存在生成器的上下文里，导致重访同一地点需重新合成，早期编辑会漂移甚至消失，缺乏持久性与可编辑性。作者希望把世界状态从生成器中剥离，实现一致、可累积编辑的世界建模。

Method: 以全景图+修订历史作为生成器之外的世界状态；MLLM 负责三件事：(1) 将每个请求解析为对状态的操作；(2) 选择局部编辑的写入区域；(3) 生成候选后先校验再提交为下一状态。图像生成器只提出视图，视频仅作呈现。支持行走重访、文本/框选编辑物体、放置主体、改变光照天气季节。

Result: 在 MBench-A 上，四项环境恢复指标均值全部最优（Epipolar 91.5 vs 基线 0.1–74.0），相机往返完成率 85.3%（基线最高 50.9%）；对 15 个基线在 CompBench 全部 15 列均最优；112 个盲评局部编辑中，生成后选择写入区域使完成率由 34.8% 提升到 83.0%；258 个 WBench 与 MBench-A 样本中，46 次重访全部精确复现，97 个通过校验的局部编辑全部保持区域外全景像素不变。

Conclusion: 将世界状态外置并由 MLLM 显式操作与校验，可在构造上保证重访一致性与局部编辑的像素级局部性；实验表明该范式在环境恢复、组合语义对齐与编辑成功率上全面优于生成器内隐式记忆的基线，为重访一致的可编辑世界生成提供了新路径。

Abstract: Video world models keep the world in the context of a generator, so a revisit is synthesized again and earlier edits can drift or disappear. We present WM^MLLM, the first interactive world model built on multimodal large language models (MLLMs), for persistent, editable world generation from a single image. WM^MLLM keeps the world state outside every generator, as a panorama with a revision history, and lets the MLLMs write it: they turn each request into operations on this state, select where a local edit is written, and check every candidate before it becomes the next state. Image generators only propose views, and video only presents them. Users can walk to new places and return, edit objects through text or a box, place subjects, and change lighting, weather or season. By construction, a revisit at the same revision reproduces the earlier view exactly, a local edit leaves every panorama pixel outside its region unchanged, and each request starts from the result of the previous one. Against six world model baselines on MBench-A, WM^MLLM has the highest mean on all four environment-recovery metrics (Epipolar 91.5 versus 0.1 to 74.0) and completes the most camera round trips (85.3% versus at most 50.9%). It has the best value in all 15 CompBench columns against 15 baselines. On 112 local edits judged blind, selecting the region to write after generation raises completion from 34.8% to 83.0%. On 258 WBench and MBench-A items, all 46 revisits recover their earlier views, and all 97 local edits that pass the check leave panorama pixels outside their region unchanged.

</details>


### [110] [Monitoring Unplanned Construction Using Satellite Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24190)
*Harsh Kashyap,Taranum Wasu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文探讨用卫星影像与简单机器学习方法检测城市建成区扩张与地表物理变化，在印度以外的两个公开数据集上取得约95%（建成区识别）与约83%（小图对是否变化）的准确率，并对阿格拉48个样本（7个正例）做了初步描述性检验；结论强调影像能反映物理变化，但无法判断建设是否经过规划或许可。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度城市土地需求增长，新建工程改变土地覆被，可能损害自然区域与水体、增加废弃物并加重公共服务负担；NITI Aayog 2021年报告显示7,933个城市聚落中65%没有总体规划，但该数字只反映规划覆盖情况，无法说明单个建筑是否获得授权。因此需要一种能从影像层面识别物理变化的手段，作为规划监管的补充信息源。

Method: 在两个印度以外的公开数据集上测试简单方法：①用预训练神经网络将每幅影像编码为特征向量，再输入标准机器学习分类器，预测土地类别与物理变化；②在标准变化检测基准上训练一个小型神经网络；③对阿格拉样本（48个影像块，其中7个正例）进行初步的描述性核查。整体采用特征提取＋浅层分类器的轻量范式，而非端到端大模型。

Result: 识别建成用地的准确率约为95%；判断一对小影像是否存在变化（每对仅一个标签）的准确率约为83%；阿格拉样本仅作探索性描述，未给出推断性结论。

Conclusion: 卫星影像确实携带有关地表物理变化的信息，简单方法即可获得较高检测精度；但影像只能揭示"是否发生变化"，无法判断建设是否属于规划内或已获许可，即物理变化检测与合法性/合规性判定之间存在本质鸿沟。

Abstract: Cities need land for homes, shops, roads, and public services. New construction changes that land. It can harm natural areas and water bodies, add to waste, and strain public services. NITI Aayog's 2021 report stated that 65% of 7,933 urban settlements in India had no master plan. That number tells us about planning coverage at the time. It does not tell us whether any single building was authorised. Artificial intelligence, which lets computers learn patterns from data, can help find physical changes by comparing satellite images taken on different dates. We test simple methods on two public datasets from outside India. First, we turn each image into a set of numbers using a pretrained neural network, a model already trained to recognise patterns in images. We feed those numbers to standard machine-learning classifiers that predict land categories and physical changes. The methods reach about 95 percent accuracy at spotting built-up land and about 83 percent accuracy at saying whether a small pair of images shows change, where each pair gets one label. We also train a small neural network on a standard change-detection benchmark. Separately, we run a preliminary descriptive check on an Agra sample of 48 patches, 7 of them positive. The results show that satellite images carry information about physical change. They do not show whether construction was planned or permitted.

</details>


### [111] [ViStep-RL: Stepwise Reinforcement Learning for Long-Horizon Visual Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24170)
*Junyang Chen,Yao Qin,Xiao Li,Ningchao Wang,Yiguo Qiao,Wenbin Li,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViStep-RL 将长程视觉智能体的执行建模为由证据支撑子目标构成的依赖图，用子图势能变化作为净进度奖励并结合主动记忆协议，在 ViInteract 上以 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 达到 60.0% 严格完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动感知方法只教智能体"看哪里、怎么看"，而最终答案奖励难以指导"哪些观测满足相互依赖的子目标、哪些证据应被保留复用"，缺少细粒度过程信号。

Method: 将任务执行为依赖链接的证据承载子目标图；验证器判定子目标是否被有效证据支持，以图势能变化构造净进度学习信号；引入主动记忆协议，跨轮保存证据并将检索与下游使用绑定；采用增量任务状态奖励而非累计奖励。

Result: ViInteract 上严格完成率 60.0%（基座 10.0%，SFT 46.5%）；增量奖励比累计奖励高 3.2 点；主动证据写入比自动写入高 7.75 点；同一交互框架下答案准确率 59.5%，比六个通用参考模型中最优者高 24.0 点；留出集在两个 Qwen 规模上均复现增益，并迁移到三个外部视觉推理基准。

Conclusion: 以依赖图上的逐步净进度为奖励信号、以主动证据记忆为状态载体，可显著提升长程视觉任务的完成质量，且增益具跨尺度与跨基准泛化性。

Abstract: Long-horizon visual agents must do more than inspect images: they must turn observations into evidence that remains usable as a task unfolds. Existing active-perception methods teach agents where and how to look, while a final-answer reward gives little structure for deciding which observations satisfy dependent subgoals or should be retained for later use. We present ViStep-RL (Visual Stepwise Reinforcement Learning), which models execution as a dependency-linked graph of evidence-bearing subgoals. A verifier tracks which subgoals are supported by valid evidence, and changes in graph potential provide a net-progress learning signal; an active memory protocol preserves evidence across turns and links retrieval to downstream use. On ViInteract, ViStep-RL reaches 60.0% strict completion with Qwen2.5-VL-7B, compared with 10.0% for the base model and 46.5% after supervised fine-tuning. Controlled studies show that incremental task-state rewards outperform cumulative rewards by 3.2 points, while active evidence writing improves strict completion by 7.75 points over automatic writing. Under a common interaction framework, the task-trained ViStep-RL policy achieves 59.5% answer accuracy, 24.0 points above the strongest of six general-purpose reference models. Held-out episodes confirm the gain at both Qwen scales, and gains extend to three external visual reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [112] [H3Remover: Reinforcing Video Object Removal without Paired Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24633)
*Kangjie Chen,Xiangyu Li,Jinhao Deng,Dongbin Zhang,Chaoda Zheng,Shijia Chen,Yuqi Zhang,Choo Sin Wai,Boyi Sun,Puyi Wang,Yu Zhang,Xianming Liu,Boyang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 H3Remover：在无成对无物体训练目标条件下，通过强化学习为 MiniMax-H3 Base Ref2VA 注入多奖励约束，实现高质量视频物体移除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视频编辑器虽有丰富的移除与补全先验，但易残留目标或改动无关内容；且缺少成对的"无目标"训练真值。

Method: 强化 MiniMax-H3 Base Ref2VA：①目标移除奖励——鼓励与"显露更远内容"一致的标定深度变化，抑制换色等表面编辑；②背景几何奖励——保持相对深度序；③外观奖励——在源图派生的保护区域内约束 RGB 变化，同时豁免目标与估计效应（阴影/反射）；④冻结 HPSv3 提供感知质量反馈。评价区域跨候选固定，用 DiffusionART 在冻结主干上优化 LoRA 适配器。

Result: 相较原始 H3 取得显著重建增益；定性对比显示移除更彻底，周边场景内容保持更好。

Conclusion: 多奖励强化 + LoRA 微调可在无配对监督下有效提升视频物体移除质量，兼顾去除彻底性与背景保真。

Abstract: Pretrained video editors possess rich removal and completion priors, yet may retain targets or alter unrelated content. We present H3Remover, which reinforces MiniMax-H3 Base Ref2VA for video object removal without paired object-free training targets. To discourage superficial edits such as recoloring, a target-removal reward favors calibrated depth changes consistent with revealing farther content. To limit unintended scene changes, a background-geometry reward encourages preservation of relative depth ordering. To preserve background appearance without retaining unwanted shadows or reflections, an appearance reward constrains RGB changes in source-derived protected regions while exempting targets and estimated effects. Frozen HPSv3 complements these constraints with perceptual-quality feedback for synthesized content. Evaluation regions remain fixed across candidates, and DiffusionART optimizes LoRA adapters with the backbone frozen. Experiments show substantial reconstruction gains over original H3, with qualitative comparisons demonstrating more effective object removal and better preservation of surrounding scene content.

</details>


### [113] [ReCoCo: From Patches to Compositions for Visual Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24806)
*Juan Gutiérrez,Saman Zadtootaghaj,José Luis Blanco-Murillo,Sara Atito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReCoCo通过在学习到的视觉原型上对齐patch级预测的“区域组合”，在无需跨视图对应关系或语义分组的前提下将跨视图一致性扩展到区域级别，从而提升稠密特征质量且不引入额外学习参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习已能产生强大的图像级表征，但强稠密特征仍难以学习。增强视图间的一致性提供了强图像级监督，但将该原则推广到局部视觉内容并不直接：现有方法要么跨视图匹配单个patch，要么识别连贯区域，它们引入了对应关系估计的不确定性以及关于视觉内容应如何分组的假设。

Method: 提出ReCoCo（Regional Composition Consistency）。方法不依赖patch对应或语义分组，而是用增强视图间共享内容在所学视觉原型上的patch级预测“组合”来表征，并通过对齐这些组合进行学习，使模型能从包含多个视觉概念的区域中学习而无需预先分离概念。ReCoCo可自然集成进teacher-student自蒸馏框架，且不增加任何额外学习参数。

Result: 大量实验表明，在冻结特征的分割、聚类与区域检索上取得显著提升，同时保持有竞争力的图像级识别性能，缓解了稠密表征与全局表征质量之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 将跨视图一致性提升到区域组合层面是一种无需对应估计与语义分组假设的简单有效方案，可在不增加参数的情况下同时改善稠密与全局表征质量。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning has produced powerful visual representations, yet learning strong dense features remains challenging. While consistency between augmented views provides strong image-level supervision, extending this principle to local visual content is less straightforward. Existing approaches often match individual patches across views or identify coherent regions, introducing uncertainties in correspondence estimation and assumptions about how visual content should be grouped. We introduce ReCoCo (Regional Composition Consistency), a principled approach that extends cross-view consistency to the regional level without requiring such correspondences or semantic grouping. ReCoCo represents the content shared between augmented views through compositions of their patch-level predictions over learned visual prototypes. By aligning these compositions, the model learns from regions containing multiple visual concepts without requiring them to be separated beforehand. ReCoCo integrates naturally with teacher-student self-distillation and introduces no additional learned parameters. Extensive experiments demonstrate substantial improvements in frozen-feature segmentation, clustering, and regional retrieval while maintaining competitive image-level recognition, alleviating the trade-off between dense and global representation quality.

</details>


### [114] [Ultimate3D: Teaching Multimodal LLMs 3D Camera-Object Relations by Large-Scale Visual Instruction Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24795)
*Liu He,Yizhi Song,Ruiqi Xu,Baris Sarper Tezcan,Xiao Zeng,Albert Y. C. Chen,Lu Xia,Shashwat Verma,Sankalp Dayal,Min Sun,Cheng-Hao Kuo,Daniel Aliaga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于3D资产的合成生成流水线，构建大规模3D-grounded视觉指令数据集Ultimate3D（24万VQA+精确相机-物体标注及基准），微调后MLLM在相机-物体关系识别上平均提升29.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM难以准确理解相机-物体关系（物体朝向、相机视角、镜头类型），根源在于训练图像中相机-物体关系多样性不足且缺乏对应文本描述。

Method: 以3D资产为输入，结合渲染与扩散模型生成保真度高、相机-物体关系精确可控的照片级图像；并利用LLM生成文本提示，用于引导视觉指令微调与图像生成控制。据此构建Ultimate3D：包含240K带精确相机-物体标注的VQA数据集及配套benchmark。

Result: 在该数据集上微调的MLLM大幅超越商业模型，相机-物体关系识别任务平均准确率提升29.7%。

Conclusion: 3D-grounded合成数据可有效弥补真实图像中相机-物体关系分布不足，代码、数据集与基准将开放以推动MLLM广泛应用。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) struggle with accurately capturing camera-object relations, especially for object orientation, camera viewpoint, and camera shots. This stems from the fact that existing MLLMs are trained on images with limited diverse camera-object relations and corresponding textual descriptions. To address this, we propose a synthetic generation pipeline to create large-scale 3D visual instruction datasets. Our framework takes 3D assets as input and uses rendering and diffusion-based image generation models to create photorealistic images preserving precise camera-object relations. Additionally, large language models (LLMs) are used to generate text prompts for guiding visual instruction tuning and controlling image generation. We create Ultimate3D, a large-scale 3D-grounded 2D visual instruction dataset. Ultimate3D consists of 240K VQAs with precise camera-object annotations, and corresponding benchmark. MLLMs fine-tuned on our proposed dataset outperform commercial models by a large margin, achieving an average accuracy improvement of 29.7% on camera-object relation recognition tasks. Our code, dataset, and benchmark will contribute to broad MLLM applications.

</details>


### [115] [Jeveration: System One Models Now Generate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24738)
*Yuhe Liu,Qi Li,Shihua Zhang,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Jeveration，将 System One 式并行预测范式用于图像生成：在冻结视觉语言模型上，以共享随机状态协调各位置并行预测，实现单次前向生成高质量图像，并统一文生图、图生图与稠密预测三类任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 System One 类模型（如 Jev）通过快速、并行的预测完成决策；作者希望验证同一种决策范式能否在单次前向传播中生成高质量图像，从而获得远超多步迭代式生成模型的推理效率。

Method: 以冻结的视觉语言模型为骨干，复用其视觉-语义知识进行整体式（holistic）图像生成。核心难点是全局一致性：单一提示对应多张合法图像，并行预测需收敛为协调整体。为此将所有位置条件化于同一共享随机状态以协调并行预测，使每次采样得到不同但一致的图像；由于直接图像监督下模型易忽略该随机状态，先学习离散图像码上的分布来引导单遍生成。进一步在同一架构内统一文生图、图生图与稠密预测，单一权重即可一次前向完成三类任务。

Result: 在 512×512 分辨率下生成耗时 78 ms，比现有统一模型最高快 122 倍，同时保持有竞争力的图像质量与稠密预测性能。

Conclusion: 并行预测式 System One 范式可有效用于高质量图像生成，共享随机状态是保证全局一致性的关键，且单模型单次前向即可覆盖生成与感知多任务，在效率上显著优于迭代式统一模型。

Abstract: System One models such as Jev return decisions through fast, parallel prediction. In this paper, we introduce Jeveration, showing that the same decision paradigm can also generate high-quality images in a single forward pass. Jeveration builds on a frozen vision-language model, repurposing its visual-semantic knowledge for holistic image generation. The key challenge is to maintain global consistency. A single prompt admits many valid images, yet parallel predictions must converge on a coherent whole. We therefore condition all positions on a shared random state to coordinate parallel predictions, so that each draw yields a different but coherent image. Since the model tends to ignore this state under direct image supervision, we first learn a distribution over discrete image codes to guide single-pass generation. Beyond text-to-image generation, we further unify image-to-image generation and dense prediction within the same architecture, so that one checkpoint supports all three tasks, each in one forward pass. Experiments show that Jeveration generates a 512x512 image in 78 ms, up to 122x faster than existing unified models, while maintaining competitive image quality and dense prediction performance.

</details>


### [116] [Improving Zero-shot 3D Semantic Mapping in Offroad with Geometric Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24696)
*Eerik Alamikkotervo,Risto Ojala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出为每个语义类别拟合"球度-高程"分布作为几何先验锚点，仅用视觉基础模型最置信预测点拟合，再与视觉预测融合，提升越野自然场景3D语义建图性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型在结构化场景零样本语义预测准确，但在自然越野环境中性能下降；3D点云在视觉模型失效场景下仍含可判别语义的几何信息，二者的互补性尚未被充分利用。

Method: 对每个语义类别，以球度（sphericity）与高程（elevation）两维特征拟合类别条件分布作为几何锚点；拟合时仅选取视觉基础模型预测置信度最高的点以保证先验质量；随后将该几何先验与视觉基础模型预测进行融合，得到最终语义建图结果。

Result: 在RELLIS-3D上相较SAM3基线提升+6.3 mIoU、+8.9 准确率；在WildScenes上提升+1.7 mIoU、+2.1 准确率。

Conclusion: 利用点云几何统计先验可有效补偿视觉基础模型在非结构化自然场景中的语义退化，方法简洁且具备跨数据集泛化性；代码将在录用后公开。

Abstract: Semantic 3D mapping is a powerful representation that enables platform agnostic mobile robot navigation in challenging offroad conditions. Current vision foundation models can accurately predict semantics zero-shot on structured scenes but performance is compromised in natural environments. On the other hand, 3D point clouds encode valuable information for discriminating semantics in scenarios where vision foundation models fail. In this work, we propose fitting a sphericity-elevation distribution for each semantic class to act as geometric anchor. Only the points with the most confident vision foundation model predictions are considered when fitting to produce high quality geometric prior distributions. The geometric prior is fused with the vision foundation model prediction to produce the final semantic mapping. The proposed prior improves SAM3 baseline by +6.3 in mIoU and +8.9 in accuracy on the RELLIS-3D dataset, and by +1.7 in mIoU and +2.1 in accuracy on the WildScenes dataset. The source code will be made public upon paper acceptance.

</details>


### [117] [CanopyKV: Remember beyond the Crop for Fast Active Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24373)
*Lei Yang,Dayan Wu,Yiliang Zhu,Xinze Liu,Hengjie Zhu,Zihao Zhang,Ding Wang,Peng Fu,Zheng Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练KV压缩框架CanopyKV，利用父子裁剪关系保留互补证据、稳定历史并保护推理状态，在多个基准上实现SOTA准确率与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动视觉推理通过迭代裁剪图像与工具调用积累细粒度证据，但裁剪与推理token不断增长使KV缓存膨胀。现有KV压缩方法基于注意力、时间近因或冗余度选token，无法区分"被局部替换的证据"与"未来探索所需的互补信息"；丢弃前者会损害准确率或引发额外搜索，而打分与缓存重组又会抵消压缩带来的计算收益。

Method: CanopyKV为免训练框架，核心机制有三：1) 利用工具元数据中的父子裁剪关系作为保留信号，无需额外注意力打分即可识别并保留互补证据；2) 保持已保留历史的稳定，避免重新评分；3) 保护持续推理所需的状态；并采用紧凑KV存储降低后续注意力开销。

Result: 在VisualProbe、V*、HR-Bench、MME-Lite上，CanopyKV在所评估的KV压缩方法中取得SOTA答案准确率与端到端效率。MME-Lite上仅保留约Dense总KV的四分之一，准确率提升10.5个百分点，完整推理加速1.64倍。

Conclusion: 以裁剪层级关系为结构化保留信号、避免额外打分与缓存重组的免训练KV压缩，可在显著降低内存与推理成本的同时提升主动视觉推理准确率，兼顾精度与效率。

Abstract: Active visual reasoning enables multimodal models to answer challenging visual questions by gathering fine-grained evidence through iterative image inspection and tool use. However, accumulating crops and reasoning tokens expands the key-value (KV) cache, making compression important for reducing memory and inference costs. Existing KV compression methods typically select historical tokens based on attention, recency, or redundancy. These criteria do not explicitly distinguish locally replaced evidence from complementary information needed for future exploration: each crop refreshes a local region, while earlier views may still support subsequent decisions. Discarding such evidence can impair accuracy or require additional search, while scoring and cache reorganization can offset the computational savings from compression. We introduce CanopyKV, a training-free KV compression framework that uses parent-child crop relationships to retain complementary evidence, keeps retained history stable, and protects the state needed for ongoing reasoning. Tool metadata provides retention signals without additional attention scoring, while compact KV storage reduces subsequent attention costs. Across VisualProbe, V*, HR-Bench, and MME-Lite, CanopyKV achieves state-of-the-art answer accuracy and end-to-end efficiency among the evaluated KV compression methods. On MME-Lite, it retains approximately one-quarter of Dense's total KV while improving accuracy by 10.5 percentage points and accelerating complete reasoning by $1.64\times$.

</details>


### [118] [AnchorUp: Anchoring Feature Upsampling on a Classical Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24296)
*Kotaro Horikawa,Keisuke Toida,Junya Komori,Kazuhiro Hotta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AnchorUp：以无参数经典插值作为锚点，仅用小型引导网络预测插值结果的残差修正，并以单一可学习标量门融合；167,425参数、NAF 1/4预算、64%时延，即可与两阶段训练的NAF持平并在DAVIS-2017上超越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型特征语义强但分辨率粗（patch网格），密集预测需像素级。现有学习式上采样器用交叉注意力从低分特征回归高分特征，但其中大部分内容（平滑区域）无需学习——双线性/双三次插值已可比拟部分学习式上采样器，说明容量被浪费在重建插值即可提供的平滑内容上，真正需要学习的是边缘与细节。

Method: 不再直接预测高分辨率特征图，而只预测相对插值结果的修正量。以参数无关的经典上采样器作anchor产生平滑基底，小型guidance网络给出第二预测，二者经单一可学习标量门加权融合；因锚点承担平滑内容，引导网络只需修正边缘与细节，故网络可极小。

Result: 参数167,425（最强基线NAF的四分之一），时延为其64%。仅在DINOv3上训练、只用NAF两阶段中的第一阶段，在该骨干及另外6个留出骨干上评估，在Pascal VOC与Cityscapes上与两阶段NAF持平；在DAVIS-2017视频目标分割上超越NAF，且提升在每个骨干上均成立，轮廓精度F的提升更大。对学习到的修正的分析证实其集中于边缘与细节。

Conclusion: 将上采样重构为"经典插值锚点+残差修正"可显著压缩模型与训练成本而不损性能，并带来下游密集/视频任务的增益；揭示了学习式上采样器中大量容量被用于冗余重建平滑内容。

Abstract: Vision foundation models produce semantically strong features on a coarse patch grid, and dense prediction needs them at pixel resolution. Existing learned upsamplers close that gap by regressing the high-resolution feature map from the low-resolution one, typically through cross-attention driven by the input image. We observe that most of what they regress needs no learning: plain bilinear interpolation already reproduces the smooth regions of the target, and recent benchmarks even report bilinear and bicubic on par with several learned upsamplers. The rest is mostly edges and fine details, which suggests that these learned upsamplers spend much of their capacity re-computing the smooth content that classical interpolation provides. We therefore introduce AnchorUp, which stops predicting the high-resolution feature map and predicts only its correction from the interpolation. It keeps a parameter-free classical upsampler as an anchor, obtains a second prediction from a small guidance network, and blends the two through a single learnable scalar gate. Because the anchor supplies the smooth content, the guidance network only has to correct what remains. An analysis of the learned correction confirms that it concentrates on edges and fine details, and so the network can be small: AnchorUp uses 167,425 parameters, a quarter of the budget of the strongest baseline, NAF, and runs at 64% of its latency. Trained once on DINOv3 with only the first of NAF's two training stages, and evaluated on that backbone plus six held-out ones, it is already on par with NAF trained through both stages on Pascal VOC and Cityscapes. Our method surpasses it on DAVIS-2017 video object segmentation; the gain holds on every backbone and is larger in contour accuracy F. Code is available at https://github.com/KotaroHorikawa/AnchorUp.

</details>


### [119] [Semantic Guidance for Joint Infrared-Visible Video Fusion and Frame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24277)
*Yushen Xu,Xiaosong Li,Huaming Wu,Dongwei Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAFI方法，利用目标时刻语义引导联合插值与融合，解决红外-可见视频高帧率融合中缺失帧、帧率不匹配、运动遮挡与亮度闪烁问题，在三个数据集上以更低计算量取得更优视觉质量与时序一致性，并保持下游跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高帧率红外与可见视频融合在相机帧率不同或低于目标输出帧率时存在缺失观测。级联"插值+融合"会累积误差并引起亮度波动；运动与遮挡增加内容选择难度；当可见帧缺失时还失去直接的语义引导。

Method: 以目标时刻语义引导联合插值与融合：可见帧率高于红外时直接由可见帧提供语义上下文；无可见观测时以运动引导局部匹配，并通过传播从邻帧恢复语义。语义上下文与目标时刻的对齐图像特征互补，匹配置信度用于筛选可靠的红外与可见信息。各尺度上重建与融合共享源选择，使缺失帧监督可指导融合。为保持可见外观并减少闪烁，对热增强进行约束并惩罚跨帧亮度不一致，同时考虑热对比度变化。

Result: 三个数据集实验表明：视觉质量提升，计算量低于级联基线；输入帧率不一致时具有稳定的时序增益；在存在缺失帧情况下仍保持较强的下游跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 语义引导的联合插值-融合框架可在帧率不匹配与缺失观测条件下同时提升融合视觉质量、时序一致性与效率，并兼容下游任务，代码已开源。

Abstract: Infrared and visible video fusion at high frame rates requires recovering missing observations when camera rates differ or fall below the desired output rate. Cascading interpolation and fusion can accumulate errors and cause brightness fluctuations. Motion and occlusion further complicate content selection, particularly when missing visible frames also remove direct semantic guidance. We propose SAFI, which uses semantics at the target time to guide joint interpolation and fusion. When the visible frame rate exceeds the infrared rate, available visible frames provide semantic context directly. At times without visible observations, motion guides local matching, and propagation recovers semantics from neighboring frames. Semantic context complements aligned image features at the target time, while matching confidence helps select reliable infrared and visible information. Reconstruction and fusion share source selection within each scale, allowing supervision on missing frames to guide fusion. To preserve visible appearance and reduce flicker, we bound thermal enhancement and penalize brightness inconsistencies across frames while accounting for changes in thermal contrast. Experiments on three datasets show improved visual quality with less computation than cascaded baselines and consistent temporal gains when input rates differ. SAFI maintains strong downstream tracking performance despite missing frames. The code is available at https://github.com/XylonXu01/SAFI.

</details>


### [120] [Do Thinking VLMs Need to Keep Looking at Long Videos? Write It Down, Then Look Away](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24259)
*Pengyu Li,Zhitao Gao,Lingling Zhang,Heng Wang,Chengqian Ma,WenHao Wang,Wenxuan Huang,Chen Zheng,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LookAway让视觉语言模型先用带时间戳的笔记记录问题所需信息，随后隐藏视频KV cache，仅凭笔记完成推理，并可通过自蒸馏和强化学习把"看视频"阶段压缩到66-68个token，在同等精度下KV读取量仅为全视频模型的27-35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频VLM推理时，每个解码步都要重读整段视频的KV cache，代价高昂。作者观察到模型对视频的依赖集中在回答早期，因此质疑"推理过程中是否必须一直看视频"。

Method: 提出LookAway：模型首先生成带时间戳的笔记，记下问题所需内容，之后推理引擎将视频KV cache从可见上下文中移除。另在模型自身正确的LookAway回答上做微调以缩短初次观看，再用强化学习在奖励正确答案的同时惩罚KV读取。

Result: 混合注意力与纯注意力VLM中，对视频的依赖均集中于回答早期；在Qwen3.6-27B上，同一步撤回时写笔记比自由推理更能保持精度；换成其他视频笔记会损害精度，而保留视频与自身笔记并存无明显收益。免训练时以0.12-0.13倍建模解码成本接近全视频精度；训练后中位数66-68个token即撤下视频，在三个held-out基准上以27-35%的KV读取量达到匹配精度，笔记token数还多于同等训练的全视频模型。

Conclusion: 长视频推理无需全程保留视频KV cache，先做笔记再撤回视频可大幅降低解码开销而几乎不损精度；结合自训练与KV惩罚的强化学习能进一步压缩观看长度，实现精度与效率的良好折中。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) reason about long videos over hundreds to thousands of tokens. Every decoding step re-reads the key-value (KV) cache of the entire video. Must the model keep looking while it reasons? In LookAway, the model first writes timestamped notes on what the question needs and then puts the video away. Once the notes close, the inference engine hides the video's KV cache for the rest of the response. Across both hybrid and pure-attention VLMs, dependence on the video is concentrated early in the response. On Qwen3.6-27B, writing notes preserves accuracy better than free reasoning at the same withdrawal step. Replacing them with another video's notes harms accuracy, whereas keeping the video visible alongside the model's own notes adds no significant benefit. Without additional training, LookAway nearly matches full-video thinking accuracy at 0.12-0.13 times its modeled decode cost. To shorten this initial look, we fine-tune the model on its own correct LookAway responses. Reinforcement learning then rewards correct answers while penalizing KV reads. The trained model puts the video away after a median of 66-68 tokens. Across three held-out benchmarks, it writes more tokens than an identically trained full-video model yet reads only 27-35% as many KV positions at matched accuracy.

</details>


### [121] [One Shot, Any View: High-Fidelity Video Reshooting with Geometry Guidance and Visual References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24253)
*Kai Huang,Runze Xu,Hui Ren,Taiyi Wu,Xinyuan Li,Xun Gao,Quande Liu,Dong Tang,Tongtong Shen,Xuefeng Su,Alan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 One Shot Any View，输入一段已拍摄视频与用户指定相机轨迹，生成同一表演的“重拍”视频；通过训练-推理一致的训练数据以及几何锚定的 RoPE 偏移，使退化点视频仍可用，并在客观与人工评估中优于现有重拍方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电影场景补拍新视角通常需昂贵重拍。现有几何先验（point video）方法在长焦、浅景深、低调光的人像电影素材上重建质量差，生成器继承退化点视频导致输出质量明显下降。

Method: 以源视频与前馈重建渲染的 point video 为条件，基于预训练 reference-to-video 模型。<br>① 训练数据除 3D 资产渲染视频外，加入真实多机位视频，其 point video 由与推理相同的重建管线生成，保证训练-推理一致，使生成器仅从 point video 取人物与场景的构图、从源视频取外观；<br>② 几何锚定的 RoPE 偏移保持生成视频与源视频时间同步，同时抑制复制点视频的退化外观；<br>③ 支持可选的角色与场景参考图，为源视频未覆盖区域提供外观。

Result: 在客观指标与人工评估中均优于现有重拍（reshooting）方法。

Conclusion: 单一已录制片段即可按任意指定轨迹生成高质量重拍视频，无需实际补拍；训练-推理一致性设计与几何锚定 RoPE 是让退化点视频可用、避免外观退化的关键。

Abstract: Filming a scene from an additional angle after production usually requires a costly reshoot. We present One Shot Any View, a system that takes a recorded clip and generates a reshot video of the same performance along a user-specified camera trajectory. As in recent geometry-grounded methods, the system conditions generation on the source video and an explicit 3D prior, namely a point video rendered along the requested trajectory from a feed-forward reconstruction of the source video. We target human-centric cinematic videos, which are often shot with long lenses, shallow depth of field, and low-key lighting, all of which degrade feed-forward reconstruction. Existing geometry-grounded methods tend to inherit the degraded point video, and their output quality drops visibly. Two designs make such point videos usable. First, in addition to rendered videos of 3D assets, we train on real-world multi-camera videos, whose point videos are produced by the same reconstruction pipeline as at inference. This training-inference consistency enables the generator to take only the composition of people and scene elements from the point video, and their appearance from the source video. Second, a geometry-anchored RoPE offset keeps the generated video temporally synchronized with the source while largely preventing the output from copying the degraded appearance of the point video. Meanwhile, since the generator is a pretrained reference-to-video (R2V) model, the system also accepts optional character and scene reference images, which supply the appearance of regions that the source never shows. In both objective and human evaluations, One Shot Any View outperforms existing reshooting methods.

</details>


### [122] [TrajWAM: Trajectory-Conditioned Future Learning for Vision-Language Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24192)
*Xuerui Fang,Gulimila Kezierbieke,Chen Wangxing,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TrajWAM：轨迹条件化的世界动作模型，以自车轨迹为查询、通过辅助 BEV 未来预测学习增强视觉-语言规划，在 Bench2Drive 全部 220 条路线上取得 91.53 DS / 75.45% SR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型需将未来场景预测与动作规划耦合，而每条驾驶记录天然提供"执行轨迹—观测未来"的事实配对，可作为监督信号；但如何在视觉-语言规划器中有效利用这一信号尚待解决。

Method: 轨迹条件化 WAM：以自车轨迹查询条件化共享规划器表征，预测与 DINOv3 对齐的 BEV 未来特征。训练含两项目标：(1) 执行轨迹下的事实重建；(2) 无标签扰动轨迹下的单边特征分离。推理时原生直接生成路点，绕过辅助未来预测分支。

Result: 在 Bench2Drive 全部 220 条路线上，驾驶分数 91.53、成功率 75.45%，较调度匹配的仅规划微调基线提升 5.31 DS 分、多 8 条成功路线。

Conclusion: 辅助 BEV 未来学习可切实提升视觉-语言驾驶规划性能，且不增加推理开销；单边特征分离提供了对扰动轨迹的有效判别约束。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) connect future scene prediction with action planning for autonomous driving. Each driving record pairs an executed ego trajectory with its observed future, providing a factual outcome for learning this connection. We present TrajWAM, a trajectory-conditioned WAM that improves vision-language driving through auxiliary bird's-eye-view (BEV) future learning. An ego-trajectory query conditions DINOv3-aligned BEV predictions from shared planner representations. Training combines factual reconstruction under the executed trajectory with one-sided feature separation under an unlabeled perturbed trajectory. Together, these objectives train the shared planner to generate driving waypoints. Native inference directly generates waypoints and bypasses the auxiliary future-prediction branch. On all 220 Bench2Drive routes, TrajWAM achieves 91.53 Driving Score and 75.45% Success Rate, exceeding a schedule-matched planning-only fine-tuning baseline by 5.31 Driving Score points and eight successful routes.

</details>


### [123] [ART: Bridging the Embodiment Gap in 3D Human-to-Robot Affordance Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24182)
*Zichen Zhang,Wei Zhai,Xuji Zhang,Yang Cao,Yu Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"3D人-机器人可供性迁移"新任务，构建HPAD配对数据集（2,137物体/80类/13动作原语/27,000+对象-动作对），并设计ART网络将人类先验重构为机器人可执行的可供性分布，真机成功率86.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D可供性方法直接把人类先验当作参考，忽视了可供性并非物体内在属性、而与智能体具身结构耦合；人手与机器人末端执行器在结构和形态上的差异，导致直接迁移产生严重不兼容。

Method: 1) 定义3D Human-to-Robot Affordance Transfer任务，处理具身结构差异导致的可供性错位；2) 构建HPAD基准，每个物体同时标注人类先验标签与仿真验证的机器人标签，覆盖80类、13种原子动作原语、27,000+对象-动作对；3) 提出ART（3D Affordance tRansfer neTwork）：先用可靠性门控流过滤人类先验中的几何假阳性，再通过层次化重构与图扩散细化重建出机器人可执行分布，以解决人-机可供性的拓扑不匹配。

Result: 在HPAD上验证了任务设定的可靠性及ART的优越性；在6自由度机械臂+平行夹爪上进行2,400次执行基准（12物体、4类动作），ART在480次试验中取得86.5%的最佳成功率。

Conclusion: 可供性应被视为具身相关而非物体内在属性；通过配对数据监督与显式几何/拓扑重构，可实现从人类先验到机器人可执行可供性的有效迁移，具备真机实用性。

Abstract: 3D affordance acts as a critical link between perception and action, providing priors for robotic manipulation. While existing approaches predominantly leverage human priors as direct references, they often overlook that affordance is not an object-intrinsic property but is inherently coupled with the agent's embodiment. Due to the structural and morphological disparities between human hands and robot end-effectors, directly applying human priors causes severe incompatibility for robotic execution. Motivated by this, we introduce a novel task setting, 3D Human-to-Robot Affordance Transfer, which addresses the affordance misalignment arising from distinct embodiment structures. To support this task, we construct the Human-Robot Paired Affordance Dataset (HPAD), the first large-scale 3D affordance benchmark in which every object is annotated with both human-prior and simulation-validated robot labels. HPAD covers 2,137 objects across 80 categories and 13 atomic action primitives, yielding over 27,000 object-action pairs. On top of HPAD, we propose the 3D Affordance tRansfer neTwork (ART). ART first filters geometric false positives in the human prior via a reliability gating stream, then reconstructs the prior into a robot-actionable distribution through hierarchical reconstruction and a graph-diffusion refinement that resolves the topological mismatch between human and robot affordances. Experiments on HPAD demonstrate the reliability of the proposed task and the superiority of ART. We further validate ART on a 6-DoF arm with a parallel-jaw gripper in a 2,400-execution benchmark across 12 objects and 4 action categories; ART attains the best success rate (86.5% over its 480 trials), confirming the practicality of the transfer paradigm. Project page: https://derek0907.github.io/ART/

</details>


### [124] [Gaussian Primitive Fusion for Any-Resolution Multimodal Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24164)
*Baoshuo Cai,Xinrui Jiang,Bing Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出高斯基元融合(GPF)，将异构分辨率多模态融合重构到共享连续高斯空间中，直接以各模态原生分辨率拟合，避免重采样伪影，并在最细原生分辨率上渲染融合结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有融合方法在统一的离散网格上估计模态贡献，当源模态原生分辨率不同时需重采样构建公共网格，易引入伪影并影响后续融合决策。

Method: GPF在共享连续高斯空间中重新表述异构分辨率融合：不先将源图像转为统一分辨率，而是用共享高斯几何与模态特定内容表示直接拟合原生分辨率观测，并在所得连续表示中学习模态贡献；优化后在最细原生分辨率渲染融合表示，全程不重采样源图像。

Result: 在异构分辨率输入下，GPF相比"先重采样再融合"流程产生更清晰的目标轮廓与更精细纹理，模糊与块状伪影更少；在多个红外-可见光基准上定量优于配备插值或超分前端的先进融合方法。

Conclusion: 以连续高斯表示替代离散公共网格，可从根本上规避重采样伪影，为异构分辨率多模态融合提供有效范式。

Abstract: Multimodal image fusion integrates complementary observations from different modalities into a unified representation. Existing methods typically estimate modality contributions on a common discrete grid. When source modalities have different native resolutions, constructing such a grid generally involves resampling, which may introduce artifacts and affect subsequent fusion decisions. To address this problem, we propose Gaussian Primitive Fusion (GPF), which reformulates heterogeneous-resolution fusion in a shared continuous Gaussian space. Instead of first converting source images to a common resolution, GPF directly fits their native-resolution observations with shared Gaussian geometry and modality-specific content representations, and learns modality contributions within the resulting continuous representation. Once optimized, the fused representation is rendered at the finest native resolution without resampling the sources. With heterogeneous-resolution inputs, GPF produces clearer target contours and finer textures, with less blurring and blocking artifacts than resample-then-fuse pipelines. Quantitatively, it achieves the best results on multiple infrared-visible benchmarks against state-of-the-art fusion methods equipped with interpolation or super-resolution frontends.

</details>


### [125] [AVTR: Adaptive Multimodal Reasoning with Visual Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24161)
*Xiaoqian Shen,Yunyang Xiong,Changsheng Zhao,Saksham Suri,Chong Zhou,Zechun Liu,Zhipeng Cai,Zhuang Liu,Qi Qian,Wei Wen,Hongyu Xu,Hanxian Huang,Ernie Chang,Yanbei Chen,Vikas Chandra,Raghuraman Krishnamoorthi,Mohamed Elhoseiny,Lemeng Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自适应视觉-文本推理（AVTR），以视觉想象作为文本推理的互补模态，并通过可学习路由按输入自适应选择推理模态与深度，在16个基准上平均提升4.5分且延迟降低33%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型主要依赖文本推理，存在两点局限：(1) 纯文本推理削弱视觉中心任务的感知能力，难以准确解读与任务相关的视觉证据；(2) 对所有输入统一使用文本推理，忽略了推理模态与推理深度的题目依赖性，在逻辑需求较低时产生冗长无用的推理链。

Method: AVTR引入"视觉想象"作为补充推理模态：模型通过生成式视觉反思重新审视视觉观察并蒸馏显著证据；同时将推理模态选择建模为可学习路由问题，使单一策略能根据输入的感知与推理需求，在视觉反思与文本推理之间自适应分配。

Result: 在16个基准上，AVTR相较Qwen3.5基线平均提升4.5分，同时推理延迟降低33%；并可有效迁移到更小规模主干，性能优于所评估的2B级基线。

Conclusion: 推理模态与深度应按输入自适应分配，视觉反思与文本推理具有互补性；统一推理范式并非最优，模态路由可在提升准确率的同时显著降低推理开销。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models increasingly rely on textual reasoning to solve advanced multimodal tasks. Despite its effectiveness, this paradigm faces two fundamental limitations. First, relying on textual reasoning alone can limit perceptual ability in vision-centric questions, where accurate answers depend on interpreting task-relevant visual evidence. Second, applying textual reasoning uniformly across inputs overlooks question-dependent variation in both reasoning modality and depth, often producing verbose and unnecessary traces when little logical inference is required. To this end, we propose Adaptive Visual-Textual Reasoning (AVTR), which introduces visual imagination as a complementary reasoning modality to textual thinking, enabling the model to revisit its visual observations and distill the salient evidence through generative visual reflection. Furthermore, we formulate reasoning-modality selection as a learned routing problem, allowing a single policy to adaptively harness the complementary strengths of visual reflection and textual reasoning in response to the perceptual and inferential demands of the input. Across 16 benchmarks, AVTR improves the average score by 4.5 points over Qwen3.5 baseline, while reducing inference latency by 33\%. It also scales effectively into a smaller size backbone, outperforming the evaluated 2B-scale baselines.

</details>


### [126] [Gaspard: Expressive unified speech and gesture synthesis with continuous autoregressive flow-matching and decoupled classifier-free guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24862)
*Téo Guichoux,Théodor Lemerle,Catherine Pelachaud,Nicolas Obin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一语音-手势生成模型 Gaspard，基于连续自回归与 next-step flow-matching，并在两大模态上解耦分类器无引导以平衡影响、避免动态坍缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类交流中语音与共语手势高度协同，但现有方法多采用级联流水线：先生成语音再用其驱动手势，导致语音生成与手势相互独立，难以建模真正的双向协调。

Method: 构建统一模型 Gaspard，采用连续自回归生成配合 next-step flow-matching 预测，在 Seamless Interaction 大规模多模态数据集上训练；针对两模态异构时间特性设计模态专属预测头，并对两条生成流施加解耦的分类器无引导（CFG）。

Result: 客观与感知评测表明 Gaspard 相对单模态基线是强手势生成模型，且相较匹配的级联系统产生高度同步的语音与手势；解耦引导在提升表现力的同时缓解连续自回归手势生成的动态坍缩。

Conclusion: 统一生成范式优于级联式方案，模态专属预测头与解耦引导是协调异构时间特性、兼顾同步性与动态质量的关键设计。

Abstract: Human communication involves highly coordinated multimodal signals such as speech and co-speech gestures. Existing approaches for generating both modalities commonly follow a cascaded pipeline, where speech is generated first and subsequently used to drive gesture generation; speech generation is therefore independent of gestures. We introduce Gaspard, a unified speech--gesture generation model based on continuous autoregressive generation with next-step flow-matching predictions, and trained on Seamless Interaction, a large-scale multimodal dataset. The heterogeneous temporal properties of speech and gesture motivate modality-specific prediction heads and decoupled classifier-free guidance over the two generation streams. This guidance rebalances the influence of speech and gesture at inference time, improving expressivity while mitigating dynamics collapse in continuous autoregressive gesture generation. Objective and perceptual evaluations show that Gaspard is a strong gesture generation model against unimodal baselines and that it produces highly synchronous speech and gestures compared to a matched cascaded system.

</details>


### [127] [Does the Action Matter? Probing Action-Conditioned Semantic Forecasting as a World Model for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24840)
*Binay Kumar Singh,Niels Da Vitoria Lobo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究动作条件语义预测中的"动作依赖性"，通过测试时动作干预并用标准分割指标评分，发现预测精度提升并不代表模型强依赖动作：正确动作仅小幅提升mIoU，错误动作则大幅下降甚至低于无动作模型；真实数据上特征级条件化比输入级条件化对动作的依赖强约1.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶世界模型评估聚焦于预测保真度与规划性能，很少验证预测是否真正依赖所条件的自车动作，缺乏对动作条件化有效性的直接检验。

Method: 在动作条件语义预测任务（由历史语义分割与自车动作预测未来语义分割）中，将带特征级与输入级动作条件化的预测器与结构相同但无动作输入的模型对比；通过测试时动作干预（替换为错误动作）并以标准分割指标（mIoU）打分量化动作依赖性；在动作动力学已知的合成环境中验证评估方法，再应用于Cityscapes与BDD100K，并做数据集内及跨两个车辆平台的迁移评估。

Result: 执行动作最多提升中期mIoU：Cityscapes +2.5点、BDD100K +0.6点；错误动作使其下降最多23.2与7.0点，低于无动作输入模型。真实数据上特征级条件化在相近精度下对动作的依赖约为输入级的1.6倍，而合成环境中两者依赖相当（因其历史不决定动作）。

Conclusion: 精度增益只能说明驾驶世界模型使用了动作信息，但不能反映其预测对动作的依赖强度；需以测试时动作干预评估动作依赖性，且条件化方式（特征级vs输入级）显著影响依赖程度。

Abstract: World models are increasingly used in autonomous driving to predict how a scene evolves under the ego vehicle's actions, supporting counterfactual reasoning and model-based planning. However, existing evaluations focus on prediction fidelity and planning performance, and rarely verify that the predictions depend on the conditioning action. In this paper, we study action dependence in action-conditioned semantic forecasting, which predicts future semantic segmentation from past segmentations and the ego action. We compare a forecaster with feature-wise and input-level action conditioning against an identical model without action input, and measure action dependence with test-time action interventions scored by standard segmentation metrics. The evaluation is validated in a synthetic environment with known action-dependent dynamics and applied to Cityscapes and BDD100K, both within each dataset and across the two vehicle platforms. Experiments show that the executed action improves mid-term mIoU by at most 2.5 points on Cityscapes and 0.6 points on BDD100K, whereas a wrong action reduces it by up to 23.2 and 7.0 points, below the model without action input. On real data, feature-wise conditioning depends 1.6 times more on the action than input-level conditioning at similar accuracy, whereas both depend on it equally in the synthetic environment, where the history does not determine the action. These results show that an accuracy gain indicates that a driving world model uses its action, but not how strongly its forecasts depend on it. Code is available at https://github.com/binayksingh/does-action-matter.

</details>


### [128] [Edge-Efficient Joint RAW Image Compression and Restoration for Smart Wearable Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24805)
*Yunong Sun,Sicheng Chen,Fei Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向智能穿戴设备的非对称RAW压缩-复原联合框架：轻量端侧编码器将有噪RAW压缩为码流，高容量后端解码器联合去噪与去马赛克，并以知识蒸馏降低端侧算力；在约0.25 bpp下SIDD PSNR较级联基线提升1.5 dB以上，蒸馏编码器较端侧NAFNet获27.6× NPU推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AR眼镜等智能穿戴设备依赖相机感知环境，但传感器紧凑导致RAW噪声大，且端侧算力与功耗受限难以高质量复原。传统两种方案各有缺陷：直接卸载复原会使RAW传输开销大，先压缩再传输则易将比特率浪费在保留传感器噪声上。二者存在根本性失配，故需在压缩时就以"干净图像重建"为目标优化传输表示，同时降低端侧计算与传输成本且不牺牲画质。

Method: 提出非对称联合RAW压缩与复原框架：端侧轻量编码器将有噪RAW测量压缩为比特流；后端高容量解码器对编码表示联合执行去噪与去马赛克，直接优化传输表示以利干净图像重建。为进一步压缩算力预算，采用知识蒸馏训练更小的编码器。在真实与合成数据上与强级联压缩-复原流水线及学习式压缩基线对比，并在商用智能眼镜采集的真实12MP RAW上验证，测量智能手机与眼镜硬件的发送端时延。

Result: 在真实与合成数据上，速率-复原性能优于强级联压缩-复原流水线和学习式压缩基线。SIDD数据集上，约0.25 bpp时PSNR较级联基线提升超过1.5 dB。真实12MP RAW（商用智能眼镜）验证有效；蒸馏编码器在256×256输出块上相较端侧NAFNet实现27.6×的NPU推理加速。

Conclusion: 以非对称压缩-复原联合优化替代"先压缩后复原"或"直接卸载"的范式，可有效缓解端侧算力/功耗与传输成本同画质之间的矛盾；知识蒸馏使端侧编码器在保持复原质量的同时大幅降低推理开销，适配智能眼镜等资源受限可穿戴平台。

Abstract: Smart wearable devices, such as augmented reality glasses, increasingly rely on cameras to understand and interact with their surroundings. However, their compact sensors often capture noisy RAW images, while limited compute and power make high-quality on-device restoration difficult. This creates an emerging need to reduce both local computation and transmission cost without sacrificing image quality. Two common solutions are to offload restoration or to compress the raw data before transmission. The first makes RAW transmission costly; the second reduces the payload but can spend bitrate preserving sensor noise. We address this fundamental mismatch with an asymmetric framework for joint RAW compression and restoration that directly optimizes the transmitted representation for clean image reconstruction. A lightweight on-device encoder compresses noisy RAW measurements into a bitstream, while a higher-capacity backend decoder jointly denoises and demosaics the encoded representation. We use knowledge distillation to train a smaller encoder for tighter compute budgets. Experiments on real and synthetic data show improved rate-restoration performance over strong cascaded compression-restoration pipelines and learned compression baselines. On SIDD, our method improves PSNR by more than 1.5 dB over cascaded baselines at approximately 0.25 bits per pixel. We further validate the method on real 12 MP RAW images captured with commercial smart glasses and measure sender-side latency on both smartphone and smart-glasses hardware. The distilled encoder achieves a 27.6$\times$ NPU inference speedup over on-device NAFNet for 256$\times$256 output patches. The code is available at https://github.com/coolsyn2000/CRAW

</details>


### [129] [Training-free motion statistics match self-supervised behavior encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24792)
*Ziwen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文质疑自监督动物行为编码器是否真正优于手工运动特征，提出一种免训练的通用逐帧测量+PCA表征，并在预注册协议下与BAMS、hBehaveMAE等编码器公平比较，结果显示该简单基线在多个基准上普遍领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动物行为自监督编码器通常在手工构建的运动特征上训练，或直接以这些特征为输入；但它们是否真正"打败"这些特征此前缺乏公平对比，作者因此希望在同等表征维度、同一分类器的条件下进行严格比较。

Method: 构建免训练表征：通用的逐帧测量在固定时间窗口上汇总，再用PCA降维；与重新训练及已发布的编码器（BAMS、hBehaveMAE）对比，遵循预注册协议——冻结表征、等维度、共享单一分类器；用三个随机种子重训编码器，并测试原始关键点（各身体自身坐标系、同样窗口汇总）的消融。

Result: CalMS21上达85.8 macro F1（最佳编码器82.4），在仅四个标注视频时持平，在标注者更换或少样本学习新行为时领先；MABe22小鼠三元组83.7 vs hBehaveMAE 80.3（+3.4，95%区间+2.1~+4.7）；MABe22果蝇分组50个任务平均F1 51.6 vs BAMS 48.2；Shot7M2上68.8 vs hBehaveMAE 64.7（后者在最短动作上仍更优）。短时间窗口贡献了大部分性能。原始关键点在两个小鼠数据集和Shot7M2上已可匹配或超越编码器，果蝇数据上不足，测量特征额外带来5.3点提升。

Conclusion: 在这些基准上，当前行为编码器尚未超越其所学习的手工特征，此类基线应与编码器一并报告。

Abstract: Self-supervised encoders for animal behavior are often trained on, or fed, hand-built movement features. We ask whether they beat those ingredients when compared fairly. A training-free representation, made of generic per-frame measurements summarized over fixed time windows and reduced with PCA, is compared with retrained and released encoders (BAMS, hBehaveMAE) under a preregistered protocol: frozen representations of equal size and one shared classifier. On CalMS21 it reaches 85.8 macro F1 against 82.4 for the best encoder, ties with four labelled videos, and leads when the annotator changes or new behaviors must be learned from a few videos; the reading holds for encoders retrained with three seeds. On the MABe22 mouse triplets it scores 83.7 against 80.3 for the released hBehaveMAE (gap +3.4, 95% interval +2.1 to +4.7). On a second species, the MABe22 fly groups, the same recipe scores 51.6 mean F1 over 50 tasks against 48.2 for BAMS, the best published method. On Shot7M2, the synthetic basketball benchmark built by hBehaveMAE's authors, it scores 68.8 against 64.7 for hBehaveMAE, the best method in that paper, though hBehaveMAE remains better on the shortest movements. Short time windows carry most of the result. Raw keypoints in each body's own frame, summarized over the same windows, already match or beat the encoders on both mouse datasets and on Shot7M2; on the fly groups they fall short, and the measurements add 5.3 points. On these benchmarks, current behavior encoders have yet to beat the hand-built features they learn from, and such baselines should be reported alongside them.

</details>


### [130] [Explainable and CPU-Efficient Brain Tumor Classification from MRI Using Two-Phase EfficientNetB3 Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24440)
*Deepro Das,Saad Kabir Uddin,Mohammad Alauddin,Bornali Sarker,Thasnim Akhter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于EfficientNetB3迁移学习与自定义分类头的四分类脑MRI自动诊断流程，采用"冻结主干训练→顶层37层低学习率微调"两阶段策略，独立测试集上达90.62%总体准确率与0.9854宏平均AUC，并集成Grad-CAM实现可解释性，兼顾高精度与仅CPU训练的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑肿瘤为高致死率神经系统疾病（全球年新增逾30.8万例），MRI肿瘤分型对治疗规划至关重要；但人工判读耗时且存在观察者间差异。既有工作往往只关注单一维度，缺乏同时兼顾高精度、仅CPU可用算力与临床可解释性的方案。

Method: 以EfficientNetB3为骨干迁移学习，接自定义分类头完成胶质瘤、脑膜瘤、无肿瘤、垂体瘤四分类。两阶段训练：阶段一冻结主干仅训练分类头（最佳验证准确率92.77%）；阶段二以降低100倍的学习率选择性微调顶部37层（最佳验证准确率94.91%）。采用Grad-CAM生成类判别性热力图以提供可视化解释。

Result: 在包含1600张MRI图像的独立均衡测试集上，系统总体准确率90.62%，宏平均AUC为0.9854；两阶段验证准确率分别为92.77%与94.91%；Grad-CAM输出有效突出与类别相关的关键病灶区域。

Conclusion: 所提系统在准确率、仅CPU计算效率与临床可解释性三个维度上同时取得可行表现，弥补了既有研究在多目标兼顾上的空白，具备作为脑肿瘤MRI辅助诊断工具的潜力。摘要未披露外部多中心验证与前瞻性临床评估，泛化性仍有待进一步检验。

Abstract: Brain tumors are among the most lethal neurological disorders, with over 308,000 new diagnoses annually worldwide. Accurate classification of tumor type from Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) scans is critical for treatment planning; however, manual radiological interpretation is time-consuming and prone to inter-observer variability. This paper proposes an automated deep learning pipeline for four-class brain MRI classification (glioma, meningioma, no tumor, and pituitary tumor) using EfficientNetB3 transfer learning with a custom classification head. A two-phase training strategy is adopted: Phase 1 trains the head with a frozen backbone (best validation accuracy: 92.77%), and Phase 2 selectively fine-tunes the top 37 layers at a 100x reduced learning rate (best validation accuracy: 94.91%). On an independent balanced test set of 1,600 MRI images, the system achieves 90.62% overall accuracy and a macro-average AUC of 0.9854. Gradient-weighted Class Activation Mapping (Grad-CAM) is integrated for class-discriminative visual explainability. The proposed system simultaneously addresses three dimensions absent in prior work: high accuracy, CPU-only computational efficiency, and clinical interpretability.

</details>


### [131] [Let a Small Model Read First: Faster Prefill for Long Interleaved Image-Text Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24431)
*Xiaolin Lin,Kai-Chiang Wu,Bing Li,Grace Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SkimPrefill：用小混合模型（Qwen3.5-0.8B）以半分辨率读完整个长交错输入，仅用早期层的一个注意力头对图像/段落等整体单元打分，保留 10% token 作为短提示交给目标模型，从而将长上下文 VLM 的 prefill 加速 3.7–11.6 倍且保持平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长交错输入（多页文档、数百张图像，约 100K token）下 VLM 的 prefill 占据首 token 等待时间的主要部分。常见做法是用目标模型自身或同族小模型先写答案再给 token 打分来剪枝，但打分本身耗时接近剪枝所节省的时间，抵消了收益。

Method: 关键观察：回答问题所需的证据集中在少数整体单元（整张图像或整段文字）中，且小混合模型某一早期层的一个注意力头即可定位这些单元，即使图像为半分辨率。据此 SkimPrefill 让小型混合模型（Qwen3.5-0.8B）先读到该早期层并处理全部输入（图像降为半分辨率；其多数层为线性复杂度，故该遍历很短），再用该注意力头对每个单元排序，将得分最高的单元拼成普通较短提示交给目标模型。该选择器无需训练，可跨模型家族通用。

Result: 在 128K token 的 MMLongBench（19 个数据集、5 类任务）上，保留 10% 输入 token 时，SkimPrefill 使 Qwen3.5-9B、Gemma-4-12B、Qwen3-VL-32B 的 prefill 分别加速 3.7 倍、11.6 倍和 8.2 倍，同时维持其平均准确率。

Conclusion: 以低成本小混合模型做单元级、单头驱动的证据筛选，可在不训练选择器、不依赖目标模型家族的情况下显著降低长上下文 VLM 的 prefill 开销，且精度无明显损失。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can answer questions about long interleaved inputs, such as documents of many pages and collections of hundreds of images, but they are slow to start: on inputs of about 100K tokens, the prefill takes most of the wait for the first output token. A common approach prunes the input by scoring its tokens with the target model itself or with a small model of the same family that first writes an answer. However, this scoring takes much of the time that pruning saves. We find that the evidence for a question lies in a few whole units (images or paragraphs), and that a small hybrid model finds them with one attention head of an early layer, even at half image resolution. Based on these findings, we propose SkimPrefill, which lets the small hybrid model read the whole input first, up to that early layer, with images at half resolution. Most layers of the small hybrid model (Qwen3.5-0.8B) run in linear time, so this pass is short. SkimPrefill then ranks every unit with that head and gives the target the top units as an ordinary shorter prompt. One untrained selector serves targets of different model families. Extensive experiments on MMLongBench at 128K tokens, with 19 datasets of five task types, show the effectiveness of SkimPrefill. With 10% of the input tokens kept, SkimPrefill speeds up the prefill of Qwen3.5-9B, Gemma-4-12B and Qwen3-VL-32B by 3.7 times, 11.6 time and 8.2 times while maintaining their average accuracy.

</details>


### [132] [DAL: Dynamic Angular Loss for Imbalanced Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24597)
*Salman Mohammad,Furkan Kasım,Manuel Günther*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对医学图像分类中类别严重不平衡问题，本文推导角度softmax尺度参数与角度间隔的数据驱动上下界，提出批自适应角度间隔(BAAM)与动态加权交叉熵(DWCE)组合成的动态角度损失(DAL)，在四个医学基准上取得最优平衡准确率和最低的每类召回变异系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分类中少数但临床重要的类别常被多数类压制。现有角度softmax(ABS)损失虽判别性强，但ABS内在参数估计依赖"类别数多"的假设，在小类别数且严重不平衡的医学场景下不成立，其行为缺乏理解。

Method: 基于负角度统计量推导尺度参数的数据驱动上下界与角度间隔的上界；据此构建批自适应角度间隔(BAAM)，并与动态加权交叉熵(DWCE)结合，形成动态角度损失(DAL)。

Result: 在皮肤科、眼科和胃肠内窥镜四个高度不平衡医学图像分类基准上，平衡准确率持续领先，且每类召回率的变异系数最低；多数类性能略有下降，但少数类收益显著。

Conclusion: DAL可在医学不平衡场景下提供可靠且公平的整体分类器，突破了ABS对类别数假设的依赖。代码已开源。

Abstract: Class imbalance remains a major obstacle in medical image classification, where rare but clinically important classes are often overshadowed by majority categories. Although current strategies for reducing bias, including angle-based softmax (ABS) losses, offer strong discriminative features, their behavior in settings with a small number of severely imbalanced classes remains insufficiently understood. Previous approaches that estimate the intrinsic parameters of ABS rely on the assumption of many classes, which does not hold in medical settings. We derive data-driven upper and lower bounds for the scale parameter and an upper bound for the angular margin using negative-angle statistics, and use these to drive a batch-adaptive angular margin (BAAM), which we combine with dynamically-weighted cross entropy (DWCE) to define our dynamic angular loss (DAL). Across four highly imbalanced medical image classification benchmarks spanning dermatology, ophthalmology, and gastrointestinal endoscopy, our method consistently achieves superior balanced accuracy and the lowest coefficient of variation of per-class recall. Although this entails a small performance reduction on majority classes, the gains on minority categories are substantial, yielding a reliable and equitable overall classifier. Our source code is available at: https://github.com/msalman95/DAL.

</details>


### [133] [BaseRSI: Recursive Self-Improvement of a Base Vision-Language Model via Training-Free Harness Internalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24578)
*Mingda Zhang,Yuanhao Dong,Qiang Huang,Rui Qian,Yanjin Li,Zhenyu Liu,Xiao Huang,Zihao Yang,Jian Wang,Pinjia He,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BaseRSI提出"递归脚手架内化"新范式：基座模型先改进其harness，再通过免训练权重编辑把harness带来的增益写进自身权重；配套ReGap机制以逐block高维激活差做监督，ReKey算法用回归把该信号写入各block的关键KV神经元，并在自身响应上测量编辑位点与强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RSI存在三大问题：(1) gains被harness绑定，增益停留在模型之外、无法进入基座权重；(2) 黑盒监督只读取模型输出，信号粒度粗；(3) 编辑的位点与强度预先固定，不够精确。

Method: 提出Recursive Harness Internalization范式：基座模型改进harness后，将harness增量通过免训练编辑写回自身权重，多轮迭代于自身响应上进行。机制ReGap：以harness在单个transformer block处产生的高维激活差作为该block的监督信号，并在低维子空间内沿该信号更新block权重。算法ReKey：通过回归将该信号写入每个block的少量key-value神经元，并在模型自身响应上测量每次编辑的位点与强度。递归中各角色仅由前向传播完成，不同位点的编辑可在同一checkpoint中组合而无需重新拟合。

Result: 在十个视觉推理基准上，BaseRSI在分布内及留出基准上均一致提升基座视觉-语言模型。

Conclusion: RSI可由外部prompt/工具层转向模型内部权重的内化；逐block激活差监督、低维子空间更新与KV神经元级精确编辑可在免训练条件下实现增益内化，并具备可组合性。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) is moving from the harness of prompts and tools around a base model toward the model itself. However, existing RSI still faces key challenges, including harness-bound gains that stay outside the base model, black-box supervision that reads only the model's outputs, and imprecise edits whose site and strength are fixed in advance. To address these challenges, we propose BaseRSI, a new paradigm of Recursive Harness Internalization: the base model improves its harness, then writes what the harness adds into its own weights through training-free edits, round after round on its own responses. To support this paradigm, we introduce ReGap, a mechanism that supervises every transformer block with the high-dimensional activation difference that the harness makes at that block alone, and updates the block's weights along this signal in a low-dimensional subspace. Building on this mechanism, we further propose ReKey, an algorithm that writes this signal by regression into a few key-value neurons of every block, and measures the site and strength of each edit on the model's own responses. Moreover, the base model fills every role of this recursion with forward passes alone, and its edits, written at different sites, compose in one checkpoint without refitting. Experimental results on ten visual reasoning benchmarks show that BaseRSI consistently improves a base vision-language model both in distribution and on held-out benchmarks. Our code is available at https://github.com/beita6969/BaseRSI.

</details>


### [134] [Beyond Appearance: Evidence-Guided Programmatic 3D Scene Reconstruction with Agentic Lighting Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24565)
*Zeting Luo,Yifan Li,Shuai Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LightHarness：将显式光照推理引入 VLM 智能体式图像到代码的 3D 场景重建，通过几何/本征/光照三类互补线索联合优化结构与外观，并用光照推理记忆维持光源假设，实现无需训练或可微渲染的高精度重建与重打光。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像到代码重建方法主要依赖外观相似度，难以区分几何、材质与光照层面的错误；而光照通过其与几何、材质的交互决定阴影、投射阴影与反射，是判别错误的关键线索，因此需要显式光照推理。

Method: 提出 LightHarness——基于 VLM 的智能体式框架：1）利用几何、本征、光照互补线索联合迭代精修场景结构与外观；2）设计 Lighting Reasoning Memory，在迭代中维护光源假设、支持证据与修订历史，从而推理部分可见及不可见光源，并以证据引导的验证驱动后续修正；3）全程无需额外训练或可微渲染。

Result: 大量实验表明，在具有挑战性的阴影与复杂光照条件下，图像到代码重建精度优于现有方法；并支持下游场景编辑、物体插入与多样化重打光。

Conclusion: 显式光照推理可有效弥补外观相似度的不足，光验证作为诊断信号能提升重建准确性与可编辑性，为图像到代码的 3D 场景重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: Image-to-code methods for 3D scene reconstruction produce executable code that generates editable digital content and supports visual simulation. However, existing methods primarily rely on appearance similarity, which provides limited guidance for distinguishing errors in geometry, materials, and illumination. Light shapes scene appearance through its interactions with geometry and materials, influencing shading, cast shadows, and reflections. We therefore present LightHarness, a VLM-based agentic framework that incorporates explicit lighting reasoning into iterative scene reconstruction. To be specific, we leverage complementary geometric, intrinsic, and illumination cues to jointly refine scene structure and appearance, and propose Lighting Reasoning Memory to maintain light-source hypotheses, which supports evidence, and revision history throughout iterations. Together, they support reasoning about partially visible and unseen sources, with evidence-guided verification directing subsequent revisions. Our framework reconstructs explicit scenes through coherent reasoning about geometry, materials, and illumination without additional training or differentiable rendering. Extensive experiments demonstrate superior image-to-code reconstruction accuracy under challenging shadows and complex illumination, further supporting downstream scene editing, object insertion, and versatile relighting.

</details>


### [135] [SCR: Detecting Backdoor-Poisoned Samples through Clean-Reference Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24558)
*Yi Yang,Jinyang Huang,Feng-Qi Cui,Yu-Tong Guo,Sirui Zhao,Haiming Jin,Weihua Li,Quan Bai,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出语义反事实残差化（SCR）方法，利用少量干净参考样本进行样本级特征重构以消除良性语义变异，从而检测投毒样本，在12种攻击与3个数据集上TPR>99.5%、FPR<2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门检测器依赖类级统计量，而良性语义变异会掩盖投毒信号：既可能把良性特征偏移误报为投毒，也可能漏检混入类分布的投毒样本。需要在无需先验攻击知识的条件下区分良性语义变化与投毒导致的偏离。

Method: SCR 使用一小撮干净参考样本，对每个待测样本做样本特定的特征重构，将良性变异建模并"残差化"掉，再在残差空间中检测与干净基线一致性的、相干性（coherent）偏离，以此判定投毒样本；方法不依赖对攻击类型的先验假设。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、Tiny ImageNet 上覆盖 12 种 dirty-label 与 clean-label 攻击的实验显示，仅用少量干净参考集，SCR 的攻击平均 TPR 均超过 99.5%，FPR 低于 2%，并在 macro-TPR 上优于 8 个代表性检测基线。

Conclusion: 通过反事实残差化抑制良性语义变异，SCR 实现了跨攻击类型与数据集的高召回、低误报投毒样本检测，验证了样本级重构相比类级统计的检测优势。

Abstract: Deep neural networks are vulnerable to backdoor attacks, in which adversaries manipulate model predictions by injecting poisoned samples into training data. Detecting these samples remains challenging because legitimate semantic variation can obscure poisoning-related signals in learned representations. Consequently, class-level statistics used by existing detectors may flag benign feature deviations while overlooking poisoned samples that blend into the class distribution. In this paper, we propose Semantic Counterfactual Residualization (SCR), which uses a small set of clean reference samples to account for benign variation through sample-specific feature reconstruction. SCR identifies poisoned samples by detecting coherent deviations from these clean baselines, without requiring prior knowledge of the attacks. We evaluate SCR through experiments covering twelve dirty-label and clean-label attacks across CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Tiny ImageNet. With a small clean-reference set, SCR achieves attack-averaged true positive rates above 99.5\% and false positive rates below 2\% on each dataset, outperforming eight representative detection baselines in macro-TPR.

</details>


### [136] [Anchoring with Visual Evidence: Explicit Semantic Anchors for Joint Change Detection and Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24462)
*Kai Zheng,Hang-Cheng Dong,Puyi Fang,Junjia Pan,Zhenkai Wu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出显式语义锚点（ESA）监督接口，将双时相遥感影像中的变化检测与描述生成联合建模，通过实体存在、时序外观变化与空间范围三类因子锚点引导描述生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合变化检测与描述的关键难点在于：如何把稠密的视觉证据转化为引导描述生成的语义条件。现有方法缺少显式、可监督的中间语义表示来桥接检测结果与语言解码。

Method: 提出ESA监督式接口，暴露实体存在、时序外观变化和空间范围三类因子；采用预测引导读出融合视觉特征与预测变化概率，由三个因子头估计锚点激活。激活通过两条路径引导生成：上下文路径将三类因子激活映射为三个独立记忆token并追加到稠密视觉记忆；词表路径在每步解码产生加性词表偏置，同时保留稠密视觉记忆以提供空间细节。锚点目标由现有影像与变化标注自动导出，无需额外锚点级语言标注；推理时所有条件均由输入图像对与模型自身预测计算。

Result: 在LEVIR-MCI和WHU-CDC数据集上取得较强的联合检测与描述性能，验证了以监督式视觉属性作为显式条件用于变化描述生成的有效性。

Conclusion: 监督式视觉属性可有效充当变化描述生成的显式语义条件，在无需额外语言标注的前提下实现检测与描述的协同增益。

Abstract: Joint change detection and captioning seeks to localize changes and describe them from bi-temporal remote sensing images. A key challenge is translating dense visual evidence into the semantic conditions that guide description generation. We propose Explicit Semantic Anchors (ESA), a supervised interface that exposes entity presence, temporal appearance variation, and spatial extent. A prediction-guided readout combines visual features and predicted change probabilities, and three factor heads estimate the corresponding anchor activations. These activations guide generation through two complementary routes. The context route maps the three factor activations to three separate memory tokens, one per factor, and appends them to the dense visual memory, while the lexicon route produces an additive vocabulary bias at each decoding step. Dense visual memory remains available for spatial detail and information beyond the explicit factors. Anchor targets are derived from existing images and change annotations, requiring no additional anchor-level language annotation. At inference, all conditions are computed from the input image pair and the model's own predictions. Experiments on LEVIR-MCI and WHU-CDC show strong joint detection and captioning performance, supporting the use of supervised visual attributes as explicit conditions for change description generation.

</details>


### [137] [MLIE-CoT: Chain-of-Thought Reasoning via Multi-Level Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24433)
*Baichuan Yang,Dongyuan Xu,Zhao Yang,Yong Liu,Bing Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MLIE-CoT，一种基于多级图像编辑的视觉思维链框架，通过让模型在推理过程中自适应选择修复粒度（三个层级）来保留图像中的正确语义并抑制错误内容，显著优于基模型与固定修复策略方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向文生图生成的 CoT 方法主要依赖文本推理，无法充分利用并保留图像中已有的正确语义信息，因为再细致的语言也难以刻画全部视觉内容；而将图像作为上下文前向传递的做法又带来两个问题：不合适的早期图像会强烈干扰模型，且反复整图重生成会在每一轮引入并累积新的幻觉。

Method: 提出 MLIE-CoT 视觉思维链框架，基于多级图像编辑实现：模型学习从三个层级中选择修复粒度，在推理过程中尽可能保留正确语义、削弱错误内容的影响；同时构建覆盖不同错误类型的图像修复数据集，并据此训练修复层级路由器（repair-level router），以提升多模态模型将不同严重程度的错误路由到合适修复层级的能力。

Result: 大量实验表明，MLIE-CoT 显著优于其基模型以及采用固定修复策略的方法，在多个基准上取得明显提升。

Conclusion: 多级、自适应粒度的图像编辑是视觉思维链推理的有效途径，能够在保留正确语义的同时抑制错误传播与幻觉累积，优于单一级别或固定策略的方案。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has proven effective in improving the reasoning abilities of large language models by breaking complex tasks into step-by-step solutions. In recent years, researchers have attempted to extend CoT reasoning to visual generative models to improve their ability to handle complex tasks. However, existing CoT methods for text-to-image generation still rely mainly on textual reasoning and therefore cannot fully use and preserve the correct semantic information already present in an image, as even the most detailed language cannot capture all visual information. With advances in image generation, models such as FLUX and BAGEL can generate images conditioned on visual inputs. Recent work has attempted to carry images forward as context in the chain of thought, but this introduces another limitation: unsuitable earlier images can strongly interfere with the model, while repeated whole-image regeneration introduces new hallucinations at each round and causes them to accumulate.
