To address these issues, we propose MLIE-CoT, a visual chain-of-thought framework based on multi-level image editing. MLIE-CoT learns to select the repair granularity from three levels, preserving correct semantics whenever possible during reasoning while reducing the effect of incorrect content. To improve the ability of multimodal models to route errors of different severity to appropriate repair levels, we construct an image-repair dataset covering different error cases and use it to train a repair-level router. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MLIE-CoT significantly outperforms both its base models and methods using fixed repair strategies, producing clear improvements across multiple benchmarks.

</details>


### [138] [ShadowTransfer: A Geographic Transfer Benchmark for Overhead Shadow Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24904)
*Shagun Mittal,Raymond A. Yeh,Satish Ukkusuri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个跨区域阴影检测基准ShadowTransfer（4,500张人工核验掩码、3座美国城市、两种原生分辨率、留一城市协议），发现显著地理迁移退化，并用"三探针"框架诊断为特征空间问题、置信度崩塌与架构相关的可恢复组件，据此设计即插即用模块SIB，在9个（架构, 留出城市）单元上取得最小平均迁移差距（6.42 mIoU）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 俯视航空影像的稠密预测模型通常在某一地理区域训练、在另一区域部署，但现有俯视阴影数据集要么缺少地理迁移评测协议，要么依赖自动生成的标签，因此尚无基准衡量阴影检测模型的跨区域迁移能力。

Method: 构建ShadowTransfer基准（4,500个人工核验阴影掩码，覆盖三座美国城市、两种原生分辨率），配套留一城市（LOCO）评估协议；在四种架构上做原始训练与八种自适应方法对比；提出三探针诊断框架（特征空间探针、像素直方图匹配探针、置信度探针、分组件重训练探针）定位迁移失效位置与机制；依据诊断设计SIB（Spectral Information Bottleneck）即插即用模块，每个发现对应一个组件。

Result: 原始训练下留出测试城市造成3.5–7.8 mIoU损失；训练集从225增至900张图像时差距仍显著；八种自适应方法中六种平均劣于原始训练；失效发生在特征空间、超出像素直方图匹配的作用范围；表现为阴影像素置信度崩塌而背景置信度保持；不同模型需重训练不同组件（部分为编码器、部分为解码器）；结论在INRIA建筑轮廓数据上复现。SIB在9个单元上平均差距最小（6.42 mIoU），与FDA持平，优于十种方法中的其余方法。

Conclusion: 地理迁移差距是结构性问题，需从特征空间与置信度层面诊断而非仅做像素级对齐；基于诊断设计的SIB实现了接近同城上界的跨城市性能。基准、LOCO协议、代码与训练模型已开源，以支持俯视稠密预测中地理迁移的可复现评测。

Abstract: Overhead aerial imagery feeds dense-prediction pipelines that are routinely trained in one geographic region and deployed in another, yet no benchmark measures how shadow detection models transfer across regions. Existing overhead shadow datasets either lack a geographic transfer protocol or rely on automatically generated labels. We introduce ShadowTransfer, a benchmark of 4,500 human-verified shadow masks across three U.S. cities at two native resolutions, paired with a leave-one-city-out evaluation protocol. Under vanilla training, holding out the test city costs 3.5 to 7.8 mIoU across four architectures, the gap remains significant when the training set grows from 225 to 900 images, and six of eight adaptation methods degrade more than vanilla training on average. We propose a three-probe framework that diagnoses where and how the transfer breaks: the failure operates in feature space, beyond the reach of pixel-histogram matching, shows up as a confidence collapse on shadow pixels while background confidence stays intact, and is recoverable by retraining different architectural components depending on the model: encoder for some, decoder for others. These findings replicate on INRIA building footprints, suggesting they describe geographic transfer beyond shadows. Guided by the diagnosis, we build Spectral Information Bottleneck (SIB), a plug-in module with one component per finding. Across the 9 (architecture, held-out city) cells, SIB has the smallest mean gap to the within-city upper bound of the ten methods we evaluate (6.42 mIoU), level with FDA. The benchmark, LOCO protocol, code, and trained models are released to support reproducible evaluation of geographic transfer in overhead dense prediction.

</details>


### [139] [NereidVLA: Learning to Understand and Navigate the Underwater World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24864)
*Chenjie Zhao,Zhiliang Zhu,Quan Chen,Yuquan Xue,Xingxue Shi,Puyue Wang,Zhiqiang Zhao,Kangye Zheng,Boyang Xu,Mingwei Lin,Dejun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NereidVLA：先用约200万海洋样本训练Sea2Know学习目标定位、空间关系与场景结构（无需机器人运动监督），再用水下载具动力学下执行的专家轨迹微调，使其由RGB历史与语言指令预测离散运动token并解码为局部3D轨迹用于闭环跟踪；在匹配设置下，海洋空间预训练将仿真成功率从72.4%提升至84.4%，未在真实测试环境微调时实验室水池实测成功率达78.75%（InternVLA-N1为47.50%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下导航需将视觉空间线索转化为运动，但现有海洋视觉-语言数据集缺少配对的机器人轨迹，导致语言条件下的3D水下导航难以直接监督训练。

Method: 两阶段训练：第一阶段Sea2Know在约200万海洋样本上学习目标grounding、空间关系与场景结构，不使用配对机器人运动监督；第二阶段在模拟水下载具动力学下采集的专家轨迹上适配为视觉-语言-动作模型。输入RGB历史与语言指令，输出离散运动token，解码为局部3D轨迹用于闭环跟踪。评测含独立六维水下导航理解基准、离线预测与闭环导航。

Result: 匹配导航训练与执行设置下，海洋空间预训练使仿真成功率由72.4%升至84.4%；未在真实测试环境微调时，实验室水池机器人试验成功率为78.75%，InternVLA-N1为47.50%。

Conclusion: 海洋空间监督可有效支撑下游运动学习与真实物理水下导航，为缺乏轨迹标注的海洋视觉-语言数据提供了可迁移的利用路径。

Abstract: Underwater navigation requires translating visual spatial cues into motion, yet marine vision-language datasets rarely include paired robot trajectories. We present NereidVLA, a vision-language-action model that transfers marine spatial knowledge to language-conditioned 3D navigation. We first train Sea2Know on approximately two million marine examples to learn target grounding, spatial relations, and scene structure without paired robot-motion supervision. We then adapt it using expert trajectories executed under simulated underwater vehicle dynamics. Given RGB histories and a language instruction, NereidVLA predicts discrete motion tokens that decode into local 3D trajectories for closed-loop tracking. Evaluation combines an independent six-dimensional underwater navigation understanding benchmark with offline prediction and closed-loop navigation. Under matched navigation training and execution settings, marine spatial pretraining increases simulation success from 72.4% to 84.4%. Without fine-tuning on the real test environment, NereidVLA achieves 78.75% success in laboratory-pool robot trials, compared with 47.50% for InternVLA-N1. These results show that marine spatial supervision can support downstream motion learning and physical underwater navigation.

</details>


### [140] [A$^3$Bench: Compositional Reasoning over Aggressive Interactions in Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24762)
*Austin C Baker,Raiyaan Abdullah,Daniel Zeev Yoffe,Shruti Vyas,Yogesh S Rawat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出A$^3$Bench基准，用于评测多模态大模型在视频攻击性场景中联合识别攻击行为、区分施暴者/受害者/旁观者并将动作正确绑定到参与者的细粒度角色推理能力，发现现有模型系统性失败，并提出角色图提示策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有暴力与犯罪数据集主要判断"是否发生攻击"，而不检验模型能否识别"谁施暴、谁受害、参与者关系如何"。MLLMs在通用视频理解上表现优异，但其对攻击性互动中参与者角色的关联推理能力尚未被系统探究，存在评测空白。

Method: 构建A$^3$Bench视频基准，考察攻击动作与角色关联的联合识别，并设置带干扰项（distractor）的高难度场景以要求动作—参与者正确绑定。评测17个多模态模型，按推理复杂度分层分析，并尝试一种简单的角色图提示（role-graph prompting）策略以引导结构化推理。

Result: 模型在细粒度攻击性场景理解上明显不足；随推理复杂度上升性能持续下降；频繁混淆施暴者与受害者或旁观者，体现角色—动作关联的系统性失败而非粗粒度识别问题；扩大模型规模或采用通用推理模型提升有限；角色图提示在组合推理上带来改进。

Conclusion: 可靠的攻击性场景理解仍是当前MLLMs的重大挑战，需超越粗粒度暴力检测，发展面向角色感知的视频推理研究。

Abstract: Recent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved strong performance on general video understanding benchmarks, yet their ability to reason about participant roles in aggressive interactions remains largely unexplored. Existing violence and crime datasets primarily evaluate whether aggression occurs, without testing whether models can identify who performed the action, who received it, and how participants are related within the scene. To address this gap, we introduce A$^3$Bench, a benchmark to study aggressive action and association in videos. The benchmark evaluates whether models can jointly recognize aggressive actions, distinguish aggressors from victims and bystanders, and correctly bind actions to participants under challenging distractor settings. We benchmark 17 multimodal models and find that they struggle substantially on fine-grained aggressive-scene understanding. Performance degrades consistently as reasoning complexity increases, with models frequently confusing aggressors with victims or bystanders, revealing a systematic failure in role-action association rather than coarse aggression recognition alone. We further observe that increasing model scale or generic reasoning models experience limited improvement. Finally, we explore a simple role-graph prompting strategy that encourages structured reasoning over participant interactions, leading to improvements in compositional reasoning. Our findings highlight that reliable aggressive-scene understanding remains a major challenge for current MLLMs and motivate future research on role-aware video reasoning beyond coarse violence detection. Code and dataset: https://github.com/austincbaker/A3Bench.

</details>


### [141] [HandWorldBench: A Unified Benchmark for Hand-Centric World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24731)
*Zhaolu Wang,Jiacheng Xu,Lihong Wang,Shuqing Wang,Jianjie Fang,Ziyou Wang,Xvyuan Liu,Rongze Tang,Yiding Ma,Xin Zhang,Jiawei Chen,Wei Wu,Chen Gao,Yong Li,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 HandWorldBench，一个面向手-物交互的世界模型统一基准，覆盖人手、仿真灵巧手与真实灵巧手，从六个维度系统评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型在细粒度具身任务上进展迅速，而手-物交互是其中最具挑战的设定（需刻画手部动作如何改变物体、以及状态如何随时间持续）。但针对这类以手为中心能力的系统性评测仍不完善。

Method: 构建数据整理流水线：从600余小时视频中抽取手-物交互片段并按质量筛选，得到20,500段高质量第一人称片段；再人工挑选500段、覆盖7类任务族用于评测。评测维度为视觉质量、手部保真度、外观一致性、指令遵循、物理一致性、时序连贯性；在统一协议下对11个模型进行基准测试。

Result: 更强的手部姿态控制并未稳定转化为一致的物体状态演化与保持，也未带来稳健的跨具身（cross-embodiment）性能；多个专用手部控制模型在灵巧手任务上反而逊于通用文本/图像到视频生成器。

Conclusion: 手部姿态控制精度与手-物交互动力学建模能力存在脱节，现有专用模型在跨具身泛化上存在明显短板，亟需面向物体状态演化与保持的建模与评测。

Abstract: Recent advances in world models are opening new possibilities for fine-grained embodied tasks, with hand-object interaction offering a particularly demanding setting. Modeling these interactions involves capturing how hand actions change objects and how the resulting states persist over time. Yet systematic evaluation tailored to these hand-centric capabilities remains underdeveloped. To address this gap, we introduce HandWorldBench, a unified benchmark that systematically evaluates hand-centric world models across human hands, simulated dexterous hands, and real dexterous hands along six dimensions: visual quality, hand fidelity, appearance consistency, instruction following, physical consistency, and temporal coherence. To construct the benchmark, we develop a data curation pipeline that extracts hand-object interaction clips and screens them for quality, yielding 20,500 high-quality egocentric clips from over 600 hours of footage. We then manually select 500 clips covering seven task families for evaluation. Benchmarking eleven models under a unified protocol reveals that stronger hand-pose control does not consistently translate into coherent object-state evolution and persistence or robust cross-embodiment performance, with several specialized hand-control models underperforming general-purpose text/image-to-video generators on dexterous-hand tasks.

</details>


### [142] [Can Omni-modal LLMs Understand Spoken Intentions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24706)
*Zijie Xin,Tianyi Wang,Daoze Zhang,Jie Yang,Changli Tang,Qijie Wei,Fengyun Rao,Xirong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出O-IN-Bench基准，用含原生语音的完整视频评测全模态大模型对口语意图的理解，将"识别是否为求助请求"与"回复是否满足需求"分离度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全模态模型的语音/视频请求依赖用户与助手共享场景，同一句话在不同场景下需不同帮助甚至无需响应；现有音视频基准多以文本单独输入问题，交互式基准仅对整体回复打分，无法判断模型是否识别用户求助意图、以及识别后回复是否达标。

Method: 构建O-IN-Bench：1,000条中英文样本，覆盖5大类16子类；模型观看含原生语音的完整视频，输出一段文本响应或在不需回应时输出[SILENCE]。设计对比样本对（相同请求搭配不同场景条件）检验响应是否随上下文变化；采用样本级评分细则分别评判语言、意图与内容，并由O-IN-Engine将每条细则对照视频核验。

Result: 在30种全模态模型配置中，最佳总分59.6由Gemini3.7-Flash取得；其意图判断通过率74.4%，内容判断仅42.6%。当语音为同伴间对话而非对助手的请求时，强模型仍常继续响应；当回复需同时满足多人时最高分仅25.9。

Conclusion: 识别求助请求与满足其要求是两种相互独立的能力，O-IN-Bench对二者分别进行度量，结果揭示当前全模态模型在内容层面与多人需求场景下仍存在明显短板。

Abstract: Omni-modal large language models (omni-modal LLMs) can respond to spoken requests directly through voice and video calls. Such requests rely on the scene shared by the user and the assistant, so the same utterance may call for different help or for no response. Most audio-visual benchmarks provide questions as separate text input, and interactive benchmarks mostly score a response as a whole. It thus remains unclear whether a model recognizes when the user is asking for help, and whether its reply meets the user's need once the request is recognized. We propose O-IN-Bench to evaluate how well omni-modal LLMs understand spoken intentions. A model watches a complete video with native speech and outputs one text response, or [SILENCE] when no response is needed. O-IN-Bench contains 1,000 Chinese and English samples in five categories and 16 subcategories. Contrastive pairs combine the same request with different scene conditions to test whether the response changes with the context. A sample-specific rubric judges language, intent, and content separately, and O-IN-Engine verifies each rubric against the video. Across 30 omni-modal model configurations, the best overall score is 59.6, achieved by Gemini3.7-Flash. Gemini3.7-Flash passes 74.4% of the intent judgments but only 42.6% of the content judgments. When the speech is a conversation between peers rather than a request to the assistant, even strong models often keep responding. When a reply must satisfy several people, the best score is only 25.9. These results indicate that recognizing a request and meeting its requirements are separate abilities, and O-IN-Bench measures each of them.

</details>


### [143] [EviScope: Evidence-Conditioned Sampling for Long-Range 3D Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24451)
*Mingyang Lyu,Yinqian Sun,Gongce Cao,Chujie Tang,Yuhao Zhang,Lu Jia,Qian Zhang,Bo Wang,Pin Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EviScope 将雷达回波构成的非学习式鸟瞰证据场引入查询式相机-雷达检测器，用其调节径向采样范围与帧间加权，仅增加0.009%参数量；与几何条件器对照，NDS/mAP均更优，但相对自身基线无显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 查询式相机-雷达检测器由查询自行决定采样位置与帧，但从未检验雷达证据是否应参与这一决策。作者在MAN TruckScenes的150米范围内观察到：各距离带内目标级雷达回波数量不同，召回率也随之不同，提示雷达证据可能是有用的分配信号。

Method: 以当前帧雷达回波构建非学习式鸟瞰证据场，每个查询在其解码中心读取该证据，用于缩放径向采样范围并对八帧加权；在RaCFormer上仅增加5,450参数（0.009%），无额外时延。采用同种子匹配的自身基线比较，并设置同参数量、仅几何的对照条件器作消融，所有比较均以端到端训练配对进行。

Result: 无模块版本在TruckScenes上mAP达25.9（7次运行），超过已发表24.1，故模块仅与自身基线比较。查询读取的证据随目标回波数上升（Spearman 0.51），对零回波目标仅部分捕捉；学习到的半径与证据负相关（4次运行ρ≈−0.91）。7对4轮训练：NDS +0.31±0.36，mAP −0.27±0.22。相对同参数量几何条件器（3个匹配种子）提升+1.25±0.72 NDS、+0.87±0.21 mAP，且每对mAP均更优，对照条件器NDS接近基线。远距离增益的探索性结果未通过内部预设的留出检验；无查询局部证据时也存在较大的推理期替换惩罚。

Conclusion: 雷达证据可被查询读取并影响采样分配，但相对自身基线未带来显著提升；其价值在与几何条件器的匹配端到端比较中才显现。学习到的分配或推理期依赖不能替代匹配的端到端对比，探索性远距离增益需严格留出验证。

Abstract: Query-based camera-radar detectors choose where and over which frames each object query samples from the query's position or feature. We test whether radar evidence around the query should also set these choices at the 150 m range of MAN TruckScenes, where recall differs across object-level radar-return counts within every range band. EviScope is a minimal test: the current frame's returns form a non-learned bird's-eye evidence field, which each query reads at its decoded centre to scale its radial sampling extent and to weight eight frames. It adds 5,450 parameters (0.009%) to RaCFormer, with no latency increase in our timing. Our module-free recipe exceeds the published TruckScenes mAP (25.9 over seven runs against 24.1), so we measure the module only against its own baseline at matched seeds. The evidence a query reads rises with its object's return count (Spearman 0.51) but captures zero-return objects only in part, and the learned radius tracks it ($ρ\approx-0.91$ in four runs). Over seven four-epoch pairs the module changes NDS by $+0.31\pm0.36$ and mAP by $-0.27\pm0.22$ (mean $\pm$ SE). Against a geometry-only conditioner with the same parameter count, trained at three matched seeds, it is higher by $+1.25\pm0.72$ NDS and $+0.87\pm0.21$ mAP, on mAP in every pair, and the control's mean NDS is near the baseline's. An exploratory far-range gain failed an internally pre-specified held-out test, and large inference-time substitution penalties also occur without query-local evidence: neither learned allocation nor inference-time dependence substitutes for matched end-to-end comparisons.

</details>


### [144] [Retroactive Concept Bottleneck Models for Exemplar-Free Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24445)
*Yang Ji,Ying Sun,Yuanxin Zhuang,Yuting Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RetroCBM：无样本类增量学习中的可解释概念瓶颈模型，通过保留少量与词表无关的统计量，对新概念实现"回溯"监督，恢复全历史平方分类损失，并用图加权惩罚抑制概念误差导致的类间混淆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续概念瓶颈模型（CBM）侧重保留已学概念或用生成特征近似旧类，导致新学概念在旧类预测中利用不足；且无样本约束下无法直接回访历史数据来校准新旧概念的贡献。

Method: 1) 储存少量与词表无关的统计量，刻画冻结视觉特征与预训练视觉-语言模型嵌入空间的映射关系；2) 提出回溯回归规则（retroactive regression rule），使每个新概念的预测器等价于在迄今所有图像上的类平衡岭回归；3) 将统计量再投影，恢复全历史平方分类损失，学习新概念对旧类预测的贡献权重；4) 引入图加权惩罚，约束概念系统性误差引起的 margin 偏移。

Result: 在六个数据集的所有类增量设置下均优于最先进的可解释方法；相对最强的无样本基线，平均增量准确率（mean average incremental accuracy）取得18.1%的相对提升。

Conclusion: 以极小的统计量存储替代样本回放，可在无样本类增量学习中兼顾可解释性与新旧概念的协同利用，显著提升增量准确率。

Abstract: Exemplar-free class-incremental learning (EFCIL) requires models to learn new classes sequentially without retaining training samples from previous tasks. Continual concept bottleneck models (CBMs) make their predictions interpretable by identifying visual concepts in an image and using them to score each class. However, these methods typically focus on preserving learned concepts or approximating past classes with generated features, which can leave new concepts underused in old-class predictions. We propose RetroCBM, which retains a small set of vocabulary-independent statistics relating frozen visual features to the embedding space of a pretrained vision-language model. Even after earlier training images have been discarded, we can use these statistics to supervise new concepts. To do so, we develop a retroactive regression rule that yields the same predictor for each new concept as class-balanced ridge regression on all images seen so far. We then reproject the stored statistics to recover the full-history squared classification loss and thus learn how much each new concept should contribute to old-class predictions. Since the classifier may use systematic concept errors to distinguish nearby classes, we further introduce a graph-weighted penalty on the margin shifts caused by those errors. Across six datasets, RetroCBM outperforms state-of-the-art interpretable methods in all class-incremental settings, with an 18.1\% relative gain in mean average incremental accuracy over the strongest exemplar-free baseline.

</details>


### [145] [Reliable Tree Recruitment from Scarce Annotations for Forest Inventory Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24401)
*Luiz Fernando Souza Luz,Pedro J. Soto Vega,Matheus Pinheiro Ferreira,Laura Elena Cué la Rosa,Hudson Franklin Pessoa Veras,Ernandes Macedo da Cunha Neto,Evandro Orfanó Figueiredo,Ana Paula Dalla Corte,Carlos Roberto Sanquetta,José Matheus Segre Moneva Viveiros,Matheus Santos Fuza,Heitor Eduardo Ferreira Campos Morato Filpi,Cristiano Vinícius Vidal,Vinícius Moura Costa,Débora Nascimento Rosário,Danilo Roberti Alves de Almeida,Paulo Guilherme Molin,Dário Augusto Borges Oliveira*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种迭代自训练的多任务DeepLabV3框架，利用置信度与形态学双重过滤扩充少量标注的单木树冠物种清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热带森林中许多树种仅有少量经野外验证的树冠样本，标注成本限制了基于UAV影像的单木树冠（ITC）物种级清单构建。

Method: 在RGB无人机正射影像上迭代自训练多任务DeepLabV3网络（语义分割+树冠距离回归头）；每轮候选树冠须通过类别概率与距离图的置信度过滤，以及树冠面积与实心度的形态学过滤，每物种最多新增5个树冠，以保持新增样本与参考清单一致。

Result: 在巴西两个站点评估：Acre亚马逊雨林（17种、355个标注树冠、4 cm GSD）与Maranhão塞拉多林地（4种、51个标注树冠、1.8 cm GSD）。20轮后保留树冠数由约500增至960（Acre）、约700增至3100以上（Maranhão）；树冠级F1超过单任务与多任务DeepLabV3基线（53.5% vs. 50.0%、45.2%；92.2% vs. 79.7%、83.6%）。

Conclusion: 该框架为借助消费级无人机与有限野外工作低成本扩充物种清单提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Species-level inventories of individual tree crowns (ITCs) from UAV imagery are limited by annotation cost: in tropical forests, many species are represented by only a handful of field-validated crowns. We propose a reliable tree recruitment framework that expands a small annotated inventory by iteratively self-training a multi-task DeepLabV3 network, combining semantic segmentation with a crown-distance regression head, on RGB UAV orthomosaics. At each iteration, candidate crowns enter the training inventory only if they pass a confidence filter on class probability and distance map and a morphology filter on crown area and solidity, with at most five new crowns per species, keeping recruited samples consistent with the reference inventory. We evaluate the framework at two Brazilian sites: an Amazonian rainforest in Acre (17 species, 355 annotated crowns, 4 cm GSD) and a forest site in the Cerrado of Maranhão (4 species, 51 annotated crowns, 1.8 cm GSD). Over 20 iterations, the number of retained crowns grew from about 500 to 960 in Acre and from about 700 to over 3100 in Maranhão, while crown-level F1 exceeded single- and multi-task DeepLabV3 baselines (53.5% vs. 50.0% and 45.2%; 92.2% vs. 79.7% and 83.6%). The framework offers a low-cost route to expanding species inventories with consumer-grade UAVs and limited fieldwork.

</details>


### [146] [One Affinity, Two Axes: Bidirectional Slot Attention for Iterative Aggregation across the Structure--Detail Spectrum in Visual Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24371)
*Zihang Wang,Wenkai Zhu,Lingdong Kong,Teng Yan,Alan Liang,Shuyuan Li,Xieyuanli Chen,Dong Kong,Guanyu Zong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双向槽注意力（BiSA），将视觉检索的特征聚合重构为迭代耦合系统，通过跨轴共识（CAC）与渐进式令牌过滤（PTF）实现结构-细粒度协同的全局描述子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将稠密 patch token 聚合为紧凑、具判别力的全局描述子对视觉检索至关重要。基础模型虽提供丰富局部特征，但现有聚合头仍依赖单次映射，缺乏显式机制来协调结构性与细粒度线索。

Method: BiSA 将聚合表述为迭代耦合系统。CAC 沿两个互补轴解读单一共享的 token–slot 亲和度以逐步精炼可学习槽：竞争轴上槽在共享温度下争夺 token，决定归属并产生非冗余场景分解；选择轴上槽专属温度控制响应广度，覆盖从宽泛布局到稀疏细粒度细节的谱系，二者共识耦合非冗余分解与多粒度槽特化。同时两轴免费提供 token 可靠性线索（竞争下的主张强度、选择下的显著性），PTF 将其转为保留概率，在下一次 CAC 迭代前降权不可靠 token，无需分割分支或外部先验，构成竞争分解—选择性聚焦—反馈净化的自增强回路。

Result: 在三类检索任务中，BiSA 于 6 个地点识别基准中的 5 个领先（Baidu Mall R@1 +4.2）；在无人机—卫星地理定位全部 16 项指标上登顶（150 m 处 R@1 +8.4）；ROxford-Hard 实例级检索 mAP 提升 4.5。选择轴仅新增 20 个可学习标量，却使平均 R@1 提升 2.8 点。

Conclusion: BiSA 以极少参数开销显著提升多类视觉检索性能，验证了双轴共识与令牌过滤自增强机制在聚合设计上的有效性。

Abstract: Aggregating dense patch tokens into compact and discriminative global descriptors is central to visual retrieval. Although foundation models provide rich local features, existing aggregation heads still rely on single-pass mappings and lack an explicit mechanism to coordinate structural and fine-grained cues. Therefore, we propose Bidirectional Slot Attention (BiSA), which reformulates aggregation as an iterative coupled system. Cross-Axis Consensus (CAC) progressively refines learnable slots by interpreting a single shared token--slot affinity along two complementary axes. Along the competition axis, slots vie for each token under a temperature shared across all of them, determining token ownership and inducing a non-redundant scene decomposition. Along the selection axis, a slot-specific temperature governs the breadth of each slot's response, spanning a spectrum from broad layouts to sparse fine-grained details. Their consensus couples non-redundant decomposition with multi-granular slot specialization. Furthermore, the two axes expose intrinsic token-reliability cues at no extra cost: a token's claim strength under competition and its salience under selection. Progressive Token Filtering (PTF) converts these cues into a retention probability that down-weights unreliable tokens before the next CAC iteration, without any segmentation branch or external prior, forming a self-reinforcing loop of competitive decomposition, selective focusing, and feedback purification. Across all three retrieval tasks, BiSA leads on five of six place recognition benchmarks (+4.2 R@1 on Baidu Mall), tops all 16 metrics of drone--satellite geo-localization (+8.4 R@1 at 150 m), and improves instance-level retrieval on ROxford-Hard by 4.5 mAP points. Notably, the selection axis adds only 20 learnable scalars, yet improves average R@1 by 2.8 points.

</details>


### [147] [CROMPT: Adapting CLIP via Cross-Modal Prompting for Robust Zero-Shot Generalization under Adverse Weather](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24284)
*Aniruddh Sikdar,Arya Kishor,Snehashis Dhibar,Suresh Sundaram*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CROMPT：面向零样本域自适应语义分割的提示学习框架，通过跨模态特征增强（CROMA）与渐进式由易到难策略提升域不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标域图像在训练时不可用，需仅依赖目标域文本描述完成域自适应；现有提示学习方法不适配基于ResNet的CLIP视觉骨干，也难以实现像素级跨模态特征对齐。

Method: 提出CROMA（随机视觉提示+混合文本提示HTP，联合调整视觉与文本表征）、CROMA-VL（借助视觉-语言模型生成域条件提示）、渐进式由易到难自适应策略。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上稳定优于强基线及SOTA，mIoU提升1.63–2.63%，且推理复杂度不变。

Conclusion: CROMPT是零样本多域语义分割中有效且高效的框架。

Abstract: Domain adaptation for semantic segmentation is challenging when target-domain images are unavailable during training. Zero-shot domain adaptation addresses this by adapting models using only textual descriptions of the target domain. However, existing prompt-learning methods are not well suited for ResNet-based CLIP vision backbones or pixel-level cross-modal feature alignment. We propose CROMPT, a prompt-learning framework for zero-shot domain adaptation in semantic segmentation. CROMPT introduces Cross-Modal Feature Augmentation (CROMA), combining Random Visual Prompting with Hybrid Textual Prompting (HTP) to jointly adapt visual and textual representations. We further propose CROMA-VL, which leverages vision-language models to generate domain-conditioned prompts, and a progressive easy-to-hard adaptation strategy for improved domain invariance. Experiments on two benchmark datasets demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines and state-of-the-art methods, achieving 1.63-2.63% mIoU gains while maintaining inference-time complexity. These results establish CROMPT as an effective and efficient framework for zero-shot multi-domain semantic segmentation. OpenReview: https://openreview.net/forum?id=FxqFsIL685

</details>


### [148] [Psychometrics-Inspired Coreset Selection for Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24233)
*Susu Jiang,Xuelin Shen,Baoliang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出质量感知表示学习框架GRAM，借鉴心理测量学中的等级反应模型（GRM），用潜质质量、内容依赖响应敏感度、失真依赖等级阈值三因子建模质量判断，为IQA标注的coreset选择提供结构化质量表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模主观标注是训练准确IQA模型的关键瓶颈，coreset选择可在标注前挑选代表性图像以减轻负担；但视觉特征空间的多样性并不等价于感知质量维度的多样性，因此需明确表示应编码哪些与质量相关的因素。

Method: 受GRM启发，GRAM通过三个互补因子建模质量判断：潜在质量、内容相关的响应敏感度、失真相关的等级阈值；三者共同决定达到不同质量等级的概率及期望质量分数，学习到的参数构成结构化质量感知表示，与通用视觉特征互补用于coreset选择。

Result: 在9个数据集上，跨不同选择策略、下游IQA模型及标注预算均取得改进。

Conclusion: 将心理测量学GRM原理与感知质量评估对齐，可获得结构化质量感知表示，有效提升IQA标注的coreset选择效率与下游性能；代码将公开。

Abstract: Collecting large-scale subjective annotations remains a critical bottleneck in training accurate image quality assessment (IQA) models. Coreset selection reduces this burden by selecting representative images before annotation. However, diversity in a visual feature space does not necessarily reflect diversity in perceptual quality, raising a fundamental question about which quality-related factors the representation should encode. We propose GRAM, a quality-aware representation learning framework inspired by the graded response model (GRM) from psychometrics. GRM describes how an individual's latent ability determines the probabilities of reaching successive assessment grades, with response sensitivity and grade thresholds governing this relationship. In this paper, we find that this principle naturally aligns with perceptual quality assessment, where the impact of image degradation depends on image content and distortion characteristics. Building on this connection, GRAM models quality judgments through three complementary factors: latent quality, content-dependent response sensitivity, and distortion-dependent grade thresholds. These factors jointly determine the probabilities of attaining different quality grades and the resulting expected quality score. The learned parameters provide a structured quality-aware representation that complements general visual features for coreset selection. Experiments on nine datasets demonstrate improvements across different selection strategies, downstream IQA models, and annotation budgets. The code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [149] [Target-Consistent Action Guidance via Semi-Unbalanced Optimal Transport for Drifting World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24222)
*Md Abtahi Majeed Chowdhury,Md Rifat Ur Rahman,Md Sakib Hossain Shovon,Jaesik Choi,Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出DriftWorld式动作条件世界模型中"排斥当前帧"的强调方式存在目标偏差（即使预测正确仍被推离），提出Mass-Share Accentuation (MSA)，将当前帧视为与真实未来竞争的半松弛最优传输反事实目的地，减去正确未来的闭式份额使引导在目标处消失；证明真实未来是唯一的运动方向不动点，且在Push-T/Robomimic/BridgeV2上以零推理开销改善动作排序、响应斜率与运动区域误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件世界模型需大量rollout用于规划与离线策略评估，多步扩散推理昂贵；drifting模型把迭代从推理移到训练、单次前向出样本，DriftWorld将其引入该领域，但为强化动作跟随而借鉴classifier-free guidance、排斥当前帧预测的做法带来目标偏差——预测已匹配真实未来时排斥力依然存在（在kernel、balanced/unbalanced Wasserstein drifting场中均成立）。

Method: 提出Mass-Share Accentuation (MSA)：把当前帧当作与记录的（真实）未来在半松弛最优传输问题中竞争的反事实目的地，减去正确未来的闭式份额，使引导在目标处归零，余下部分由所有预测共享。

Result: 证明在坍缩预测下真实未来是沿运动方向的唯一不动点，且引导随预测误差衰减；在Push-T、Robomimic、BridgeV2上（训练模型表现为反应不足而非过冲）相较潜空间基线提升动作排序、响应斜率与运动区域误差，且无推理开销；但基准未能隔离"偏差消除"这一因果因素。

Conclusion: MSA以零额外推理成本消除DriftWorld类引导的目标偏差，理论保证真实未来为不动点，经验上提升动作跟随质量，但因果归因受基准设计限制，需进一步实验隔离偏差消除的贡献。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models let robots imagine the outcomes of their actions for planning and offline policy evaluation, but these uses need many rollouts, which multi-step diffusion makes expensive. Drifting models move the iteration from inference to training and generate samples in one network pass. DriftWorld brought this paradigm to action-conditioned world models; to strengthen action following, it repels predictions from the current frame, a construction borrowed from classifier-free guidance. We show that this accentuation has a target bias: the push persists even when a prediction matches the recorded future, in kernel, balanced, and unbalanced Wasserstein drifting fields alike. We introduce Mass-Share Accentuation (MSA) to remove this bias. MSA treats the current frame as a counterfactual destination competing with the recorded future in a semi-relaxed optimal transport problem; subtracting the closed-form share of the correct future makes the guidance vanish at the target, and the remainder is shared by all predictions. We prove that the recorded future is the unique fixed point along the motion direction for collapsed predictions, and that the guidance fades with prediction error. On Push-T, Robomimic, and BridgeV2, where trained models under-react rather than overshoot, MSA improves action ranking, response slope, and moving-region error over latent-space baselines at no inference cost, though these benchmarks do not isolate bias removal as the cause.

</details>


### [150] [From Neural Tokens to Language: Dual-View EEG Semantic Bridging for Brain-to-Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24197)
*YaoYang Liu,Jun Dai,ZhuoShi Tang,Weijun Sun,Xiaozhao Fang,Jie Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出双视角语义令牌桥接框架，利用原始图像与素描图像作为互补视觉监督，学习两路EEG语义令牌流，并通过分支特定的语义查询桥将其转为LLM可用的软提示，配合渐进式适应实现从有图像辅助到无图像的EEG条件文本生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG到文本方法将EEG压缩为单一全局表示并直接投影到LLM隐空间，在EEG信号噪声大且存在被试差异的情况下，难以保留互补的视觉语义信息。

Method: 以原始图像和素描图像为互补视觉监督，分别学习raw-oriented与sketch-oriented两路EEG语义令牌流；引入分支特定语义查询桥，将每路令牌流聚合成紧凑语义槽并转换为LLM兼容的软提示；再采用渐进式适应策略，将视觉-语义接口从图像辅助训练过渡到无图像的EEG条件生成。

Result: 在逐被试EEG分类任务上表现强劲，并在多种LLM骨干上取得具竞争力的EEG-to-text生成效果。消融、分支级表示及EEG-标签解耦分析表明两路EEG流学到互补的视角导向表示，且EEG导出的提示在类别标签之外提供样本特异性信息。

Conclusion: 双视角互补监督与语义查询桥设计能有效提升EEG表示的结构化与LLM对齐质量，验证了EEG提示超越类别标签的样本级信息含量。

Abstract: Visually evoked EEG-to-text generation aims to produce natural-language descriptions from neural responses elicited by visual stimuli. Existing approaches typically compress EEG signals into a single global representation and directly project it into the hidden space of a large language model (LLM), which may limit the preservation of complementary visual semantics under noisy and subject-dependent EEG signals. We propose a dual-view semantic token bridging framework that exploits raw images and sketch images as complementary visual supervision to learn raw-oriented and sketch-oriented EEG semantic token streams. To interface these structured neural representations with an LLM, we introduce a branch-specific semantic query bridge that aggregates each token stream into compact semantic slots and converts them into LLM-compatible soft prompts. A progressive adaptation strategy further transitions the learned visual-semantic interface from image-supported training to image-free EEG-conditioned generation. Experiments demonstrate strong subject-wise EEG classification performance and competitive EEG-to-text generation across multiple LLM backbones. Ablation, branch-level representation, and EEG-label disentanglement analyses further show that the two EEG streams learn complementary view-oriented representations and that EEG-derived prompts provide sample-specific information beyond category labels.

</details>


### [151] [Stabilizing Neural-to-Image Retrieval with Frequency-Domain Priors and Adaptive Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24163)
*YaoYang Liu,ZhuoShi Tang,Haopeng Lai,Weijun Sun,Xiaozhao Fang,Jie Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个统一的神经到图像检索框架，通过在共享语义空间中对齐神经与图像表征，集成双流自适应、频域校准与相位引导温度调制，以应对神经信号噪声大、试次变异高、被试依赖等难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉神经解码可从脑活动中推断感知到的视觉刺激，对理解人类视觉认知及脑机接口、人工智能应用具有重要价值。预训练视觉-语言模型提供了语义丰富的表征空间，但由于EEG/MEG神经响应噪声大、跨试次变异高且存在被试依赖性，神经信号与视觉嵌入之间的可靠对齐仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 作者提出统一的神经到图像检索框架，在共享语义空间中对齐神经与图像表征。框架包含三项关键组件：(1) 双流自适应：建模低维共享子空间并施加相关性正则化，以抑制冗余噪声、增强跨模态一致性；(2) 频域校准：利用幅度与相位统计量对神经表征进行条件调制；(3) 相位引导温度调制：依据神经响应的稳定性调整对比学习中的监督强度。

Result: 在THINGS-EEG与THINGS-MEG数据集上，涵盖被试内与被试间两种设定，所提框架一致优于强基线方法。代码将发布于 github.com/wanfkuabc/Geometry-Regularized-Brain-Embeddings。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，在异质神经条件下实现稳健的神经到图像检索，得益于稳定的共享语义空间、频域信息驱动的表征校准以及自适应监督策略。

Abstract: Visual neural decoding aims to infer perceived visual stimuli from brain activity, offering important insights into human visual cognition while enabling transformative applications in brain computer interfaces and artificial intelligence. Recent advances in pretrained vision-language models have provided a semantically rich representation space for this task. However, reliable alignment between EEG/MEG signals and visual embeddings remains challenging because neural responses are noisy, highly variable across trials, and subject dependent. To address this issue, we propose a unified neural-to-image retrieval framework that improves retrieval performance by aligning neural and image representations in a shared semantic space. The framework integrates dual-stream adaptation, frequency-domain calibration, and phase-guided temperature modulation to promote more robust cross-modal semantic alignment. Specifically, dual-stream adaptation models a low-dimensional shared subspace with correlation regularization to suppress redundant noise and enhance cross-modal consistency. Frequency-domain calibration conditionally modulates neural representations using amplitude and phase statistics. Phase-guided temperature modulation further adjusts the supervision strength in contrastive learning according to the stability of neural responses. Experiments on THINGS-EEG and THINGS-MEG under both intra-subject and inter-subject settings demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms strong baselines. These results suggest that robust neural-to-image retrieval under heterogeneous neural conditions benefits from a stable shared semantic space, frequency-informed representation calibration, and adaptive supervision. Code will be released at github.com/wanfkuabc/Geometry-Regularized-Brain-Embeddings.

</details>


### [152] [Enhancing Active Learning Performance with Initial Data Selection Based on Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24384)
*Mohammad Mirzaee,Mehrshad Fallah Estalkhzir,Mohammad Reza Mohammadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向目标检测主动学习的初始训练轮次优化框架：利用YOLO-World特征聚类并依objectness分数精炼图像代替初始轮不确定性采样，后续轮次用一致性采样；在Aquarium与NWPU VHR-10上提升mAP50（平均+6.6%）并缩短标注时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动目标检测中初始标注池的选择至关重要，尤其在标注成本高、资源预算低的复杂数据集上；传统不确定性采样在初始轮次效果有限。

Method: 基于YOLO-World模型的特征进行聚类，以获得特征多样的样本；再依据objectness分数对图像进行精炼，优先训练基础模型最有信心的区域；后续主动学习轮次采用一致性采样以维持训练稳健性。在两个复杂数据集（Aquarium、NWPU VHR-10）上评测。

Result: NWPU数据集图像级评测mAP50平均提升6.6%；图像精炼方法在目标级评测中提升约11%；同时改善模型性能与标注时间。

Conclusion: 在主动学习早期轮次引入基础模型（YOLO-World）进行特征聚类与objectness引导的样本选择，可有效提升目标检测主动学习的性能与标注效率。

Abstract: In active object detection, selecting the initial labeled pool is essential, especially in complex datasets with expensive labeling and low resource budget. This study presents an active learning framework designed to optimize the initial training cycle of active learning for the object detection task. Our approach diverges from traditional methods by utilizing feature-based clustering from the YOLO-World model and refining images based on objectness scores rather than relying on uncertainty sampling in the initial cycle. This approach focuses on finding samples with various features and emphasizes training in areas where the foundation model is most confident. Doing so helps the model better understand different types of objects. For subsequent cycles, we employ a consistency-based sampling technique to maintain the robustness of the training process. To evaluate our method, we use two complex datasets called Aquarium and NWPU VHR-10, which are well known for their diverse object sizes and challenging conditions. In the NWPU dataset, our method increases mAP50 by an average of 6.6% in image-based evaluation. Moreover, the image refinement method shows an improvement of approximately 11% in object-based evaluation. The results show improvements in model performance and labeling time, demonstrating the advantages of using foundation models in early cycles of active learning. An implementation of the algorithm is available at https://github.com/Mhrshd-fllh/ALBoost-FM

</details>


### [153] [Where Should a Diffusion Transformer Meet Its Teacher?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24380)
*Zhuoqi Hu,Shiwei Li,Haozhao Wang,Xiandi Luo,Yihao Ouyang,Wenting Shen,Ang Wang,Jiamang Wang,Minyi Guo,Ruixuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AdaREPA，用梯度兼容性在线度量并动态选择对齐层，使 REPA 监督随训练阶段自适应，加速 DiT 收敛并提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 REPA 类方法将预训练视觉教师的监督固定挂在单一中间层上，但学生表示随训练演化，最有效的对齐层在不同训练阶段会变化，固定层位次优；而在线搜索最佳层代价过高。

Method: 测量 flow-matching 目标与对齐目标之间的梯度兼容性（gradient compatibility），对平滑后的逐层兼容性分数做 softmax，得到层上的概率分布，每个训练步按该分布采样一层进行对齐监督，实现无额外搜索的在线路由。

Result: 在 ImageNet-256 上，AdaREPA 在不同模型规模下均加速收敛并提升生成质量，且开销极小、无额外推理成本；同时揭示了最适合监督的层随训练进程发生迁移的规律。

Conclusion: 自适应层选择优于固定层对齐，表明"对齐层位"应作为随训练动态调整的对象；梯度兼容性可作为廉价且有效的路由信号。

Abstract: Representation Alignment (REPA) significantly accelerates Diffusion Transformer training by guiding intermediate student features with a pretrained visual teacher, yet existing methods typically attach supervision to a single fixed layer. As student representations evolve, however, we find that the most effective layer for alignment varies across training stages. To track this evolving layer suitability online without costly search, we measure gradient compatibility between the flow-matching and alignment objectives, and propose AdaREPA, an online routing framework that adaptively selects the supervision layer as training progresses. AdaREPA uses a softmax distribution over smoothed layer compatibility scores to sample a layer for alignment at each training step. This probabilistic routing adapts teacher supervision to the student's changing learning needs, with minimal training overhead and no additional inference cost. On ImageNet-256, AdaREPA accelerates convergence and improves generation quality across model sizes, while revealing how the most suitable layers for supervision shift over training.

</details>


### [154] [Pixel2Perspective: A Benchmark for Multi-Modal and Multi-View Generalization in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24327)
*Aditya Gandhamal,Aniruddh Sikdar,Arya Kishor,Suresh Sundaram*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Pixel2Perspective 基准，用于跨空-地视角、多传感器（含红外）的开放词汇语义分割与目标定位评估，并提出 MSRIQ 不确定性量化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能（无人机、自动驾驶）需在含未见物体的环境中提升感知；CLIP 类 OVSS 模型虽图像理解强，但在室外天气、视角变化下跨域泛化差，实际部署受阻，且缺乏系统性评测平台。

Method: 构建覆盖空中与地面双视角、多传感器（RGB、红外）的多角度目标定位与开放词汇分割基准，数据集涵盖多样室外条件、仿真到现实场景；同时对 CLIP 等基础模型与先进 OVSS 模型进行系统评估；提出 Multi-Source RGB-IR Uncertainty Quantification（MSRIQ）框架，在 RGB 与红外图像上联合建模偶然不确定性与认知不确定性。

Result: 提供面向鲁棒性的评测基准，揭示基础模型与 OVSS 模型在跨视角、跨域、多模态条件下的泛化表现，并通过 MSRIQ 支撑不同环境条件下 OVSS 的稳健评估。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了鲁棒性驱动的基准与不确定性量化方法，为开放世界具身 AI 感知的后续研究提供关键洞见与基础。

Abstract: Embodied AI systems, such as drones and autonomous vehicles, improve navigation by enhancing perception in environments containing unseen objects. While Visual Language Models (VLMs) offer strong image understanding, their real-world application is underexplored. For effective deployment, open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) models, leveraging models like CLIP to assign pixel-level labels from text, must be robust to outdoor conditions, adverse weather, and varying viewpoints. Despite their potential, these models encounter significant challenges in cross-domain generalization, hindering their practical use in embodied AI applications. In this work, we present Pixel2Perspective, a benchmark for multi-angle object localization and open-vocabulary segmentation across both aerial and ground perspectives. This benchmark supports thorough evaluation across different viewpoints and sensor modalities. We assess foundational models like CLIP and advanced OVSS models using a curated dataset that spans varied outdoor conditions, sim-to-real scenarios, and multiple sensors, including infrared, to support the development of robust and generalizable perception systems. We also present a novel framework, Multi-Source RGB-IR Uncertainty Quantification (MSRIQ), which jointly models aleatoric and epistemic uncertainties across RGB and infrared imagery. This approach supports robust evaluation of open-vocabulary segmentation models across diverse environmental conditions. Our work sets a robustness-driven benchmark, providing key insights and laying the foundation for future research in open-world embodied AI perception. The benchmark and project page are available at https://aniruddhsikdar1.github.io/publications/pixel2perspective/

</details>


### [155] [Spark-H3: Adaptive Block Sparse Attention for Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24256)
*Zecheng Tang,Jiaye Fu,Youtian Lin,Xinyao Rao,Liuxuan Sun,Jian Zhang,Yao Yao,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的块稀疏注意力方法 Spark-Attn（含 Spark-Reblock 与 Spark-Reweight），在 MiniMax-H3 上实例化为 Spark-H3，10% 精确块密度下实现 2.49× DiT 加速、24.81 dB PSNR，达到训练免稀疏注意力的 SOTA 质量-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频序列使注意力成为视频扩散模型的主要开销。现有块稀疏注意力（BSA）存在两点缺陷：固定 token 分组会把注意力偏好不同的 token 分到同组；对压缩块做均匀 key 平均会低估其注意力质量（attention mass）。

Method: Spark-Attn 为免训练 BSA 方法，由两部分组成：Spark-Reblock 按注意力偏好递归地对 token 分组，同时保持规则块形状以保证 GPU 执行效率；Spark-Reweight 以全局平均 query 为锚点，为压缩块构造注意力加权的 key-value 表示与 logit 偏置，以校准其注意力质量。

Result: 在 MiniMax-H3 中实例化为 Spark-H3。设置（20 步去噪、345 帧、1344×768）：10% 精确块密度下获 2.49× DiT 加速，相对稠密输出 PSNR 24.81 dB；提升至 20% 密度时 PSNR 达 26.76 dB，仍有 2.26× DiT 加速。

Conclusion: Spark-H3 在视频扩散模型的免训练块稀疏注意力上取得 SOTA 的质量-效率权衡，验证了“注意力偏好感知重分块 + 压缩块注意力质量校准”的有效性。

Abstract: Long video sequences make attention a substantial cost in video diffusion models. Block sparse attention (BSA) reduces this cost by computing selected blocks exactly while compressing the remaining blocks. However, fixed token grouping can place tokens with dissimilar attention preferences in the same group, while uniform key averaging can underestimate the attention mass of compressed blocks. We introduce Spark-Attn, a training-free BSA method comprising Spark-Reblock and Spark-Reweight. Spark-Reblock recursively groups tokens by attention preference while preserving regular block shapes for efficient GPU execution. Spark-Reweight uses the global mean query as an anchor to construct attention-weighted key-value representations and logit biases for the compressed blocks, calibrating their attention mass. We instantiate Spark-Attn in MiniMax-H3 as Spark-H3. At 10% exact-block density, Spark-H3 delivers a 2.49$\times$ DiT speedup and reaches 24.81 dB PSNR against the corresponding dense outputs on MiniMax-H3 (20 denoising steps, 345 frames, 1344$\times$768). Increasing the density to 20% further improves PSNR to 26.76 dB while still achieving a 2.26$\times$ DiT speedup. These results demonstrate that Spark-H3 achieves a state-of-the-art quality-efficiency trade-off for training-free BSA on video diffusion models.

</details>


### [156] [DetailScale-SR: Decoupling Restoration and Continuous Detail Control for Real-World Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24212)
*Shengchuan Gao,Zhongjie Duan,Yingda Chen,Jingjing Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DetailScale-SR——一种偏好条件化的真实世界图像超分框架，可在固定低分辨率输入下连续控制重建细节量，实现从保守恢复到超参考增强的平滑过渡，并在文本恢复上优于现有生成式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景超分中"更多细节"并非总被需要；同一退化图像可能依用户意图需要克制恢复、忠实重建或增强视觉细节。现有方法依赖后处理或反复改写提示词，难以在不改变场景内容的前提下灵活调节细节水平。

Method: 构建偏好条件化框架，学习一族连续的重建结果，而非依赖后处理或提示词重写；将内容恢复与细节偏好解耦，使输入与语义约束保持一致的同时，通过连续偏好变量从保守恢复平滑过渡到丰富纹理。

Result: 可控范围超越常规恢复：可增强高频结构、生成比配对高分辨率参考更具感知丰富度的纹理；在文本丰富场景（尤其中文海报与标牌）中，在同等文本引导下比既有生成式超分方法恢复出更清晰、更忠实于字符的文本。

Conclusion: DetailScale-SR 统一了连续细节控制、超参考增强与语义重要内容的可靠恢复三项能力，为按用户意图定制超分结果提供了单一可控框架。

Abstract: Real-world image super-resolution often seeks the most detailed plausible reconstruction, although more detail is not always desirable. The same degraded image may require restrained restoration, faithful reconstruction, or enhanced visual detail according to user intent. We present DetailScale-SR, a preference-conditioned framework that continuously controls reconstructed detail for a fixed low-resolution input without altering the scene being restored. Rather than relying on post-processing or repeatedly rewriting prompts, it learns a continuous family of reconstructions while separating content recovery from detail preference. This enables a smooth transition from conservative restoration to richly textured results under consistent input and semantic constraints. Its controllable range also extends beyond conventional restoration: DetailScale-SR can enhance high-frequency structures and generate perceptually richer textures than the paired high-resolution reference. Text restoration is another distinct strength. For text-rich scenes, especially Chinese posters and signs, it recovers more legible and character-faithful text than prior generative SR methods under matched textual guidance. DetailScale-SR therefore unifies continuous detail control, beyond-reference enhancement, and reliable recovery of semantically important content. Our model is available at https://modelscope.cn/models/DiffSynth-Studio/Qwen-Image-2.1-SR

</details>


### [157] [GenRF: Generative Registration-free Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24157)
*Linfeng Tang,Zhijun Fan,Yang Chen,Zizhuo Li,Hao Zhang,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GenRF，基于预训练FLUX（LoRA微调）在生成式基础模型框架下统一处理未配准、退化及视场缺失的多模态图像融合，无需显式配准与预处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实采集存在几何差异（视差、视场不匹配）与退化，现有方法依赖显式配准（大视差难、缺对应区），独立复原未利用跨模态信息，复原-融合方法假设对齐而对误配准脆弱。

Method: 从生成式基础模型视角重审问题：交叉注意力实现位置无关的互补信息聚合，生成先验支持退化恢复与缺失区域推断。据此用LoRA适配预训练FLUX，联合增强退化观测、推断缺失跨模态上下文并融合，不显式估计形变场。

Result: 真实红外与可见光数据上，GenRF可从无配准、退化图像直接获得高质量融合，无需预处理，并在误配准下提升下游任务性能；还可零任务微调泛化到误配准多曝光与多聚焦融合。

Conclusion: 生成式基础模型为免配准、抗退化的多模态融合提供了统一范式，具备跨任务泛化潜力。

Abstract: Multi-modal image fusion combines complementary observations to improve scene visibility and perception. However, in real-world acquisition, differences in sensing principles and configurations introduce geometric discrepancies, while degradations further compromise source quality. Existing methods typically rely on explicit registration, yet large parallax complicates correspondence estimation, while mismatched fields of view leave some regions without cross-modal counterparts. Meanwhile, independent restoration underuses cross-modal information, whereas restoration-fusion methods assuming alignment remain vulnerable to misregistration. This study revisits misaligned and degraded image fusion in the era of generative foundation models, in which cross-attention enables position-agnostic complementary information aggregation, while generative priors support degradation recovery and inference in missing regions. Building on this perspective, we propose GenRF, which adapts pretrained FLUX through LoRA to jointly enhance degraded observations, infer missing cross-modal context, and fuse complementary information without explicitly estimating deformation fields. Extensive experiments on real-world infrared and visible captures show that GenRF delivers high-quality fusion directly from unregistered, degraded images without preprocessing, while improving downstream task performance under misalignment. Additional experiments demonstrate that GenRF can generalize to misaligned multi-exposure and multi-focus image fusion without task-specific fine-tuning. Project: https://linfeng-tang.github.io/GenRF.

</details>


### [158] [UltraTex: Aligning Physical Knowledge for High-Fidelity PBR Texture Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24941)
*Jingzhi Bao,Xiaohe Ma,Hong Li,Yuanming Hu,Xiaoguang Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UltraTex：级联多视角扩散框架，通过注入VLM材料语义先验与光度表征对齐，从单张参考图生成高质量PBR贴图（反照率+金属粗糙度）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图生成PBR贴图高度病态：观测外观纠缠了材料身份、表面反射率与光照。现有生成方法虽能产出逼真反照率图，但难以推断准确的金属度与粗糙度属性，导致重光照效果不佳。作者主张超越"外观匹配"，将物理知识融入多视角扩散生成过程。

Method: 级联多视角框架：(1) 首先联合预测反照率与世界空间着色法线，以获得更精细的反照率图；(2) 以生成的反照率、参考图及材料语义先验为条件预测金属粗糙度（MR）图。语义注入方面，轻量适配器将VLM隐层特征转换为本征材料表征token，提供部件级知识锚点；物理建模方面，采用光度表征对齐，将扩散中间特征对齐到受控光照下真实材料渲染结果，使金属度/粗糙度变化可通过高光强度、镜面锐度和局部反射对比体现，无需显式光照监督。

Result: 大量实验表明UltraTex可生成高质量纹理，在视觉保真度、材料准确性与物理合理性方面优于现有开源及商业方法。

Conclusion: 将物理渲染知识与语义先验嵌入多视角扩散，可显著提升单图PBR反演中金属度与粗糙度的估计精度与重光照可信度。

Abstract: Generating physically based rendering (PBR) texture maps for a 3D mesh from a single reference image is highly ambiguous, as the observed appearance entangles material identity, surface reflectance, and illumination. While current generative methods produce realistic albedo maps, they still struggle to infer accurate metallic and roughness attributes for relighting. We present UltraTex, a cascaded multi-view framework that moves beyond appearance matching by integrating physical knowledge into multi-view diffusion-based PBR generation. UltraTex first jointly predicts albedo and world-space shading normals to generate more detailed albedo maps, and then predicts metallic-roughness (MR) maps conditioned on the generated albedo, reference image, and material semantic priors. To inject material semantics, a lightweight adapter transforms hidden-layer features of a vision-language model (VLM) into intrinsic material representation tokens, providing part-level knowledge anchors for MR estimation. To model material-light interaction, photometric representation alignment guides intermediate diffusion features toward ground-truth material renderings under controlled lighting. This makes metallic and roughness variations observable through highlight intensity, specular sharpness, and local reflectance contrast, providing stronger guidance for fine-grained material prediction without explicit lighting supervision. Extensive experiments show that UltraTex generates high-quality textures and outperforms existing open-source and commercial methods in visual fidelity, material accuracy, and physical plausibility.

</details>


### [159] [Read Together, Answer Apart: How Earlier Questions Help Vision-Language Models Look](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24785)
*Xinyi Guo,Kaile Huang,Xitong Ling,Mingyi He,Chende Zheng,Jianyu Xu,Chao He,Dongqing Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过逐题配对比较22个开源VLM的单问与共问答案，揭示共问的双向效应机制，并提出免训练解码掩码"Read Together, Answer Apart"以保留跨题视觉互助、隔离答案干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测仅在提示级别报告共问（同一图像多题同提示）的准确率增益与幻觉，结果相互矛盾，且未追踪单条答案如何变化，缺乏对共问机制的细粒度理解。

Method: 对22个开源权重VLM（1B–35B）逐题配对单问与共问答案；在DCI数据集上量化错误修复率与正确转错率；借助注意力读出与层窗口干预，在14个支持自定义注意力掩码的模型上定位跨题视觉互助发生的层与阶段；据此设计免训练解码掩码，保留共享prefill、隔离答案解码时对其他题干词的注意力。

Result: 共问下所有模型修复15.4%–29.8%的单问错误，正确转错率随模型强度从30.8%降至4.7%；前序题目在早中层prefill将视觉信息读入问题token，助后续题目聚焦目标，且跨非重叠区域累积；阻断该注意力在14个模型中均降低定位与准确率；所提方法在DCI上提升12/14模型（最高+4.71pp，弥补与单问差距的70%），并在WebUI、FinTabNet、GQA上分别提升10、8、5个模型（最高+11.4、+5.3、+1.1pp）。

Conclusion: 共问净效应是"跨题视觉互助"与"答案解码阶段题干干扰"两种对立效应的叠加；二者在时间与机制上可分，因而可通过仅在解码阶段施加掩码、保留prefill共享来同时获益，实现无需训练的即插即用改进。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are often asked several questions about the same image in a single prompt (co-asking), yet existing evaluations report conflicting accuracy gains and hallucinations at the prompt level without tracking how individual answers change. By pairing each question's single-question and co-asked answers across 22 open-weight VLMs (1B-35B), we find that erratic net shifts reflect two opposing effects: on Densely Captioned Images (DCI), every model repairs 15.4%-29.8% of its single-question errors under co-asking, while the share of correct answers turned wrong falls from 30.8% on weaker models to 4.7% on stronger ones. Across all 14 models supporting custom attention masks, we show through attention readouts and layer-window interventions that earlier same-image questions read visual information into their question tokens in the early-to-middle layers during prefill, helping later questions concentrate image attention onto their queried targets. This visual help accumulates across the scene even across non-overlapping regions, whereas blocking earlier questions from attending to the image lowers both target localization and accuracy in all 14 models. While cross-question help is established during shared question reading, part of the co-asking damage enters later during answer decoding when the decoder attends to other question stems. We therefore introduce Read Together, Answer Apart, a training-free decoding mask that keeps shared prefill intact and isolates each answer from other stems, improving co-asking accuracy on 12 of 14 models on DCI (up to +4.71 percentage points, closing 70% of the pooled gap to asking alone) and on 10, 8, and 5 of the 14 models on WebUI, FinTabNet, and GQA (up to +11.4, +5.3, and +1.1 points).

</details>


### [160] [EgoJev: Finding Evidence in Egocentric Video by Reading VLM Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24616)
*Chunggi Lee,Hanspeter Pfister*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoJev：利用冻结VLM自身输出分布做无训练帧选择的自我中心视频问答方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时自我中心视频的问题往往只依赖某一短暂瞬间（如手放下物体的一秒），均匀采样的少量帧容易漏掉该证据。现有无训练方法依赖独立图文模型打分或让VLM直接写出时间，均不够稳健。

Method: 受控实验发现：在候选帧带标签时，VLM从输出分布中"选择"某一帧比图文相似度打分或生成时间跨度更能准确指向证据，且可在一次决策中比较全部候选。据此提出EgoJev：读取带标签缩略图上证据位置的分布，再在分布指向的窗口内二次观察，并按所得分布的分数位放置少量答案帧。单个冻结VLM同时完成选择、回答与证据时间输出，无需第二模型；设备可在录制时编码粗粒度缩略图。

Result: 使用Qwen3.5-4B与8帧答案帧时，QAEgo4D准确率由均匀采样的51.2%提升至62.0%，并在QAEgo4D与MyEgo上优于近期无训练帧选择器；单层级版本可在Mac与iPhone上运行。

Conclusion: 将帧选择转化为冻结VLM的候选选择问题，是一种简单、免训练且可端侧部署的长视频证据定位与问答范式。

Abstract: Questions about long egocentric video often depend on one brief moment, such as the second a hand puts an object down. A vision-language model (VLM) that sees a few evenly spaced frames usually misses it. Training-free methods rank frames with a separate image-text model or ask the VLM to write a time. In a controlled test, the VLM itself locates the moment better when it chooses among labeled frames, and we read this choice from its output distribution. With the same candidates, this choice lands on the evidence more often than image-text scores or a written time span, and it compares all candidates in one decision. Based on this finding, we propose EgoJev, a training-free frame selector for wearable assistants. EgoJev reads where the evidence is from labeled thumbnails and looks again at the windows it points to. It places its few answer frames at the quantiles of the resulting distribution. One frozen VLM selects, answers and returns the evidence time, with no second model. A wearable device can encode its coarse thumbnails while it records. With Qwen3.5-4B and 8 answer frames, EgoJev raises QAEgo4D accuracy from 51.2% with uniform sampling to 62.0%, and it outperforms recent training-free frame selectors on QAEgo4D and MyEgo. A single-level version runs on a Mac and an iPhone.

</details>


### [161] [SCRiD: Spatial and Contextual References for Generalizable AI-Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24524)
*Hao Tan,Zekai Zuo,Boyang Chen,Jinghui Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SCRiD框架，通过空间与上下文参照互补解读视频差异，实现可泛化的AI生成视频检测，仅用4个生成器训练即在88个生成器/任务子集上平均ACC 72.72%、AP 74.23%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然运动与内容变化会与合成伪影相似，导致孤立视频差异不可靠，检测器难以泛化到未见生成器。

Method: 构建三个组件：(1) 匹配坐标残差响应MCR，在匹配亚像素采样条件下刻画运动与坐标参照下的时序残差关联；(2) 真实条件分数自适应RCSA，从真实视频学习内容条件去噪参照，在多个噪声尺度与质量视角下探测其响应以引导特征自适应；(3) 可逆证据交换REE，通过可逆辛更新实现两路证据信息交互。

Result: 仅用4个生成器合成视频训练，在6个基准、88个生成器或任务子集上平均ACC 72.72%、AP 74.23%，较最强基线分别提升3.24与0.81个百分点；消融与鲁棒性实验验证组件有效性与常见后处理下的稳定性。

Conclusion: 互补的空间与上下文参照建模可有效区分真实内容变化与合成伪影，提升跨生成器泛化能力，且对后处理扰动保持稳健。

Abstract: Generalizable AI-generated video detection remains challenging because natural motion and content variations can resemble synthesis artifacts, making isolated video discrepancies unreliable indicators of authenticity. To address this challenge, we propose Spatial and Contextual Reference-informed Detection (SCRiD), a framework that interprets video variations through complementary spatial and contextual references. Specifically, we introduce Matched-Coordinate Residual Response (MCR) to characterize temporal residual associations under motion and coordinate references with matched subpixel sampling conditions. We further develop Real-Conditioned Score Adaptation (RCSA), which learns a content-conditioned denoising reference from authentic videos and probes its responses across multiple noise scales and quality views to guide feature adaptation. To integrate these complementary sources of evidence, we design Reversible Evidence Exchange (REE) to enable information exchange through reversible symplectic updates. We train SCRiD using synthetic videos from only 4 generators and evaluate it across 88 generator or task subsets spanning 6 benchmarks. Our method achieves average ACC and AP of 72.72% and 74.23%, respectively, outperforming the strongest baseline by 3.24 and 0.81 percentage points.Ablation and robustness experiments further validate the effectiveness of the proposed components and the stability of SCRiD under common post-processing operations.

</details>


### [162] [NativeSeg: Native Vision Language Segmentation of 3D Computed Tomography via Spatial Visual Tokens and Verified Local Zoom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24443)
*Chengqian Ma,Gaole Dai,Zijie Meng,Weixuan Jin,Yijie Du,Yuexiao Ma,Xiawu Zheng,Fei Chao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NativeSeg：在LoRA微调的Qwen3.8-27B上实现原生稠密掩码解码与体积级分诊，不依赖外部SAM解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本提示3D CT分割需全切片上下文与精细边界，且80%以上被提示切片无目标；现有医学VLM多非原生分割器，靠1D [SEG]向量外接SAM、有损离散码本量化、或无状态外部SAM框提示，缺乏空切片筛选与空间状态记忆。

Method: 在VLM上层2D空间视觉token上直接稠密解码：融合五切片U-Net通路生成置信图；原生存在门控(<pres>)实现体积分诊，>91%独立评估零缩放即终止；20格多尺度误差门控(<err>)定位不确定边界；仅当预测误差下降时接受缩放更新；多轮局部放大自诊断。

Result: 仅用成人CT训练，Round 0 DSC较离散码本与1D [SEG]读出分别提升+19.81与+7.04；成人86.81%、零样本儿科87.04%；可安全细化2D nnU-Net（成人89.52%、儿科86.86%）、3D SegVol（86.81%）与3D VoxTell（89.85%）。

Conclusion: VLM原生空间token即可胜任稠密体积分割，配以存在门控与误差自诊断可兼顾效率与边界精度，并对现有分割模型形成互补式细化。

Abstract: Text prompted 3D computed tomography (CT) segmentation requires both full slice context and fine boundary precision across volumes where over $80\%$ of organ prompted slices lack the queried target. Existing medical vision language models (VLMs) are largely non native segmenters: they delegate pixel delineation outside the VLM by compressing reasoning into a 1D [SEG] vector for an external Segment Anything Model (SAM) decoder, quantizing masks into lossy discrete codebooks, or issuing box prompts to stateless external SAM tools without empty slice screening or spatial state memory. We introduce NativeSeg, a native vision language segmentation framework on Low Rank Adaptation (LoRA) adapted Qwen3.8-27B that performs dense mask decoding, volumetric triage, spatial error self diagnosis, and multiround local zoom directly over the VLM's 2D spatial visual tokens without external SAM decoders. NativeSeg decodes confidence maps from upper layer 2D spatial tokens fused with a five slice U-Net pathway, halts at zero zooms on $>91\%$ of standalone evaluations via a native presence gate (<pres>), localizes uncertain boundaries via a 20 cell multiscale error gate (<err>), and accepts zoomed updates only when predicted error decreases. Trained solely on adult CT, NativeSeg improves Round $0$ DSC by $+19.81$ and $+7.04$ points over discrete codebook and 1D [SEG] readouts on the identical backbone, reaches $86.81\%$ adult and $87.04\%$ zero shot pediatric DSC, and safely refines 2D nnU-Net ($89.52\%$ adult; $86.86\%$ pediatric), 3D SegVol ($86.81\%$), and 3D VoxTell ($89.85\%$).

</details>


### [163] [AggCLIP: Aggregating CLIP for Zero-Shot Multimodal Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24643)
*Zhi Li,Hanyu Wang,Fei Shen,Zhengtao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AggCLIP框架，通过几何感知与上下文感知空间聚合模块以及双流提示对比损失，改进冻结CLIP在零样本多模态异常检测中的细粒度缺陷定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CLIP的零样本多模态异常检测存在三个问题：(i) 3D到2D点云渲染造成严重几何信息损失；(ii) 原始CLIP patch嵌入产生空间噪声大的2D特征图，且模态融合过浅；(iii) 处理流解耦导致严重的跨模态语义错配。

Method: 在不训练目标数据的前提下适配冻结CLIP：GSA模块通过密度自适应邻域加权细化反投影的3D点特征；CSA模块用轻量可训练适配器平滑多尺度2D patch嵌入；DSPC损失在点云流与RGB流之间对齐正常/异常的抽象概念，抑制语义漂移。

Result: 在MVTec 3D-AD与Eyecandies基准的one-vs-rest及跨数据集零样本设置下均达SOTA，像素级AUPRO最多超过最强对比方法2.9%。

Conclusion: 显式引入局部几何结构与视觉空间上下文可有效提升冻结CLIP在零样本多模态异常检测中的外观与几何缺陷定位性能。

Abstract: Zero-Shot Multimodal Anomaly Detection (ZSMAD) aims to identify both appearance and geometric defects in novel categories without relying on target-specific training data, making it especially valuable in real-world scenarios constrained by data scarcity, privacy concerns, or high annotation costs. While recent approaches successfully adapt vision-language models like CLIP to achieve this open-vocabulary generalization, they still face significant challenges: (i) severe geometric information loss during 3D-to-2D point cloud rendering; (ii) spatially noisy 2D feature maps produced by raw CLIP patch embeddings, compounded by shallow late-stage modality fusion; and (iii) severe cross-modal semantic misalignment due to decoupled processing streams. To address these limitations, we propose AggCLIP, a novel framework that adapts frozen CLIP representations for ZSMAD by explicitly incorporating local geometric structure and visual spatial context. Specifically, to accurately localize fine-grained defects, we design a Geometry-Aware Spatial Aggregation (GSA) module that refines back-projected 3D point features via density-adaptive neighborhood weighting, alongside a Context-Aware Spatial Aggregation (CSA) module that smooths multi-scale 2D patch embeddings using a lightweight trainable adapter. Furthermore, to prevent semantic drift between modalities, we introduce a Dual-Stream Prompt Contrastive (DSPC) loss, which aligns the abstract concepts of normality and abnormality across the point cloud and RGB streams. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed AggCLIP framework achieves state-of-the-art performance on the MVTec 3D-AD and Eyecandies benchmarks under both one-vs-rest and cross-dataset zero-shot settings, notably surpassing the strongest competitive methods by up to 2.9% in pixel-level AUPRO.

</details>


### [164] [Fuse Before Reconstruct: A Novel Reconstruction Paradigm for Multimodal Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24541)
*Zhi Li,Hanyu Wang,Qiyu Chen,Fei Shen,Zhengtao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"先融合后重建"（Fuse Before Reconstruct, FBR）的多模态特征重建范式，用于工业异常检测，在 MVTec 3D-AD 与 Eyecandies 上分别达到 98.3% 和 92.6% 的 I-AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态联合分析的两种特征重建范式存在固有缺陷：模态内重建忽视跨模态互补信息；跨模态重建易产生投影误差与一对多映射歧义（如相同纹理映射到多种几何），导致检测失败。

Method: 提出 FBR 范式：先融合多模态特征再重建。具体包含三部分：(1) 双向跨模态注意力机制，通过通道对齐与动态空间先验整合实现图像与点云特征的深度互补并缓解投影偏差；(2) 可学习的逐像素门控融合策略，在优化局部特征组合的同时保持多模态结构一致性；(3) 双路径协同重建，从融合表示中恢复各原始模态特征，并借跨模态一致性约束消除映射歧义。

Result: 在 MVTec 3D-AD 上取得 98.3% I-AUROC，在 Eyecandies 上取得 92.6% I-AUROC，分别较此前最优方法提升 +2.9% 与 +4.1%。

Conclusion: 将重建置于融合之后可规避投影误差与一对多映射歧义，同时保留跨模态互补性，是一种有效的多模态工业异常检测新范式。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly detection plays a pivotal role in precision manufacturing, where joint analysis of multimodal data emerges as a critical approach to enhance detection accuracy. Feature reconstruction, a dominant paradigm for such joint analysis, is primarily implemented through intra-modal and cross-modal methods, both of which are plagued by significant limitations. Intra-modal reconstruction neglects complementary cross-modal information, while its cross-modal counterpart is prone to projection errors and ambiguous one-to-many mappings (e.g., same textures mapping to multiple geometries), leading to detection failures. To address these limitations, we propose a novel feature reconstruction paradigm, Fuse Before Reconstruct (FBR), which first fuses multimodal features and then performs reconstruction for anomaly detection. Firstly, we propose a bidirectional cross-modal attention mechanism to achieve deep feature complementarity between image and point cloud features, mitigating projection bias through channel alignment and dynamic spatial prior integration. Besides, a learnable pixel-wise gated fusion strategy is adopted to preserve multi-modal structural consistency while optimizing local feature combination. Finally, dual-path collaborative reconstruction is implemented to recover original modal features from the fused representations, where cross-modal consistency constraints effectively eliminate mapping ambiguities. Extensive experiments show that FBR achieves 98.3% I-AUROC on MVTec 3D-AD and 92.6% I-AUROC on Eyecandies, surpassing previous state-of-the-art methods by +2.9% and +4.1%, respectively.

</details>


### [165] [Training Denoising Models of Any Architecture Without Stored Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24223)
*Christos Koutlis,Ioannis Kompatsiaris,Symeon Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出教师监督训练：任意架构与规模的扩散/流模型可从随机初始化训练，无需存储图像，仅需回归教师的velocity。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究预训练去噪模型能否替代其训练数据集，以实现大数据集的存储节省，并适用于未公开或私有数据。

Method: 学生在滚动的噪声样本池上回归教师的velocity；样本沿随机步数的轨迹推进，以获得独立、稠密且近似连续的噪声水平。

Result: 实验涵盖6个族的12个教师模型与6个数据集；在CIFAR-10上，学生比教师小4倍时优于同网络标准训练；与教师同规模时FID差距为0.75–1.92；优于在教师生成图像上训练及无教师初始化的步数削减方法。

Conclusion: 教师监督训练可在无原始图像条件下有效训练扩散与流模型，为存储受限及数据不可获取场景提供可行方案。

Abstract: Can a pretrained denoising model replace the dataset used to train it? We investigate this question through teacher-supervised training, showing for the first time that continuous-time diffusion and flow models of any architecture and size can be trained from random initialization without stored images. Our students regress the teacher's velocity on a rolling pool of noisy samples, advanced along trajectories of random step counts to obtain independent, dense and approximately continuous noise levels. This saves considerable storage for large datasets and applies even when the data are unreleased or private. Our experiments include 12 teachers of 6 families and 6 datasets. Our method outperforms standard training of the same network on CIFAR-10 for students 4x smaller than the teacher, comes within 0.75 to 1.92 FID of it at the teacher's size and surpasses training on teacher-generated images and step reduction methods without teacher-based initialization. Our code is available at https://github.com/mever-team/ddm.

</details>


### [166] [Why Multi-Source Audio Fails to Compose: A Geometric Diagnosis and Its Remedy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24410)
*Anurag Singh,Prajwal Singh,Shanmuganathan Raman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文形式化多源音频到图像生成中的"几何位移"现象，揭示音频token在CLIP文本token空间中位置偏移、语义无锚定导致组合失败，并提出无需重训扩散骨干的多token方法与A*空间分离损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多源音频到图像生成中，音频token在CLIP文本token空间内处于几何上位移、语义上无锚定的位置，扩散U-Net缺乏先验去可靠关注并将其锚定到不同空间区域，导致一致的组合性失败。

Method: 通过两级聚类分析（原始编码器层与投影后CLIP token层）比较BEATs与CLAP嵌入，提出类内紧凑性、与CLIP文本流形的角度对齐、token间空间分离三项几何性质；进而引入多token扩展：为每个音频源分配独立占位token（无需重训扩散骨干），并采用结合Attend-and-Excite与A*空间分离损失的混合推理目标以消除残余跨token干扰。构建来自VGGSound的多音频组合基准（VGG2、VGG3、VGGN），并将六项标准音频-图像指标扩展到多源设置。

Result: CLAP在三项几何性质上全面优于BEATs，轮廓系数提升超过3倍，Audio-Image Content得分亦提升。在五个基准、四个强基线对比下，方法在与组合保真度最直接相关的指标上表现突出：AIC最高提升2.25倍，两源生成FID降低近2倍。

Conclusion: 提出"几何位移"作为将学习嵌入投影到预训练对比空间时跨模态生成系统的诊断框架，并验证基于该分析的推理时方法能有效改善多源组合生成的保真度。

Abstract: Multi-source audio-to-image generation exhibits consistent compositional failures when audio tokens occupy geometrically displaced, semantically unanchored positions in the CLIP text-token space, where the diffusion U-Net lacks the prior to reliably attend to them or ground them in distinct spatial regions. We formalize this as the geometric displacement phenomenon and expose it through a two-level clustering analysis comparing BEATs and CLAP embeddings at the raw encoder and projected CLIP token levels. Three geometric properties emerge as important: intra-class compactness, angular alignment with the CLIP text manifold, and inter-token spatial separation. We show that CLAP, whose contrastive language-audio pretraining aligns embeddings with natural language semantics, substantially outperforms BEATs on all three properties, improving the silhouette score by more than 3x and the Audio-Image Content score as well. Building on this analysis, we introduce a multi-token extension that requires no diffusion-backbone retraining and assigns each audio source to a distinct placeholder token in the CLIP tokenizer, complemented by a mixed inference-time objective that combines Attend-and-Excite with an A* spatial separation loss to resolve residual cross-token interference. For evaluation, we construct multi-audio compositional benchmarks from VGGSound (VGG2, VGG3, and VGGN) and extend six standard audio-image metrics to the multi-source setting. Across five benchmarks and four strong baselines, our method achieves strong results on the metrics most directly tied to compositional faithfulness, with AIC improvements of up to 2.25x over the strongest baseline and FID reduced by nearly 2x on two-source generation. We propose geometric displacement as a diagnostic framework for cross-modal generation systems that project learned embeddings into a pretrained contrastive space.

</details>


### [167] [DrawScore: Evaluating Generated CAD Models against Engineering Drawings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24681)
*Haorui Chen,Shiteng Lu,Zhanyu Guo,Zaixi Li,Yunhe Ni,Yaosong Du,Yichen Xiang,Xiande Huang,Rui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出体积IoU、Chamfer Distance与表面F-score对局部CAD错误不敏感，提出统一积分形式分析其衰减阶数，并据此设计DrawScore：将模型投影为带可见/隐藏线的三视图，沿投影曲线施加饱和惩罚，使局部错误权重仅线性衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动CAD生成评测指标（volumetric IoU、Chamfer Distance、表面F-score）几乎不惩罚仅占部件体积或表面积很小比例的局部错误，如孔位偏移或槽开在错误面上，而这类错误对制造至关重要。需要一种对局部几何错误敏感的度量。

Method: 将现有指标形式化为统一积分形式，推导局部错误权重随其尺寸以(k+q)次幂衰减，其中k为积分域维度、q由惩罚函数决定。基于该分析提出DrawScore：把生成模型与参考模型投影为三张正交视图（含可见线与隐藏线，类似工程图），在投影曲线上施加饱和惩罚，使局部错误权重随尺寸仅线性衰减。另将其用作强化学习奖励与测试时验证器（无需3D真值或额外奖励模型）。

Result: 受控实验验证了预测的衰减阶数；在现有CAD生成智能体输出上，DrawScore能揭示被IoU评为近乎完美的输出中的错误；作为强化学习奖励可提升生成质量；在工程图到CAD生成任务中可作为无需3D真值的测试时验证器。

Conclusion: DrawScore通过工程图式三视图投影与曲线上的饱和惩罚，实现了对局部CAD错误线性衰减的敏感性，弥补了体积/表面类指标对局部缺陷的盲区，并兼具奖励函数与测试时验证器功能。

Abstract: Automatic CAD generation is commonly evaluated with volumetric IoU, Chamfer Distance, and surface F-score, which barely penalize local errors that occupy only a small fraction of a part's volume or surface area, such as a misplaced hole or a slot on the wrong face, even though such errors are critical to manufacturing. We formalize these metrics in a unified integral form and show that the weight of a local error decays as the $(k+q)$-th power of its size, where $k$ is the dimension of the integration domain and $q$ is determined by the penalty function. Guided by this analysis, we propose DrawScore, which projects both models into three orthographic views with visible and hidden lines, as in engineering drawings, and applies a saturating penalty along the projected curves, so that the weight of a local error decays only linearly. Controlled experiments confirm the predicted decay orders, and on outputs of existing CAD generation agents, DrawScore exposes errors in outputs that IoU rates as nearly perfect. DrawScore further improves generation as a reinforcement learning reward and, for CAD generation from engineering drawings, serves as a test-time verifier without 3D ground truth or an additional reward model. Our code is available at https://github.com/UESTC-Simon/DrawScore.

</details>


### [168] [ALIVE: Bring Foundation Features to Life with Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24579)
*Rong Zou,Haiqing Hao,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ALIVE提出一种基于事件相机的方法，在两次图像观测之间增量更新DINOv3基础模型的冻结多层特征，无需重跑图像编码器，在检测、分割、深度任务上全面优于现有事件方法并大幅降低计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型特征在图像观测之间会因场景变化而过时（stale），刷新特征需要新帧并重复运行昂贵的图像编码器，因此需要一种低成本的特征更新机制。

Method: 以锚点图像为起点，利用后续事件流对冻结DINOv3编码器提取的多层特征进行"搬运+校正"（transport and correct）；轻量级updater模块端到端训练，仅用合成数据、无需下游任务监督，并可零适配直接迁移到真实事件流。

Result: 在3个真实数据集、5个任务-数据集设定（目标检测、语义分割、深度估计）中所有指标均优于对比的事件方法；DSEC上锚帧滞后200ms时，检测mAP提升30.9、分割mIoU提升11.6、深度AbsRel改善15.9%；相比图像重编码，FLOPs减少82.4%，延迟降低2.5倍。

Conclusion: 事件驱动的增量特征更新可作为图像编码重算的高效替代方案，兼具精度与效率优势，且具备合成到真实的良好泛化能力。

Abstract: Visual foundation models provide powerful representations for diverse tasks, but their features become stale as visual content changes between image observations. Refreshing them requires new frames and repeated runs of a computationally expensive image encoder. We introduce ALIVE, an event-based method that incrementally updates native foundation model features between image observations. Given an anchor image, ALIVE uses subsequent events to transport and correct existing multi-layer features extracted by a frozen DINOv3 encoder. Its lightweight updater is trained end-to-end using only synthetic data, without downstream task supervision, and transfers directly to real-world event streams without any adaptation. Across three real-world datasets and five task-dataset settings spanning object detection, semantic segmentation, and depth estimation, ALIVE outperforms all evaluated event-based methods in every metric. On DSEC, when the anchor frame is 200,ms old, ALIVE improves over anchor-image predictions by 30.9,mAP in detection, 11.6,mIoU in segmentation, and 15.9% in depth AbsRel. Compared with image re-encoding, ALIVE reduces FLOPs by 82.4% and latency by a factor of 2.5.

</details>


### [169] [Every Query Routes Itself: Accelerating Video Diffusion Transformers with Per-Query Routed Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24479)
*Zhen Huang,Shuwei Li,Ruizhe Yao,Xinrui Chen,Danyi Liu,Haoyuan Liang,Zhiyu Ye,Qiuyan Sun,Bingwei Chen,Yiyuan Li,Xu Zhou,Haohuan Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出免训练的逐查询路由稀疏注意力（PQRS），只对键聚类、让每个查询独立选择键簇并用簇心补偿未选簇，通过以键簇为遍历单位、按相同选择分桶查询并单次归并，实现视频扩散Transformer的加速与高保真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列自注意力是视频扩散Transformer推理成本的主要来源。现有基于聚类的稀疏注意力让同一查询簇内所有查询共享同一套键簇选择，作者指出这种"共享"会损失精度：在键簇数量与密度固定时，查询簇划分越细注意力越准确，最优情形是每个查询各自独立选择（即完全不做查询聚类）。

Method: PQRS为免训练方法：仅对键进行聚类；每个查询独立选择自己的键簇；对被选中的键簇做精确注意力，对未选中的键簇用其簇心进行补偿。由于逐查询选择使任何查询块都不再有共享键列表，实现上改为以键簇为遍历单位，把选择相同的查询分桶为稠密任务，并在一次归并中合并部分结果，同时沿用上一去噪步的固定softmax偏移量以保证数值一致。

Result: 在Wan与HunyuanVideo两族共四个文生视频/图生视频模型上，PQRS在所有对比的稀疏注意力方法中速度最快且输出最接近稠密结果：Wan2.1-T2V上PSNR达28.9 dB，HunyuanVideo上达35.4 dB，端到端加速1.66–2.10倍。

Conclusion: 查询侧精细化（直至逐查询独立路由）是提升稀疏注意力精度的关键，而免训练的PQRS在无需任何额外训练的前提下，同时取得当前最优的加速比与最高的稠密输出保真度，为视频扩散模型的高效推理提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Self-attention over long token sequences dominates the inference cost of video diffusion transformers. Clustering-based sparse attention clusters both queries and keys and lets all queries in a cluster share one selection of key clusters. We show that this sharing limits accuracy: with key clusters and density fixed, attention grows more accurate as the query clusters are refined, and is most accurate when each query selects on its own, which needs no query clustering at all. We therefore propose Per-Query Routed Sparse Attention (PQRS), a training-free method that clusters only keys, lets each query select its own key clusters, and compensates for the unselected ones with their centroids. Per-query selections leave no query tile with a shared key list, so PQRS traverses key clusters instead of queries, buckets queries with identical selections into dense tasks, and merges their partial results in one pass under a fixed softmax offset carried from the previous denoising step. On four text-to-video and image-to-video models from the Wan and HunyuanVideo families, PQRS is the fastest of all the sparse attention methods we evaluate and the closest to the dense output, reaching $28.9$ dB PSNR on Wan2.1-T2V and $35.4$ dB on HunyuanVideo at $1.66$--$2.10\times$ end-to-end speedup.

</details>


### [170] [ShoWorld: Shape-Faithful Multi-Hypothesis Occupancy World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24457)
*Zhiqiang Yan,Guangfa Wang,Yuan Wu,Jian Yang,Gim Hee Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ShoWorld 通过分离运动与形状表示解决占用世界模型中运动不确定性与形状畸变问题，在 Occ3D-nuScenes 上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 占用世界模型在预测运动物体时面临两大困难：一是运动不确定性，单一轨迹会把多种可能的未来平均成不合理的路径；二是形状畸变，反复体素化会在运动正确的情况下仍侵蚀或膨胀物体形状。

Method: 将运动与形状分离建模。运动预测器以场景布局、邻近智能体和未来自车轨迹为条件，预测概率化轨迹；每个假设渲染为占用后按概率融合，以保留多种未来。形状模块从局部几何预测带占用概率与偏移的亚体素点，这些点随物体刚性运动，体素占用由"至少一个被占点落入其中"的概率决定。

Result: 在 Occ3D-nuScenes 上达到 SOTA。在给定真值占用与自车轨迹条件下，整体 mIoU 与 IoU 分别提升 6.56 和 9.16 点，动态类别上分别提升 6.06 和 5.53 点。

Conclusion: 以概率化轨迹融合处理运动多模态、以刚性亚体素点表示抑制重体素化畸变，是一种有效的解耦式占用预测范式。

Abstract: Occupancy world models struggle to forecast moving objects due to motion uncertainty and shape distortion. A single trajectory can average plausible futures into an implausible path, and repeated re-voxelization can erode or inflate object shape even under correct motion. We introduce ShoWorld to address these challenges through separate motion and shape representations. The motion forecaster predicts probabilistic trajectories conditioned on scene layout, nearby agents and the future ego trajectory. Each hypothesis is rendered into occupancy and fused according to its probability to retain alternative futures. The shape component predicts sub-voxel points with occupancy probabilities and offsets from local geometry. These points move rigidly with the object, and voxel occupancy follows the probability that at least one occupied point falls within it. This representation reduces shape distortion under motion. ShoWorld achieves state-of-the-art results on Occ3D-nuScenes. With ground-truth occupancy and ego trajectory, it improves mIoU and IoU by 6.56 and 9.16 points overall and by 6.06 and 5.53 points on dynamic classes.

</details>


### [171] [OCTR2A: Real-Time RGB-to-BEV Trajectory Projection and Forecasting at Urban Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24352)
*Emanuele Iacobelli,Simone Teglia,Francesco Pro,Claudia Melis Tonti,Irene Amerini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OCTR2A模块化流水线，仅用城市基础设施上对角安装的两路RGB相机完成BEV轨迹重建与预测，并发布合成基准R3ST++。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路口轨迹预测研究普遍假设已有可靠BEV运动历史，而实际获取需LiDAR、航拍、HD地图或标定基础设施，代价高昂。作者关注这一"感知到预测"的上游问题，希望在仅用现有城市基础设施上可变高度安装的对角RGB相机的条件下完成BEV轨迹重建与预测。

Method: 提出OCTR2A（Opposite-Corner Trajectory Reconstruction and Anticipation Architecture）：集成单视图检测与类别精化、几何跨相机匹配、基于Transformer的BEV投影、以及自车中心GRU预测，采用级联训练，主要以软多尺度空间重叠损失监督。由于无公开基准，构建Blender合成基准R3ST++（基于SIND轨迹），提供同步对角视图、跨视图对应关系与BEV真值，并可作为部署支持仿真器用于站点特定微调。
