Result: 在10Hz、12帧观测与12帧预测设定下，OCTR2A在单张消费级GPU上实时达到投影-预测ADE/FDE为0.144/0.140～0.154/0.241米；在真值BEV历史上评估时，预测模块在SIND上较最强SOTA提升66.7%/41.7% ADE/FDE。TUMTraf-V2X迁移实验表明R3ST++预训练可支撑向不同传感配置的真实路侧域适配。

Conclusion: 仅依赖对角RGB相机即可实现实时BEV轨迹重建与预测；合成基准R3ST++兼具基准评测与部署前仿真微调双重作用，并具备向真实路侧场景迁移的潜力。

Abstract: Trajectory forecasting at urban intersections is often studied assuming that reliable bird's-eye-view (BEV) motion histories are available, which in practice requires costly resources such as LiDAR, aerial views, HD maps, or calibrated infrastructure. We address this upstream perception-to-forecasting problem by reconstructing and predicting agents' BEV trajectories only from paired opposite-corner RGB cameras mounted on existing urban infrastructure at variable heights at inference time. We introduce the Opposite-Corner Trajectory Reconstruction and Anticipation Architecture (OCTR2A), a modular pipeline integrating per-view detection with class refinement, geometric cross-camera matching, transformer-based BEV projection, and egocentric GRU forecasting, trained in cascade and mainly supervised with a soft multi-scale spatial-overlap loss. Since no public benchmark provides this sensing configuration, we introduce R3ST++, a Blender-based synthetic benchmark built from SIND trajectories that provides synchronized opposite-corner views, cross-view correspondences, and BEV ground truth, while also serving as a deployment-support simulator for site-specific fine-tuning. Experiments on these datasets show that, with 12 observed and 12 predicted frames at 10\,Hz, OCTR2A achieves projection~-~forecasting ADE/FDE of 0.144/0.140~-~0.154/0.241~meters in real time on a single consumer GPU. Evaluated on ground-truth BEV histories, the forecasting module improves over the strongest state-of-the-art methods on SIND by 66.7\%/41.7\% ADE/FDE. Transfer experiments on TUMTraf-V2X show that R3ST++ pretraining supports adaptation to real-world roadside domains with different sensing configurations.

</details>


### [172] [Fuse to Recognize the Unseen: Class-Relative Infrared-Visible Fusion for Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24316)
*Zehao Gao,Jie Feng,Wenda Zhao,Junpeng Zhang,Ronghua Shang,Weisheng Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EmergeFuse，一种面向开放词汇语义分割的红外-可见光融合框架，将热红外互补线索选择性地转化为对未见类别的语义修正，而非直接注入IR特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OVSS模型（如基于CLIP）主要在RGB图像上预训练，红外信息虽能提升视觉感知互补性，但直接引入会破坏RGB-语言对齐，把热红外线索变成语义干扰，损害未见类识别。

Method: 像素级RGB-IR语义分析发现类相关热线索可纠正部分RGB误分类且保留可靠RGB预测。据此设计两步机制：(1) 类相对线索选择，以RGB预测为参照评估RGB-IR语义响应与校准后的热证据，通过竞争式选择生成类相对修正请求；(2) 文本对比图像校正，用文本对比把请求转译为语义修正方向，引导证据门控的特征更新。

Result: 在三个基准上，未见类mIoU最高提升4.755%，严格互补转换率提升15.730%，同时保持有竞争力的融合质量。

Conclusion: 以RGB为锚、类相对地选择性利用热红外证据，可在不破坏RGB-语言对齐的前提下实现对未见类语义的可控修正，优于现有融合方法。

Abstract: Visible-infrared image fusion leverages complementary thermal cues to enhance visual perception, particularly under challenging illumination. However, such perceptual complementarity does not readily translate into semantic gains in open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS), where CLIP-based models are predominantly pretrained on RGB imagery. Directly introducing IR information may instead disrupt RGB-language alignment, turning promising thermal cues into semantic interference that undermines unseen-class recognition. Our pixel-level RGB-IR semantic analysis reveals that class-relevant thermal cues can correct certain RGB misclassifications while preserving reliable RGB predictions, motivating RGB-anchored, class-relative thermal cue selection. Based on this insight, we propose EmergeFuse, an infrared-visible fusion framework for OVSS that selectively converts complementary thermal evidence into unseen-class semantic corrections. Specifically, class-relative cue selection evaluates RGB-IR semantic responses and calibrated thermal evidence relative to RGB predictions, generating class-relative correction requests through competitive selection. Text-contrast image rectification then translates these selected requests into semantic rectification directions through text contrasts, guiding evidence-gated feature updates to realize complementary thermal evidence for unseen-class recognition. Experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate gains of up to 4.755% in unseen-class mIoU and 15.730% in strict complementary conversion rate over existing fusion methods, while maintaining competitive fusion quality.

</details>


### [173] [Look Closer, Look Back, or Wait for Agentic Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24267)
*Pengyu Li,Zhitao Gao,Lingling Zhang,Wenxuan Huang,YiCheng Xiao,Chen Zheng,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ActiveForager 流式视频智能体，维护干净短时工作窗口并按需取证（直接回答/放大高清、召回历史、等待未来帧），用共享权重子智能体隔离验证历史片段并返回精简证据摘要，配合流解耦注意力掩码与反事实信息增益训练策略。在 OVO-Bench 上 Qwen3-VL-8B 总体准确率 47.6%→65.9%，Video-Holmes 零样本翻倍，且每查询新增视觉 token 极少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频问答受严格内存与延迟约束：只喂最新若干帧可保持当前感知锐利，但加入历史帧会稀释对当前场景的注意力并引发幻觉；现有级联式方法仅在不确定时检索历史帧，遇到当前帧模糊或决定性未来线索尚未出现时仍失效。

Method: ActiveForager 为流式视频智能体：保持短时工作窗口清晰，仅在需要时取证。对每个查询可选四种动作——直接回答、放大高分辨率近期帧、从记忆召回历史事件、等待后续帧。召回历史时由共享权重子智能体在隔离条件下验证候选片段并返回紧凑证据摘要；再用流解耦注意力掩码阻止历史证据干扰当前视觉 token。策略训练采用反事实信息增益：仅当所收集证据使真实答案概率提升时才奖励该工具调用，以避免所有动作上的工具坍缩。

Result: 在 OVO-Bench 上，Qwen2.5-VL-7B 与 Qwen3-VL-8B 同时提升实时感知、历史召回与主动响应三项能力，Qwen3-VL-8B 总体准确率由 47.6% 升至 65.9%；在需多跳推理的 Video-Holmes 上零样本准确率翻倍，且每查询新增视觉 token 极少。

Conclusion: 按需取证＋隔离验证＋流解耦注意力的主动式流式视频智能体，可在低内存与低延迟约束下同时兼顾实时感知、历史召回与面向未来的主动响应，并以极小的视觉 token 开销获得显著性能提升。

Abstract: Streaming video models must answer questions about a live video under strict memory and latency limits. Feeding only the latest few frames keeps present perception sharp, whereas adding past frames dilutes attention on the current scene and triggers hallucinations. Recent cascade methods retrieve past frames when uncertain, but they still fail when current frames are blurry or when decisive future clues have not appeared yet. We introduce ActiveForager, a streaming video agent that maintains a clean short working window and gathers extra evidence only on demand. For each query, the agent either answers directly, zooms into high-resolution recent frames, recalls past events from memory, or waits for upcoming frames. When recalling history, a shared-weight sub-agent verifies candidate clips in isolation and returns a compact evidence summary. A stream-decoupled attention mask then keeps historical evidence from interfering with present visual tokens. We train this policy using counterfactual information gain, which rewards a tool call only when the gathered evidence makes the true answer more likely and prevents tool collapse across all actions. Across Qwen2.5-VL-7B and Qwen3-VL-8B on OVO-Bench, ActiveForager improves real-time perception, historical recall, and proactive response at once, lifting overall accuracy on Qwen3-VL-8B from 47.6% to 65.9%. It also doubles zero-shot accuracy on the multi-hop Video-Holmes benchmark while adding minimal visual tokens per query.

</details>


### [174] [Allocentric Memory, Egocentric Acting: A Dual-Frame VLA for Urban Aerial Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24247)
*Haotian Xu,Yue Hu,Zhengqiu Zhu,Tuo Feng,Wenguan Wang,Quanjun Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CityNav-VLA，一种双坐标系视觉-语言-动作模型，将动态自我中心感知与持久的异中心空间记忆结合，实现语言目标城市空中导航，并以早停目标承诺减少长程目标漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言目标空中导航的核心难点在于：飞行中的自我中心感知不断变化，需与锚定于稳定位置的空间证据建立联系，以支撑长距离导航；现有方法难以在视角变化下保持证据一致性并兼顾全局推理与自我中心执行。

Method: 构建以固定北向地图为载体的异中心空间记忆，初始化注入地理与地标先验，增量累积视觉观测；用由指令、当前观测、UAV状态与运动历史条件化的动态查询检索记忆证据，预测地图坐标系目标；再由确定性控制器依据当前位姿将目标转换为机体坐标系指令；并提出无额外可训练参数的早期目标承诺（EGC），以前K次预测的逐坐标中位数固定执行目标。

Result: Test Unseen上NE降低21.2%，SR与SPL分别提升15.8%和30.9%；数据集精化后相对前SOTA，NE降低6.2%，SR与SPL分别提升5.6%和23.2%。消融显示导航地图特征贡献最大，度量坐标进一步提升终点精度。4B骨干下单张A100推理平均0.68 s/决策，峰值显存8.6 GB。

Conclusion: 双坐标系设计与地图化空间记忆能有效桥接全局推理与自我中心执行，EGC以零参数代价稳定长程目标；模型在精度与效率上均具优势，导航地图特征为主要增益来源。

Abstract: Language-goal aerial navigation requires a UAV to reach a destination specified in natural language using observations collected during flight. A key challenge is linking changing egocentric perception with spatial evidence anchored to stable locations for long-range navigation. We propose CityNav-VLA, a dual-frame vision-language-action model for urban navigation integrating dynamic egocentric context with persistent allocentric spatial memory. The UAV incrementally accumulates visual observations on a fixed north-up map initialized with geographic and landmark priors, preserving evidence across viewpoint changes. A dynamic query conditioned on the instruction, current observations, UAV state, and motion history retrieves relevant memory evidence to predict a map-frame target. A deterministic controller converts this target into body-frame commands using the UAV's current pose, connecting global reasoning with egocentric execution. To reduce target fluctuations over long horizons, early goal commitment (EGC) fixes the execution target to the coordinate-wise median of the first $K$ predictions without extra trainable parameters. On Test Unseen, CityNav-VLA reduces NE by 21.2% and improves SR and SPL by 15.8% and 30.9%, respectively. It also reduces NE by 6.2% and improves SR and SPL by 5.6% and 23.2%, respectively, relative to the former SOTA after dataset refinement. Cumulative ablations show that navigation-map features yield the largest gains, while metric coordinates further improve endpoint accuracy. With a 4B-scale backbone, inference averages 0.68 s per decision with 8.6 GB peak memory on a single A100, presenting remarkable model efficiency.

</details>


### [175] [SpiderMark: Hybrid Watermarking for Generative AI via Algorithmic Injection and Learning-Based Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24200)
*Chihcheng Hsieh,Yang Song,Flora Salim,Martin Bell,Jacinto C. Nascimento,Catarina Moreira*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpiderMark：将蛛网状结构频域水印注入扩散模型初始潜噪声，并用轻量ResNet-18概率验证器替代手工阈值检测的混合水印框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散生成模型普及使AI图像来源追溯需求增强；现有水印多用各向同性频率模式与手工检测指标，存在方向性脆弱与跨图像变换阈值不稳定问题。

Method: 在初始潜噪声的傅里叶表示中嵌入由径向辐条与同心多边形环构成的蛛网图案（不改动预训练权重）；训练轻量ResNet-18验证器，融合PSNR与L1辅助相似度线索，输出连续水印置信度用于检测。

Result: 九种评测条件下八项取得最高固定阈值准确率；在未重训练验证器的未见COCO提示词上仍保持强检测；CLIP相似度与FID显示检测提升仅伴随较小的提示对齐与分布质量差异。

Conclusion: 结构化水印几何与学习式概率验证可统一为单一实用框架，兼顾不可感知性、鲁棒性与真实场景可验证性，服务生成内容归属溯源。

Abstract: The proliferation of diffusion-based generative models has intensified the need for reliable methods to trace and verify the provenance of AI-generated images. Existing watermarking approaches for diffusion models typically rely on isotropic frequency patterns and handcrafted detection metrics, which are prone to directional vulnerability and threshold instability across diverse image transformations. We propose SpiderMark, a hybrid watermarking framework that combines algorithmic frequency-domain injection with learning-based probabilistic verification, requiring no modifications to pretrained diffusion model weights. SpiderMark embeds a structured spider-web pattern, comprising radial spokes and concentric polygonal rings, into the Fourier representation of the initial latent noise, and trains a lightweight ResNet-18 verifier augmented with auxiliary similarity cues (PSNR and L1) to produce a continuous watermark confidence score for watermark detection. Across nine evaluation conditions, SpiderMark achieves the highest fixed-threshold Accuracy in eight, while retaining strong detection on unseen COCO prompts without verifier retraining. CLIP similarity and FID measurements show that these detection gains are accompanied by small measured differences in prompt alignment and distributional image quality. SpiderMark establishes that structured watermark geometry and learned probabilistic verification can be unified into a single, practical framework, bridging the gap between imperceptibility, robustness, and real-world verifiability for generative AI content attribution.

</details>


### [176] [VideoACE: Benchmarking Agentic Video Understanding through High-Frame-Rate Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24184)
*Luoyuan Zhang,Xuanyu Zheng,Yancheng Long,Haoyang Xu,Bin Wen,Tingting Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VideoACE 基准，用于在支持持续交互的条件下评估智能体式视频理解，聚焦高帧率动作视频，包含 1000 道多选题、时序证据标注与用户模拟器，并对比单次观测、自主工具使用及工具+用户反馈三种设置下五个模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体式视频理解要求模型主动与环境交互以收集证据并形成判断，因此需评估模型能否把握交互机会转化为更好的理解；高帧率动作视频中快速细微的变化使定向观测尤为有价值，而现有评估多局限于固定观测设定下的性能。

Method: 构建 VideoACE 基准：1,000 道多选题，涵盖六类细粒度动作理解，配有时序证据标注和经答案泄漏筛查的用户提示；评估环境结合视频检查工具与用户模拟器，允许模型调整时间覆盖范围、采样密度与空间细节，并重访证据、在模拟用户反馈下修正判断；设置三种评估条件——单次观测、自主工具使用、工具使用结合用户反馈。

Result: 对五个模型的评估显示出在证据获取方式及对进一步交互机会的响应上存在不同模式（未给出具体数值）。

Conclusion: VideoACE 可作为超越固定观测设定下性能评测的智能体式视频理解测试平台。

Abstract: Agentic video understanding requires models to actively interact with video environments to gather evidence and form judgments. Evaluating this capability therefore requires assessing whether models can translate opportunities for interaction into better understanding. We introduce VideoACE, a benchmark for assessing video understanding under conditions that support sustained interaction. We focus on high-frame-rate action videos, where rapid, subtle changes make targeted observation especially valuable. The benchmark comprises 1,000 multiple-choice questions spanning six categories of fine-grained action understanding, accompanied by temporal evidence annotations and user hints screened for answer leakage. Its evaluation environment combines video inspection tools with a user simulator, allowing models to adjust temporal coverage, sampling density, and spatial detail, and to revisit evidence and revise their judgments under simulated user feedback. We evaluate single-pass observation, autonomous tool use, and tool use combined with user feedback to examine how effectively models exploit these opportunities. Evaluation of five models reveals distinct patterns in how they acquire evidence and respond to opportunities for further interaction. These findings establish VideoACE as a testbed for examining agentic video understanding beyond performance under a fixed observation setting.

</details>


### [177] [Unified Law for Visual Tasks (The Four Laws of Computer Vision)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24917)
*Sen Fang,Yalin Feng,Yancheng Zhang,Yanxin Zhang,Zonglin Lyu,Hongbin Zhong,Liwei Che,Rui Wu,Chen Chen,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"视觉任务统一定律"（含视觉四定律），主张将几乎所有视觉/可视化任务重述为生成任务，从而用统一的"世界快照模型"（WSM）训练与执行，思路类比大语言模型对NLP的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉任务高度碎片化，各任务各有专属框架与训练范式，缺乏像LLM统一NLP那样的通用方法。作者基于一个启发式假设：若世界信息被编码进图像（World Snapshots），则图像处理即世界处理、图像生成即世界快照生成。

Method: 以"图像即世界信息编码"为出发点提出统一定律与四条子定律；在此框架下把各类视觉与可视化任务统一改写为生成式任务，并给出统一训练框架，用World Snapshot Models在大量任务上验证。

Result: 在超过1000个已训练与评估的视觉任务（以不同输入-输出映射定义）上对该定律进行了验证，并给出一套面向未来视觉任务研究与应用的统一训练框架。

Conclusion: 若所有视觉任务均可重述为生成，则单一WSM方法有望统一绝大多数视觉任务，为视觉领域提供类似LLM的统一范式；该定律在千余任务上的验证支持这一统一框架的可行性。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a Unified Law for Visual Tasks, which could be further divided into Four Laws of Computer Vision, with the goal of enabling a single framework or method to uniformly train on and perform nearly all visual tasks. Our starting point is an inspiration and hypothesis: if the world information is encoded into image, which we call World Snapshots, then image processing is world processing, and image generation is World Snapshot generation. Furthermore, if all visual and visualization tasks can be reformulated as generation tasks, then a unified method, World Snapshot Models (WSMs), can perform the vast majority of visual tasks, much as large language models have largely unified natural language processing. This paper validates these laws across more than 1,000 trained and evaluated visual tasks (defined by distinct input-output mappings) and presents a unified training framework for future visual-task research and applications.

</details>


### [178] [PrismRestore: A Compact Restoration Toolbox for Agentic Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24915)
*Zhaocheng Yu,Kui Jiang,Jing Cao,Yiang Chen,Xiaocheng Feng,Junjun Jiang,Xianming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PrismRestore 从单个冻结的 all-in-one 修复模型中构建紧凑的可调用动作库，并用状态条件策略组合这些动作，实现无需多个骨干网络的 agentic 图像修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic 图像修复通过组合操作处理混合退化，但工具箱通常需要存储多个修复网络，成本高。作者希望仅用单个修复骨干就获得可显式选择与组合的动作集。

Method: 基于共享模型中"任务相关但相互重叠"的通道响应：1) 离线任务先验搜索，用激活与梯度统计将响应相似的通道分组，在非代表性通道上搜索任务特定调制系数，代表性通道不调制，形成先验库；2) 状态条件策略用 Group Relative Policy Optimization 学习动作组合，其目标在同一输入采样的轨迹组内分别归一化保真度与感知反馈，并结合终端评估与由逐步质量变化导出的 return-to-go。

Result: 在合成复合退化与真实多任务基准上，优于所评估的 all-in-one 基线，修复质量与多模型 agent 相当；且仅用单一修复骨干，推理速度快于所评估的 agentic 基线。

Conclusion: 利用单一冻结 all-in-one 模型的通道级任务先验即可构造可组合动作库，配合组相对策略优化实现高效 agentic 修复，避免多网络存储与任务专用适配模块的开销。

Abstract: Agentic image restoration composes operations to handle mixed degradations, yet its toolbox often requires storing multiple restoration networks. We propose PrismRestore, which constructs a compact bank of callable restoration actions from a single frozen all-in-one model. Our approach builds on task-dependent yet overlapping channel responses in the shared model. An offline task-prior search uses activation and gradient statistics to group channels with similar response profiles, then searches task-specific modulation coefficients over non-representative channels while leaving representative channels unmodulated. The resulting prior bank enables explicit action selection and composition without additional restoration backbones or task-specific adapter modules. A state-conditioned policy learns to compose these actions using Group Relative Policy Optimization. Its objective separately normalizes fidelity and perceptual feedback within groups of trajectories sampled for the same input, and combines terminal evaluation with return-to-go derived from stepwise quality changes. Experiments on synthetic composite-degradation and real-world multi-task benchmarks show improvements over the evaluated all-in-one baselines and restoration quality competitive with multi-model agents. PrismRestore achieves these results with a single restoration backbone and faster inference than the evaluated agentic baselines.

</details>


### [179] [Generalization of Text Line Segmentation for HTR in Historical Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24726)
*Gayan H. Pathirage,Stephan M. Unter,Simon Corbillé,Elisa H. Barney Smith*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究Hi-SAM、YOLO、RT-DETR等目标检测模型在历史文档文本行分割上的泛化能力与少样本迁移表现，并讨论mAP作为评价指标的不足、给出CRNN端到端CER结果与训练推理资源/时间需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 博物馆与档案馆难以为每个新馆藏标注大量样本，因此模型需具备跨数据集泛化能力，并需明确少样本微调的可行性与资源代价，以支持模型与硬件的合理选型。

Method: 在视觉版式与语言差异显著的九个数据集上评测Hi-SAM、YOLO、RT-DETR的跨域泛化；在每目标数据集仅用少量样本进行少样本微调；用CRNN识别分割出的文本行并计算CER；同时分析mAP作为文本行分割评价指标的局限性，并统计训练与推理的资源与时间开销。

Result: 视数据集不同，CRNN在整页文本行上的CER为2.48%–7.07%，足以支撑阅读理解与简单文本检索。

Conclusion: 现有检测模型在历史文档文本行分割上具备可用的泛化与少样本适应能力；mAP不足以评价文本行分割质量，应以CER等下游指标衡量；资源与时间量化结果可为不同应用场景下的模型与硬件决策提供依据。

Abstract: In this paper we investigate the generalization capability of state-of-the-art object detection models (Hi-SAM, YOLO, and RT-DETR) for the automatic segmentation of text lines in historical documents. Generalization is needed because it is not practical for museums and archives to produce large amounts of labeled samples for each new collection. We also evaluate the few-shot setting by fine-tuning the networks with only a small number of training samples per target dataset. To this end, we use nine different datasets that differ significantly in visual layout and language. Additionally, we discuss why mean average precision (mAP), a classic evaluation metric for object detection, is insufficient for assessing the quality of a text line segmentation model. Depending on the dataset, our CRNN models achieve character error rates (CER) ranging from 2.48% to 7.07% per page for the segmented text lines. This is sufficient for reading comprehension and simple text searches. Finally, we quantify the resource and time requirements of training and inference in different use case settings. This forms the basis for more informed decisions about model and hardware choices based on available resources.

</details>


### [180] [Predicting Species Splits: A Challenging Fine-Grained Benchmark for Category Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24672)
*Christian Lange,Joakim Bruslund Haurum,Bingchen Zhao,Oisin Mac Aodha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出PS-SPLIT——基于真实分类学拆分构建的大规模细粒度类别发现基准（79K鸟类图像、771物种、257次专家认证拆分），并定义新任务"按类别拆分进行发现"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类别发现基准日趋饱和，难以反映真实世界的发现场景，不利于新方法的长期评估；希望用真实分类学过程（一个物种被拆分为多个物种）构造更具挑战性、更贴近实际的细粒度基准。

Method: 基于分类学拆分构建基准：含79K全球分布、社区采集的高质量鸟类图像，覆盖771个物种，源自257次近期专家认证的分类学拆分，并附带地理元数据与完整分类层级。在此基础上提出新任务"discovery by category splitting"：判定哪些已知类别应被细分为更细粒度子类别；并适配四种广义类别发现（GCD）方法在该设定下进行评估。

Result: 真实拆分产生的类别间视觉差异极为细微，常需专业人类知识才能区分，使基准具有较强挑战性；对四种GCD方法改编评估显示现有方法在该任务上仍有较大提升空间。

Conclusion: PS-SPLIT提供了更贴近现实、长期可用的细粒度类别发现评测平台，并开辟了类别拆分式发现这一新研究方向，呼吁后续研究改进现有方法。

Abstract: Widely used category discovery benchmarks have become increasingly saturated and often fail to reflect real-world discovery settings, making long-term evaluation of new methods difficult. We introduce PS-SPLIT, a large-scale fine-grained benchmark for category discovery, motivated by taxonomic splitting: the real-world process by which what was once considered a single species is found to comprise multiple distinct ones. PS-SPLIT contains 79K globally distributed, high-quality, community-collected bird images spanning 771 species, derived from 257 recent real expert-verified taxonomic splits, with associated geographic metadata and full taxonomic hierarchy. The visual differences between categories arising from real taxonomic splits can be very subtle, often requiring significant human expertise to tell apart, making PS-SPLIT a challenging benchmark which we hope will assist in advancing fine-grained category discovery research. Based on taxonomic splitting, we introduce the task of discovery by category splitting, where the goal is to determine which known categories should be subdivided into new finer-grained subcategories. We adapt four generalized category discovery methods to this setting, evaluate existing methods, and show that there is substantial room for future progress.

</details>


### [181] [Comparing Each Generation with Independent Siblings Gives Video World Model Post-Training a Proper Objective for Outcome Frequencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24512)
*Xin Li,Yiwen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SibPO（Sibling-paired Policy Optimization）提出用同一历史下独立噪声生成的“兄弟”样本估计截断逆频率权重，使视频世界模型训练目标成为对结果单元的严格proper散度，从而还原数据分布而非常采最可能结局；在PisaBench上相对最强非本奖励族方法将着陆时间KS距离降低9.1–10.2%（四个模型中三个），并将机器人策略成功率估计误差降低13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型作为模拟器使用时，首帧允许多个物理结局，重复生成应按数据中发生频率产出各结局。现有物理后训练以单条记录未来对每次生成打分，期望分数对模型分布是线性的，最优解退化为总输出最可能结局，从而丧失多模态分布；而最大化记录结局的对数似然虽可达数据分布，却需要按其生成频率的倒数加权。

Method: 用同一历史下独立噪声生成的兄弟样本估计“生成该结局的频率”，据此构造截断逆频率权重用于记录一致项；再加第二项减去同权重乘以与兄弟样本的一致性，使有限组规模下的期望目标在每个结果单元上均为严格proper散度（弥补有限样本偏差）。

Result: PisaBench上，四个视频模型中三个的着陆时间KS距离相对最强非本奖励族方法降低9.1–10.2%，且单条视频质量不退化；估计的机器人策略成功率误差降低13%。

Conclusion: 以兄弟配对构造逆频率加权可修正打分式后训练的模式塌缩，在有限组规模下仍保持严格proper性，提升世界模型的多结局分布保真度及下游策略评估精度。

Abstract: Video world models are used as simulators, so when a first frame leaves several physical outcomes open, repeated generations should produce each outcome as often as it occurs in the data. Physics post-training scores every generation against the single recorded future of its history, which makes single videos more faithful, but the expected score is linear in the model distribution and is maximized by always producing the most likely outcome. The log-likelihood of the recorded outcome is maximized by the data distribution, and its gradient weights agreement with the record by the inverse of how often the model produces the generated outcome, a frequency that other generations of the same history with independent noise, its siblings, can estimate. To train with this weight from a finite group, we propose SibPO, which performs Sibling-paired Policy Optimization: siblings estimate a truncated inverse-frequency weight for agreement with the record, and a second term subtracts the same weight times the agreement with the siblings, which makes the expected objective a strictly proper divergence over outcome cells at every group size. On PisaBench, SibPO lowers the KS distance to simulated landing times by 9.1-10.2% relative to the strongest method outside its reward family on three of four video models without degrading single videos, and it lowers the error of estimated robot policy success rates by 13%.

</details>


### [182] [Multi-Band Galaxy Novel-View Synthesis with Radiative Gaussian Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24723)
*Yuzhe Wu,Jialin Zhu,Doğa Yılmaz,Daisuke Kawata,He Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 GalaxyGS，一种受物理启发的 Gaussian Splatting 方法，用于星系多波段观测的新视角合成，具备刻画天体能量发射机制与光传播过程的物理组件，在精度与物理保真度上优于多种替代方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系观测与 RGB 数据存在本质差异：包含海量天体的多波段信号、复杂的跨波段相互作用模式，以及高度非均匀介质中的光传播过程。这些物理过程给模型表示与优化带来巨大挑战，而现有研究尚未对此进行探讨。

Method: 提出物理启发的 Gaussian Splatting 方法 GalaxyGS，其组件具有物理意义，分别建模不同天文天体的能量发射机制以及天体间的光传播过程。由于缺乏可直接比较的基线方法，采用多种可能的替代方案进行对比。

Result: 在多个数据集与评价指标上，对比结果一致表明 GalaxyGS 在准确性和物理保真度方面均优于替代方法。

Conclusion: GalaxyGS 将物理机制融入 Gaussian Splatting，为星系多波段观测的新视角合成提供了有效方案，在精度与物理一致性上取得优势。

Abstract: We propose a novel view synthesis tool for galaxies based on Gaussian Splatting (GS). Unlike RGB data, where GS has achieved success, galaxy observations consist of multi-band signals from a massive number of bodies, contain complex patterns of cross-band interactions, and capture light propagation in highly heterogeneous media. As a result, the underlying physical process poses great challenges in model representation and optimization, which have not been investigated in existing research. To this end, we propose a physically inspired Gaussian Splatting method, GalaxyGS, which is equipped with physically meaningful components that capture both the energy emission mechanism of different astronomical bodies and the light propagation between them. In the absence of directly comparable baseline methods, we adopt a wide range of possible alternative approaches. Comparisons conclusively demonstrate that GalaxyGS is better in accuracy and physical fidelity across datasets and metrics.

</details>


### [183] [StrideWorld: Benchmarking Scene Rigidity and Action Consistency in Interactive Video World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24408)
*Chuang Wang,Rui Nie,Jie Song,Junyuan Gao,Jiahe Song,Haitao Zhou,Boyu Zhu,Xing Li,Jiang Wu,Conghui He,Qian Yu,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 StrideWorld 基准，用运动前固定的参考（起始视图真值几何与首个动作步长）评估交互式视频世界模型的场景刚性与动作一致性，避免参考文献吸收形变与节奏误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准的参考依赖被测视频本身（自重建场景或自由尺度对齐的相机轨迹），会吸收渐进式场景形变以及节奏、路径长度的变化，导致错误无法被检出。

Method: 基准参考在运动评估之前固定：场景刚性以起始视图的真值几何为参考；动作一致性以每个动作（一条命令对应的相机运动）的"步长"为参考，步长由模型最初几步测得，再用于检验后续步，实现各模型自比。测量在已知相机运动的渲染视频上验证，并与人类判断一致性检验。

Result: 在 140 个起始视图、多个开源模型生成的视频上，除一个模型外所有模型在相机移动时均产生场景形变，偏移最高达同一相机路径刚性渲染的 7.3 倍；单动作保持时多数模型节奏稳定；组合路径上多个模型常停滞，多数完成路径的终点偏离按各自步长预测的位置，仅两个按估计场景深度缩放命令的模型接近该位置；对两个模型用少量渲染片段微调可消除大部分中途停滞。

Conclusion: 固定参考的评估能暴露被传统对齐式基准掩盖的形变与节奏漂移问题，为交互式视频世界模型提供更严格、与人判断一致的评测，并公开代码、数据与排行榜。

Abstract: Interactive video world models turn user commands into the exploration of a generated scene, which requires the scene to keep its 3D structure as the camera moves and each action to keep a stable effect. Existing benchmarks mostly evaluate these properties against references fitted to the video under test, either a scene reconstructed from its own frames or a camera trajectory aligned to the command path with a free scale. Such references can absorb gradual scene deformation and changes in pace and route length, so these errors can pass undetected. We therefore introduce StrideWorld, a benchmark whose references are fixed before the motion they judge. It evaluates scene rigidity against the ground-truth geometry of the start view, and action consistency against the stride of each action, the camera motion of one command. The stride is measured on the model's first steps and then used to test the steps that follow, so each model is judged only against its own steps. Its measurements are validated on rendered videos with known camera motion, and its scores agree with human judgments. Across \nvideos{} videos generated by \nmodels{} open models from $140$ start views, all models but one deform their scenes as the camera moves, with offsets up to $7.3$ times those of a rigid rendering along the same camera path. Most models keep a steady pace when a single action is held. Along composed routes, several models often stall, and most completed routes end away from the positions predicted by each model's own strides. Only the two models that scale commands by the estimated scene depth bring most routes close to these positions. Fine-tuning two models on a few rendered clips removes most of their mid-route stalls. Code, data, and a leaderboard are publicly available at https://github.com/chuangwang123/strideworld.

</details>


### [184] [RAPID: Reconstruction Accelerated by Polarization-Informed Depth for 2D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24404)
*Daisong Ouyang,Linlin Xia,Liangkun Yan,Song Gu,Shedong Zhao,Xin Dai,Yi Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RAPID，一个几何优先框架，利用多视角偏振线索恢复稠密表面先验，以加速高斯泼溅的三维重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯泼溅重建方法依赖稀疏几何初始化与迭代致密化，优化开销大，且难以在反射和弱纹理区域兼顾渲染质量与几何精度。

Method: 利用多视角偏振线索，借助稀疏几何锚点将表面法线融合进深度图，并进行跨视角深度融合；随后采用曲率自适应采样与有向高斯初始化支持高效优化。

Result: 在PISR数据集上相较SOTA方法PhyGaP约5倍加速，平均优化时间从约21分钟降至4分钟，同时保持有竞争力的新视角合成质量与网格重建精度。

Conclusion: RAPID验证了从偏振多视角输入加速高斯优化的有效性，通过稠密几何先验初始化显著提升效率并保持精度。

Abstract: Efficient and accurate 3D surface reconstruction from multi-view images remains a fundamental challenge in Gaussian splatting. Many existing methods rely on sparse geometric initialization and iterative densification, resulting in substantial optimization overhead and difficulties in balancing rendering quality and geometric accuracy, particularly in reflective and weakly textured regions. To address these limitations, we propose RAPID, a geometry-first framework that uses multiview polarization cues to recover dense surface priors. We integrate surface normals into depth maps using sparse geometric anchors. These depth maps are then fused across views. Curvature-adaptive sampling and oriented Gaussian initialization then support efficient optimization. Experiments on the PISR dataset demonstrate an approximately \(5\times\) speedup over PhyGaP, which is a state-of-the-art Gaussian-based reconstruction method. RAPID reduces the average optimization time from approximately 21 minutes (PhyGaP) to 4 minutes while maintaining competitive novel-view synthesis quality and mesh reconstruction accuracy. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of RAPID in accelerating Gaussian optimization from polarimetric multi-view inputs.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [From Arraignment to Resolution: Transformer Models of Court Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23518)
*Kory Rosen,Aron Culotta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Transformer的时间序列模型，将案件建模为事件序列，预测案件下一次庭审的结果（如受理指控、设定保释、达成认罪协议等），并在美国某中型城市公开法庭记录数据集上验证，取得0.57 micro-F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刑事司法系统由多个复杂决策点构成，决策影响深远且亟需提升透明度；既有机器学习工作多聚焦单一决策（如再犯预测），缺乏对案件全流程决策序列的建模，难以支持有效监督与理解。

Method: 将案件形式化为事件序列，构建Transformer时间序列模型预测下一程序结果；融合书记员笔记文本信息与被告、既往指控、法庭等结构化特征；基于新构建的美国某中型城市公开法庭记录数据集，对数千案件进行实证分析，并与多个基线及相同输入下的GRU模型对比。

Result: 在20类多标签任务上，Transformer模型达到0.57 micro-F1，显著优于多个基线，且与在相同输入上训练的GRU相当。

Conclusion: 该序列建模方法可支持对司法决策的更细致分析，帮助法庭观察者洞察决策过程并识别该辖区中浮现的问题，从而提升司法系统透明度。

Abstract: The criminal legal system is composed of many complex decision points, such as which charges are pursued, how bond is determined, plea deal negotiations, and sentencing. Given the profound impact of such decisions, there is a growing need to increase the transparency within this system. While prior machine learning approaches have focused on modeling single decisions (e.g., recidivism), this paper proposes a transformer-based time series model to forecast the outcome of the next proceeding in a case (e.g., charges accepted, bond set, plea deal made, etc.). By modeling a case as an event sequence, we enable a more nuanced analysis to support better oversight and understanding of decision making. We conduct an empirical analysis of thousands of cases using a newly constructed dataset derived from publicly available court records of a mid-sized U.S. city. We fit models that combine textual data from clerk notes with structured information about the defendant, prior charges, and courtroom. Our transformer model achieves $.57$ micro-F1 on a 20-class multi-label task, a significant improvement over several baselines and comparable to a GRU trained on the same input. We demonstrate how the model can be used to provide insights into decision-making and help court observers identify emerging issues within the jurisdiction studied.

</details>


### [186] [Dreaming Up Authors: Factual Prevalence and LLM Hallucinations in Academic Authorship Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23530)
*Sijia Zhong,Tamir Yirga,Yijia Ma,Thai Le,Yifan Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文以学术论文作者归属为任务，用引用量作为事实流行度代理，在192万篇论文上验证了"事实流行度越低、幻觉率越高"的假设，并揭示其机制主要源于检索受限而非知识缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM广泛用于问答但易产生幻觉，其成因尤其是训练数据中事实流行度（factual prevalence）的作用尚缺乏系统研究，现有工作缺少可控、可量化的流行度分层实验设计。

Method: 选取学术作者归属作为探测任务，以引用量作为事实流行度的代理变量；构建覆盖8个学科、按引用水平分层抽样的192万篇论文数据集；在三个SOTA LLM上进行实验，并开展失败归因分析（检索限制 vs. 知识缺失）以及开源模型的隐状态探针（hidden-state probing）分析。

Result: 实验证实假设：在所有模型与学科上，幻觉率与引用量呈稳健负相关；低引用论文幻觉率超过98%，高引用论文低于37%。归因分析表明失败主因是检索限制而非知识缺失；隐状态探针显示事实流行度在生成前表征中已被编码，提供收敛的机制性证据。

Conclusion: 幻觉与训练数据中的事实流行度存在强因果性关联，且可通过生成前表征预测；缓解幻觉应侧重提升低流行度事实的检索与调用能力，而非单纯扩充知识。代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely used for question answering but are prone to hallucinations, yet the underlying causes, particularly the role of factual prevalence in training data, remain underexplored. In this work, we study this through academic authorship attribution, hypothesizing that hallucinations are more likely for less prevalent papers. Using citation count as a proxy for factual prevalence, we curate a dataset of 1.92 million papers across eight scientific disciplines with stratified samples by citation level. Experiments on three state-of-the-art LLMs confirm our hypothesis, revealing a robust inverse relationship between hallucination rate and citation count across all models and disciplines, with hallucination rates exceeding 98% for rarely cited papers and falling below 37% for highly cited ones. Our in-depth analysis further shows that these failures primarily reflect retrieval limitations rather than knowledge absence, and hidden-state probing of open-source models shows that factual prevalence is encoded in pre-generation representations, providing convergent mechanistic evidence for the prevalence-hallucination relationship. The code and data are available at https://github.com/quinnzhong1/dreaming-up-authors.

</details>


### [187] [FutureBench: A Renewable Framework for Evaluating Knowledge-Context Conflict in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23601)
*Yi-Cheng Lai,Hen-Hsen Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 FutureBench 框架，用带时间戳问题、受控段落三元组和无上下文探针评估知识-上下文冲突，发现"检查+段落优先"的 Verify 提示会增加模型采纳错误答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型被要求核查检索文本时，可能反而学会该信任哪个来源，从而在知识-上下文冲突中更易采纳错误段落证据；现有评测缺乏可控、可重复冲突场景。

Method: 构建 FutureBench：3,629 道带时间戳问题、受控段落三元组与无上下文探针；在六个模型上比较 Baseline（同时允许段落证据与模型知识）与 Verify（核查+段落优先）等提示，并对 902 道截止后问题做四提示对照，另设 Adversarial-Aware（警示误导段落、优先模型知识）。

Result: 截止前事件上，Verify 在五个被测模型均提高错误答案采纳，主要挤占 Baseline 下原本正确的答案；截止后事件仅 o4-mini 上升 13.7 个百分点。四提示对照显示仅段落优先（不核查）提高采纳，改为 Verify 在五个模型均降低。Adversarial-Aware 恢复 82.5–98.9% 被挤占的截止前答案，真段落准确率变化不足 1 个百分点。

Conclusion: 核查与来源优先提示会系统性地诱发错误答案采纳，而显式告警加模型知识优先可低成本恢复正确性；FutureBench 支持固定队列重复评测与随模型更新扩展新事件。

Abstract: Asking a model to check retrieved text can also tell it which source to trust. We introduce FutureBench, a renewable framework for evaluating knowledge-context conflict using timestamped questions, controlled passage triplets, and no-context probes. Across 3,629 questions and six models, Verify (a prompt combining checking with passage priority) raises false-answer adoption in all five models tested on pre-cutoff events, mainly by displacing answers that were correct under Baseline, which allows both passage evidence and model knowledge. On post-cutoff events, only o4-mini increases adoption (+13.7 percentage points). A four-prompt comparison on 902 post-cutoff questions finds that passage priority without checking raises adoption, while moving to Verify lowers it in all five models. Adversarial-Aware, which warns of misleading passages and prioritizes model knowledge, restores 82.5-98.9% of displaced pre-cutoff answers with under one point of change in true-passage accuracy. FutureBench supports repeated evaluation on fixed cohorts and extension to new events as models change.

</details>


### [188] [Prioritize, Don't Decide: What We Learned Auditing a Deployed Human-in-the-Loop CV Sorter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23359)
*Hadi Mohammadi,Jeroen Sparla,Alexandros Paliouras,Georg Krempl,Robert A. Bagheri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是对一个已部署的"简历优先排序器"的审计报告：它提出阅读顺序、决策留给委员会，靠冻结多语言编码器+每会话线性模型实现，能将相关候选人集中在简历池前50%；但审计发现三项结果改变了产品：文档长度捷径使精度指标失效、四级一致性分数实为阈值化二分类概率而随标注增加下降、不确定性采样反而抬高总阅读量；另发现托管LLM嵌入在零标注冷启动阶段显著提升序数判别力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个学术岗位空缺可引来数百份申请，首轮筛选耗去委员会大部分时间。作者团队已将该工具部署于国家级学术空缺平台并接入其职位数据库，但论文关注的不是系统本身，而是对"工具顺序下50%简历即覆盖95%相关候选人"这一性能声明的审计，以及审计结果对产品设计的反作用。

Method: 系统层面：冻结的多语言Transformer编码器 + 每会话线性模型（每标注一次即重训，会话结束即丢弃），系统只输出阅读顺序、不做录用决策。审计层面：(1) 在300份合成简历池上检验仅凭文档长度是否可排序；(2) 分析系统报告的四级一致性分数与二分类阈值概率的关系；(3) 评估不确定性采样（"建议下一份简历"按钮）对委员会总阅读量的影响；(4) 在长度控制条件下比较托管LLM嵌入与本地cross-encoder的零标注序数判别能力。

Result: (1) 原简历池可仅凭文档长度排序，使precision类指标失去信息量；在共享长度预算下重新生成后该捷径被消除，而"阅读深度"结论依旧成立。(2) 四级一致性分数实为固定阈值切割的二分类概率，因此跟随分类器置信度而非排序，并随标注累积而下降。(3) 不确定性采样保持模型质量不变，但使委员会总阅读量高于无标注基线，因为它询问的恰是本会被跳过的简历。(4) 在长度控制下，托管LLM嵌入将零标注序数判别从0.66提升至0.80，但会话模型积累约两打标注后该优势消失；本地cross-encoder可在不外传数据的前提下匹配该冷启动增益。

Conclusion: 论文主张：合成评测池上的排序类指标需警惕长度等浅层捷径，需以长度控制复核；一致性分数应报告为排序/序数指标而非阈值化概率；不确定性采样用于"下一份建议"时应评估委员会总阅读成本而非仅模型质量；冷启动阶段LLM嵌入或本地cross-encoder有明确价值，但收益随标注积累迅速饱和。作者并报告了各项发现所迫使的产品修正。

Abstract: A single academic vacancy can draw hundreds of applications, and the first screening round consumes most of a committee's time. We describe a CV Priority Sorter deployed at a national platform for academic vacancies and integrated with its vacancy database. It proposes a reading order for a pile of CVs and leaves every decision to the committee, pairing a frozen multilingual transformer encoder with a per-session linear model that retrains after each label and is discarded when the session ends. It works: on a pool of 300 synthetic CVs, a committee reading in the tool's order reaches 95% of the relevant candidates after half the pile, where an arbitrary order needs almost all of it. This paper is about what we found when we audited that claim, rather than about the system itself. Three findings changed the product. The pool turned out to be sortable by document length alone, which makes precision-style metrics on it uninformative; regenerating it under a shared length budget removes that shortcut and leaves the reading-depth result standing. The four-level agreement score such systems report, ours included, is a binary probability cut at fixed thresholds, so it follows the classifier's confidence rather than its ranking and falls as labels accumulate. And uncertainty sampling, the obvious way to implement a "suggest the next CV" button, leaves model quality intact but raises the committee's total reading load above the no-label baseline, because the CVs it asks about are the ones that would otherwise be skipped. A fourth result shows where to look next: a hosted LLM embedding model lifts zero-label ordinal discrimination from 0.66 to 0.80 under length control, then stops mattering once the session model has two dozen labels; a local cross-encoder matches that cold-start gain without sending data out. We report the fixes each finding forced.

</details>


### [189] [NarrowSpec: Draft Hidden Width Need Not Match the Target in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23835)
*Alagappan Valliappan,Ankit More,Dheevatsa Mudigere,Jatin Mitra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 将读取目标模型隐状态的草稿模型（DFlash、EAGLE-3）残差流宽度压缩到原宽度的1/2或1/4，可在基本保持接受率的前提下显著加速端到端解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码中，读取目标模型隐状态的草稿模型通常继承目标的隐宽度，导致草稿模型过宽、推理开销大，成为加速比的上限瓶颈。作者希望在不损失接受率的情况下降低草稿模型的计算成本。

Method: 将草稿模型的整个残差流宽度减半或减至1/4；对借用目标词表投影的DFlash，将训练好的输出适配器折叠进该投影，得到单一更窄的投影且logits仅受浮点舍入影响。在SGLang上对DFlash与EAGLE-3两个草稿族、Qwen3-8B（NVFP4）与GPT-OSS-20B（MXFP4）两个目标模型，于B200与DGX Spark（GB10）上评测。

Result: batch size 1、温度0时，半宽或1/4宽草稿使端到端解码在B200上加速1.06–1.12倍，在GB10上加速1.13–1.44倍；加速在大batch下依然成立，batch 32至各GPU可容纳的最大batch，两种GPU上最高达1.21倍，且没有任何窄草稿比全宽更慢。

Conclusion: 草稿模型无需继承目标模型的完整隐宽度，宽度压缩是一种简单、稳健且普适的投机解码提速手段，在边缘设备（GB10）上收益尤为明显。

Abstract: Speculative decoding is widely used to speed up generation in large language models (LLMs), with a small draft model proposing tokens that one forward pass of the target model verifies. Draft models that read the target's hidden states, such as DFlash and EAGLE-3, typically inherit the target's hidden width. We show that the draft's whole residual stream can run at half or a quarter of that width while largely preserving acceptance. For DFlash, which borrows the target's vocabulary projection, the trained output adapter folds into it, giving one narrower projection with logits unchanged up to floating-point rounding. We test two drafter families, DFlash and EAGLE-3, on two targets, Qwen3-8B (NVFP4) and GPT-OSS-20B (MXFP4), in SGLang on a B200 and a DGX Spark (GB10). At batch size 1 and temperature 0, narrowing the draft to half or a quarter width speeds up end-to-end decoding by 1.06-1.12x on B200 and 1.13-1.44x on GB10. The speedup holds at larger batch sizes. From batch 32 to the largest batch each GPU fits, narrowing reaches up to 1.21x on both GPUs, and no narrow draft is slower than full width.

</details>


### [190] [Jailbreak Detection on Large Language Models via Contrastive Prototype Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24936)
*Yan Qiu,Yuanyuan Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出JDPI（原型推断越狱检测），用对比学习表征+二值安全标签构建有害/良性原型、以相对原型距离做预测，无需额外下游分类器；对比微调使检测准确率提升9.1–18.0个百分点，并在跨数据集迁移上优于分类器基线，数据集适配后超过Llama Guard 3-8B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越狱尝试与有害提示词仍是LLM安全部署的主要障碍；作者希望验证对比学习得到的表征能否在不再训练额外下游分类器的前提下支持安全检测，从而减少标注开销与模型复杂度。

Method: 提出JDPI：仅使用二值安全标签完成编码器适配、原型构建与验证，分别为有害与良性样本构建原型，基于相对原型距离进行预测；由于省去了训练下游分类器所需的独立划分，所用标注样本少于分类器基线（Method A）。另设计多轮重放实验（TomGibbs）累积用户历史，并做12组定向迁移测试。

Result: 在覆盖不同输入格式的四个基准上，对比微调使JDPI准确率提升9.1–18.0个百分点；同编码器下JDPI在一个基准上ROC-AUC略高，四个基准与基线差距均在0.017以内；经数据集特定适配后，ROC-AUC超出Llama Guard 3-8B达0.173–0.310。多轮重放的对话级准确率高于仅用当前轮；12组定向迁移中准确率平均高1.59个百分点。

Conclusion: 对比学习表征配合原型推断可作为一种轻量、标签高效的安全检测方案，在精度与跨数据集鲁棒性上可与显式分类器基线相当或更优，并明显优于Llama Guard 3-8B；累积对话历史对多轮场景检测有益。

Abstract: Jailbreak attempts and harmful prompts remain a challenge for the safe deployment of large language models. We study whether contrastively learned representations can support safety detection without training an additional downstream classifier. We propose Jailbreak Detection with Prototype Inference (JDPI), which uses binary safety labels to construct harmful and benign prototypes and makes predictions from relative prototype distances. JDPI uses only binary safety labels for encoder adaptation, prototype construction, and validation. In our setup, it uses fewer labeled examples than the classifier baseline (Method A) by avoiding the separate split used to train the downstream classifier. Across four benchmarks covering different input formats, contrastive fine-tuning improves JDPI's detection accuracy by 9.1-18.0 percentage points. Using the same encoder, JDPI achieves slightly higher ROC-AUC on one benchmark and stays within 0.017 of the baseline across all four. With dataset-specific adaptation, JDPI exceeds the released Llama Guard 3-8B baseline by 0.173-0.310 in ROC-AUC across the four benchmarks. In multi-turn replay on TomGibbs, JDPI uses accumulated user history as the conversation unfolds, achieving higher conversation-level accuracy than when it uses the current turn alone. Across 12 directed transfers, JDPI outperforms the classifier by an average of 1.59 percentage points in accuracy, demonstrating stronger cross-dataset robustness.

</details>


### [191] [What Majority Labels Hide: Annotator Disagreement and Model Errors in Multi-Dialect Arabic Sexism Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24383)
*Alaa Bazaid,Arkaitz Zubiaga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MuDAS——沙特、埃及、摩洛哥三种阿拉伯语方言YouTube评论数据集，保留三位母语者各自标注；比较微调编码器（硬标签/软标签/视角主义目标）、GPT-5.6 Terra与四个阿拉伯语大模型。发现模型在标注者分歧样本上错误更多，且不同模型错误模式相反：GPT-5.6 Terra过度预测性别相关内容，编码器漏检隐性及规约式性别歧视。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 性别歧视判断主观、跨社群存在差异，但现有检测数据集通常把标注压缩为多数标签，掩盖了分歧及模型在争议样本上的行为，而这对内容审核至关重要。

Method: 构建MuDAS：三种阿拉伯语方言（沙特、埃及、摩洛哥）YouTube评论，每条由三位本方言母语者独立标注并保留个体标注。在此数据上评估：以硬标签、软标签与视角主义（perspectivist）目标微调的阿拉伯语编码器；GPT-5.6 Terra；四个阿拉伯语为中心的大模型。分析分歧感知学习的效果、错误模式、方言差异及测试集构成/方言度的影响。

Result: 模型在存在标注分歧的评论上错误率更高。GPT-5.6 Terra与微调编码器Macro-F1接近但错误模式相反：前者即便给定标注者的性别歧视定义，仍过度分类性别相关内容（如关于端庄的宗教建议）；编码器则漏检隐性与规约式性别歧视。阿拉伯语中心模型过度预测性别歧视且整体低于编码器。分歧感知学习保持Macro-F1，软标签在争议样本上提升与标注者的一致性但牺牲一致样本表现，两种范式均无法可靠识别争议评论。埃及阿拉伯语上性能较低（大模型尤为明显），且现有测试集构成与方言度指标未能充分解释。

Conclusion: 仅报告多数标签得分会掩盖对内容审核有实质影响的相反错误模式；需保留个体标注、区分争议样本并采用分歧感知评估。

Abstract: Judgements of sexism are subjective and may vary across communities, yet sexism detection datasets usually reduce annotator judgements to a single majority label, obscuring disagreement and how models behave on contested cases. We introduce MuDAS, a dataset of YouTube comments in Saudi, Egyptian and Moroccan Arabic, each labelled by three native speakers of its dialect, with the individual annotations preserved. We use it to evaluate Arabic encoders fine-tuned with hard-label, soft-label and perspectivist objectives, GPT-5.6 Terra, and four Arabic-centric large language models. Model errors are more frequent on comments with annotator disagreement. Despite relatively close Macro-F1 scores, GPT-5.6 Terra and the fine-tuned encoders exhibit contrasting error patterns: GPT-5.6 Terra, even with the annotators' definition of sexism, over-classifies gender-related content such as religious advice on modesty, whereas the encoders miss implicit and prescriptive sexism. The Arabic-centric models also over-predict sexism and remain below the encoders. Disagreement-aware learning preserves Macro-F1, but soft labels improve alignment with annotators on contested comments at the expense of unanimous ones, and neither paradigm reliably identifies contested comments. Lower performance on Egyptian Arabic, clearest for the large language models, is not fully explained by the examined measures of test-set composition and dialectness. Majority-label scores can therefore hide contrasting error patterns that matter for content moderation.

</details>


### [192] [Once Upon a Bias: Gender Stereotyping in LLM-Generated Children's Stories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24156)
*Sayumi Muthukumarana,Roshan Godaliyadda,Parakrama Ekanayake,Roshan Ragel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大规模研究发现，16个LLM在35个国家设定、12种叙述者角色下生成的睡前故事存在系统性性别刻板差异：面向儿子的故事强调更高能动性与更低共生性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益用于生成儿童睡前故事，而此类故事会塑造儿童对社会角色的认知，因此需检验模型是否会依据目标读者性别调整叙事刻画，并弥补短文本基准难以捕捉叙事层面偏见的不足。

Method: 采用配对女儿/儿子提示与五次生成设计，从16个LLM、35个国家设定、12种叙述者角色中保留62,873篇故事；构建多层次评估框架，结合上下文校正的词汇率对比、相对刻板印象对齐度、叙述者—受众交互分析、人工评估以及表征层干预与隐藏状态分析。

Result: 面向儿子的故事在能动性上更高、共生性上更低；叙述者性别调节作用有限，而高社交性呈现更明显的交互效应；人工评估结果一致，但其与词汇指标的对应度随叙事维度变化；隐藏状态中可解码出受众性别信息。

Conclusion: LLM生成的儿童故事中存在持续但依赖语境的表现差异，说明需要超越短文本基准、面向叙事层面的偏见评估。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to create stories for children. Since bedtime stories can shape children's perceptions of social roles, this raises concerns about whether LLMs adapt narrative portrayals according to the intended recipient's gender. We conducted a large-scale study to examine gender stereotyping in LLM-generated bedtime stories. Using matched daughter/son prompts and a five-generation design, we analyze 62,873 retained stories from 16 LLMs across 35 country settings and 12 narrator roles. We introduce a multi-level evaluation framework combining context-adjusted lexical rate contrasts, relative stereotype alignment, narrator-recipient interaction analysis, human evaluation, and representation-level interventions to examine both the consistency and interpretability of these differences. Our results reveal systematic differences in narrative framing: son-directed stories emphasize higher agency and lower communion compared to daughter-directed stories. While narrator gender expresses limited moderation on these patterns, high sociability exhibits a more pronounced interaction. Human evaluations demonstrate consistent agreement, although correspondence with lexical measures varies across narrative dimensions. Additionally, hidden-state analyses reveal decodable recipient-gender information. These findings demonstrate persistent yet context-dependent representational disparities in LLM-generated children's stories and motivate narrative-specific bias evaluation beyond short-form benchmarks.

</details>


### [193] [A Mechanistic Analysis of In-Context Learning and Out-of-Context Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24000)
*Tianxiang Chen,Jingyuan Zhou,Guoyao Yang,Kaidi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文从"动态上下文中功能角色"的统一视角，解释ICL规则遵循与OOC幻觉这两种看似对立结果背后的共同机制，提出角色效应的分解与两个必要条件，并通过因果分析验证角色跨度贡献对关键token决策的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作分别研究ICL与OOC幻觉的内在机制，二者割裂使得"模型既能学规则又会违背证据"这一看似矛盾的现象缺乏统一机理解释，论文希望弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 以动态上下文中的功能角色为统一视角，观察到角色信息可在内部被表示却未可靠约束输出；推导角色效应的一阶分解，指出其显著影响需满足贡献幅度充分与符号聚合一致两个必要条件；并通过因果分析考察角色跨度贡献对响应结构、内容选择与事实取值等关键token选择的作用。

Result: 角色跨度贡献影响涉及响应结构、内容选择与事实值的关键token选择；角色一致的选择依赖于这些关键决策处获得充分的角色跨度支持，而当该支持足以弥补缺口时，原本失败的选择可被逆转。

Conclusion: ICL与OOC幻觉共享统一机制：每个功能角色定义了输出决策的模式，角色跨度贡献须在该模式上保持有效，这种组织方式的差异解释了两种看似对立的结果。

Abstract: Large language models can infer and follow specific rules through in-context learning (ICL), yet may fail to remain faithful to provided evidence, giving rise to out-of-context (OOC) hallucination. Existing work has largely investigated the underlying mechanisms of ICL and OOC hallucination along separate lines. However, this separation obscures the mechanistic basis for these seemingly opposing outcomes. In this work, we study this contrast through the unified view of functional roles in dynamic context. The key observation is that role information can be internally represented without reliably constraining output. We derive a first-order decomposition to characterize the role effect, showing that its substantial influence depends on two necessary conditions, namely sufficient contribution magnitude and coherent signed aggregation. Causal analysis further shows that role span contributions affect key token choices involving response structure, content selection, and factual values. We further show that role-consistent choices depend on sufficient role span support at these key decisions, while failing choices can be reversed when this support overcomes the deficit. These findings support a common mechanistic view of ICL and OOC hallucination: each functional role defines the pattern of output decisions over which role span contributions must remain effective, and differences in this organization account for the seemingly opposing outcomes.

</details>


### [194] [Piccolo: Co-Evolving Decision Models and Data through Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24651)
*Zhenqing Ling,Qirui Jiao,Daoyuan Chen,Cheng Chen,Xinji Mai,Haowen Gao,Zhuo Zhang,Xuchen Pan,Mengsu Ding,Jiaji Deng,Yuexiang Xie,Xiaoming Qin,Weirui Kuang,Zhaoyang Liu,Yaliang Li,Bolin Ding,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Piccolo 是 0.8B–27B 的多模态决策模型系列，通过 Agentic Data Engineering 与递归自我改进（RSI）实现模型能力与训练数据协同进化。Piccolo-27B 以 75.59 分登顶 Decision Index Vision Full 榜，9B/4B 分别为 68.35/67.46，三者在各自规模档的 Private 榜均排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策模型开发多依赖单一扩大模型规模或使用最新骨干网络，缺乏模型能力与训练数据协同演化的系统方法；作者希望验证"数据驱动 RSI"能否作为构建前沿决策模型的可复制路径，并检验规模、数据与骨干新旧之间的常规假设是否成立。

Method: 提出 Piccolo 模型族（0.8B–27B），对通用文本与视觉判断、agent harness、数据工作流与交互游戏输出候选答案概率分布。由研究型 agent 分析模型行为、构造训练数据、开展实验并据结果迭代后续提案；通过语义切片（semantic slices）与共享研究记忆（shared research memory）在模型族开发中串联各环节。训练配方包含数十万级样本，数据选择与反馈驱动精修是自改进核心。

Result: Piccolo-27B 在 Decision Index Vision Full 榜首（75.59），Piccolo-9B 与 4B 为 68.35 与 67.46，三者均在其规模档的 Private 榜位列第一。大规模 RSI 探索得到三点反直觉结论：(1) 骨干-配方协同适配优于单纯使用更新模型，相同配方下旧骨干在视觉任务上可超越新一代；(2) 定向数据精修可使 4B–9B 模型在专业工作流上媲美 27B 基线；(3) 连续损失下降可与离散决策准确率脱钩甚至带来退化。

Conclusion: Piccolo 连同 Data-Juicer 与 AgentScope 生态发布的权重与工具，为构建前沿决策模型及规模化数据驱动 RSI 提供了可操作的蓝图；数据选择与反馈驱动精修的价值高于单纯扩大模型规模或追逐骨干新旧。

Abstract: We present Piccolo, a family of multimodal decision models (0.8B to 27B) that coevolves model capabilities and training data via Agentic Data Engineering and recursive self-improvement (RSI). The models predict probabilities over candidate answers for general-purpose text and visual judgments, agent harnesses, data workflows, and interactive games. Piccolo-27B tops the Decision Index Vision Full leaderboard with a score of 75.59, while Piccolo-9B and Piccolo-4B achieve 68.35 and 67.46, respectively. All three rank first on the Private benchmark in their respective model-size categories. Research agents analyze model behavior, construct training data, conduct experiments, and use the results to refine subsequent proposals. Semantic slices and a shared research memory connect these actions across the model family's development. With training recipes comprising hundreds of thousands of examples, data selection and feedback-driven refinement are central to the self-improvement process. Our large-scale RSI exploration yields three counter-intuitive insights for decision-model development: (1) Backbone-recipe co-adaptation outweighs raw model recency: earlier backbones can surpass newer generations on visual tasks under identical recipes; (2) Targeted data curation enables 4B-9B models to rival 27B baselines on specialized workflows; and (3) Continuous loss reduction can decouple from and even degrade discrete decision accuracy. Together with the released weights and tools from the Data-Juicer and AgentScope ecosystems, Piccolo provides an actionable blueprint for building frontier decision models and scaling data-driven RSI.

</details>


### [195] [Chain-of-Thought Hidden States Reveal Task-Solving Directions for Direct Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24598)
*Houman Mehrafarin,Ioannis Konstas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过线性探针发现模型对CoT中"恢复态"与"失败态"的编码可区分，并提出沿每题的特定方向旋转直接回答提示的最后隐藏状态，在更短生成下大幅超越CoT准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT提示虽能激发中间推理步骤提升LLM表现，但存在缺陷：部分任务上不如直接回答提示，另一些任务需生成数百token才得正确答案。作者旨在探究CoT是否真的是达到该准确率的最短路径。

Method: 将每个CoT隐藏状态标注为"恢复态"（patch入直接提示可得正确答案）或"失败态"，用线性探针验证两类可区分；进而在直接提示的表示空间中，为每题由自身恢复态与失败态构造一个方向，在生成前将直接提示最后隐藏状态旋转一次朝向该方向。

Result: 在25个任务上，该方法将直接提示平均准确率从46%提升至85%（LLaMA 3.1 8B，CoT为69%），从52%提升至86%（Qwen 2.5 7B，CoT为77%），且仅用约CoT十分之一的token。

Conclusion: CoT并非达到该准确率的最短路径；所构造的方向可为未来工作所用，通过训练模型使其状态与这些方向对齐。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting improves the performance of Large Language Models by eliciting intermediate reasoning steps. However, CoT is far from perfect: on some tasks, models perform worse than with direct-answer prompting, and on others they generate a few hundred tokens to reach the correct answer. We start by labelling each CoT hidden state as recovering if patching it into the direct prompt yields the correct answer, and failed otherwise. A linear probe shows that the model encodes these two classes differently. We then show that the representation space of the direct prompt holds a direction for each question that leads the model, in a short generation, to the correct answer more often than CoT does. Specifically, we rotate the direct prompt's last hidden state once, before generation, towards a direction built for each question from its own recovering and failed states. Across 25 tasks, these directions raise the direct prompt's mean accuracy from $46\%$ to $85\%$ on LLaMA 3.1 8B (CoT: $69\%$) and from $52\%$ to $86\%$ on Qwen 2.5 7B (CoT: $77\%$), with a tenth of CoT's tokens. CoT is thus not the shortest route to this accuracy, and these directions can be used in future work to train models to align their states with them.

</details>


### [196] [Late Entry in Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24576)
*Ziqi Jin,Jiaxi Li,Weiwen Xu,Aixin Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"晚期进入"(late entry)解码策略与Wake验证机制:新token跳过前若干轮循环、直接进入最后K次深度缓存传递,可在单一静态CUDA图内统一精确贪心、裕度门控与非回滚有损三种解码模式,在Ouro-2.6B与Huginn-3.5B上取得5.2x–12.5x加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环(looped)语言模型通过每token重复共享层堆栈T次提升推理深度,但递推深度直接转化为串行解码延迟。传统early-exit在初始若干pass后即停止,导致已生成token的KV缓存仅停留在浅层深度,无法利用深层上下文;作者希望探索相反策略,即利用深缓存上下文替代被跳过的token本地计算,从而在不牺牲质量的前提下降低墙钟时间。

Method: 1) 晚期进入:新token跳过前T-K次pass,直接在深上下文缓存上执行最后K次pass;经轻量微调修正进入边界处的隐藏状态范数失配。2) 为消除构建深缓存的停顿,候选块仅在关键路径上执行最后K次pass验证,已提交token的pass 1→T与后续步骤并行补全,不增加串行调用。3) 在既有输出GEMM内投影完成T-pass的wake状态(Wake verification),由logit-margin容差τ∈[0,∞]统一三种模式:τ=0为贪心精确解码(在bf16归约序下与K=T块解码一致),τ>0为裕度门控验证,τ=∞为非回滚有损晚期进入;整体封装于单一静态CUDA图。

Result: Ouro-2.6B(T=4),K=2:τ=0取得5.2x贪心精确加速(较K=4块解码在GSM8K上+26%);τ=∞取得6.8x,并在同等速度下较训练式early-exit提升16.9个点。Huginn-3.5B上将T扩展至{8,16,32,64},K=T/4的回滚次数随深度降低21.5x;τ=0加速达6.1x(T=32)与8.4x(T=64,较K=64块解码+153%);τ=∞加速达10.6–12.5x。

Conclusion: 深缓存上下文可有效替代被跳过的token局部pass,使late entry在多个模型族与pass预算下优于匹配的early exit。Wake验证将精确、门控与有损三种解码范式统一于单一静态CUDA图,且加速比随测试时递推深度T增大而扩大,表明晚期进入是释放循环语言模型深度-延迟权衡的有效路径。

Abstract: Looped language models deepen reasoning at a compact parameter count by iterating a shared layer stack T times per token, turning recurrence depth directly into sequential decoding latency. Conventional early-exit decoding halts after initial passes without cache completion, truncating cached key-value states of past tokens to shallow depth. We study the opposite strategy, late entry, where new tokens skip initial passes and enter directly into the final K < T passes over a deep context cache. Once lightweight fine-tuning resolves hidden-state norm mismatch at the entry boundary, deep cached context substitutes for skipped token-local passes, allowing late entry to outperform matched early exit across model families and pass budgets. To turn this asymmetry into wall-clock speedups without stalling to build deep caches, candidate blocks verify through only the final K passes on the critical path while previously committed tokens complete passes 1 -> T alongside subsequent steps at zero extra serial calls. Projecting these finished T-pass wake states inside the existing output GEMM (Wake verification, governed by logit-margin tolerance tau in [0, inf]) unifies greedy-exact T-pass decoding (tau = 0, matching K = T block decoding within bf16 reduction order), margin-gated verification (tau > 0), and non-rollback lossy late entry (tau = inf) inside a single static CUDA graph. On Ouro-2.6B (T = 4), K = 2 Wake reaches 5.2x greedy-exact speedup at tau = 0 (+26% over K = 4 block decoding on GSM8K) and 6.8x at tau = inf (+16.9 points over trained early exit at matched speed). Scaling test-time recurrence across T in {8, 16, 32, 64} on Huginn-3.5B cuts K = T/4 rollbacks 21.5x with depth, driving greedy-exact (tau = 0) speedups to 6.1x (T = 32) and 8.4x (T = 64, +153% over K = 64 block) and tau = inf speedups to 10.6 to 12.5x.

</details>


### [197] [Beyond Replacement: Levenshtein Editing for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23750)
*Shuaidi Wang,Zhuocheng Gong,Xuejie Liu,Jianguo Li,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LED后训练框架，将扩散语言模型的"仅替换"编辑扩展为插入/删除的Levenshtein式编辑，实现更鲁棒的自我纠错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型（DLM）解码方法多将已解码token视为固定，部分模型允许"仅替换编辑"（ROE）。ROE虽能纠正单个token，但对缺失/多余token引起的序列位移效率低，且难以应对多位置同时出错的场景。

Method: LED为后训练框架，在ROE基础上增加插入与删除操作。含两项训练设计：(1) 在SFT阶段借助最长公共子序列（LCS）对齐在线重建编辑监督，无需人工标注即可学习插入/删除；(2) 多阶段训练，将前一阶段的状态作为下一阶段输入，使模型暴露于自身早期错误并学会纠正。

Result: 在16B参数的LLaDA2.1-Mini Base上：置信度引导解码下，十二个基准平均分由54.86升至57.10，每次前向token数由6.14升至7.05；无置信度解码下平均分由27.52升至47.19。大k时pass@k优于无编辑的阈值解码，体现更强生成多样性；块大小128时平均分由36.97升至51.34，对大步长更鲁棒。

Conclusion: LED通过引入插入/删除编辑与多阶段自纠错训练，使DLM具备更鲁棒的自我纠错能力，并释放无置信度引导下的多样性潜力。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) offer a flexible alternative to autoregressive models, enabling any-order infilling. While most existing decoding methods treat decoded tokens as fixed, some recent models allow previously decoded tokens to be revised only by replacing them with alternative tokens, a paradigm we refer to as replacement-only editing (ROE). Although ROE can correct individual tokens, it is inefficient for sequence shifts caused by missing or extra tokens and struggles when errors occur across many positions. To address these limitations, we propose LED (Levenshtein Editing for Diffusion Language Models), a post-training framework that extends ROE with insertion and deletion, enabling Levenshtein-style refinement of generated sequences. It uses two training designs. First, it reconstructs edit supervision on the fly during supervised fine-tuning using longest-common-subsequence alignment, enabling insertion and deletion learning without manually annotated labels. Second, it adopts multi-stage training, where the state from one stage becomes the input to the next, exposing the model to its earlier errors and enabling it to correct them. When applied to the 16B-parameter LLaDA2.1-Mini Base, LED raises the average score across twelve benchmarks from 54.86 to 57.10 under confidence-guided decoding while increasing tokens per forward from 6.14 to 7.05, whereas under confidence-free decoding it raises the average score from 27.52 to 47.19. We further show that LED achieves higher pass@k at large k than thresholded decoding without editing, demonstrating improved generation diversity and revealing the diversity potential of DLMs without confidence guidance. LED also improves robustness to large block sizes, raising the average score from 36.97 to 51.34 at block size 128. Together, these results suggest that LED supports more robust self-correction in DLMs.

</details>


### [198] [Formal Adequacy Index (based on Polish-language material)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23482)
*Paweł Patreucha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文提出"正式度适配指数"（formal adequacy index），通过分别建模文本观测正式度与交际情境期望正式度，并引入情境容忍阈值进行比较，从而定量评估文本正式度是否符合交际语境期待；以波兰语致歉迟到邮件（人类撰写 vs 大模型生成）为例进行初步演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语体正式度是言语风格变异的一个维度，但仅测量正式度的高低无法判断所选交际方式在特定情境中是否恰当，缺少一个衡量"正式度—语境匹配程度"的量化指标。

Method: 基于波兰语材料构建正式度适配指数：(1) 依据文本语言特征单独建模"观测正式度"；(2) 依据交际情境特征单独建模"期望正式度"；(3) 比较二者并结合情境容忍阈值（正式度不足与正式度过剩可采用不同阈值）评估适配度；并以人类用户与大语言模型书写的波兰语"迟到致歉"邮件为案例作示例分析。

Result: 指数可对正式度与语境的匹配程度给出量化评价，并通过人类与LLM生成邮件的对比展示其潜在应用；作者明确指出该实现为初步性质，尚需进一步校准与实证验证。

Conclusion: 文章主要贡献在于提出一个可跨多种交际语境开展正式度适配定量研究的通用方法论框架，而非成熟的度量工具。

Abstract: Linguistic formality is one of the dimensions of stylistic variation in speech; however, simply measuring its level does not allow us to determine whether the chosen mode of communication is appropriate in a given situation. The aim of this article is to present the concept of a formal adequacy index (based on Polish-language material), which enables a quantitative assessment of how well the formality of a text matches the expectations arising from the communicative context. The proposed approach is based on the separate modelling of observed formality, determined on the basis of the linguistic features of the text, and expected formality, determined on the basis of the characteristics of the communicative situation. Adequacy is assessed by comparing these two measures, taking into account contextual tolerance thresholds, which may differ for a lack of formality and an excess of formality. The potential applications of the index are illustrated by an analysis of Polish-language emails containing apologies for being late, written by human language users or generated by a large language model. The implementation presented here is preliminary in nature and requires further calibration and empirical validation. The main contribution of this article is the proposal of a general methodological framework for the quantitative study of formal adequacy in various communicative contexts.

</details>


### [199] [A Foundation Model to Measure and Predict Human Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24830)
*Haoran Ye,Tianrui Huo,Hangzhen Li,Xin Zhang,Guojie Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Chiron——通过联合微调 LLM 同时表征心理构念、预测个体行为并对齐群体分布的人类行为基础模型，在 36 个行为域、78 个社会科学实验上显著优于前沿 LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 虽能生成看似合理的心理与行为响应，但常误表征个体、扭曲群体统计量，因而不足以为心理测量与行为预测提供有效工具。

Method: 构建 Chiron 基础模型，对 LLM 进行联合微调，同时优化三项目标：（1）表征心理构念；（2）预测个体行为；（3）将群体响应分布与人类统计分布对齐。训练与评测数据覆盖 36 个行为域、78 个社会科学实验，含 288,307 条个人记录、1,411,750 条原子行为决策、99,953 份心理测量（109 个心理构念）。

Result: 心理测量结果与人类自我报告更吻合、削弱心理测量效度偏差；个体行为预测优于前沿 LLM（相对提升最高 28.4%）；实验仿真中对人类效应方向与量级的刻画更准确（效应量估计误差降低 82.2%–94.3%）。

Conclusion: Chiron 可作为统一的计算工具，用于人类行为的测量与预测。摘要未给出跨域泛化边界、构念效度外部验证及各子任务贡献的消融分析。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to infer psychological attributes, predict individual behaviour, and simulate human populations. However, despite generating plausible responses, they often misrepresent individuals and distort population statistics. Here we introduce Chiron, a foundation model to measure and predict human behaviour. We jointly fine-tune an LLM to represent psychological constructs, predict individual behaviour, and align population response distributions with human statistics. The training and evaluation data span 36 behavioural domains and 78 social science experiments, comprising 288,307 person records, 1,411,750 atomic behavioural decisions, and 99,953 psychometric assessments over 109 psychological constructs. In our evaluations, Chiron's psychological measurements agree more closely with human self-reports and exhibit fewer biases that undermine psychometric validity. It predicts individual behaviour more accurately than frontier LLMs (up to 28.4% relative improvement). In simulations of social science experiments, it more accurately captures the direction and magnitude of human experimental effects (82.2--94.3% lower effect-size estimation error). Together, these results establish Chiron as a unified computational instrument for measuring and predicting human behaviour.

</details>


### [200] [On-Policy Morphing into Hybrid Attention Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24809)
*Disen Lan,Xuanda Wang,Jianbin Zheng,Huafeng Kuang,Wei Tang,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 FlashMorph2：一种 on-policy 层选择方法，直接以离散混合架构自身隐状态端到端评估，并联合优化层选择分数与模型权重，实现更优质量-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将预训练 Transformer 转换为全注意力+线性注意力混合模型时，需在给定预算下确定哪些层保留全注意力。FlashMorph 存在两处 off-policy 失配：(i) 连续分支插值与 teacher-forced 隐状态偏离离散混合计算；(ii) 冻结权重未考虑后续恢复训练中的参数自适应。

Method: FlashMorph2：(I) 采样离散混合架构，用其自身隐状态进行端到端评估；(II) 在恢复目标下联合优化层选择分数与模型权重，以反映自适应后的性能。

Result: 在 Qwen3 多规模模型与多种线性注意力变体上一致优于现有层选择方法；平均分较 FlashMorph 提升 RULER +2.42、LongBench +1.08、召回密集型任务 +0.76；0.6B–8B 模型层选择仅需 0.72–3.57 GPU 小时。

Conclusion: 纠正 off-policy 失配可显著提升混合架构层选择质量；FlashMorph2 兼具高精度与高效率、可扩展性。

Abstract: Hybrid attention models combine full and linear attention to achieve a better quality--efficiency trade-off. Converting pretrained Transformers into such models requires identifying which layers should retain full attention under a prescribed budget. We identify two off-policy mismatches in FlashMorph's layer selection: (i) continuous branch interpolation and teacher-forced hidden states differ from discrete hybrid computation; and (ii) frozen model weights fail to account for parameter adaptation during subsequent recovery. We introduce FlashMorph2, an on-policy layer-selection method that (I) evaluates sampled discrete hybrid architectures end to end using their own hidden states, and (II) jointly optimizes layer-selection scores and model weights under the recovery objective to reflect post-adaptation performance. Experiments across Qwen3 model scales and linear-attention variants show that FlashMorph2 consistently outperforms existing layer-selection methods, improving average scores over FlashMorph by $+2.42$ points on RULER, $+1.08$ on LongBench, and $+0.76$ on recall-intensive tasks, while completing layer selection in only $0.72$--$3.57$ GPU hours across 0.6B--8B models, demonstrating high efficiency and scalability.

</details>


### [201] [Induction Head Origins of SLAM-ASR Speech Recognition Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24721)
*Artem Fedorchenko,Tanel Alumäe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文揭示SLAM-ASR依赖冻结语言模型中的归纳头（induction heads）和成对的差分注意力头来完成语音到文本的映射，并据此提出改进噪声下转写质量的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLAM-ASR架构的前提是冻结语言模型具备足够丰富的语言知识以完成识别任务，但这一假设虽合理却无法解释模型内部的具体机制。作者希望揭示这些模型实际依赖的内部结构，而非停留在"语言模型提供语言知识"的抽象层面。

Method: 通过机制可解释性分析，定位SLAM-ASR中承担关键作用的具体注意力头：识别出与文本中复制重复token序列相同的归纳头，并发现其负责将语音embedding映射为转写token；进一步找出成对注意力头，它们表现出一种受限形式的差分注意力，用于抵消注意力噪声并将注意力锐化到前一个已生成词上。

Result: 实验表明，归纳头对语音embedding到文本token的映射至关重要；成对头的差分注意力机制能够抑制噪声、提升对上一生成词的注意力聚焦；基于归纳头还能在噪声音频条件下改善转写质量。

Conclusion: SLAM-ASR的竞争力可归因于冻结语言模型中可识别的具体注意力结构（归纳头与差分注意力头对），这些结构层面的发现既解释了模型机理，也为噪声鲁棒性改进提供了可操作的切入点。

Abstract: What makes a small trained map from encoder states to the embedding space of a frozen language model sufficient for competitive speech recognition? The premise of the SLAM-ASR architecture is that a frozen language model possesses linguistic knowledge rich enough to perform the recognition task. This assumption, while reasonable, provides little insight into the mechanisms underlying these models. We therefore seek the concrete internal structures on which they rely. This work shows that SLAM-ASR models rely on induction heads of the frozen language model: the same heads that copy repeated token sequences in text are also important for mapping speech embeddings to transcript tokens. Moreover, we find pairs of heads that behave as a constrained form of differential attention, cancelling attention noise and sharpening attention onto the previously generated word. Finally, we show how induction heads could be used to improve transcription quality under noisy audio.

</details>


### [202] [CurvDelta: Low-Curvature Delta Modeling For Surgical Learning and Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24654)
*Zeguan Xiao,Zhuocheng Liang,Yun Chen,Yong Wang,Guanhua Chen,Pinyan Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CurvDelta框架，将"能力保持"约束嵌入参数更新的参数化中，通过低曲率空间实现更新时保持损失近似不变，并给出低秩变体CurvLoRA，在数学推理、代码生成与知识遗忘任务上取得更好的获取—保持权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM适配的核心难题是能力保持：适配在达成目标的同时常导致通用能力退化，需要在优化中显式约束保持损失。

Method: 分析保持约束在适配前模型附近的局部几何，构造低曲率子空间，使参数更新几乎不改变保持损失；用Kronecker因子近似曲率（K-FAC）与因子化投影使构造对LLM可扩展；并在同一保持几何下提出低秩变体CurvLoRA以提升效率。

Result: 在数学推理与代码生成上取得有竞争力的下游性能且更好地保持通用能力；在TOFU与MUSE上的知识遗忘任务中，定向遗忘与知识保持的平衡优于基线，保持性能接近从头重训练参考。

Conclusion: 将保持约束参数化到更新几何中是可行且可扩展的，可同时支持能力学习与选择性遗忘等受约束适配任务，并提供更优的获取—保持权衡。

Abstract: A central challenge in adapting large language models (LLMs) is capability preservation: an adaptation can achieve its target while degrading general capabilities. We introduce CurvDelta, a scalable framework that incorporates the preservation constraint into the parameterization of the model update. By analyzing the local geometry of the preservation constraint around the pre-adaptation model, CurvDelta constructs a low-curvature space in which parameter updates leave the preservation loss nearly invariant. We make its construction practical for LLMs through Kronecker-factored approximate curvature (K-FAC) and a factorized projection. We also introduce CurvLoRA, a low-rank variant within the same preservation geometry, designed for more efficient adaptation. Both methods apply to preservation-constrained adaptation tasks such as capability learning and selective unlearning. We evaluate our methods on mathematical reasoning, code generation, and knowledge unlearning. For mathematical reasoning and code generation, our approaches achieve competitive downstream performance while better preserving general capabilities, offering a favorable acquisition--retention trade-off relative to the baselines. For knowledge unlearning, our main results on TOFU and MUSE show a better balance between targeted forgetting and knowledge retention than the baselines, with retention performance close to the from-scratch retraining reference.

</details>


### [203] [ProbMedTOD: A Bayesian Network Guided Task-Oriented Dialogue System for Patient History Taking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24535)
*Vishal Vivek Saley,Bhavesh Gurnani,Dinesh Raghu,Mausam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ProbMedTOD，从少量临床笔记自动合成临床贝叶斯网络并与小型语言模型（SLM）结合，实现多轮问诊中的可解释概率诊断推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮问诊需在迭代提问中维护并更新诊断不确定性（对应临床推理的"System 2"），而SLM仅具备快速直觉式的"System 1"能力；同时隐私、成本、延迟限制使大LLM不适用，而人工构建临床贝叶斯网络又依赖专家标注结构、依赖与参数，构建困难。

Method: ProbMedTOD分三阶段自动合成临床贝叶斯网络：本体构建、结构预测、参数估计；随后与SLM集成：BayesNet Agent维护实时诊断后验分布，Policy Agent选择诊断信息量最大的问题，从而在对话中引入概率推理。

Result: 在DDXPlus与MIMIC-IV Notes上，ProbMedTOD优于纯LLM及检索式基线，且对更小规模模型的提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 将自动构建的贝叶斯网络与SLM互补结合，可在低成本、隐私友好的前提下为多轮问诊提供可审计的概率信念追踪与高效提问策略，是替代大LLM的可行方案。

Abstract: Patient history taking is a strategic, multi-turn process that refines diagnostic hypotheses through iterative questioning, mirroring clinical reasoning where each inquiry updates beliefs over candidate diagnoses. Deploying AI in this setting is constrained by privacy, cost, and latency, making Small Language Models (SLMs) a more desirable backbone compared to larger LLMs. However, SLMs capture only the fast, intuitive "System 1" side of this process and lack explicit mechanisms for the deliberative "System 2" reasoning needed to maintain and update diagnostic uncertainty over time. Bayesian Networks (BayesNets) offer a natural complement, providing an interpretable framework for auditable probabilistic belief tracking. Yet, clinical BayesNets are difficult to construct due to their reliance on expert curated structure, dependencies, and parameters.
  We introduce ProbMedTOD, which automatically synthesizes a clinical BayesNet from a small number of clinical notes in three stages: ontology building, structure prediction, and parameter estimation. ProbMedTOD then integrates the resulting BayesNet with SLMs, enabling probabilistic reasoning in multi-turn dialogues. A BayesNet Agent maintains real-time posterior distributions over diagnoses, while a Policy Agent picks diagnostically informative questions. Experiments on DDXPlus and MIMIC-IV Notes show that ProbMedTOD outperforms LLM-only and retrieval-based baselines, with especially strong gains for smaller models.

</details>


### [204] [MECI: Multi-Element Collaborative Interaction for Multimodal Entity Linking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24522)
*Jie Peng,Yongxue Shan,Yongfu Zha,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MECI框架，通过多视图专家网络、层次化多模态交互模块与LLM零样本重排序，缓解多模态实体链接中的元素主导问题，在三个数据集上Hits@1提升1.95%/7.30%/2.31%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MEL的核心挑战是捕捉多级对应关系：mention-entity语义一致性与跨模态互补相关性。现有方法依赖耦合交互或粗糙的全局聚合，易产生"元素主导"（element dominance）问题，即某单一元素或模态过度主导表示。

Method: 1) 多视图专家网络：以"分而治之"策略进行解耦特征学习，并用KL引导的路由机制控制专家专化与协作；2) 层次化多模态交互模块：设计动态模态感知加权网络，在分层语义层级上精化交互，整合多粒度证据；3) 生成式语义精化阶段：利用大语言模型进行零样本重排序。

Result: 在WikiDiverse、RichpediaMEL、WikiMEL三个数据集上持续优于SOTA，Hits@1分别提升1.95%、7.30%、2.31%。

Conclusion: 解耦式特征学习结合层次化模态感知交互可有效缓解元素主导问题，且LLM零样本重排序可进一步提升链接性能。

Abstract: Multimodal Entity Linking (MEL) aims to disambiguate mentions in multimodal contexts by grounding them to specific entities in a knowledge base. A pivotal challenge in MEL is capturing multi-level correspondences: the semantic consistency between mention-entity pairs and the complementary correlations across modalities. However, existing methods often suffer from element dominance due to their reliance on coupled interactions or coarse global aggregations. In response, we propose the Multi-Element Collaborative Interaction (MECI) framework. First, to capture multi-element mention-entity correspondences, we develop a Multi-view Experts Network that leverages a "divide-and-conquer" strategy for decoupled feature learning to mitigate element dominance, supported by a KL-guided routing mechanism that governs expert specialization and collaboration. Furthermore, to model cross-modal complementary correlations, we propose a Hierarchical Multimodal Interaction Module, where a dynamic modality-aware weighting network refines interactions across hierarchical semantic levels, thereby integrating multi-granular evidence to counteract element dominance. Finally, we incorporate a generative semantic refinement stage that utilizes large language models for zero-shot re-ranking. Extensive experiments on WikiDiverse, RichpediaMEL, and WikiMEL show that MECI consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, improving Hits@1 by 1.95%, 7.30%, and 2.31%, respectively.

</details>


### [205] [ReflectRec: Cycle-Consistency Preference Verification for LLM-based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23969)
*Dae Kyoo Kim,Heejung Cho,Sungmoo Hong,Eunil Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 ReflectRec，用"偏好—历史往返验证器"的召回分数作为可操作信号，驱动条件重生成与候选选择，并配合语义化 Stage-1 提示与 Forward-Look 检索注入行为相似用户的未来观看作为协同提示，在 ML-100K/1M 上显著超越 CoT4Rec-ICL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT 式 LLM 推荐器把自然语言偏好笔记当作排序的思维链前缀，但该笔记未经核验即被信任，且排序器完全不参考行为相似用户的信息，存在偏好可信度不可控与协同信号缺失两个缺陷。

Method: 以偏好到历史的往返验证器为核心，其召回分数刻画"偏好可被历史回溯恢复"的程度，作为操作性信号用于：对低可恢复性偏好做条件重生成、在多次生成间做选择；外围接入语义化 Stage-1 提示、条件重生成与 Forward-Look 检索，将相似用户的未来观看记录作为协同提示注入。

Result: 在 MovieLens-100K 与 MovieLens-1M 上，完整流程较 CoT4Rec-ICL 的 Hit@10 分别提升 +16.25 / +4.46 个百分点；各 ValScore 分桶均为正增益，并在 Qwen3-30B、Qwen2.5-72B、Llama-3.3-70B 三个骨干上成立；Amazon Electronics 上亦观察到类似的验证器引导重生成效应。

Conclusion: 将偏好笔记的可验证性显式量化并回馈到生成与选择环节，同时补齐协同信号，是提升 CoT 式 LLM 推荐效果的有效且具跨数据集、跨骨干稳健性的路径。

Abstract: CoT-style LLM recommenders generate a natural-language preference note as a chain-of-thought prefix to ranking, but the note is trusted without verification, and the ranker never consults behaviorally similar users. We propose ReflectRec, organized around a preference-to-history round-trip verifier whose recall score serves as an operational signal of preference-to-history recoverability, enabling conditional regeneration for low-recoverability preferences and selection among regeneration attempts. Around this verifier, we wire a semantic Stage-1 prompt, conditional regeneration, and Forward-Look retrieval that injects future watches of behaviorally similar users as a collaborative hint. On MovieLens-100K and MovieLens-1M, the full pipeline outperforms CoT4Rec-ICL by +16.25 / +4.46 pp Hit@10, with positive gains across all ValScore buckets and three LLM backbones (Qwen3-30B, Qwen2.5-72B, and Llama-3.3-70B). We also observe a similar verifier-guided regeneration pattern on Amazon Electronics.

</details>


### [206] [Tracking Representations in LLM Reasoning Traces for Iterated Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23403)
*Chaipat Tirapongprasert,Rajarshi Das,Parijat Dube*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文用机制可解释性方法研究LLM智能体在不完全信息迭代博弈中，外部化思维链（CoT）轨迹与模型内部实际计算之间的关系，发现模型在CoT中形成并迭代修正对博弈的内部表征，一旦过早固化错误信念便难以恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体广泛依赖CoT推理并取得SOTA表现，但对外部化推理轨迹与模型真实内部计算之间的关系理解甚少；同时多智能体系统日益普及，其策略推理错误的诊断需求迫切。
