Method: 以不完全信息迭代博弈为任务场景，让LLM智能体进行决策，并采用一系列机制可解释性技术（分析各层激活等）追踪模型内部表征在CoT过程中的形成与演化，同时对照文本轨迹进行分析。

Result: 模型在整个CoT过程中构建并迭代修正对博弈的内部表征；当模型过早固着于错误信念后，极少能够自我纠正，这一失败同时体现在字面文本轨迹与层级激活中。

Conclusion: 外部化CoT轨迹与模型实际计算存在可探测的对应关系，错误信念的早期固化是策略推理失败的关键机制；该工作为多智能体系统策略推理错误的针对性诊断开辟了方向，并提供了方法可视化工具。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents achieve state-of-the-art performance across domains through chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning. Despite their ubiquitous use, little is understood about the relationship between the externalized trace and the model's actual computation. We address this by investigating how LLM agents make decisions in iterative games with incomplete information. Using a series of mechanistic interpretability techniques, we find that the model forms and iteratively revises an internal representation of the game throughout the CoT. Once the model prematurely commits to an incorrect belief, it rarely recovers. This failure is manifested in both the literal trace and the layer activations. Ultimately, as multi-agent systems are more prevalent, our work opens the door to a more focused diagnosis of their strategic reasoning errors. For illustrative purposes, we offer an interactive visualizer of our methodology on selected sample traces at https://interp-self.github.io/gametrace/

</details>


### [207] [Nothing to Declare: Steering a single neuron increases concealment in AI teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24475)
*Edoardo Chidichimo,Felix P. S. Wallis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现对Qwen3.5-4B中单个可解释神经元进行输出方向引导，会提高"团队情境下比单独情境下更多隐瞒信息"的倾向；全员受引导时集体隐瞒在四个场景中上升，而智能体间通信会抑制该效应，且移除该神经元贡献影响甚微。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究提示单个神经元可具有可解释的行为角色，但这类内部干预的效应是否受社会/集体情境调节尚不清楚。作者关注集体环境是否会改变内部干预对AI行为的影响。

Method: 在Qwen3.5-4B中定位一个与隐瞒信息相关的神经元，沿其输出方向进行activation steering；在五个场景中比较"单独"与"团队"条件（并控制队友无消息的基线），再测试全员同时受引导时的集体行为，以及智能体间消息传递的作用；同时做消融（移除该神经元贡献）对照。

Result: 沿该神经元输出方向引导后，团队情境下的隐瞒决策多于单独情境，且在队友不发送任何消息时依然成立；全员参与并受引导时，集体隐瞒在四个场景中增加；智能体间消息传递有助于在引导条件下缓和隐瞒；移除该神经元贡献的效应可忽略。

Conclusion: 集体情境会改变内部干预对AI行为的作用方式，单神经元级引导的效果具有情境依赖性；单纯消融与正向引导并不对称，提示可解释性与因果干预需在多智能体环境中评估。

Abstract: Recent research suggests that individual neurons can have interpretable roles in AI behaviour. In Qwen3.5-4B, steering along one neuron's output direction increased decisions to conceal information more in teams than alone across five scenarios, even when teammates provided no messages. When all members participated and received steering, collective concealment increased in four scenarios, while inter-agent messaging helped temper concealment under steering. Moreover, removing the neuron's contribution had negligible effects. Collective settings can therefore change how an internal intervention affects AI behaviour.

</details>


### [208] [Objective-Specialized Residual Streams in Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24567)
*Shantanu Agarwal,Ruchita V. Maitri,Zhiqi Huang,Vivek Datla,Alfy Samuel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 为 mHC 的不同并行流分配不同学习目标（稀疏词法检索与稠密语义检索），可在全融合范围、2/4 流及两种规模上稳定优于共享目标训练，并诱发出深层化的目标特异化结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 mHC 将残差流扩展为多路并行流并学习跨流连接，但所有流共享同一目标训练；作者想检验是否可通过给不同流分配不同目标来提升任务性能并产生目标特异化。

Method: 以互补的词法（稀疏）与语义（稠密）检索目标为测试用例，对比目标特异化 mHC 与非特异化 mHC，覆盖完整稀疏–稠密融合区间、2 流与 4 流、两种模型规模；再用流表示探针、残差混合分析、目标特异性梯度流和读/写门分析内部机制；并加入将残差混合固定为单位矩阵的消融。

Result: 特异化模型在所有设置下均稳定优于非特异化模型。两者在网络大部分层行为相似，仅在最后几层分化：特异化模型呈现日益增强的目标特异性梯度流、不同的读/写路由模式、更弱的跨流混合；不同目标的流间相似度下降，同目标流保持高度相似。固定残差混合为单位矩阵后特异化收益仍保留。

Conclusion: 将不同目标分配给 mHC 各流可提升多目标检索性能，并在残差计算中诱导出结构化、随深度演化的特异化；收益主要来自目标特异的读/写路由而非学习到的跨流混合。

Abstract: Manifold-constrained Hyper-Connections (mHC) extend a Transformer's residual stream into multiple parallel streams with learned cross-stream connections. Prior work trains all streams toward a shared objective. We instead study whether assigning distinct learning objectives to different streams can improve task performance and induce objective-specific specialization. Using complementary lexical (sparse) and semantic (dense) retrieval objectives as a test case, we find that objective-specialized mHC consistently outperforms a non-specialized mHC model across the full sparse--dense fusion range, for both two- and four-stream models and at two model scales. We then examine how specialization changes the model internally using probes of stream representations, residual mixing, objective-specific gradient flow, and read/write gates. The specialized and non-specialized models behave similarly through most of the network but diverge in the final layers: the specialized model develops increasingly objective-specific gradient flow, distinct read/write routing patterns, and reduced cross-stream mixing, and streams assigned to different objectives become increasingly dissimilar while those trained toward the same objective remain highly similar. Fixing the residual mixing to the identity leaves the specialization benefit intact, indicating that the benefit stems from objective-specific read/write routing rather than learned cross-stream mixing. These results show that assigning distinct objectives to mHC streams can improve multi-objective retrieval while inducing structured, depth-dependent specialization in the residual computation.

</details>


### [209] [Ground, Iterate, Trace: Decomposing AI Research Ideation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24887)
*Samson Yu,Qiran Zou,Jing Yu Lim,Rushi Shah,Anirudh Goyal,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CAR智能体框架，通过文献语义知识图谱与四类概念算子实现可追踪的科研创意生成，在方法新颖性与可行性上优于基线，但消融显示显式算子的增益在多重比较校正后不稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型已广泛用于生成科研想法，但"自动化创意为何有效"以及"新颖提案如何从既有文献中涌现"仍不清楚。作者希望把创意的生成过程显式化、可检查，从而将"想法质量评价"与"过程可追踪性"分离。

Method: 构建Creative AI Researcher（CAR）智能体框架：从检索文献构建语义知识图谱，为创意智能体配备图检索工具与四个显式概念算子（bisociation 双联想、analogy 类比、exploration 探索、elaboration 精化）；每次算子调用需通过结构化的接受条件校验并记录，形成中间变换轨迹与文献来源声明。评估上使用两种语言模型骨干、40个LiveIdeaBench主题及两个执行基准，并做十条件消融实验。

Result: CAR的方法学新颖性（MN）显著高于单次提示与The AI Scientist；与Research Ideation Agent相比MN无显著差异，但可行性在两个骨干上均更高；在共享实验执行器下，CAR于FML-bench-Lite与BioXArena取得最高成对胜率。十条件消融中，MN点估计偏向CAR，但经多重比较校正后无任何MN对比显著。

Conclusion: 显式算子相对于自由形式迭代并未带来一致性质量提升，故不能确立其质量收益；CAR的主要贡献在于使操作序列与文献来源可被检视，从而实现想法质量评判与过程可追踪性的分离。

Abstract: Large language models have been widely used to generate plausible research ideas, yet it remains unclear what makes automated ideation effective and how generated novel proposals arise from prior scientific work. We introduce the Creative AI Researcher (CAR), an agentic framework combining literature grounding, iterative development and explicit conceptual operators. CAR constructs a semantic knowledge graph from retrieved literature and equips an ideation agent with graph-retrieval tools and four operators: bisociation, analogy, exploration and elaboration. Each operator call is checked against structural acceptance conditions and recorded, producing a trace of intermediate transformations and declared literature sources. We evaluate CAR with two language-model backbones across 40 LiveIdeaBench research topics and two execution benchmarks. CAR achieves significantly higher methodological novelty (MN) than single-shot prompting and The AI Scientist. Its methodological novelty does not differ significantly from the Research Ideation Agent, while its feasibility scores are higher on both backbones. With a shared experiment executor, CAR achieves the highest pairwise win rate among the evaluated AI methods on FML-bench-Lite and BioXArena. In a ten-condition ablation study, MN point estimates favor CAR over one-step proposal on both backbones, but no MN contrast survives correction for multiple comparisons. The results do not establish a consistent quality benefit from explicit operators beyond free-form iteration. CAR makes the sequence of operations and declared literature sources inspectable, separating judged idea quality from procedural traceability.

</details>


### [210] [General progress substitutes for specific training data in language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24844)
*Mark Marosi,Peter Antal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究"训练数据审查/过滤"对模型能力延迟的有效性:被刻意剔除的技能信号可被其余训练数据的通用进步所"替代",延迟时长可由小模型预测,且后续用极少数据即可重新习得该技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者通过在训练中剔除数据来阻止模型习得可能被滥用的能力,但这种做法的实际效果未知:能力究竟被延迟多久、事后重新习得又需多少数据,缺乏定量证据。论文旨在量化"数据过滤"作为能力管控手段的有效性与局限。

Method: 在象棋、黑白棋(Othello)、分子字符串、括号语言与网络文本等多个领域中,分别剔除单项技能的训练信号,观察能力出现时点;用小模型的延迟推断大模型的延迟;比较被剔除技能与同等难度对照规则的学习速度;对70亿参数模型剔除某编程语言文本后再行训练,检验技能回归规律。

Result: 未被显式训练回应"将军"的象棋模型,对合法着法的排序几乎与受过训练者相当;被剔除技能在大模型中的延迟可由小模型预测;随后用被剔除数据再训练时,所需数据仅为从零训练的一小部分,且该比例随模型能力提升而下降;同等难度的对照规则学习慢得多;70亿参数模型中剔除的编程语言以相同方式被重新获得。

Conclusion: 数据过滤只能"延迟"而非"消除"能力:模型其余训练带来的通用进步会替代被剔除的数据,且后续重新习得所需数据随模型变强而递减。因此单纯数据审查作为能力管控手段效果有限,评估与治理需考虑"延迟"这一本质。

Abstract: Language models acquire their capabilities from training data, and developers withhold data during training to keep models from learning capabilities that could be misused. How long this delays a capability, and how much data is needed to acquire it later, is unknown. Here we show that the general progress a model makes on the rest of its training data substitutes for the data that was withheld. We withheld the training signal for individual skills in models of chess, Othello, molecular strings, bracket languages and web text. The delay of a withheld skill in larger models was predicted from smaller ones. A chess model never explicitly trained to respond to check ranked the legal responses almost as well as a model that was. When models were then trained on the withheld data, they acquired the skill with a small fraction of the data needed from initialization, and this fraction decreased as the models progressed. A control rule of equal difficulty was learned much more slowly. A programming language withheld from the training text of a 7-billion-parameter model was regained in the same way. Data filtering therefore delays a capability, and the data required to regain it decreases as models improve.

</details>


### [211] [Do Frontier Model Ingredients Add Up? A Replicated Factorial Study of Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24540)
*Biswa Sengupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在相同模型、相同数据和相同训练预算下比较五种已发表的改进，并用第二个随机种子重复验证，发现MoE、Muon优化与Gated DeltaNet混合收益最大，额外归一化亦有帮助，注意力门控在参数匹配预算下平均增益很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建小型语言模型时需要决定组合哪些已发表的改进，但缺乏在统一条件下对各改进增益及相互作用的可比评估。

Method: 固定同一模型、同一数据与同一训练预算，对比五种改动；测试选定组合，并对每种设置使用第二个随机种子重复实验，考察增益可加性、组件间交互及统计估计的稳健性。

Result: MoE层、Muon优化器与Gated DeltaNet混合结构带来最大提升；额外归一化有帮助；带参数匹配预算的注意力门控平均增益甚微。各改进增益大体可叠加，但重复实验重现了改动间的小幅交互作用；重复运行还揭示出初次统计估计被判为噪声的收益。

Conclusion: 结论限于小模型训练设定，且专家模型总容量更大。实践建议：将候选组件放在一起测试，并重复比较后再决定保留哪些组件。

Abstract: Building a small language model requires choosing which published improvements to combine. We compare five changes in the same model, using the same data and training budget. We test selected combinations and repeat each one with a second random seed. Mixture-of-experts layers, Muon optimisation and a Gated DeltaNet hybrid give the largest gains. Extra normalisation also helps, while an attention gate with a matched parameter budget adds little on average. The gains mostly combine, but small interactions between changes appear again in the repeated runs. Repetition also reveals benefits that an initial statistical estimate treated as noise. These findings apply to our small-model training setting; the expert models also have greater total capacity. The practical lesson is to test promising components together and repeat the comparisons before deciding which ones to keep.

</details>


### [212] [LipSpell: A Language-Independent Phonetic Scoring Framework for Spelling Correction Combining Distinctive-Feature Edit Distance, Keyboard Geometry, and Corpus Frequency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24285)
*Leonardo Araujo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LipSpell：语言无关的音素评分引擎 + 可插拔语言前端，结合IPA区别特征音距矩阵、键盘几何与语料先验，两阶段索引加速，在英、葡、俄、土、希伯来、法等语言上超越Norvig/Hunspell/Aspell。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拼写纠错要么依赖正交编辑距离与键盘邻近性（对多字符偏离的音位错误失效），要么依赖英语中心的语音哈希（Soundex/Metaphone）。需要一个语言无关、能统一处理键盘误触与认知/音位拼写错误的框架。

Method: 统一的语言无关语音评分引擎，配合可替换的语言特定G2P前端、键盘映射与词典。评分融合：(1) 由通用IPA区别特征预计算的|P|×|P|音素距离矩阵；(2) 物理键盘几何；(3) 语料一元频率先验。用两阶段对称删除 + 七类语音编码索引进行加速。在六个英语基准（含TOEFL-Spell 1000条L2错误）及六种语言（日耳曼、罗曼、斯拉夫、闪米特、突厥五个语支，三大语系，三种文字）上评测固定权重迁移与调优效果。

Result: 英语六个基准全部优于Norvig与Hunspell，并在Norvig1(78.52% vs 75.56%)、Holbrook(32.00% vs 26.67%)、TOEFL-Spell(77.30% vs 72.80%)上超过Aspell。巴西葡语Top-1 73.00%（含NFD容差75.80%，对比Norvig 66.40%、Hunspell 64.20%、Aspell 64.00%），俄语西里尔85.67%（vs Norvig 78.50%）。50/50干净非词切分下调优后：葡语86.97% vs 76.75%、土耳其语81.67% vs 79.05%、希伯来辅音文字74.59% vs 62.30%、法语74.53% vs 67.92%；英语(80.37%)与俄语(85.67%)固定权重即最优。

Conclusion: 语音特征驱动的统一评分引擎可在多语系、多文字下实现强跨语言迁移，固定权重对部分语言已接近最优，调优对另一些语言增益显著；该设计为语言无关拼写纠错提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Automatic spelling correction must handle both keyboard typographical slips and cognitive or phonetic misspellings where writers approximate a word's pronunciation. Conventional spell checkers rely either on orthographic edit distance and keyboard proximity--which struggle when phonetic errors diverge by multiple characters--or on English-centric phonetic hashes such as Soundex and Metaphone. We present LipSpell, a unified, language-independent phonetic scoring engine paired with swappable language-specific G2P frontends, keyboard maps, and lexicons integrating: (1) a precomputed |P|x|P| phone-distance matrix from universal IPA distinctive features, (2) physical keyboard geometry, and (3) corpus unigram frequency priors, accelerated by a two-stage symmetric-delete and seven-class phonetic-code index. Across six English benchmarks (including 1,000 L2 errors from TOEFL-Spell), LipSpell outperforms Norvig and Hunspell on every dataset and beats Aspell on Norvig1 (78.52% vs. 75.56%), Holbrook (32.00% vs. 26.67%), and TOEFL-Spell (77.30% vs. 72.80%). Evaluated across six languages spanning five branches (Germanic, Romance, Slavic, Semitic, Turkic), three families (Indo-European, Afro-Asiatic, Turkic), and three scripts, LipSpell achieves strong fixed-weight transfer on Brazilian Portuguese (73.00% Top-1 on n=500 Wikipedia without NFD tolerance and 75.80% with NFD vs. 66.40% Norvig, 64.20% Hunspell, 64.00% Aspell) and Russian Cyrillic (85.67% vs. 78.50% Norvig). On 50/50 clean non-word splits with lexicon cleaning and weak-character indels, tuning yields strong gains over Norvig in Portuguese (86.97% vs. 76.75%), Turkish (81.67% vs. 79.05%), Hebrew Abjad (74.59% vs. 62.30%), and French (74.53% vs. 67.92%), while fixed weights remain optimal for English (80.37%) and Russian (85.67%).

</details>


### [213] [CounselTraj: Synthesizing Process-Aware Psychological Counseling Dialogues via Dialogue-Act Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24168)
*Zhiyang Qi,Michimasa Inaba*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出CounselTraj，一种对话行为轨迹规划框架，将简短的合成心理咨询对话扩展为具有过程感知的会话级长对话，并据此构建CounselTrajD数据集与CounselTrajLM模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的心理咨询辅助系统需要大规模高质量对话数据训练，但真实咨询记录因隐私问题难以收集共享；现有合成数据虽覆盖多场景与多元来访者画像，但多为简短交换，无法刻画会话级的咨询过程。

Method: CounselTraj从人类咨询师产出的角色扮演咨询对话中学习对话行为（dialogue-act）模式，为来访者与咨询师共同规划协同轨迹，并将其作为灵活引导用于长程对话生成；在此基础上构建含1万余条过程引导中文心理咨询对话的CounselTrajD，并训练响应生成模型CounselTrajLM。

Result: 在三个中文合成心理咨询数据集上，CounselTraj相较直接扩展与随机采样轨迹扩展，生成更完整连贯的会话及更高质量的咨询师干预；在响应层与会话层评估中，CounselTrajLM在响应质量、治疗性理解与干预、咨询过程连贯性与推进度上均优于公开可用的中文咨询模型。

Conclusion: 以对话行为轨迹规划显式建模咨询过程，可有效弥补简短合成对话在会话级过程建模上的不足，并为训练具备过程感知能力的心理咨询对话模型提供可行路径。

Abstract: The growing demand for psychological counseling has motivated the development of LLM-based systems that support human counselors, but training such systems requires large-scale, high-quality dialogue data. Authentic counseling transcripts are difficult to collect and share because of privacy concerns, making synthetic dialogues an important alternative. However, existing synthetic datasets, despite covering diverse scenarios and client profiles, often consist of brief exchanges and fail to capture session-level counseling processes. We propose CounselTraj, a dialogue-act trajectory planning framework for expanding brief synthetic dialogues into process-aware, session-level psychological counseling dialogues. CounselTraj learns dialogue-act patterns from role-play counseling dialogues produced by human counselors, plans coordinated trajectories for clients and counselors, and uses them as flexible guidance for long-form dialogue generation. Experiments across three Chinese synthetic counseling datasets show that CounselTraj produces more complete and coherent sessions with higher-quality counselor interventions than both direct expansion and expansion with randomly sampled trajectories. Based on CounselTraj, we construct CounselTrajD, a dataset containing over 10K process-guided Chinese psychological counseling dialogues, and use it to train CounselTrajLM for counseling response generation. Evaluations at both the response and session levels show that CounselTrajLM outperforms publicly available Chinese counseling models in response quality, therapeutic understanding and intervention, and counseling-process coherence and progress. Our code and model are publicly available at https://github.com/UEC-InabaLab/CounselTraj.

</details>


### [214] [Compression Effects on Low-Resource Multilingual Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24043)
*Abbas Sani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该试点研究考察训练后量化对三种非洲语言（豪萨语、斯瓦希里语、约鲁巴语）多语机器翻译质量与推理成本的影响，覆盖三个模型族：Qwen2.5-1.5B、Aya-23-8B、NLLB-200-distilled-1.3B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言（非洲语言）的机器翻译部署受算力与显存约束，需要在消费级硬件上量化模型的可用性与量化压缩的实际收益，并厘清专用MT模型与通用大模型之间的性价比差异。

Method: 在FLORES-200 devtest（每个方向1,012句）上，于消费级NVIDIA T4 GPU上比较FP16、bitsandbytes INT8与NF4 INT4三种精度，对三个模型族在三个语言方向上的翻译质量（chrF）与推理成本（显存、延迟）进行评测。

Result: (i) 专用MT模型在豪萨语与斯瓦希里语方向比两个LLM高32.7–43.3 chrF，在约鲁巴语方向高12.9–24.7 chrF，而参数量约为8B指令微调LLM的六分之一；(ii) 训练后量化使总体翻译质量下降约在1 chrF以内，最差单例为2.19 chrF；(iii) INT4下显存节省49–61%，但INT8/INT4未降低延迟，反而增加1.3–5.3倍；(iv) 语言间存在与训练资源可得性一致的性能梯度（斯瓦希里语>豪萨语>约鲁巴语）。

Conclusion: 训练后量化在低资源多语MT中可近似无损地压缩显存，但在消费级GPU上并不带来延迟收益；专用MT模型在参数效率上显著优于通用LLM。作者据此提出面向低资源部署的硬件感知压缩与超越聚合指标的错误类型分析研究议程。

Abstract: We present a pilot study characterising the effect of post-training quantisation on multilingual machine translation quality and inference cost for three African languages - Hausa, Swahili, and Yoruba - across three model families: a small general-purpose LLM (Qwen2.5-1.5B), a larger multilingual instruction-tuned LLM (Aya-23-8B), and a dedicated multilingual MT model (NLLB-200-distilled-1.3B). Evaluating on the FLORES-200 devtest set (1,012 sentences per direction) under FP16, bitsandbytes INT8, and NF4 INT4 precisions on consumer-grade NVIDIA T4 GPUs, we find: (i) the specialised MT model outperforms both LLMs by 32.7-43.3 chrF on the Hausa and Swahili directions, and by 12.9-24.7 chrF on Yoruba, despite being roughly a sixth the size of the 8B instruction-tuned LLM; (ii) post-training quantisation preserves aggregate translation quality within approximately 1 chrF across all model families and language directions, with a single worst case of 2.19 chrF; (iii) on consumer GPUs, INT8 and INT4 quantisation provide substantial memory savings (49-61% at INT4) but do not reduce inference latency relative to FP16 - in fact, latency increases by 1.3x to 5.3x; (iv) a clear performance gradient tracking training-resource availability is visible across languages (Swahili > Hausa > Yoruba). These findings motivate a research agenda focused on hardware-aware compression and error-type analysis beyond aggregate metrics for low-resource deployment.

</details>


### [215] [Can LLMs Understand What They Predict? Evaluating Latent Causal Structure Inference in Financial Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23533)
*Yixiang Zheng,Yuechen Jiang,Zhiwei Liu,Jimin Huang,Yuyan Wang,Sophia Ananiadou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 LUCID 基准，检验 LLM 能否从事件描述与历史金融观测中推断潜在市场结构；结果显示生成的事件因果图反而普遍降低重构质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常以"预测未来结果"作为 LLM 理解系统的证据，但准确的预测并不必然意味着模型恢复了生成可观测结果的潜在机制，这一假设缺乏检验。

Method: 构建 LUCID 基准：给定事件描述与历史金融观测，要求模型生成事件诱导的因果图以表示推断出的市场结构，并采用基于重构的评估框架进行评测，覆盖 6 个开源权重与专有 LLM。

Result: 总体而言，将模型生成的因果图与历史观测结合，重构质量相较仅使用历史观测大多下降，仅少数指标有所改善。

Conclusion: 从可观测证据中推断具有信息量、事件特异性的市场结构，对当前评测的 LLM 仍是显著挑战；预测表现不足以支撑"理解机制"的推断。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly evaluated through their ability to predict future outcomes, implicitly assuming that predictive success reflects an understanding of the underlying system. However, accurate predictions can often be achieved without recovering the latent mechanisms that generate observable outcomes. To examine this assumption, we introduce LUCID, a benchmark that evaluates whether models can infer latent market structures from event descriptions and historical financial observations. Models generate event-induced causal graphs representing inferred market structure and are evaluated through a reconstruction-based framework. Our evaluation of 6 open-weight and proprietary LLMs shows that adding their generated graphs generally reduces reconstruction quality compared with using historical observations alone, although a few metrics improve. These findings suggest that inferring informative, event-specific market structures from observable evidence remains challenging for the evaluated LLMs.

</details>


### [216] [Localizing Text Anonymization for Trustworthy AI: Extending RAT-Bench to Malaysian Microdata and PII](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23704)
*David Hong Liang Chew,Zexi Yao,Nataša Krčo,Matthieu Meeus,Waqas Khalid Obeidy,Yves-Alexandre de Montjoye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 将RAT-Bench扩展至马来西亚语境（本地微观数据、马来西亚PII格式、马来英语与马来语的本地化语料），用LLM攻击者评估NER类与LLM类匿名化器的再识别风险与文本效用。结果显示LLM匿名化器在隐私-效用权衡上最优，非LLM工具存在明显失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政府与组织在公共服务和办公场景中采用基础模型，敏感文本会经由推理、检索、微调或本地模型开发进入LLM流程，因此需检验匿名化器能否迁移到新的部署环境（语言、标识符格式、文化语境）。

Method: 把RAT-Bench扩展到马来西亚：使用本地微观数据、马来西亚PII格式，以及马来英语和马来语的本地文化语境转写文本；评估基于NER和基于LLM的匿名化器；采用LLM攻击者从匿名化文本中推断属性，同时衡量再识别成功率与文本效用（BLEU）。

Result: 两种LLM匿名化器在马来英语和马来语上都给出最强的显式隐私-效用权衡，把Easy/Hard再识别风险降到23–29%，同时BLEU保持在0.77–0.94。非LLM工具失效更明显：或保留效用但残余风险高，或通过严重过度脱敏降低风险，马来语中尤甚。

Conclusion: 匿名化器在用于公共部门、办公或本地AI流程前，应针对与部署相关的语言、标识符格式和文化语境进行评估，不能默认其可跨环境迁移。

Abstract: As governments and organizations adopt foundation models for public services and workplace productivity, sensitive citizen, employee, and administrative text may enter LLM workflows through inference, retrieval, fine-tuning, or local model development. We examine whether text anonymizers transfer to new deployment settings by extending RAT-Bench to Malaysia using local microdata, Malaysian PII formats, and culturally grounded transcripts in Malaysian English and Bahasa Malaysia. We evaluate NER- and LLM-based anonymizers using an LLM attacker that infers attributes from anonymized text, measuring both re-identification success and text utility. Across Malaysian English and Bahasa Malaysia, the two LLM anonymizers provide the strongest explicit privacy-utility trade-offs, reducing Easy/Hard re-identification risk to 23-29% while preserving BLEU scores of 0.77-0.94. Non-LLM tools show sharper failure modes, either preserving utility while leaving high residual risk or reducing risk through severe over-redaction, especially in Bahasa Malaysia. These findings suggest that anonymizers should be evaluated against deployment-relevant languages, identifier formats, and cultural contexts before being relied on in public-sector, workplace, or local AI pipelines.

</details>


### [217] [When Does Continual Pre-Training Help? A Controlled Study of Dialectal Adaptation for Québec French](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24699)
*Eeham Khan,Richard Khoury,Leila Kosseim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对九个大模型开展魁北克法语持续预训练(CPT)控制实验，发现CPT收益高度依赖基座模型既有能力，且主要来自语料构成（文学文本）而非数据量；CPT主要提升表达理解而非语法能力，并伴随被聚合分数掩盖的阅读能力下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPT广泛用于将预训练模型适配方言与非标准语言变体，但其必要性条件、收益来源以及聚合评测能否充分反映影响均不清楚，因此需要受控研究加以厘清。

Method: 跨模型家族与规模选取9个LLM，采用每实验3000万token的预算匹配统一协议，进行魁北克法语理解的CPT控制实验；开展语料组成与数据量的对比分析，以及魁北克法语与标准法语的配对评测。

Result: CPT收益取决于基座模型对目标变体的既有能力；有益时提升主要由语料构成（文学文本贡献最大）而非数据规模驱动；CPT主要改善表达理解而非语法能力，语体（register）决定所习得的地区表达；同时出现能力权衡，如阅读理解下降而聚合分数不可见。

Conclusion: 方言适配的CPT不应被视为普遍有效的默认手段；适配策略应先评估基座模型的既有能力，并结合来源级数据分析与任务级评测。

Abstract: Continual pre-training (CPT) is widely used to adapt pretrained language models to dialectal and non-standard language varieties, yet it remains unclear when this additional training is necessary, what drives its benefits, and whether aggregate evaluation scores adequately capture its effects. In this paper, we present a controlled study of CPT for Québec French language understanding across nine LLMs spanning different families and scales. Using a budget-matched protocol of 30M tokens per experiment, our results show that the benefit of CPT depends strongly on the starting model's existing competence in the target variety. When CPT is beneficial, the improvements are driven more by corpus composition than by data volume, with literary text accounting for most of the gain. Paired Québec and standard French evaluations further show that CPT primarily improves expression comprehension rather than grammatical competence, and that corpus register determines which regional expressions are acquired. These gains also involve capability-specific trade-offs, including declines in reading comprehension that aggregate scores can conceal. Overall, these findings show that CPT for dialectal adaptation should not be treated as a uniformly beneficial default. Instead, adaptation strategies should begin with a careful assessment of the base model's existing competence, accompanied by source-level data analysis and task-level evaluation.

</details>


### [218] [Large Language Models or AI Agents for English to Arabic Translation? An assessment on WikiEn-Ar190](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24588)
*Fatima Zahra Iguenfer,Yousra Chtouki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究构建WikiEn-Ar190数据集，比较GPT-5-mini作为独立翻译器与"自我评估-改进"自主Agent流程在英译阿（低资源语言）任务上的表现，发现Agent流程在保持语义质量的同时显著提升词汇准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语属低资源语言，其在网络上的表征不足。作者希望厘清：独立大模型与具备自主推理能力的AI Agent，哪一方在英译阿任务上表现更优，并借此探索提升低资源语言翻译一致性的可行路径。

Method: 构建WikiEn-Ar190数据集：从18个领域精选190篇维基百科文章，由两名阿拉伯语母语者创建并校验，确保意义与风格的双重准确。以该数据集为基准，对比评估GPT-5-mini的两种使用方式——作为独立神经翻译器，以及嵌入包含自我评估与自我改进的复合Agent流程。

Result: 独立模式下，GPT-5-mini译文流畅度高，COMET达0.8649，人工评分在充分性、流畅性与风格维度均值为4.53/5。在"翻译＋自我评估"的Agent流程下，词汇准确性提升28.76%（BLEU 22.83），语义质量维持在相近水平。

Conclusion: 引入自主推理的方法可提升低资源语言对的翻译一致性。除结果本身外，该工作提供了一套翻译评估与改进的框架，有望将此类工具用于辅助扩展阿拉伯语等低资源语言在网络上的表征。

Abstract: This research investigates whether a large language model or an autonomous AI Agent performs better at translating from English to Arabic, which is considered a low-resource language. The authors introduce WikiEn-Ar190, a new dataset of 190 carefully selected Wikipedia articles from 18 different areas, which were created and verified by two native Arabic speakers to ensure that they accurately reflect both meaning and style. Using this dataset, the study evaluates the translation quality of GPT-5-mini both as an independent neural translator and when used within a more complex process that includes self-assessment and improvement. As a standalone model, GPT-5-mini produced very fluent translations , achieving a COMET score of 0.8649 and receiving high human scores for adequacy, fluency, and style (averaging 4.53 out of 5). When used in a system that combined translation with self-assessment, it improved lexical accuracy by 28.76% (BLEU score of 22.83) while maintaining a similar level of semantic quality. These findings show that methods involving autonomous reasoning can improve translation consistency for language pairs with limited resources. Beyond the results, the approach offers a framework for evaluating and improving translations, potentially using them as assisting tools to expand the representation of low resource languages, such as Arabic, on the web.

</details>


### [219] [Coverage Governs Accuracy: When to Expand the Lexicon and When to Fall Back in Low-Resource Manchu OCR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23900)
*Ruixin Yu,Zifan Wang,Mingxin Ding,Xiaoxin Sun,Dongjie Wang,Suhua Wang,Minghao Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究形态学扩展词表对低资源满文OCR词表约束解码的影响，发现适度扩展（+25万）提升词型与词元准确率，但全量扩展因词表内精度下降反而降低词元准确率；基础词表加置信度回退效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字符级OCR在低资源满文上易产生近似拼写错误，词表约束解码可借助词汇知识纠错，但固定词表无法覆盖黏着构词产生的众多未登录词形，故考察形态学扩展是否能缓解该限制，以及扩展规模与置信度回退的影响。

Method: 用基于词典的词干表切分训练转写文本，抽取词根与后缀链生成约51万候选；在验证集上按频率排序选择扩展规模；将硬词表约束下的准确率分解为覆盖率与词表内精度的乘积；在CRNN三个训练随机种子上与基础词表（约2.9万词）对比，并测试置信度回退与Transformer识别器。

Result: 基础词表加入25万候选后，已见词根未见组合的词型准确率从63.6%升至86.3%，整体词元准确率从82.4%升至84.2%；加入全部候选则词元准确率降至81.5%。基础词表加置信度回退在CRNN系统中整体最佳，词型76.1%、词元85.5%，优于扩增词表（含与不含回退）；Transformer呈现相似趋势。

Conclusion: 形态学扩展可缓解固定词表的限制，但更大词表并不必然带来更好识别；在低资源满文OCR场景下，可靠词汇约束与开放识别的选择性结合更为有效。

Abstract: Character-level OCR for low-resource Manchu often produces near-miss spelling errors. Lexicon-constrained decoding can correct such errors with lexical knowledge, but a fixed dictionary cannot cover the many unseen word forms produced by agglutinative word formation. We study whether morphological expansion alleviates this limitation, and how the expansion size and confidence fallback affect recognition. We segment the training transcriptions with a dictionary-based stem list, extract roots and suffix chains from them, generate about 510k candidates, select the expansion size through frequency ranking on the validation set, and decompose accuracy under hard lexicon constraints into the product of coverage and in-lexicon precision. Across three CRNN training seeds, adding 250k candidates to a base dictionary of about 29k words raises the type accuracy on unseen combinations of seen roots from 63.6% to 86.3%, and overall token accuracy from 82.4% to 84.2%. Adding all candidates lowers overall token accuracy to 81.5%, showing that the coverage gain comes with a loss of in-lexicon precision. A further comparison shows that the base dictionary with confidence fallback performs best overall among the CRNN systems tested, reaching 76.1% type and 85.5% token accuracy and outperforming the expanded lexicon both with and without fallback. A Transformer recognizer shows similar trends. These results indicate that morphological expansion relieves the constraint of a fixed dictionary, but a larger lexicon does not guarantee better recognition; in the present low-resource Manchu OCR setting, a selective combination of reliable lexical constraints and open recognition is more effective.

</details>


### [220] [MIRAGE: A Framework for Multi-hop Reasoning under Dynamic Knowledge Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24058)
*Heyang Xu,HanWen Hao,Haifeng Wang,Haoyi Zhou,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出面向"知识注入"场景的多跳问答基准NEXUS与免训练框架MIRAGE，通过置信度路由解决逐跳知识来源信任错误，显著提升最终答案与逐跳推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多跳问答（MQA）知识更新研究多聚焦简单事实编辑，忽视了更贴近现实的"知识注入"设定：模型需同时吸收冲突性更新（K2，与参数记忆矛盾）与全新信息（K3，参数中不存在）。该设定下逐跳答案出错往往源于模型信任了错误的知识来源。

Method: 构建NEXUS基准，首个显式覆盖冲突性（K2）与增量性（K3）知识的多跳推理知识注入评测集；提出免训练框架MIRAGE，核心为相关性感知双路径推理器（CDR），基于P(True)置信度路由在参数记忆与注入知识两条路径间分配信任，并辅以轻量模板引导的纠正器修复残余结构偏差。

Result: 在四个LLM系列、两个基准上实验，MIRAGE在最终答案准确率与逐跳推理准确率上均稳定提升，在该设定下取得新的SOTA。

Conclusion: 将MQA知识更新研究推进到更现实的"知识注入"设定，并给出无需训练的逐跳来源信任纠错方案，验证了置信度路由在缓解K2/K3错误上的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-hop Question Answering (MQA) requires large language models (LLMs) to retrieve and synthesize multiple pieces of evidence into a coherent reasoning path, which is essential for complex tasks. However, prior work on knowledge updating for MQA has largely focused on simple fact edits, overlooking a more challenging and realistic setting---\emph{Knowledge Injection}---in which the model must simultaneously accommodate both conflicting updates and entirely novel information. Under this setting, a hop-level answer is often wrong simply because the model trusts the wrong knowledge source: it clings to outdated parametric memory when the injected knowledge conflicts with it (K2), and it cannot answer when the required fact is entirely new (K3).
  To investigate this, we introduce NEXUS, the first benchmark tailored for multi-hop reasoning under Knowledge Injection, which explicitly covers both conflicting (K2) and incremental (K3) knowledge. We further propose MIRAGE, an efficient, training-free framework whose core component, the Correlation-aware Dual-path Reasoner (CDR), resolves such per-hop source-trust errors via P(True)-based confidence routing, complemented by a lightweight template-guided corrector for residual structural deviations. Extensive experiments across four LLM families and two benchmarks demonstrate that MIRAGE consistently improves both final-answer accuracy and hop-level reasoning accuracy, establishing a new state of the art in this challenging setting.

</details>


### [221] [Grading Executable Legal Rules Against Independent Records: Transitional Provisions and Korean SME Status](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24055)
*Jinhyung Bae*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出LexTemporis，用真实披露记录（10,901个公司-年的韩国SME认定）而非评分者自读法条来评测LLM编写的法律程序，发现按评分者解读打分会高估程序效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律正被转化为判定案件的程序，而LLM开始编写这些程序；但现有评测通常以"读过同一法条的人给出的答案"为标准，程序可能因与评分者共享同一误读而获得高分，而非真正符合法律。

Method: 以韩国上市公司披露的10,901个公司-年SME状态及全部规则版本为真值（附录加入美国所得税与德国医院最低手术量案例）；比较不同法条供给条件下四个LLM编写的规则；进行预注册对比与探索性分析，并检验过渡条款、争议条款解读的影响。

Result: 日期主要通过主法条之外的条款改变结果，一条过渡条款使与披露的一致率从83–86%升至89–90%；披露在189个解读分歧公司-年中130个支持作者解读；预注册比较未发现对四个模型都更优的条件；探索性分析显示提供正确条款在"对作者解读打分"下优势更大，对三个模型该优势缩小（其中一个降至原幅度的1/4至3/5），且在作者解读有误的公司-年上该优势大数倍。

Conclusion: 以评分者自身解读为基准评测法律程序会系统性高估其实际效果，应改用真实事件记录作为评测标准。

Abstract: Laws are increasingly turned into programs that decide cases, such as whether a firm was a small or medium enterprise (SME) on a given date, and large language models (LLMs) now write them. The programs are usually graded against answers written by someone who read the same law, so a program can score well by sharing the grader's reading rather than by getting the law right. LexTemporis grades them instead against records of what actually happened: 10,901 firm-years of SME status disclosed by Korean listed companies, with every version of the governing rules. An appendix adds comparison cases from US income tax and German hospital minimum volumes. The date changes outcomes mainly through provisions outside the main statutory text: one transitional provision raises agreement with the disclosures from 83-86% to 89-90%. The disclosures also test the author's reading of a contested provision, favouring it in 130 of 189 firm-years where readings diverge. Four LLMs wrote the rules, given different selections of the law. A pre-registered comparison finds no condition better across all four models. In exploratory analyses, supplying the right provisions looks better graded against the author's reading than against the records: the advantage shrinks for three models (for one, to between a quarter and three-fifths of its size), and per firm-year it is several times larger where the author's reading is wrong, partly by construction, since there reproducing that reading more faithfully must lose. Grading legal programs against the grader's own reading can overstate how well they work.

</details>


### [222] [LoopCache: Accelerating Long-Context Self-Speculative Decoding for Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24301)
*Zizhuo Fu,Haochen Huang,Shengxuan Qiu,Linye Wei,Ziyu Tang,Runsheng Wang,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 LoopCache：面向长上下文 Loop 变换器的自推测解码框架，用置信度引导的动态草稿更新和循环 KV 缓存，实现相对 WFD 10.25x、相对 LoopSpec 14.56x 的平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权共享块在 R 个循环步上反复施加形成大有效深度，每步需维护循环专属 KV 缓存，顺序执行慢；WFD、LoopSpec 等 wave 式自推测虽能单步流水化草稿与验证，但存在两点缺陷：(1) 只在固定循环处起草，忽略中间预测变化，浪费算力于过期轨迹；(2) 批量混合不同循环的 token 导致每步加载最多 R 倍 KV 缓存，长上下文下内存流量急剧放大。

Method: 1) 基于“预测切换与输出置信度相关”这一观察，引入 confidence-guided dynamic draft updating，在所有中间循环处动态修正飞行中的草稿；2) cyclic loop KV cache 策略，按循环轮转使用循环专属 KV 缓存，将每步 KV 流量限制在单循环内，并把 token 批处理到最匹配的周期以无损验证；3) 对少数对循环敏感的浅层选择性保留独立 KV 缓存，以可忽略开销提升接受率。

Result: 实验显示，相对 WFD 平均加速 10.25x，相对 LoopSpec 平均加速 14.56x。

Conclusion: LoopCache 通过置信度驱动的动态草稿更新与循环 KV 缓存调度，在保持无损验证的前提下同时缓解过期草稿计算浪费与 KV 内存流量瓶颈，为长上下文 Loop 变换器提供高效自推测解码方案。

Abstract: Looped Transformers achieve large effective depth with compact models by repeatedly applying a weight-shared block across R recurrent steps, each maintaining a loop-specific key-value (KV) cache. To accelerate sequential loop execution, recent wave-style self-speculative methods like WaveFront Decoding (WFD) and LoopSpec pipeline draft and target tokens across loops in a single step. However, they face two critical limitations. First, they draft only at fixed loops and ignore intermediate prediction changes, wasting computation on stale trajectories. Second, batching tokens from different loops loads up to Rx the KV cache per step, severely amplifying memory traffic in long-context scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose LoopCache, an efficient self-speculative decoding framework for long-context Looped Transformers. Based on the correlation between prediction switches and output confidence, LoopCache introduces confidence-guided dynamic draft updating to refine drafts in flight across all intermediate loops. Furthermore, LoopCache employs a cyclic loop KV cache strategy that rotates through loop-specific KV caches, restricting per-step KV traffic to a single loop while batching tokens at the nearest matching cycle for lossless verification. By also selectively retaining independent KV caches for a few loop-sensitive shallow layers, LoopCache improves acceptance rates with negligible overhead. Experiments show that LoopCache achieves average 10.25x and 14.56x speedup over WFD and LoopSpec.

</details>


### [223] [Language Models Make New Research Sound Old](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23546)
*Guy Zyskind*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM辅助判断研究问题时存在"报告缺口"：模型能正确判断开放问题未被研究，却极少在常规回答中明说；通过固定答案流程与引用文献独立核查可将清晰报告率从8%提升至95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者越来越多地用LLM来验证研究问题的新颖性，但LLM在常规回答中会弱化"未研究"这一事实，把开放问题说成"已有部分研究"，并夸大相关文献的结论，可能误导研究者。

Method: 在40个来自新录用论文的研究问题上测试GPT-6-Astra及六个前沿系统；对22个模型提供相同论文，比较能力与判断准确性、报告缺口的关系；检查模型在单独核查全文时能否识别夸大表述；构建"固定答案"方法：将模型内部判断与对引用文献的独立核查相结合生成回答；最后用多模型书面评分加从标注样例学到的规则预测审稿人新颖性打分。

Result: Astra对98%的开放问题判断正确，但仅8%的常规回答明确说明其未被研究；六个前沿系统报告缺口达30-90个百分点；能力越强判断越准但报告缺口越大；相反，在提供解答文献时，模型88-100%能正确识别已研究问题（非对称性）；固定答案方法将Astra清晰报告率从8%提升至95%，六个系统均有改善；新颖性评分预测达到Think-Probe-Respond水平，且无需模型权重或内部激活。

Conclusion: LLM的判断能力与报告行为存在系统性脱节，且随能力增强而加剧；该缺口可通过外部核查与固定答案流程有效纠正，并可在闭源前沿模型上实现与内部探针方法相当的新颖性评估性能。

Abstract: Researchers are increasingly using large language models (LLMs) to validate research questions. We find that LLMs make open research questions sound partly studied and overstate what related papers establish. Models recognize most of these overstatements when asked to check the papers' full texts separately. On 40 questions drawn from newly accepted papers, GPT-6-Astra correctly judges 98% to be unstudied, but states that status plainly in only 8% of ordinary answers. All six frontier systems show reporting gaps of 30-90 percentage points. Across 22 models given identical papers, higher capability is associated with more accurate judgments and larger reporting gaps. This reporting failure is asymmetric: models correctly identify studied questions in 88-100% of ordinary answers when we supply the answering papers. To correct this, we build a fixed answer from each model's judgment and separate checks of cited papers. This raises Astra's clear reporting of open status from 8% to 95%, with improvements in all six systems. Finally, we predict reviewers' novelty grades by combining several models' written ratings with a rule learned from graded examples, reaching the performance range reported by Think-Probe-Respond. Unlike TPR, our approach needs no access to model weights or internal activations, so it also works with closed-weight frontier models.

</details>


### [224] [Chorus: Shared N-gram Embeddings with Layer-wise Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23386)
*Stephen Chung,Wenyu Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Chorus：全网络共享一组 n-gram 嵌入表，各层通过基于隐藏状态计算的门控缩放与独立投影层以各自方式读取同一张表，从而在保持性能的同时将 n-gram 表参数减少 87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: n-gram 嵌入表能以较低计算开销为语言模型扩容。但现有设计通常为每层配置独立的 n-gram 表，内存开销大且层间无法共享知识；若改为单表共享，则所有层获得相同嵌入、缺乏层间偏好差异，实际效果很差。因此需要一种既共享又保留层特异性的方案。

Method: Chorus：1）全网络共用一套 n-gram 嵌入表；2）每层根据当前隐藏状态计算缩放因子，对精炼后的嵌入各分量进行重加权（门控）；3）再经该层专属的投影层映射。此外结合内存高效优化器 Ember 训练这些表。

Result: 以 87% 更小的表达到现有 n-gram 方法的质量：1.2B 表参数的语言建模效果优于使用 9.6B 参数的 FactorEngram 与 DeepSeek Engram。在 20M 至 9.6B 参数的全部比较设置中均超过 Engram、TN-gram 和 FactorEngram。与 AdamW 相比，Ember 在训练性能不下降的前提下节省 99.2% 的表优化器内存。

Conclusion: 共享表+层特异读取的 Chorus 兼顾知识复用与层间差异，显著提升参数效率；配合 Ember 大幅降低优化器内存，使沿表规模轴进一步扩展 n-gram 嵌入表成为可行。

Abstract: Language models can gain capacity at little computational cost from n-gram embedding tables: large tables of learned vectors for short sequences of consecutive tokens, which are looked up and added to the model's input or to its intermediate layers. Recent designs commonly use a separate n-gram embedding table for each layer, which makes the tables costly in memory and prevents layers from sharing what they learn. Sharing a single table, however, gives every layer the same embedding and leaves no room for layer-wise preferences, and it performs poorly in practice. We propose Chorus, in which one set of n-gram embedding tables serves the whole network and every layer reads it in its own way. Each layer maps the refined embedding through its own projection layer, after rescaling its components by factors computed from its current hidden state. Chorus matches the quality of existing n-gram methods with 87% smaller tables: with 1.2B table parameters it predicts text better than FactorEngram and DeepSeek's Engram do with 9.6B. It outperforms Engram, TN-gram and FactorEngram in every setting where we compared them, across table sizes from 20M to 9.6B parameters. We also show that the recently proposed memory-efficient optimizer Ember works well with Chorus, saving 99.2% of table-optimizer memory compared with AdamW without degrading training performance. These results enable further scaling of n-gram embedding tables along the table-size axis by reducing memory use and allowing shared knowledge to be reused across layers.

</details>


### [225] [A Hybrid RAG Framework for Deceptive Review Detection: Integrating RoBERTa-LoRA, FAISS-Based Retrieval, and LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24049)
*Andrei Kostin,José Ángel Diaz-Garcia,Karel Gutiérrez-Batista*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种RAG系统自动识别垃圾/虚假评论，融合RoBERTa+LoRA分类、FAISS语义检索与LLM推理，在平衡与严重不平衡的真实场景中评测，跨域F1达0.90，优于OpenAI Detector(0.82)，接近域内SOTA fakeRoBERTa(0.97)，并提供有证据支撑的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线欺骗性观点（如垃圾评论）的蔓延带来误导信息与欺诈风险，需要自动、可靠且可解释的虚假评论检测方法；现有方法在跨域迁移、类别严重不平衡场景及可解释性方面存在不足。

Method: 构建RAG式架构：RoBERTa结合LoRA进行低秩适配分类，FAISS做语义向量检索以获取证据，最终由大语言模型完成推理判定；在平衡与严重不平衡的真实数据场景下评测，并采用5个epoch的微调。

Result: 5个epoch微调即可实现稳健的域迁移；跨域评测F1=0.90，超过OpenAI Detector基线（F1=0.82）；与域内专用SOTA模型fakeRoBERTa（F1=0.97）相比具竞争力；精确率提升具统计显著性，且整体检测性能相当。

Conclusion: 该架构在保持整体检测性能的同时，提供了有证据支撑的可解释性与显著的精确率增益，是一种可迁移、可解释的虚假评论检测方案。

Abstract: The growth of deceptive online opinions poses a critical risk of misinformation and fraud. This paper proposes a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system to automatically distinguish spam reviews. The architecture integrates RoBERTa with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) classification, FAISS semantic vector retrieval, and a Large Language Model (LLM) for final reasoning. The system was evaluated across balanced and severely imbalanced real-world scenarios. Results demonstrate that five epochs of fine-tuning enable robust domain transfer, achieving an F1-score of 0.90 in cross-domain evaluation, outperforming baselines (OpenAI Detector,F1 = 0.82) and demonstrating competitive performance against specialized in-domain SOTA models (fakeRoBERTa,F1 = 0.97). The proposed architecture provides evidence-backed explainability and statistically significant precision gains while maintaining comparable overall detection performance.

</details>


### [226] [Clover-35B-A3B-Preview Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24636)
*DiffuSpace Technical Team*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该摘要介绍Clover-35B-A3B-Preview，一个350亿总参数、30亿激活参数的混合专家扩散语言模型（MoE diffusion LLM），支持思考模式，在七项智能体基准评测中领先，并具备高吞吐与低显存部署能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型在长上下文建模、块内双向建模与历史记忆整合上存在权衡，且在智能体任务上的质量—成本—部署三角难以兼顾，因此作者提出在块扩散框架下融合门控Delta网络循环记忆与块内双向softmax注意力的方案。

Method: 将Gated Delta Networks适配到块扩散（block diffusion）：用循环记忆编码已生成的上下文历史，块内采用双向softmax注意力；历史记忆读出与当前块token在同一softmax中竞争，并引入可学习的块大小校准（learnable block-size calibration）以及独立的、零初始化的右上下文卷积分支（zero-initialized right-context convolution）。此外还设计了decision-model变体，在一次去噪过程中联合生成一组相关问题的答案。

Result: 在七项智能体指标上全部领先，SWE-bench Verified达73.6，Terminal-Bench 2.1达51.7；内部智能体负载在单卡NVIDIA GB300、BF16、batch size为1时报告生成速度680 tokens/s；decision-model变体在内部500组数据上，联合预测将问题准确率从90.37%提升至93.88%，全对组准确率从65.4%提升至77.0%。

Conclusion: Clover表明混合专家+块扩散+循环记忆的架构可在保持高质量智能体能力的同时实现高吞吐与受限显存下的部署，且联合去噪的多问题预测能显著提升一致性正确率；评估围绕模型质量、起草/验证成本与受限显存部署三方面组织。

Abstract: We introduce Clover-35B-A3B-Preview (Clover), a mixture-of-experts diffusion language model with 35 billion total parameters and 3 billion activated parameters, supporting thinking-mode generation. Clover adapts Gated Delta Networks to block diffusion by combining recurrent memory of previously generated context with bidirectional softmax attention inside the current block. Historical memory readout competes with current-block tokens in a shared softmax, with learnable block-size calibration and a separate, zero-initialized right-context convolution branch. Clover leads the seven evaluated agent metrics, including 73.6 on SWE-bench Verified and 51.7 on Terminal-Bench 2.1. On internal agent workloads, it reaches a reported generation speed of 680 tokens/s using one NVIDIA GB300 GPU at BF16 precision and batch size one. We organize the evaluation around model quality, drafting and verification costs, and deployment under constrained memory. We also describe a decision-model variant that generates answers to related questions in one denoising pass. On an internal set of 500 groups, joint prediction raises question accuracy from 90.37% to 93.88% and all-correct group accuracy from 65.4% to 77.0%.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [BOS Summary: The First Token Matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23508)
*Hui Zhu,Saleh Zare Zade,Rafi Ibn Sultan,Deng Pan,Arun Ross,Dongxiao Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练推理期方法 BOS Summary，让 BOS 位置在 prefill 的单个层中聚合全部输入信息，从而把原本"空载"的注意力汇变成可复用的上下文摘要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM/LLM 存在注意力汇（attention sink）现象，BOS token 获得不成比例的关注；但在标准因果掩码下 BOS 查询只能看到自身，因此后续 token 反复读取的这条"持久通路"并不携带任何输入信息，形成信息浪费。

Method: 免训练、推理期干预：在 prefill 阶段仅对 BOS 查询行放开因果约束（单个 transformer 层），使其可注意全部视觉与文本输入 token，生成一个自导出的上下文表示；其余 token 保持标准因果注意力。该表示在解码过程中持续可被访问，无需额外参数、辅助模块或解码期开销。

Result: 在多个 VLM 与 LLM 系列、8 个多模态与纯文本基准上，优于直接提示与 CoT 提示，并在多步推理任务上超过近期 sink 干预方法。机制分析显示：使 BOS 输入相关会削弱其 sink 主导性，将注意力重新分配给承载内容的 prompt token。

Conclusion: 改变"汇"所承载的内容即可重塑后续层的注意力分配；BOS 通路可作为零成本的上下文聚合通道加以利用。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) and language models (LLMs) often exhibit attention sinks, whereby particular positions, such as the beginning-of-sequence (BOS) token, receive a disproportionate share of attention. Under standard causal masking, however, the BOS query attends only to itself, so the representation that later tokens repeatedly access carries no information about the input. We introduce BOS Summary, a training-free inference-time method that repurposes this persistent pathway to carry input context. At a single transformer layer during prefill, BOS Summary relaxes the causal constraint for the BOS query row alone, letting BOS aggregate all visual and textual input tokens into a self-derived context representation while every other token retains standard causal attention. The enriched representation then remains accessible to the model throughout decoding, with no additional parameters, auxiliary modules, or decoding-time overhead. Across multiple VLM and LLM families and eight multimodal and text-only benchmarks, BOS Summary improves over direct and Chain-of-Thought prompting and outperforms recent sink-intervention methods on multi-step reasoning tasks. A mechanistic analysis shows that making BOS input-specific also weakens its sink dominance and redistributes attention toward content-bearing prompt tokens, indicating that changing what a sink carries reshapes how later layers allocate attention.

</details>


### [228] [Vibe-Trading: Towards Agent-Native Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24845)
*Haozhe Wu,Zhonghang Li,Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Vibe-Trading：一个 agent-native 的金融交易智能体框架与一体化工作台，通过将结果、证据、研究历史与交易权限在全流程中保持关联，实现可溯源、可复现、且需显式授权方可执行的交易智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI Agent 多基于相对静态的工作流，难以在环境变化时保持可靠状态。金融交易中该缺陷尤为严重：价格、新闻、市场状况、持仓与可用资金持续演变，结果只有在绑定正确的时间、数据快照与执行上下文时才有价值。

Method: 构建统一的交易框架，整合市场研究、金融数据、策略生成、回测、组合分析、模拟盘、多智能体协作与可选券商执行；核心为保持"结果—证据—研究历史—交易权限"贯通的架构：每个结果保留来源、时间范围、配置与生成该结果的运行记录；共享证据锚定支持验证；版本化研究记录支持可复现的延续；显式权限闸门隔离策略研究与实盘执行。

Result: 将研究到行动的全过程打通，使结果具备可追溯性（来源、时间范围、配置、运行）、可验证性（共享证据锚定）、可复现性（版本化研究记录）与受控执行性（权限闸门）。

Conclusion: 该框架为交易智能体提供了实践基础：其结果可被溯源、复现，且仅在显式授权下才被用于执行。

Abstract: Existing AI agents can retrieve information, call tools, run experiments, and produce multi-step outputs. Yet most are built around relatively static workflows and struggle to preserve reliable state as the environment changes. Financial trading makes this limitation especially consequential: prices, news, market conditions, portfolio positions, and available capital evolve continuously. A result is useful only when it remains tied to the correct time, data snapshot, and execution context. We present Vibe-Trading, a comprehensive agent-native trading framework and all-in-one workspace for building, evaluating, and evolving agents for financial trading. It unifies market research, financial data, strategy generation, backtesting, portfolio analysis, paper trading, multi-agent collaboration, and optional broker execution. Its central contribution is an architecture that keeps results, evidence, research history, and trading authority connected throughout the research-to-action process. Each result retains its source, temporal scope, configuration, and producing run. Shared evidence grounding enables verification, versioned research records support reproducible continuation, and an explicit authority gate separates strategy research from live execution. Together, these designs provide a practical foundation for trading agents whose results can be traced, reproduced, and acted on only under explicit authority.

</details>


### [229] [GRAFT: Execution-Guided Genetic Recombination for Agent Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23509)
*Sevda Ebrahimi,Mohammed Saidul Islam,Xiaodan Zhu,Elham Dolatabadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GRAFT 用父代专属的执行证据引导演化式智能体架构设计中的语义交叉，在六个推理与编程任务上平均测试准确率达 82.0%，比最强基线高 9.8 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有演化式智能体设计仅依赖聚合适应度指导搜索，掩盖了每个分数背后的实例级成功与失败信息，导致交叉操作缺乏可解释的改进信号。

Method: 提出 Execution Guided Genetic Recombination for Agent Design（GRAFT）：选择与存活仍用聚合适应度，但以父代专属的执行证据引导带集成变异的语义交叉；其反馈装置复用适应度评估中有限数量的成功/失败运行样本，隐去参考答案且不额外执行任务。

Result: 在六个推理与编程设置下（开放权重优化器与执行器模型），测试准确率均值 82.0%，超最强基线 9.8 个百分点；控制消融表明，在初始化、搜索规则、修复策略与评估预算固定时，加入执行证据可提升测试准确率。

Conclusion: 父代专属执行证据对演化式智能体设计具有明确价值，可作为优于纯聚合适应度引导的搜索信号。

Abstract: Automated agent design searches for executable architectures that improve LLM-based agent performance, with evolutionary methods using language-model crossover to recombine promising mechanisms for task decomposition, model coordination, and verification. This search, however, is typically guided by aggregate fitness alone, which hides the instance-level successes and failures behind each score. We introduce Execution Guided Genetic Recombination for Agent Design (GRAFT), which uses aggregate fitness for selection and survival and parent-specific execution evidence to guide semantic crossover with integrated mutation. Its feedback harness reuses bounded samples of successful and failed runs from fitness evaluation, withholding reference answers and requiring no additional task executions. We evaluate GRAFT across six reasoning and programming settings using open-weight optimizer and executor models. It achieves a mean test accuracy of 82.0%, exceeding the strongest baseline by 9.8% points. Controlled ablations show that adding execution evidence improves test accuracy with initialization, search rules, repair policies, and evaluation budgets held fixed. Our results support the value of parent-specific execution evidence for evolutionary agent design.

</details>


### [230] [Self-Rewarded Reinforcement Learning for Better-Structured Medical Explanations in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24650)
*Prakhar Mishra,Bhavya Yajush Awasthi,Tanuja,Nishant*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在Meerkat指令微调的Mistral-7B基础上，用LoRA+PPO并配以自监督奖励（偏好更长、词汇多样、标点规范的回答）对2000条医学提示做约170步强化学习，使小模型生成的医学解释更长、更有条理，同时保留医学内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型（SLM）可在普通硬件上部署、支持隐私保护的就地临床使用，但其医学解释相对大模型偏短、结构差；现有Meerkat等监督式方法虽把思维链推理迁移到7B-8B骨干，训练目标却未直接针对解释的风格与组织性，且缺少人工偏好标签。

Method: 以指令微调的Mistral-7B为骨干，在Meerkat衍生的2000条医学提示上应用LoRA适配的PPO，奖励函数为简单自监督信号：鼓励更长、词汇多样、标点良好的回答，惩罚过短回答；单张A100上训练约170个PPO步。

Result: 平均奖励稳定上升；定量与定性比较显示RL微调后模型生成更长、更有组织、多要点的解释，同时保持原有医学内容。

Conclusion: 自奖励RL可在无需人工偏好标签的情况下有效塑造医学解释的风格与结构；作者同时讨论了该方法的潜力与局限（如自监督奖励与内容正确性之间的偏差风险）。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) can run on commodity hardware and support privacy-preserving, on-premises clinical deployment, but their medical explanations are often short and poorly structured compared with large language models. Recent supervised approaches such as Meerkat transfer chain-of-thought reasoning into 7B-8B backbones, yet their training objective does not directly target the style or organization of the generated rationales. Building on the instruction-tuned Mistral-7B backbone, we investigate whether a lightweight reinforcement learning (RL) step can improve explanation structure without human preference labels.We apply Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) with LoRA adaptation to 2,000 Meerkat-derived medical prompts, guided by a simple self-supervised reward that favors longer, lexically diverse, and well-punctuated responses while penalizing very short ones. Training for about 170 PPO steps on a single NVIDIA A100 GPU yields a stable increase in the average reward. Quantitative and qualitative comparisons show that the RL-tuned model produces longer, more organized, multi-point explanations while preserving the underlying medical content.We discuss the promise and the limitations of self-rewarded RL for shaping the style of medical explanations.

</details>


### [231] [SpecLook: Speculating Where to Look for Fast and Reliable Agentic MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23483)
*Xi Ding,Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的"推测-验证"框架SpecLook，通过推测智能体下一步观测（而非答案）来打破多模态智能体的串行缩放-推理循环，在提速的同时提升精度并给出无标签可靠性评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以图像思考的智能体式MLLM通过交错的推理与zoom-in工具调用工作，但该串行循环速度慢，且答案缺乏可靠性分数。已有的token、推理步或答案推测仍保留循环，且答案推测会接受"自信的错误"。作者发现循环大多只增加一步zoom-in，而在多数划分上小模型已领先智能体，因此希望借此规避循环。

Method: 关键洞察是推测智能体的"下一步观测"，因为观测可被验证、纠正并用于检验答案，而草拟答案不能。SpecLook为三级"推测-验证"框架：①小模型草拟一个局部概览(glimpse)并据此重新作答，若答案不变且置信则结束；②否则由智能体的大模型在不使用工具的情况下验证并纠正该glimpse，融合两者答案；③仅仍缺证据的问题才进入智能体，且仅用一次前向（SpecLook-Fast直接跳过该步），从而无需任何问题运行循环。轨迹可为每个答案打无标签可靠性分。

Result: 在V*、HR-Bench、POPE上，两个变体在每个划分上均优于所有基线。SpecLook在DeepEyes-7B与Thyme-RL智能体上分别取得87.2%和86.2%的平均准确率，加速7.7x和4.7x；SpecLook-Fast达9.9x和6.3x加速。

Conclusion: 推测可验证的观测而非答案，是替代串行工具调用循环的有效途径，可同时实现更高精度、显著加速与无标签可靠性评估，为高效的智能体式MLLM推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Agentic multimodal large language models (MLLMs) that think with images interleave reasoning with zoom-in tool calls. However, this sequential loop is slow, and its answers carry no reliability score. Prior speculation of tokens, reasoning steps, or answers keeps the loop, and answer speculation also accepts confident errors. We find that the loop mostly adds one zoom-in step, with which a small model is ahead of the agent on most splits. Our key insight is to speculate the agent's next observation, which, unlike a drafted answer, can be verified, corrected, and used to test the answer. We propose SpecLook, a training-free speculate-and-verify framework with three levels. A small model drafts a glimpse and re-answers from it, and the question ends if the answer is unchanged and confident. Otherwise, the agent's large model verifies and corrects the glimpse without tools, and their answers are fused. Only questions that still lack evidence reach the agent, in one forward pass that SpecLook-Fast skips, so no question runs the loop. The trace scores every answer's reliability without labels. Extensive experiments on V*, HR-Bench, and POPE show that both are more accurate than every baseline on every split. SpecLook reaches 87.2% and 86.2% mean accuracy with 7.7x and 4.7x speedups over the DeepEyes-7B and Thyme-RL agents, and SpecLook-Fast reaches 9.9x and 6.3x.

</details>


### [232] [A System 1 Decision Model for Medical Multiple-Choice Questions Built on Frozen Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23832)
*Pavan Kumar Mantha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文从零复现了一个基于冻结语言模型的"System 1"式单选题判别方案，并在MedMCQA上评估其性能边界。仅训练两个投影头与温度参数，在三个冻结模型上准确率从33%左右提升至46.0%（Qwen3-8B），显著高于随机但远低于医学专用大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次前向的"System 1"式多选题判别设计已在智能体决策中展示过，但尚不清楚其在需要医学知识的考试题上能达到何种水平——这类题目中正确选项在语义上往往并不比错误选项更接近题干，因此需要实证检验该范式的适用边界。

Method: 在冻结的Qwen3.5-0.8B、Granite-4.2-3B与Qwen3-8B之上，从零重建流程，仅训练两个小型投影头和一个温度参数，将题干与各选项映射到共享空间并取最近者。采用对比损失，结合批内负样本与难负样本，并对重复答案加掩码；此外将每段文本嵌入"一词总结"提示中，并降低投影头学习率。

Result: 仅用普通末位词向量时，两个小编码器在3,842道留出题上准确率分别为33.1%与33.0%（随机为25.0%）；加入一词总结提示与降低学习率后升至41.7%与42.5%，Qwen3-8B达46.0%±0.2（三个随机种子）。将题干与选项随机错配后准确率回落至随机水平，说明投影头确实利用了题干信息。

Conclusion: 该方案是一个廉价、快速的基线，而非具备竞争力的系统（Flan-PaLM与Med-PaLM 2在不同协议下报告57.6%与72.3%）。作者亦指出证据局限：验证集增益约为测试集增益的1.7–2倍，且无法将"读取多层编码器表示"的作用与其他改动区分开。

Abstract: A System 1 model answers a multiple-choice question in a single pass. A frozen language model turns the question and each option into vectors, two small, trained heads place them in a shared space, and the closest option wins. The design has been shown on agent decisions, but we are not aware of a study of how far it goes on exam questions that need medical knowledge, where the right option is often no more similar to the question than the wrong ones. We rebuilt the recipe from scratch for MedMCQA and trained only two projection heads and one temperature on top of frozen Qwen3.5-0.8B, Granite-4.2-3B and Qwen3-8B, using a contrastive loss with in-batch and hard negatives and a mask for repeated answers. With plain last-token vectors, both small encoders answer about one question in three correctly (33.1% and 33.0% on 3,842 held-out questions, where chance is 25.0%). Writing each text inside a one-word-summary prompt and lowering the learning rate of the heads raised this to 41.7% and 42.5% for the two small encoders and to 46.0% +-0.2 (three seeds) for Qwen3-8B. Shuffling the questions against the options returns accuracy to chance, so the heads do use the question. Flan-PaLM and Med-PaLM 2 report 57.6% and 72.3% on MedMCQA under a different protocol, so ours is a cheap, fast baseline and not a competitive system. We also say what our evidence cannot show: validation gains were about 1.7 to 2 times the test gains, and we could not separate the effect of reading several encoder layers from the other changes.

</details>


### [233] [Thinking Fast, Anywhere: One System-1 for the Decision Points of Any Agent Workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23751)
*Rui Li,Liyang He,Zheng Zhang,Zhenya Huang,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AnyS1 将人工编写的流程性技能文档转化为跨领域通用的“决策点”来源，离线挖掘可枚举决策、采集执行落地状态构建 Any-DP 数据集，训练单一 4B 指针式 System-1 模型，运行时由编译流程一次前向解决常规决策并低置信度回退 System-2，在三个未见基准上成本降 75-91%、调用时间降 63-92% 且可靠性不降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主 LLM 代理在每一步都调用大模型（System-2）导致高成本与高延迟，而许多中间动作只是在少量已知候选中做选择。现有双系统代理虽将此类决策下放给轻量 System-1，但依赖按环境逐个编写的 workflow-specific hooks，缺乏通用性。

Method: 以人类编写的流程性技能说明作为决策点来源（其指令天然指明选择出现的位置与适用选项）；离线在 1000+ 公开技能上挖掘可落地的可枚举决策，在隔离容器中采集执行落地轨迹状态构建 Any-DP 数据集；训练单个带标签平滑的校准 4B 指针式（pointer）System-1；运行时编译工作流以单次前向解决例行决策，置信度低于校准阈值时回退 System-2。

Result: 在三个未见基准与不同 System-2 模型族上，相比未编译的 System-2 代理，AnyS1 在保持任务可靠性的同时将单回合成本降低 75-91%、模型调用时间降低 63-92%，并在零样本阈值迁移下维持性能。

Conclusion: 流程性技能文档可作为领域通用的决策点来源，替代按环境定制的钩子；单一校准的轻量指针模型配合置信度门控回退，可在跨领域、跨 System-2 模型族的未见任务上显著降低开销而不牺牲可靠性。

Abstract: Autonomous LLM agents incur substantial cost and latency by invoking a large model (System-2) at every step, even when many intermediate actions choose among a small set of known candidates. Existing dual-system agents offload such decisions to a lightweight System-1 model, but rely on workflow-specific hooks authored per environment. We present AnyS1, a framework that turns human-authored procedural skills, whose instructions naturally specify where choices arise and which options apply, into a shared, domain-general source of decision points. Offline, AnyS1 mines grounded enumerable decisions across over 1,000 public skills, gathers execution-grounded trajectory states inside isolated containers to construct Any-DP, and trains a single calibrated 4B pointer System-1 with label smoothing. At run time, compiled workflows resolve routine decisions in one forward pass and fall back to System-2 when confidence falls below a calibrated threshold. Across three unseen benchmarks and distinct System-2 model families, AnyS1 preserves task reliability while cutting episode cost by 75-91% and model-call time by 63-92% relative to an uncompiled System-2 agent, and maintains performance under zero-shot threshold transfer.

</details>


### [234] [MoE-Context: Show the Model Only What It Needs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24379)
*Vedant Navale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MoE-Context 将上下文分块，每步用一次快速"是/否"判定决定哪些块参与计算，其余块折叠为一行摘要（可随时召回、不删除）；在单一 KV 缓存中以掩码隐藏块。笔记本电脑上 Qwen3-1.7B 的合成查找任务中，仅显示所需块使准确率从 93/120 升至 119/120；模型自身的选择是薄弱环节，监督线性探针可将 80/120 提升至 110/120，但不跨任务泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体在每一步都读取全部历史，与人类"按需保留、其余粗略备注"的记忆方式不符，造成冗余计算。作者希望以类似人类记忆的机制降低每步上下文开销，同时保证信息不丢失。

Method: 将上下文切分为块；每步前先做一次快速的二元（是/否）判定，选出本步所需块，其余块保留为单行"笔记"并可随时召回，不做任何删除。实现中由同一模型先决策后作答，共用单一 KV 缓存，隐藏块以掩码而非移除方式处理；另以监督线性探针写入空闲输出行，改进选块决策。

Result: Qwen3-1.7B + 合成查找任务（120 题）：仅显示所需块使准确率由 93 提升至 119；掩码块与删除块在效果上等同；模型自主选块较差（80），线性探针提升至 110，但简单关键词检索在该任务上表现完美，且探针无法迁移至第二任务。

Conclusion: 块级选择+掩码 KV 缓存在证据上可行且能显著提升准确率，但"由模型自主选块"仍是关键瓶颈，线性探针收益有限且不泛化。作者另报告两种低成本用法：撤销失败尝试、无需新建上下文即可运行子智能体；并公开全部（含负面）结果。

Abstract: AI agents read their whole history at every step. People do not work like that: we keep what the moment needs and hold the rest as a rough note we can recall. MoE-Context applies this to language models. The context is split into blocks. Before each step a fast yes or no pass decides which blocks the step needs; the rest stay as one-line notes that can be brought back, and nothing is deleted. In the version we build, one model makes this decision and then answers, over a single key-value cache where hidden blocks are masked rather than removed. On a laptop with Qwen3-1.7B and a synthetic lookup task, showing only the needed block raises accuracy from 93 to 119 of 120, and a masked block is as hidden as a deleted one. The model's own picking is the weak part. A supervised linear probe written into spare output rows lifts its accuracy from 80 to 110 of 120, but simple keyword search picks perfectly on this task, and the probe does not transfer to a second task. We report these results openly, along with two cheap usage patterns that block switching enables: undoing a failed attempt and running subagents without a new context.

</details>


### [235] [Space-X: Self-Evolving Spatial Agents Weaving Clues into Evidence Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24004)
*Zijian Zhang,Wenqing Tian,Qiang Liu,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Space-X：面向空间推理的自我进化工具增强框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具增强智能体在空间推理中缺乏明确的证据获取目标，跨工具输出存在物体指代与参考系语义不一致，且对证据充分性判断不足，导致增加工具或调用次数并不能稳定提升推理质量。

Method: 提出 Space-X 框架，由两个智能体构成闭环：Execution Agent 借助角色专属记忆对每个问题进行证据的获取、关联与判断；Control Agent 从执行轨迹中诊断证据链断点，并联合修订工具、接口、技能、记忆组织与决策规则，形成"证据需求驱动能力发展、可用结果引导行动"的共进化循环。

Result: 在多个基准与四个基座模型上均取得提升；相较基线，GPT-6-sol 在 ViewSpatial 与 MMSI 上的答案准确率分别提升 50.39% 与 23.07%，Gemini-3.8-Flash 分别提升 47.50% 与 25.22%。

Conclusion: Space-X 建立了从碎片化空间线索到可追溯、可推导答案的统一路径，通过证据链完整性与连续性驱动的自我进化，实现能力与证据使用的协同提升。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning connects visual observations, geometric estimates, and linguistic decisions into a coherent derivation. Yet tool-augmented agents often acquire evidence without clear targets, combine outputs under inconsistent object or reference-frame semantics, and misjudge evidence sufficiency. Thus, more tools or calls do not reliably improve reasoning. We introduce Space-X, a spatial-reasoning framework that self-evolves toward complete, continuous evidence-chain construction. An Execution Agent acquires, associates, and judges evidence per problem using role-specific memory. A Control Agent diagnoses evidence-chain breaks from execution traces and jointly revises tools, interfaces, skills, memory organization, and decision rules. This loop co-evolves capability and evidence use: evidence needs guide capability development; usable results guide actions. Across multiple benchmarks, Space-X yields gains with four base models. Relative to baselines, GPT-6-sol improves answer accuracy on ViewSpatial and MMSI by 50.39% and 23.07%, respectively; Gemini-3.8-Flash gains 47.50% and 25.22% on these benchmarks. This provides a unified route from fragmented spatial clues to traceable, derivable answers.

</details>


### [236] [Why Sub-4-Bit Vision CNNs Collapse and Where Mixed-Precision Recovery Stops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23445)
*Michael Schroeder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文系统剖析了视觉CNN在亚4比特量化下的崩溃机理（非线性、集中于后期pointwise投影与depthwise层），提出多步选择性混合精度的恢复流程，用<5%关键层以INT8保留可恢复92%的损失精度；并在ARM/x86 CPU上实测发现低比特带来的解包开销导致1.48x–21.5x执行减速，揭示存储收益与边缘吞吐的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限环境下深度视觉模型高度依赖低比特量化，但权重压缩至4比特以下常引发精度灾难性崩溃；现有混合精度恢复策略虽能补救精度，却缺乏对"崩溃发生在何处、为何发生"的机理理解，且缺少对真实硬件部署开销的评估。

Method: 在ResNet与EfficientNet骨干上、基于完整ImageNet-1k 50,000张验证集进行系统分析：先逐层映射退化进程以定位崩溃位置；再构建结构化的多步恢复"瀑布"流程，评估选择性整型精度（INT4/INT8）与选择性浮点缩放；最后在ARM与x86 CPU上评测实际部署性能。

Result: 退化高度非线性，集中于后期pointwise投影层与depthwise层；仅保留不足5%的关键层激活为8比特即可恢复激进亚4比特压缩所损失精度的92%；低比特虽降低内存占用，但运行时软件位解包造成1.48x至21.5x的执行减速，且随batch size变化。

Conclusion: 论文给出亚4比特部署的实用蓝图：崩溃具有可预测的结构性定位，少量关键层的选择性高精度保留即可高效止损；但需正视存储足迹与边缘实际吞吐之间的关键权衡，不能仅以模型体积衡量收益。

Abstract: Deep vision models operating in resource-constrained environments depend heavily on low-bit quantization, yet compressing weights below 4 bits frequently induces catastrophic accuracy collapse. While mixed-precision recovery strategies aim to rescue accuracy by selectively retaining high-precision parameters, the fundamental mechanics of where and why these models collapse remain poorly understood. In this study, we systematically analyze the anatomy of quantization collapse across standard Vision CNNs (including ResNet and EfficientNet backbones) evaluated on the full 50,000-image ImageNet-1k validation set. First, we map the exact progression of degradation, demonstrating that collapse is highly non-linear and concentrated in late-stage pointwise projections and depthwise layers. Second, we introduce a structured, multi-step recovery waterfall that evaluates selective integer precision (INT4/INT8) and selective floating-point scales. We show that keeping less than 5% of critical layer activations in 8-bit precision recovers up to 92% of the accuracy lost during aggressive sub-4-bit compression. Finally, we bridge the hardware reality gap by evaluating deployment performance on standard ARM and x86 CPU architectures. We show that while low-bit quantization reduces memory storage requirements, standard runtime software bit-unpacking introduces a 1.48x to 21.5x execution slowdown depending on batch size, highlighting a critical trade-off between model footprint and actual edge throughput. Our findings provide a practical blueprint for navigating the limits of sub-4-bit deployment without sacrificing hardware performance.

</details>


### [237] [Can you make models believe false facts in the prompt?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23938)
*Ananya Sutradhar,Fabien Roger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将合成文档微调（SDF）扩展为"提示条件化 SDF"，训练出单个模型，使其相信提示 `<facts>` 标签内任意给定的事实，且信念深度随训练事实数近似对数线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让 LLM 相信虚假事实有助于对齐评估与构建"模型生物"（model organisms）。但原始 SDF 需为每套注入的虚假信念单独微调一个新模型，成本随评估规模线性膨胀，难以支撑大规模对齐评估。

Method: 提出提示条件化 SDF：在覆盖数千个事实的合成文档上微调单一模型，每篇文档以提示中给出的一条事实为条件；评估时只需在提示 `<facts>` 标签中放入目标事实。采用前人评估指标及新提出的 Jacobian lens 评估信念深度；并尝试在提示条件化 SDF 之上用 RL 降低模型对提示中虚假事实的"口头复述"率，以提升信念深度。

Result: 对未见事实的信念深度随训练事实数量近似对数线性增长；RL 在提示条件化 SDF 之上降低了口头复述率，且可泛化到留出评估集，在部分评估上接近原生 SDF 的水平。但用其中一个信念深度指标作为训练目标会削弱该指标作为评估的可信度。

Conclusion: 提示条件化 SDF 是一种廉价、有前景的"让模型相信虚假事实"的规模化路径。但其在对齐审计中的初步实验呈负面结果：受研究的小模型在普通蜜罐系统提示下本已表现得像是相信所给事实，导致注入收益不明显。未来需在更大模型上验证，并注意指标被优化后产生的评估失真问题。

Abstract: Making LLMs believe false facts is useful for alignment evaluations and for building model organisms. Previous work explored doing so with Synthetic Document Finetuning (SDF). But SDF requires fine-tuning one new model for each set of injected false beliefs. To enable larger scale SDF-powered alignment evaluations, we attempt to produce a single model that believes any fact placed in a <facts> tag in the prompt. To do so, we explore doing prompt-conditioned SDF: fine-tuning a model on synthetic documents covering thousands of facts, with each document conditioned on a fact presented in the prompt. We evaluate how deeply the model believes the facts provided in the prompt using evaluations from prior work and a new Jacobian lens evaluation. We find that the belief depth on unseen facts scales roughly log-linearly with the number of training facts, making this a cheap, promising avenue for making models believe false facts, though preliminary experiments on applying it to alignment audits on the small model we studied showed negative results due to these small models often already acting as if they believed facts provided in regular honeypot system prompts. Using RL to reduce the rate at which models verbalize following a false fact in the prompt, on top of prompt-conditioned SDF, generalizes to held-out evals and almost matches SDF on some evaluations. But training against one of our metrics for belief depth makes it harder to trust those evaluations.

</details>


### [238] [ABSClaw: From Invented Worlds to Reality-Grounded Social Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24517)
*Shaobin He,Yan Gao,Quan Zheng,Zhenyang Lin,Zhen Liu,Jiyan He,Shuxin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ABSClaw 是一个零代码智能体，将自然语言请求编译为可执行、基于现实数据的社交模拟，并配套提出包含193个真实世界任务的 ABSBench 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的人工社会缺乏科学依据：人格与环境为假定值、交互网络为合成数据、模拟器由自由生成的代码构成，导致结果难以与真实人群对应且对骨干模型敏感。作者主张模拟世界应由观测数据采样，模拟逻辑应由共享的、任务无关的操作词汇组合而成。

Method: 提出 ABSClaw：在"谁行动、在哪行动、与谁交互"三个层面分别锚定调查与人口普查数据、地理空间数据源和真实网络；以26个原子操作表达状态转移；通过生命周期作用域的技能机制在不改动核心的前提下扩展能力；并提出包含193个真实世界社交模拟任务的基准 ABSBench。

Result: 在 ABSBench 上，ABSClaw 在所评估的社交模拟器中取得最佳整体表现；三项真实案例表明，分别锚定人格、环境和交互网络均能提升与观测结果的一致性；借助一个运行时技能，ABSClaw 执行了13个种群规模模拟，合计涉及2500万个智能体。

Conclusion: 结果共同证明 ABSClaw 具备现实保真度、可扩展性与可伸缩性，为数据驱动的社交模拟提供了可复用的框架与评测基准。

Abstract: LLM-based artificial societies exhibit rich behavior but often lack scientific grounding: personas and environments are assumed, interaction networks are synthetic, and simulators are generated as free-form code, making results difficult to tie to real populations and sensitive to the backbone model. We argue that simulated worlds should be sampled from observed data, while simulation logic should be implemented by composing operations from a shared, task-independent vocabulary. In this paper, we introduce ABSClaw, a zero-code agent that compiles natural-language requests into executable, reality-grounded social simulations. ABSClaw grounds who acts, where they act, and with whom they interact in survey and census data, geospatial sources, and real networks. It expresses state transitions through 26 atomic operations. To support an open and extensible ecosystem, ABSClaw further allows capabilities to be added as lifecycle-scoped skills without modifying its core. Furthermore, we propose ABSBench, a benchmark of 193 real-world social-simulation tasks. On ABSBench, ABSClaw achieves the best overall performance among the evaluated social simulators. Three real-world case studies show that grounding personas, environments, and interaction networks each improves agreement with observed outcomes. With one runtime skill, ABSClaw executes 13 population-scale simulations, collectively involving 25 million agents. Together, these results demonstrate ABSClaw's real-world fidelity, extensibility, and scalability.

</details>


### [239] [Study and Improvement of Search Algorithms in Stochastic Perfect-Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24486)
*Quentin Cohen-Solal,Tristan Cazenave*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将确定性完全信息零和二人博弈的最先进搜索算法 Unbounded Minimax 推广到随机（含机会节点）完全信息零和二人博弈框架，并通过实验证明其性能优于主流随机搜索算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Unbounded Minimax 是目前确定性、完全信息、零和二人博弈中最先进的搜索算法，但随机博弈（存在机会/随机节点与环境不确定性）场景下尚缺乏对应的推广，而现有随机搜索算法在该场景下性能有限，因此有必要将该算法范式拓展至随机博弈以提升搜索效率与决策质量。

Method: 把 Unbounded Minimax 的算法框架从确定性博弈推广到概率性（随机）完全信息零和二人博弈，核心在于在博弈树中引入对随机节点期望的处理，并使算法保留原方法在搜索规模与解质量之间的无界/渐进特性；随后在实验中将该推广算法与若干主流随机搜索算法进行对比评测。

Result: 实验结果表明，该推广后的 Unbounded Minimax 算法取得了优于主要随机搜索算法的性能表现。

Conclusion: Unbounded Minimax 可自然地推广到随机完全信息零和二人博弈，且推广后的算法在实证上优于现有主流随机搜索算法，说明该确定性搜索范式在随机博弈领域具有良好适用性与竞争力。

Abstract: In this article, we generalize Unbounded Minimax, the state- of-the-art search algorithm for deterministic zero-sum two- player games with perfect information to the framework of stochastic zero-sum two-player games with perfect informa- tion. We experimentally show that this generalized algorithm achieves better performance than the main stochastic search algorithms.

</details>


### [240] [Benchmarking LLM abilities on postdoc-level natural science problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24321)
*Alina Avanesyan,Alexey Boldyrev,Liqun Deng,Denis Derkach,Mary Godunova,Mikhail Hushchyn,Dmitry Ilvovsky,Irina Knyazeva,Irina Piontkovskaya,Dmitry Polubotko,Fedor Ratnikov,Ekaterina Trofimova,Zhenhe Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: STEMBench 以各领域"同行评审认可"的标准（field's figure of merit）直接评测智能体：12 个高能物理、天体物理与神经科学科研问题各自按领域内公认指标打分，并给出两个实测锚点（无领域知识基线 vs. 复现已发表方法的专家参照），用两者区间衡量"科学洞察"而非"工程能力"的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学问题没有标准答案，评测智能体就必须在运行前先确定"何为更好解"。现有基准只用符号答案比对、单元测试、评分量表或竞赛排名，这些都不是一个学科判定"某方法优于另一方法"的真实准则，因而缺乏生态效度。

Method: 构建含 12 个研究问题的 STEMBench，覆盖高能物理、天体物理、神经科学；每题采用本领域 own figure of merit 评分；为每题提供两个由基准自带代码运行得到的实测锚点（无领域知识提交、复现已发表方法的专家提交）；据此定义"科学洞察区间"；对 8 个前沿模型进行评测，含高投入跨模型比较，并分别报告数值分数与官方提交有效性。

Result: 任务级胜出与领域级领先出现解离：赢得 12 题中 5 题的模型并未在任何领域均值上领先。高投入比较中，至少一个模型在 7 题上均值超过专家锚点；剩余 5 题集中在不确定性估计、潜变量与因果推断、跨站点临床分类。数值分数与官方提交有效性反映不同侧面，需分别报告。

Conclusion: 以领域公认指标加双锚点区间作为评测准则，可更真实地刻画智能体的科研能力并区分"领域洞察"与"工程调优"；单一聚合均值或单一胜场数会掩盖能力结构差异，应同时报告分数与提交有效性。基准的任务、评分器、锚点与运行框架已开源。

Abstract: Scientific problems have no answer key, so evaluating an agent means deciding what counts as a better solution before any agent is run. Existing benchmarks either match a symbolic answer against a key or, where they require executable work, score it by unit tests, by a rubric, or by position on a competition leaderboard. None of these is the criterion by which a field decides that one published method beats another. STEMBench adopts that criterion directly. Each of its twelve research problems in high-energy physics, astrophysics and neuroscience is scored by its own field's figure of merit, and each comes with two measured anchors: a submission that uses no domain knowledge, and an expert reference reproducing a published method, both obtained by running code shipped with the benchmark. The interval between them states how much of a problem is crossed by scientific insight rather than by engineering. Across eight frontier models, task-level wins and domain-level leadership dissociate: a model that wins five of the twelve tasks leads no domain mean. In the high-effort cross-model comparison, at least one model's mean score exceeds the expert anchor on seven of twelve tasks. The remaining five span uncertainty estimation, latent and causal inference, and cross-site clinical classification. Numerical scores and official-submission validity capture different aspects of performance and are reported separately. We release the tasks, graders, anchors and harness.

</details>


### [241] [GALA: Gallery-Level Alignment for Real-Life Vision Decoding from EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24206)
*Philippe Forest,Giulia Lioi,Mai Pham Quyen,Alix Lamouroux,Javen Shi,Adrien Merlini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“增量转导校准”新设定与免标签方法 GALA（画廊级对齐），在 THINGS-EEG-2 上把新被试的 top-1 准确率从 28% 提升到 67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG 视觉解码在被试内表现良好，但跨被试泛化差（THINGS-EEG-2 上 top-1 从 80%+ 降至约 32%）；而适配新被试/新会话通常需要标注数据，标注采集耗时成为设备部署瓶颈。实际 BCI 中候选集（画廊）事先已知，用户的无标签 EEG 随使用持续累积，故有必要提出更贴近部署条件的免标签校准设定。

Method: 提出“增量转导校准”设定：解码器在目前已接收的全部无标签 EEG 与已知画廊上定期校准；协议中查询逐条到达、频率未知且可能不均衡，每条查询先解码再用于校准。方法 GALA 通过收缩白化、hubness 校正，以及带缓冲估计查询频率的 Sinkhorn–Procrustes 对齐，将无标签 EEG 嵌入配准到画廊空间，每处理一条查询后更新。

Result: 在 THINGS-EEG-2 的 200 类画廊上，新被试经 53 分钟使用后 top-1 由 28% 升至 67%；在改变画廊规模、查询频率、每查询平均重复次数的 60 种评估设置中，GALA 均为最准确的免标签方法，流结束时平均领先最佳对比方法 6.6 个百分点。

Conclusion: 免标签、增量式的转导校准可显著缓解跨被试泛化差距，GALA 在多种部署条件下稳定优于现有免标签方法，为实用 BCI 的在线自适应提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Neural decoding of visual experience from electroencephalography (EEG) has achieved strong performance in intra-subject settings, but current methods fail to generalize across individuals: on the standard 200-way benchmark THINGS-EEG-2, top-1 accuracy drops from above 80% within subjects to about 32% for an unseen subject. Adapting a pre-trained encoder to new subjects and sessions typically requires labeled data, a bottleneck whose collection time severely limits practical device deployment. In a practical Brain-Computer Interface (BCI), however, the possible outcomes are known in advance and form a gallery of candidates, and the unlabeled EEG of the user accumulates as soon as the device is used. We propose a new setting, incremental transductive calibration, in which the decoder is calibrated regularly on all the unlabeled EEG received so far and on the known gallery, without any labels. This setting is closer to the conditions of a deployed BCI than the zero-shot and batch evaluations in current use. We evaluate it with a protocol in which queries arrive one at a time, with unknown and possibly unequal frequencies, and each query is decoded before it is used for calibration. In this setting, we introduce GALA : GAllery-Level Alignment, a label-free calibration method which registers the unlabeled EEG embeddings onto the gallery through shrinkage whitening, hubness correction and a Sinkhorn--Procrustes alignment with query frequencies estimated from the buffer, updated after every query. On THINGS-EEG-2, with a gallery of 200 images, it raises the top-1 accuracy on a new subject from 28% to 67% over 53 minutes of use.
